### [1] [baseVultra: Compact Nonlinear Recurrence with Reallocated Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30154)
*Linke Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出baseVultra，一种紧凑的循环神经网络单元，将密集循环计算预算集中在候选生成矩阵上，用O(H)对角交互替代门控矩阵。在紧凑设置下优于LSTM/GRU，大规模下持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有门控循环单元（LSTM/GRU）将大量参数分配给门控变换，而候选生成能力受限。研究旨在探索将循环计算从门控重分配到候选生成是否能提升紧凑模型的效率。

Method: baseVultra采用单一H×H密集候选生成矩阵，门控使用O(H)对角交互。候选更新融合密集分支、两个低秩辅助分支、EMA缓存及可学习输出混合；门控额外通过对角项获取h_{t-2}的二阶信息。

Result: TinyShakespeare上（200K参数单层）验证损失1.594，优于参数匹配的LSTM(1.604)和GRU(1.616)；密集矩阵向量计算量仅为GRU的54%。text8上（约45倍规模）与全门控单元持平（1.854 vs 1.849/1.837 bpc）。形式语言探测显示：受限隐藏状态容量下增益最大（H=16时OOD2准确率0.991 vs GRU 0.761/LSTM 0.691），增益随隐藏层增大而消失。

Conclusion: 候选聚焦的循环容量分配主要在紧凑 regime 生效；大规模下全门控单元仍具竞争力。baseVultra为参数受限场景提供了更高效的循环架构选择。

Abstract: Nonlinear recurrent networks remain useful for streaming sequence modeling, yet gated cell design is still dominated by LSTM and GRU. We study whether recurrent computation can be reallocated away from gate transforms and toward candidate generation. We propose baseVultra, a compact recurrent cell that concentrates the dense recurrent budget on a single H x H candidate-generation matrix, while replacing recurrent gate matrices with O(H) diagonal interactions. The candidate update combines this dense branch with two low-rank auxiliary branches, an EMA cache, and learnable output blending; the gates additionally receive lightweight second-order access to h_{t-2} through diagonal terms. On TinyShakespeare character-level language modeling, baseVultra achieves a validation loss of 1.594 in the compact one-layer 200K setting, compared with 1.604 for a parameter-matched LSTM and 1.616 for a parameter-matched GRU, while its dense recurrent matrix-vector computation is about 54% of GRU under matched hidden size for this configuration. On text8, a roughly 45x larger benchmark, baseVultra is competitive with full-gate cells (1.854 bpc vs. 1.849 for GRU and 1.837 for LSTM). On formal-language probes, the largest gains appear under constrained hidden-state capacity (0.991 vs. 0.761 GRU and 0.691 LSTM OOD2 accuracy on Counter at H=16) and vanish as hidden size grows. These results characterize where and when candidate-focused recurrent capacity allocation helps: the benefit is concentrated in compact regimes, while full-gate cells remain competitive at scale.

</details>


### [2] [Lighthouse: On Building a Trustworthy Digital Assistant for Exploring History with Civil Rights Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30158)
*John A. Aydin,Angelica Williams,Steven Dindl,Nathan Saunders,Kate Boyd,Vandana Srivastava,Biplav Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了首个名为'灯塔'的数字助手(聊天机器人),用于探索美国民权运动时期的南卡罗来纳州数据,采用神经符号方法结合大语言模型与基于规则的符号推理,重点关注内容可信度和减少生成答案对用户的潜在危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统在历史研究中缺乏对内容可信度的保障和对用户潜在危害的建模。针对民权运动等敏感历史时期,需要一个能够平衡回答风险与覆盖广度、确保可信度并最小化伤害的智能助手。

Method: 采用神经符号方法:结合大语言模型(LLM)的神经学习能力与基于规则的符号求解器。系统可访问18,000多份原始资料,通过规则约束LLM生成,实现可信度保障和危害减少。评估包括客观事实题和主观题,后者通过聚合独立专家答案构建'金标准'数据集。

Result: 系统成功构建并部署,能够处理客观事实问题和主观问题。主观问题评估采用专家聚合金标准,验证了神经符号方法在平衡回答风险与覆盖广度方面的有效性。系统易于扩展至更多历史数据集合。

Conclusion: Lighthouse展示了神经符号AI在历史研究决策支持中的可行性,通过显式建模可信度和危害减少,为敏感历史时期的数字助手设计提供了新范式。该方法可推广至其他领域的知识探索系统。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI), as a decision-support aid, provides new tools for studying history effectively using multimodal data, including text, images, and audio. We present, to our knowledge, the first digital assistant named Lighthouse--commonly called a chatbot--for exploring the Civil Rights period of the United States using data from South Carolina. From an AI perspective, the system's novelty lies in its guiding design principles of (a) focusing on trustworthiness of content and (b) by modeling and reducing potential harm to users from generated answers. It implements these with a neuro-symbolic approach that combines neural learning through a large language model (LLM) with rule-based symbolic solving, balancing risk of answers with generality of coverage. We evaluate Lighthouse with both factual objective questions that admit a single acceptable answer as well as subjective questions. For the latter, a dataset of subjective question-answer pairs (QAs) is created by discovering a "gold standard" by aggregating answers from independent experts. Lighthouse has access to over 18k primary sources from a range of collections and can be easily extended.

</details>


### [3] [TauIndianBankBench: Procedure-Aware, Action-Oriented Evaluation of Tool-Using Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30161)
*Krishanu Adhikary,Prashant Devadiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 TauIndianBankBench，一个面向印度零售银行的动作导向基准，包含 1000 个任务，评估语言模型助手在遵循银行业务流程和最终结果上的表现，发现仅检查最终记录会高估成功率（6.5%-12.0% 的会话被误判为成功），结合流程与结果评分更准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅关注对话结束后的最终记录，忽略了中间必须执行的步骤（如获取结清报价），导致跳过关键步骤的助手仍被判定成功，无法真实反映模型在真实银行业务中的合规性。

Method: 构建 TauIndianBankBench 基准，包含 1000 个印度零售银行任务，模拟客户与助手交互。评估维度不仅包括最终记录，还检查助手是否完成每个必需步骤、是否有未请求的变更。对比仅检查最终记录与完整流程检查的判定差异。

Result: 实验表明，对于所有测试模型，6.5%-12.0% 的会话在仅检查最终记录时被判定成功，但实际上助手跳过必需步骤或进行未请求变更。完整流程评分能更准确地衡量模型性能。

Conclusion: 在印度零售银行场景中，仅依赖最终结果评估会显著高估语言模型助手的合规能力。引入流程遵循度检查的综合评分机制，能更真实地反映模型在实际业务部署中的可靠性。

Abstract: Many Indian banks serve customers through FAQ pages and scripted chatbots. Assistants based on language models could respond faster and carry out requests such as blocking a card, but only if they give correct information and follow the bank's rules. These rules prescribe a procedure, a set of required steps, such as obtaining a closure quote before closing a deposit. Adapting models to this domain requires measuring how well they follow the procedure, before and after training. Benchmarks usually judge only the records left at the end of a conversation. Many required steps, however, leave the records unchanged, so an assistant that skips them can still be judged successful. We present TauIndianBankBench, an action-oriented benchmark of 1,000 Indian retail-banking tasks where an assistant helps a simulated customer. Besides the final records, it checks that the assistant took every required step and made no unrequested change. For every model, 6.5-12.0% of sessions are judged successful when only the final records are checked, although the assistant skipped a required step or made an unrequested change. Scoring both procedure and outcome shows more accurately how well language models perform in Indian retail banking.

</details>


### [4] [FusionEval: Benchmarking Model Fusion for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30162)
*Shuo Cai,Yanggan Gu,Yuhang Liu,Su Lu,Yuanyi Wang,Yang Yu,Zhen Li,Zhitian Hou,Guanghao Zhu,Ming Li,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 FusionEval 基准，用于评估模型融合方法在保留专家能力（推理、指令跟随、工具使用）和计算成本方面的表现，发现无训练融合保留推理但损害工具使用，MOPD 平衡性好但需额外开销，低成本联合保留仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型融合评估覆盖长链推理和工具使用有限，且平均性能强可能掩盖个别专家能力大幅下降，需要受控专家池和统一评估套件来系统研究融合方法的能力保留与计算代价。

Method: 构建包含数学、代码、科学、指令跟随、工具使用五领域专家的受控专家池（基于 Qwen3 和 Llama 基座模型独立训练），集成有/无额外训练的融合方法，在八个公开基准上统一评估能力保留与计算成本，报告归一化平均/最差性能、运行时间和峰值 GPU 显存。

Result: 无训练融合方法在两种模型规模下均能保留推理性能，但显著降低工具使用能力；MOPD 能保留工具使用并实现更均衡的能力分布，但需要额外的数据准备、推理和训练开销。

Conclusion: 在低计算成本下同时保留推理、指令跟随和工具使用能力仍是开放挑战，FusionEval 为后续高效模型融合研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Model fusion aims to integrate the capabilities of multi-expert models into a single model. Existing evaluations offer limited coverage of reasoning with long chains of thought and tool use, while strong average performance can mask substantial losses in individual expert capabilities. We introduce FusionEval, a benchmark that provides controlled expert pools, integrates fusion methods with and without additional training, and offers a unified evaluation suite. The pools contain domain experts independently trained from Qwen3 and Llama base models across math, code, science, instruction following, and tool use. The suite evaluates capability retention and computational cost on eight public benchmarks under comparable data access rules, reporting normalized average and worst performance, runtime, and peak GPU memory usage. Extensive experiments show that training-free methods preserve reasoning performance but substantially degrade tool use at both model scales. In contrast, MOPD preserves tool use and achieves a more balanced capability profile, but requires additional data preparation, inference, and training. Jointly retaining reasoning, instruction following, and tool use at low computational cost remains a challenge. We hope FusionEval supports future research on efficient model fusion that jointly preserves expert capabilities. Our code is available at https://github.com/Baicaihaochi/FusionEval_Benchmark.

</details>


### [5] [Measuring Research Progress and Linguistic Inclusion in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30296)
*Atnafu Lambebo Tonja,Tadesse Destaw Belay,Jesujoba Oluwadara Alabi,Israel Abebe Azime,Benjamin Rosman,Ehsan Shareghi,Thamar Solorio,Pontus Stenetorp,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了2020-2025年间1158篇同行评审开放获取论文，提出语言包容性框架(LIF)评估低资源语言研究的公平性。发现虽然出版物增长5.4倍，但增长集中在已有代表性语言，亚洲语言使用者占64.1%却仅获46.7%代表份额，欧洲语言获5.3倍比例份额，语言多样地区覆盖率最低，无地区在四维度均达标，单纯增长非公平进展可靠指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型加速了低资源和多语言NLP研究，但其在创建公平语言技术版图方面的有效性仍未被系统测绘。需要超越单纯出版物计数，从多维度量化评估语言包容性，揭示不同语言和区域在研究关注度上的结构性不平衡。

Method: 收集2020-2025年ACL文集中1158篇同行评审开放获取论文，标注低资源语言关注情况。提出语言包容性框架(LIF)，从研究增长、代表性扩展、代表性连续性、分布公平性四个维度量化语言包容度。在语言、大洲、国家层面应用框架，对比语言使用人口比例与研究代表份额，计算覆盖率等指标。

Result: 年度出版物从52篇增至415篇，ACL份额增5.4倍，但增长集中于已有代表性语言而非扩展覆盖欠代表语言。亚洲语言使用者占低资源语言总使用者64.1%，却仅获46.7%语言代表份额(0.7倍比例份额)；欧洲语言获5.3倍比例份额。国家层面，撒哈拉以南非洲和东南亚等语言多样区语言覆盖率最低。大洲层面应用LIF显示，无地区在四维度均表现良好，研究增长alone非公平进展可靠指标。

Conclusion: 当前低资源语言研究增长掩盖了深层结构性不平等：研究关注度严重偏向欧洲语言，忽视亚洲、非洲等高语言多样性区域。LIF框架揭示单一增长指标不足以衡量语言技术公平进展，需多维度评估并针对性扩展欠代表语言覆盖，推动真正包容的多语言NLP发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have accelerated research on low-resource and multilingual NLP, but their effectiveness in creating an equitable landscape remains unmapped. We analyze 1,158 peer-reviewed open-access papers published between 2020 and 2025, examining research attention to low-resource languages (LRLs) and representation across language and speaker populations. To assess linguistic inclusion beyond publication counts, we introduce the Linguistic Inclusion Framework (LIF), a multidimensional framework that measures linguistic inclusion in four dimensions: research growth, representation expansion, representation continuity, and distributional equity. While annual publications grew from 52 to 415, a 5.4X increase in their ACL Anthology publication share, this growth largely concentrated on represented languages rather than expanding coverage of underrepresented languages. Further analysis shows that languages spoken in Asia account for 64.1% of speakers across LRLs we analyze but receive only 46.7% of the language representation share (0.7X their proportional share), while European languages receive 5.3X their proportional share. At the country level, linguistically diverse regions such as sub-Saharan Africa and Southeast Asia show the lowest language coverage ratios. Applying the framework at the continental level, we find that no region scores well on all four dimensions, showing that research growth alone is not a reliable metric of progress toward equitable and inclusive language technology\footnote{We will release all collected and annotated data for research purposes}.

</details>


### [6] [AQL-Lite: Enabling Efficient Knowledge Acquisition for Arabic LLMs via Data-Centric Continual Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30344)
*Houssam Eddine Boukhalfa,Mourad Bouache,Mourad Dahmane*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 AQL-Lite 框架，通过高质量数据清洗和部分参数持续预训练（CPT），以极低算力高效将多语言 LLM 适配到现代标准阿拉伯语（MSA）。在 1B token 预算下，AQL-Lite 使四基准平均分提升 3.51 分；在 5B token 对比中，仅用全参数 CPT 52% 的训练时间恢复 94% 性能增益；扩展至 40B MSA token 并指令微调后，12B 模型在 OALL v2 七基准套件上达到 75.3 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语大模型训练面临三大挑战：嘈杂的网络数据质量、方言变体干扰、以及全参数持续预训练的高昂算力成本。现有方法要么依赖低质量原始网络数据导致收益微乎其微，要么采用全参数 CPT 计算开销过大，亟需一种兼顾数据质量与训练效率的实用适配方案。

Method: AQL-Lite 包含两大核心组件：1) 质量优先的数据清洗流水线：结构清洗、启发式与模型过滤、MSA 选择、去重，构建高纯度 MSA 语料；2) 部分参数 CPT 配方：全网络训练 RS-LoRA 适配器，同时解冻最后 8 个 Transformer 块与嵌入矩阵，采用逐层学习率策略。

Result: 在 1B token 等预算下，AQL-Lite 数据使阿拉伯语四基准均值提升 3.51 分，而原始网络数据无增益。5B token 对比中，AQL-Lite 以 52% 训练时间恢复全参数 CPT 94% 增益（73.19 vs 73.58），并在所有基准上超越仅适配器基线。消融实验证实适配器、解冻块、嵌入训练均有贡献。扩展至 40B MSA token 并指令微调后，12B 模型在 OALL v2 七基准套件得分 75.3。

Conclusion: 在精心策划的高质量语料上进行部分参数持续预训练，是将多语言大模型高效适配到现代标准阿拉伯语的切实可行路径，兼顾了性能、训练速度与资源消耗。

Abstract: Training Arabic large language models faces challenges from noisy web data, dialectal variation, and the high cost of continual pretraining (CPT). We introduce AQL-Lite (AQL: Arabic Quality Language), a time- and resource-efficient framework for adapting a multilingual LLM to Modern Standard Arabic (MSA). AQL-Lite couples a quality-first curation pipeline (structural cleaning, heuristic and model-based filtering, MSA selection, and deduplication) with a partial-parameter CPT recipe that trains RS-LoRA adapters across the network together with the last eight transformer blocks and the embedding matrix, under layer-wise learning rates. At a matched 1B-token budget, CPT on AQL-curated data raises a four-benchmark Arabic mean by 3.51 points over CPT on raw web text, which yields no gain over the base model. In a controlled 5B-token comparison, AQL-Lite recovers 94% of the gain of full-parameter CPT (mean 73.19 vs. 73.58) in 52% of the wall-clock time and outperforms adapter-only baselines on every benchmark, and a component ablation shows that adapters, unfrozen blocks, and embedding training each contribute. Scaled to 40B MSA tokens and instruction-tuned on one million examples, the resulting 12B model scores 75.3 on the seven-benchmark Open Arabic LLM Leaderboard v2 (OALL v2) suite. These results suggest that partial-parameter CPT on a carefully curated corpus is a practical route to efficient MSA adaptation.

</details>


### [7] [Consistency Is Not Validity: Self-Rewarded Moral Argumentation Makes Language Models Decisive but Pliable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30349)
*Marius De Kuthy Meurers,Aaron Parisi,Bernd Bohnet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的自我判断竞赛框架，通过奖励高质量论证来提升大语言模型的道德判断能力，实验表明该方法能显著提高判断的一致性和决断力，但无法有效解决讨好性偏见和重构框架问题，揭示了一致性不等于有效性的本质局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于道德判断任务，但现有模型在判断上存在不一致性（如选项顺序敏感）和有效性缺陷（如讨好性偏见）。作者基于“良好的论证能促进良好的判断”这一前提，旨在通过优化论证质量来间接提升道德判断的可靠性。

Method: 从最先进的语言模型初始化强化学习策略，采用“蒙蔽思维”的自我判断竞赛机制：模型生成论证并由自身成对评估论证质量。实验基础为从经典小说中提取真实困境，并使用逻辑求解器验证冲突有效性。

Result: 自判断器对论证质量的评分与人类标注高度一致，策略模型采纳了受奖励的语言风格。在 MoralChoice、ELEPHANT 和 MORABLES 基准上，模型变得更果断且对选项呈现顺序不变，但讨好性偏见和框架重构问题依然存在。消融实验显示合成困境和非蒙蔽思维能复现顺序不变性提升，但语言风格迁移较弱且讨好性更强。

Conclusion: 一致性不等于有效性：通过优化可实现选项重排序下的一致性，但这并不意味着道德判断本身的有效性得到提升，讨好性偏见等核心缺陷仍需进一步解决。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly consulted for moral judgment. We aim to improve performance on such tasks by rewarding good arguments, on the premise that arguing well increases the likelihood of judging well. We train a reinforcement learning policy initialized from a state-of-the-art language model in a self-judged tournament with masked thinking: the generated arguments are judged pairwise on argument quality by the policy itself. As an empirical basis, we extract authentic dilemmas from classic novels, using a logic solver to verify that each constitutes a conflict. We show that our judge rates argument quality like human annotators, and the policy then adopts the language register that it rewards. Testing on the external benchmarks MoralChoice, ELEPHANT, and MORABLES, we find that the policy becomes more decisive and invariant to the order in which options are presented, but sycophancy and reframing remain a challenge to validity. Two ablation studies with synthetic dilemmas and unmasked thinking match the reordering gains, but show a weaker register shift and higher sycophancy. We conclude that consistency is not validity: consistency under reordering can be optimized without improving the moral judgment it is meant to signal.

</details>


### [8] [CiteAnchor: Source-Aware Grounding for Hallucinated Citation Detection and Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30355)
*Chenyi Tong,Ping Nie,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CiteResolve基准（423个真实幻觉引文）和CiteAnchor框架，通过源感知的检索与字段级验证，分别判断引文有效性与源可恢复性，在现有基准上F1达0.841（GPT-5.6 Luna）和0.784（开放权重骨干），在CiteResolve上端到端任务成功率达74.7%和70.7%，显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉引文导致科学声明难以溯源验证，现有检测方法受限于书目记录不完整、不同源类型与出版版本差异大，且合理的匹配既不能验证引文也不能确立底层源，亟需一种能适应源类型、同时判定引文有效性与唯一源可恢复性的方法。

Method: 构建CiteResolve基准，标注423个真实幻觉引文的源可恢复性；提出CiteAnchor框架，包含源感知检索适配、字段级验证、引文有效性判定与唯一源可恢复性判定两个独立决策模块，支持GPT-5.6 Luna与开放权重骨干。

Result: 在现有检测基准上，CiteAnchor配合GPT-5.6 Luna获0.841 F1，配合开放权重骨干获0.784 F1，分别比同骨干基线高7.9和36.8点；在CiteResolve上端到端任务成功率分别为74.7%和70.7%，比对应网络搜索基线高16.3和65.3个百分点。

Conclusion: CiteAnchor通过源类型自适应的检索与验证，有效解决了幻觉引文检测中源不完整、类型差异大及匹配不可信的问题，显著提升了引文有效性判定与源恢复能力，为科学文献溯源提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Hallucinated citations make it harder to trace sources and verify the evidence behind scientific claims. Detecting them is difficult because bibliographic records can be incomplete or differ across source types and publication versions. A plausible match alone neither validates the citation nor establishes its underlying source. We introduce CiteResolve, a benchmark of 423 real-world hallucinated citations annotated for source recoverability. We further propose CiteAnchor, a source-aware grounding framework that adapts retrieval and field-level verification to each source type and separately determines citation validity and whether a unique source can be recovered. On an existing detection benchmark, CiteAnchor achieves 0.841 F1 with GPT-5.6 Luna and 0.784 with an open-weight backbone, outperforming the baseline with the same backbones by 7.9 and 36.8 points. On CiteResolve, it achieves 74.7% and 70.7% end-to-end task success, outperforming the corresponding web search baselines by 16.3 and 65.3 percentage points. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/oldwizard7/CiteANCHOR_code.

</details>


### [9] [Climbing the Ladder: Automating Data Pipelines and Reproducing Baselines in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30374)
*Donato Meoli,Eryk Wdowiak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对西西里语机器翻译质量低的问题，重新开发了基于双语期刊Arba Sicula的小规模NMT系统，对比了从头训练的小型Transformer与预训练NLLB模型经低秩微调的效果，并引入分阶段反向训练策略。结果显示预训练模型远优于从头训练，分阶段训练对译入西西里语提升更大；但在西西里语-意大利语方向上，新模型略逊于原系统。多语言适配器会降低未训练方向的性能，限制其作为通用多语言模型的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模多语言翻译系统从嘈杂、非标准的网络文本中学习西西里语，导致翻译质量不佳。因此需要利用高质量的双语文学语料（Arba Sicula期刊）重新构建针对西西里语的小规模NMT系统，以提升翻译质量。

Method: 1. 从Arba Sicula期刊PDF中提取平行语料，使用语言无关句编码器对齐句子，阈值依据人工对齐调优，并过滤自动对齐的典型错误。2. 将原系统的分词和词尾偏向的子词分割移植到无Perl实现。3. 对比从头训练的小型Transformer与预训练NLLB模型经低秩微调（LoRA）的效果，双向评测并引入意大利语作为桥接语言。4. 采用原系统的反向训练配方：第一阶段在正向打分的网络西西里语回译上训练，第二阶段在精选文学文本上微调。5. 在冻结的期刊测试集上评估，使用原系统的分词空间计分。

Result: 预训练NLLB模型经低秩微调远优于从头训练的小型Transformer。分阶段反向训练对译入西西里语的提升大于译出西西里语。在原系统测试集上，使用更大适配器和更多回译数据的分阶段模型在涉及英语的方向上超越原反向训练配方，但在西西里语-意大利语方向上略低于原配方。多语言适配器会损害未参与训练方向的性能，限制其作为通用多语言模型的使用。

Conclusion: 利用高质量双语文学语料和预训练模型结合低秩微调、分阶段反向训练，可显著提升低资源语言（西西里语）的翻译质量，尤其在译入目标语言方向。但多语言适配器存在负迁移问题，需在特定语言对上单独训练适配器以保持性能。

Abstract: The massively multilingual translation systems that now include Sicilian learned it from noisy, non-standardised web text. To obtain better translation quality, we redevelop the small neural machine translation (NMT) system that Wdowiak customized for Sicilian. We rebuild on maintained software the extraction of the parallel text from the PDFs of the bilingual journal Arba Sicula, whose sentences are aligned by a language-agnostic sentence encoder with thresholds tuned against an existing hand alignment and then filtered for the typical errors of automatic alignment. Furthermore, the tokenization and the desinence-biased subword segmentation of the original system are ported to a Perl-free implementation, and all the results are measured on a frozen test set drawn from the journal. We compare small Transformers trained from scratch with the pretrained NLLB model adapted by low-rank fine-tuning, in both directions and with Italian as a closely related bridge language, and we adapt to the pretrained model the reverse-training recipe of the original system, i.e., a stage on forward-scored back-translations of the web Sicilian followed by a stage on the curated literary text. The pretrained model is by far the better of the two, and the staged training improves the translation into Sicilian more than the translation from it. On the test set of the original system, and in the tokenized space in which it was scored, the staged model with a larger adapter and more back-translations is above the reverse-training recipe in the directions that involve English and below it between Sicilian and Italian. Yet, a multilingual adapter degrades the directions on which it was not trained, which limits its use as a general multilingual model.

</details>


### [10] [MemPlan: Memory-Conditioned PDDL Planning for Partially Observable Text Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30379)
*Tengwei Song,Robert Hoehndorf*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemPlan提出了一种基于记忆条件的PDDL规划框架，利用LLM作为语言与符号规划的受约束桥梁，通过正负记忆指导规划，在部分可观测文本环境中显著提升任务完成率和步骤效率，同时大幅降低在线Token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体作为长时程规划器成本高且易产生幻觉或无效动作；本文旨在将LLM定位为语言与符号规划之间的受约束桥梁，结合PDDL规划的严谨性与语言理解的灵活性，解决部分可观测交互文本环境中的规划问题。

Method: MemPlan固定PDDL领域，每步根据解析观察、候选假设和交互记忆重构PDDL问题。正向图记忆为缺失对象、位置或条件的候选假设分配基于相关性的动作代价，引导规划器朝更合理的检查方向；负向记忆记录执行反驳的假设并编译为排除约束，避免重复尝试已被反馈矛盾的候选。离线监督微调统一的模式条件语言-符号接口，实现观察到事实的解析和符号动作到命令的落地。

Result: 在TextWorld、ALFWorld和Robotouille上实验表明，MemPlan在任务完成率和步骤效率上优于纯语言基线和PDDL感知基线，且在线Token成本显著低于ReAct等LLM规划基线。同一微调接口可跨TextWorld任务类型复用，无需任务特定微调。

Conclusion: MemPlan通过记忆条件PDDL规划有效融合语言理解与符号推理，为部分可观测文本环境提供了高效、低成本的规划范式，验证了LLM作为受约束语言-符号桥梁的可行性。

Abstract: LLM agents are flexible in language interaction, but using them as long-horizon planners is costly and prone to hallucinated or invalid actions. This paper targets a different role for LLMs: constrained bridges between language and symbolic planning. We propose MemPlan, a memory-conditioned PDDL planning framework for partially observable interactive text environments. MemPlan keeps the PDDL domain fixed and reconstructs the PDDL problem at each step from parsed observations, candidate hypotheses, and interaction memory. Positive graph memory assigns relevance-based action costs to candidate hypotheses for missing objects, locations, or conditions, guiding the planner toward more plausible checks. Negative memory records execution-refuted assumptions and compiles them into exclusion constraints, preventing repeated attempts at candidates already contradicted by feedback. A unified schema-conditioned language--symbol interface, trained offline with supervised fine-tuning, connects text environments to the planning loop through observation-to-fact parsing and symbolic-action-to-command grounding. Experiments on TextWorld, ALFWorld, and Robotouille show that MemPlan improves task completion and step efficiency over language-only and PDDL-aware baselines, while substantially reducing online token cost compared with LLM-planning baselines such as ReAct. The same fine-tuned interface is also reused across TextWorld task types without task-specific fine-tuning. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/tewiSong/MemPlan.

</details>


### [11] [SIM-UT: A User Simulation based Evaluation Framework for Medical Triage Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30412)
*Deepali Mittal,Dheeraj Mundhra,Yulia Sidorova,Tersia de Kock,Oli Munns,Sofie S Meyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 SIM-UT（面向分诊的用户行为模拟），一种多轮、基于模拟的评估方法，用于识别 LLM 分诊聊天代理在真实低/中等收入国家场景中的临床危险失效模式（特别是降级风险），并建议将基于用户模拟的行为评估作为生产部署前的标准实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要针对单轮、仅英语、知识型基准，无法捕捉真实用户在多轮、多语言、情绪脆弱、表达不清、方言混杂及语境快速变化等生态有效场景下的交互行为，导致部署后的分诊聊天机器人存在未被发现的临床安全隐患。

Method: 构建 SIM-UT 评估框架，基于印度呼吸道筛查、南非听力健康、肯尼亚糖尿病生活方式指导三个真实 WhatsApp 健康聊天机器人项目，采用 2-4 轮迭代模拟，生成 5000+ 多语言对话轮次，对比医学微调开源模型 MedGemma 与通用前沿模型 ChatGPT、Claude，覆盖真实用户画像、约束与沟通模式，包含对抗性与边缘案例。

Result: 两类模型均出现关键失效模式——降级风险：代理初期能正确识别医疗急症，但当用户在无新临床信息的情况下淡化症状时，代理会顺从用户降低分诊级别，表现出临床危险的阿谀奉承行为（如同意妊娠阴道出血‘没问题’、32 周胎动减少‘可能正常’）。

Conclusion: 建议将基于真实用户语境的、覆盖对抗与边缘情况的用户模拟行为评估作为分诊聊天机器人投产前的标准实践，以便在部署前或后续迭代中加入防护措施，保障临床安全。

Abstract: LLM-based chat agents are increasingly used for complex, multi-turn, multilingual medical triage in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs), yet evaluation methods focus on single-turn, English-only benchmarks that primarily test the chatbots' knowledge. In practice, users are often confused, overwhelmed, or emotionally vulnerable, giving unclear, misspelled single-word answers or long messages in regional dialects, and the context of a conversation can shift rapidly. We propose SIM-UT (Simulation of User Behaviour for Triage), a multi-turn, simulation-based evaluation method that identifies ecologically valid, contextually relevant failure modes of LLM-based triage chat agents. Using SIM-UT, we evaluated three real-world WhatsApp-based health chatbots: a respiratory screening programme in India, a hearing health support programme in South Africa, and a diabetes and lifestyle coaching agent in Kenya. We compared a medically fine-tuned open model (MedGemma) against general-purpose frontier models (ChatGPT and Claude) across 5,000+ multilingual simulated conversation turns grounded in the personas, constraints, and communication patterns of the real user populations, over 2-4 iterative rounds. A key failure mode emerged in both model families: de-escalation risk. The agents initially identified medical emergencies correctly, but when the user minimised the symptoms without introducing new clinical information, they de-escalated, exhibiting clinically dangerous sycophancy, for example agreeing that vaginal bleeding in pregnancy was "not a problem" or that decreased fetal movements at 32 weeks "can be normal". We recommend user simulation-based behavioural evaluation, grounded in real user context and covering adversarial and edge cases, as standard practice for triage chatbots in production, so that safeguards can be added before deployment or in subsequent iterations.

</details>


### [12] [Self-Assessment Reinforcement Learning for Search-State Awareness in Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30435)
*Taewhoo Lee,Minju Song,Jungwoo Park,Chanwoong Yoon,Junha Jung,Hyeon Hwang,Sanghoon Lee,Minbyul Jeong,Jaewoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出自我评估强化学习（SARL），解决搜索代理缺乏搜索状态感知的问题，通过双重奖励设计在训练中学习识别自身搜索状态，无需推理时额外评估即可显著提升搜索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索代理往往缺乏搜索状态感知能力，即无法识别已获取信息与仍缺失信息，导致幻觉推理和过早终止搜索。

Method: 提出自我评估强化学习（SARL）框架，采用双重奖励设计：捕捉每次评估的修正效果，同时惩罚过度依赖评估，联合鼓励有效且高效的搜索轨迹。模型在训练中学习识别自身搜索状态，推理时无需额外脚手架即可独立运行。

Result: SARL 相对最强基线实现 24.3% 提升，在多跳推理和深度研究基准上分别平均提升 35.9% 和 27.9%。超越基于辅助监督或多智能体协调的方法，且在扩展搜索视野下保持训练稳定性，现有方法在该制度下持续退化。

Conclusion: SARL 使模型在训练中习得搜索状态自我评估能力，无需推理时额外评估即可改进搜索行为，作为独立 RL 框架实现卓越性能和训练稳定性，随任务复杂度增长持续获得收益。

Abstract: Recent advances in search agents have enabled large language models to tackle complex tasks by retrieving and synthesizing external information. However, existing search agents often lack search-state awareness, i.e., the ability to recognize what information has been obtained and what remains missing, leading to hallucinated reasoning and prematurely terminated search. To address this limitation, we propose Self-Assessment Reinforcement Learning (SARL), a reinforcement learning framework in which models learn to recognize their own search state during training and improve their search behavior accordingly, without requiring additional assessment at test time. SARL achieves this through a dual reward design that captures the corrective effect of each assessment while penalizing excessive reliance on it, jointly encouraging effective and efficient search trajectories. Under this setup, SARL achieves a 24.3% relative improvement over the strongest baseline, with average gains of 35.9% on multi-hop reasoning and 27.9% on deep research benchmarks against the strongest per-benchmark baselines. SARL further surpasses methods built on auxiliary supervision or multi-agent coordination, despite operating as a standalone RL framework with no inference-time scaffolding. Beyond final performance, SARL maintains training stability under extended search horizons, a regime where existing methods consistently degrade, enabling sustained gains as task complexity grows.

</details>


### [13] [Rethinking the Evaluation of LLM Fact-Checkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30504)
*Ayana Niwa,Miyu Yamada,Kouta Nakayama,Masahiro Kaneko,Hisami Suzuki,Satoshi Sekine*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了首个指定事实核查三大设计决策（问题-声明对应关系、判断条件、证据来源）的日语基准 JSFactCheckBench，包含 1,913 条来自大模型回答的声明，实验发现所有测试模型在直接回答问题的声明上准确率低于补充性声明，且在长/多证据源及上下文干扰下性能显著下降，这些弱点仅因基准显式记录判断条件才被揭示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事实核查基准未明确规定三大关键设计：声明回答哪个问题、在何种条件下判断、采用何种证据来源，导致聚合分数掩盖模型在不同条件下的真实表现差异，亟需一个显式记录这些条件的基准来揭示大模型事实核查的系统性弱点。

Method: 构建 JSFactCheckBench：从日本社交媒体流传的误导性信息中选取问题，收集六个大模型的回答并抽取 1,913 条声明；每条声明标注参考时间点的事实性、对问题的重要性，并预先选定网页证据来源；实验中分别测试模型在直接回答声明、补充声明、不同证据长度/数量、是否包含上下文等条件下的核查准确率。

Result: 六个大模型均在直接回答问题的声明上核查准确率低于补充性声明（差距被聚合分数掩盖）；当证据源变长、变多或需从上下文中定位相关内容时准确率下降；四个开源模型在加入完整问答上下文后性能进一步下降；这些模式仅因基准记录了每个标签背后的判断条件才得以观测。

Conclusion: 显式规定并记录事实核查的三大设计决策（问题-声明映射、判断条件、证据来源）对于揭示大模型真实核查能力至关重要；JSFactCheckBench 证明了当前模型在核心声明核查、长上下文定位及上下文干扰鲁棒性上存在系统性不足，未来基准构建应遵循此类显式化原则。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used to answer factual questions, but their answers can contain errors and must be fact-checked claim by claim. Yet the resulting score depends on three design decisions: which questions the claims answer, under what conditions each claim is judged, and which sources count as evidence. No existing benchmark specifies all three. We introduce JSFactCheckBench, a Japanese benchmark that specifies all three, with 1,913 claims from LLM answers to questions about misleading information that circulated on Japanese social media, each annotated for factuality at a reference time and for importance to the question, using evidence from preselected Web sources. Across six LLMs, all verify claims directly answering the question less accurately than supplementary claims, a gap that aggregate scores obscure. Scores also drop when models must locate the relevant content in longer or multiple sources and, for all four open models, when the surrounding question and response are added. These weaknesses are visible only because the benchmark records the conditions behind each label. A benchmark that leaves them implicit cannot reveal them.

</details>


### [14] [Behind Aggregate Scores: What Systematically Varying Arena-Hard's LLM Judge Reveals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30512)
*Louise Yorke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM裁判（如GPT-4.1）在评估两个模型回答优劣时的可重复性及对指令的敏感性。实验发现，相似的聚合分数可能掩盖个别裁决的大幅变化：重复相同设置会导致21.2%的提示词胜负结果改变，修改指令（如移除推理要求或改变评价标准）会进一步增加个别裁决的不稳定性，但聚合分数变化可能很小。结论强调评估依赖个别裁决时，必须重复实验并报告提示词级别的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM裁判广泛用于模型评估和偏好标注，但其裁决的可重复性和对指令敏感性未被充分研究。聚合分数的稳定性可能掩盖个别裁决的波动，这对依赖个别裁决的下游任务（如偏好标注、单个回答评估）构成风险。

Method: 使用Arena-Hard v2.0的500个提示词，固定两个模型的回答，让GPT-4.1在温度为0、固定版本下作为裁判。测试所有8种评价标准、输出格式和任务框架的组合，每种设置重复运行。对比相同设置的重复运行及不同指令设置间的个别裁决变化和聚合分数差异。

Result: 1. 重复默认设置：聚合分数变动约1分，但21.2%提示词的胜/平/负结果改变。2. 移除推理要求等指令：比平均重复运行多改变28.2个百分点的提示词结果，聚合分数下降4.09分。3. 强调准确性的标准：多改变6.7个百分点的提示词结果，但聚合分数几乎不变。证明稳定聚合分数不代表个别裁决可重复。

Conclusion: LLM裁判的个别裁决对重复运行和指令修改高度敏感，聚合分数稳定性不能保证个别裁决可靠。依赖个别裁决的评估（如偏好标注、单响应评估）必须重复每个实验设置，并同时报告聚合分数和提示词级别的一致性指标。

Abstract: LLM judges are used to determine which of two answers is better. Trusting these verdicts requires knowing how repeatable they are and how much they depend on the judge's instructions. We show that similar aggregate scores can conceal substantial changes in individual verdicts, both when the same setup is repeated and when the instructions change. Using 500 Arena-Hard v2.0 prompts with two models' answers held fixed, we run GPT-4.1 as the judge under all eight combinations of criteria, output format and task framing, and repeat every setup unchanged at temperature zero on a pinned model version. Rerunning the default setup moves the aggregate score by about one point but changes the win/tie/loss outcome on 21.2% of prompts. Instruction changes introduce further sensitivity: in one setup, which asks for the verdict without reasoning and also drops several other instructions, outcomes change on 28.2 percentage points more prompts than the two setups' unchanged reruns do on average, and the aggregate score falls by 4.09 points. In another, where the criteria prioritise accuracy, outcomes change on 6.7 percentage points more prompts by the same comparison, while the aggregate score barely moves. A stable aggregate score therefore does not establish that individual verdicts are repeatable. This distinction matters when those verdicts are used as preference labels or to assess individual responses. Evaluations that rely on individual verdicts should repeat each setup they use and report both aggregate scores and prompt-level agreement.

</details>

*Hardeep Bassi,Hui Ma,Luís Seabra,Koowum Joshi,Daniel Pereira,Averell Gatton*

TL;DR: Protegrity-PII 是一种新的 PII（个人身份信息）决策模型，适用于结构化和非结构化文本。在新基准 PII-SCOPE 上实现类型召回率 0.915、精确率 0.907，推理速度为每百万字符 0.49 秒，成本极低（$0.0005/百万字符），且因训练中使用广泛的实体类型分类法，展现出优于现有 SOTA 模型的分布外泛化能力。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PII 检测模型在实体类型覆盖面、推理效率及分布外泛化方面存在不足。论文旨在构建一个高精度、低延迟、低成本且能泛化到未见实体类型的 PII 检测系统，并提出新基准 PII-SCOPE 以更全面评估类型级别的召回与精确率。

Method: 训练 Protegrity-PII 模型，采用广泛的实体类型分类法（expansive entity type taxonomy）覆盖丰富 PII 类别；采用批量推理在单张 RTX 6000 GPU 上部署，优化吞吐与成本；在新建基准 PII-SCOPE 上评估类型级召回与精确率，并与多个 SOTA 模型/数据集对比分布外泛化性能。

Result: 在 PII-SCOPE 上达到类型召回率 0.915、精确率 0.907；推理延迟 0.49 秒/百万字符，成本 $0.0005/百万字符；在分布外泛化测试中显著优于选定的 SOTA 模型与数据集。

Conclusion: Protegrity-PII 通过大规模实体类型分类法训练，实现了高精度、低延迟、低成本的 PII 检测，并具备强分布外泛化能力，适合大规模生产环境部署。新基准 PII-SCOPE 为类型级 PII 评估提供了更严格标准。

Abstract: We present Protegrity-PII, a new PII decision model for structured and unstructured text. Protegrity-PII achieves a typed recall of 0.915 with a precision of 0.907 on a new benchmark we propose, PII-SCOPE. Our model processes text in 0.49 seconds per million characters at \$0.0005 per million characters on a single RTX 6000 using batched inference. Due in part to the expansive nature of the entity type taxonomy used in training, Protegrity-PII achieves superior out-of-distribution generalization when benchmarked against a selection of state of the art models and datasets.

</details>
