Method: 1. 冻结预训练表 T^0，仅在被寻址行维护稀疏补丁 Δ。2. 4.1M 参数的写入器对每个 (位置, 哈希头) 事件输出目标向量与门控值。3. 采用分块平均的 Delta 规则：每行保持为旧值与门控目标的凸组合。4. 写入器在原始文本上用预训练目标离线训练，推理时仅前向传播。5. 对比两种写入器：plain writer 仅用 T^0 编码证据；delta writer 编码时读取当前记忆（含 Δ）。

Result: • 0.6B 原型：二次写入后保持 16/16 事实，梯度更新仅 2–4/16。• Qwen3.8-Flash-Next：plain writer 使续写 NLL 降 0.068 nats/token（vs 空记忆）、0.024（vs 他文档记忆）；16 篇干扰文后仍为 0.063/0.013。delta writer 文档特异性更强，干扰下保持率 70% vs 54%。• 3.5 万次写入七任务链：两 writer 均稳定，delta writer 漂移更小（层-2 数学漂移 176% vs 188%），准确率变化 -0.26/-0.52pp（梯度更新 -7.29pp），small-k_i 漂移 17.9/17.5%（39.2%）。• 通用输入漂移 32.8%（20.75%）：全样本读取的格式行范数膨胀至预训练的 4–12 倍；置零 8 行消除 >98% 层-2 数学漂移及其 NLL 收益。

Conclusion: Delta N-gram 通过冻结预训练表 + 稀疏补丁 + 仅前向写入，实现了高保真、可逆、抗干扰的持续记忆写入。Delta writer 进一步利用当前记忆编码新证据，提升文档特异性与抗遗忘。长链实验证实其稳定性远超梯度更新。但需注意格式行范数膨胀带来的通用漂移，可通过置零关键行缓解。

Abstract: Our previous analysis found that a model's native hashed N-gram table is where continual updates disturb the base least, but its writes needed backpropagation and changed the pretrained table. We present Delta N-gram, a forward-only write path into that table. The pretrained table T^0 is never modified. Evidence is written into a sparse patch Δon the rows it addresses: a 4.1M-parameter writer emits a target and a gate per (position, hash head) event, and a block-averaged delta rule keeps each row a convex combination of its old value and the gated targets. The writer is trained offline on raw text with the pretraining objective; at use time writing takes forward passes only, and clearing Δrestores the original model. A 0.6B prototype trained to answer questions keeps 16/16 facts after a second write; online gradient writes keep 2--4/16. We study two writers: a plain writer that encodes evidence with the T^0-only model, and a delta writer that reads its current memory while encoding. On Qwen3.8-Flash-Next, the plain writer's memory is document-specific: on 64 held-out news events it lowers continuation NLL by 0.068 nats/token against empty memory and by 0.024 against another document's memory, and 0.063 and 0.013 after 16 intervening articles. The delta writer's memory is more document-specific and keeps more of it under distraction (70% vs 54%). Over 35,000 writes on our previous seven-task chain both writers stay stable and the delta writer drifts less (layer-2 math drift 176% vs 188%); later accuracy changes by -0.26/-0.52pp (-7.29pp for gradient updates) and small-k_i drift is 17.9/17.5% (39.2%). Acquisition is negative, and general-input drift reaches 32.8% (20.75%): format rows read by every example grow to 4--12 times their pretrained norm. Zeroing eight such rows removes over 98% of layer-2 drift on math inputs, together with their NLL gain.

</details>


### [51] [Mixing Matters: Evidence Position Bias Across Sequence Mixers in Long-Context Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31135)
*Aman Behera,Namit Solanki,Mehul Anand*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文探究长上下文模型中证据位置对性能的影响，比较Transformer、状态空间模型和混合Mamba架构的位置偏差，发现架构家族、能力、任务和提示词均影响上下文有效利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文模型在处理远离提示词边界的证据时效果较差，存在位置偏差，但不同架构家族的位置偏差模式尚不明确，需系统比较。

Method: 通过在固定的九个干扰文档中移动一个金标准文档的位置，评估Transformer、状态空间模型和混合Mamba架构在不同位置证据下的表现；同时进行合成检索任务、语料库交换、提示词变更等实验，并分析注意力集中度与位置敏感性的关系。

Result: Transformer表现出开头优势，状态空间模型缺乏此优势，两者都偏好结尾附近的证据；纯Mamba与混合Mamba对比方向一致但统计不显著；家族差异出现在能完成任务的模型中；合成检索任务中Transformer首位优势重现，状态空间模型天花板效应阻碍家族比较；语料库交换改变整体准确率但不改变曲线形状；提示词变更可创建或几乎消除开头优势；位置在各架构中可解码，注意力集中在初始token与Transformer的开头敏感性共变。

Conclusion: 有效上下文利用取决于架构家族、模型能力、任务类型和提示词设计，不能单纯归因于序列混合器。

Abstract: Long-context models often use answer-bearing evidence less effectively when it appears away from a prompt boundary. We compare this position bias across Transformer, state-space, and hybrid Mamba architectures by moving one gold document through a fixed set of nine distractors. The evaluated Transformers show a beginning advantage that the evaluated state-space models lack, while both families favor evidence near the end. A matched pure-versus-hybrid Mamba comparison points in the same direction but remains statistically uncertain. The family difference appears among models that can answer the task. Transformer primacy reappears on synthetic retrieval, where state-space ceiling prevents a family comparison. A corpus swap within one state-space architecture changes overall accuracy without a detectable change in curve shape. Prompt changes can create or nearly remove a beginning advantage. Position remains decodable across architectures even when response curves differ, and attention concentration near initial tokens covaries with beginning sensitivity in the tested Transformer family. These exploratory results show that effective context use depends on the evaluated architecture family, capability, task, and prompt, without isolating the sequence mixer as the sole cause.

</details>


### [52] [Prompt Sensitivity in Tool-Augmented Model Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31148)
*Mateusz Modrzejewski,Tomasz Radzikowski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型在问答基准上对不同措辞问题的比较稳定性，提出匹配协议分离工具使用的各步骤，发现工具虽能提升准确性但往往扩大措辞敏感性，且稳定性取决于调用频率、接口与指令设计，建议除准确率外报告调用频率及多措辞下的比较稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有问答基准仅用单一措辞提问，重述问题可能改变两模型的相对得分；需探究赋予模型工具后是否能使比较更稳定，且单纯准确率提升无法回答此问题，因为工具同时改变从问题到评分答案的多个步骤。

Method: 提出匹配协议，将工具使用过程分解为五个步骤：答案读出、推理、调用决策、工具回答、无调用时的报告。在五个领域、五种措辞下对六个开源模型应用该协议，分析工具对准确率、措辞敏感性、调用频率及其与接口、指令的关系的影响。

Result: 工具在程序可直接回答问题的领域提升准确性，但扩大了许多成对比较的措辞敏感性。敏感性扩大随调用频率变化，调用频率对措辞的依赖因模型而异。原生工具调用接口和第二工具描述在分子领域消除扩大，但工具返回中间结果时敏感性持续存在。确定性报告不保证稳定比较。

Conclusion: 工具使用引入的不稳定性源于调用决策对措辞的敏感性。建议在报告模型性能时，除准确率外，还应报告调用频率及在多种措辞下的比较稳定性。

Abstract: Language models are compared on question-answering benchmarks that pose each question in one wording, and a reworded question can change which of two models scores higher. We ask whether giving the models tools makes such comparisons more stable. An accuracy gain cannot answer this, because tools change several steps between the question and the scored answer at once. We propose a matched protocol that separates those steps: the answer readout, reasoning, the decision to call, the tool's answer, and the report supplied without a call. We apply it to six open models in five domains under five wordings. We find that tools raise accuracy where a program answers the question and widen the wording sensitivity of many pairwise comparisons. The widening follows how often a model calls, and call frequency follows the wording for some models and not others. It also depends on the interface and the instructions: a native tool-calling interface and a second tool description leave no detected widening in molecules, and sensitivity persists where tools return intermediate results. These findings show that deterministic reports do not guarantee stable comparisons. We recommend reporting call frequency and comparison stability under several wordings beside accuracy.

</details>


### [53] [Prompting Strategy Effects Depend on the Judge: A Multilingual Study of LLM-as-a-Judge Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31172)
*Asal Mehrabi,Mohammad Aliannejadi,Clemencia Siro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大语言模型作为评判者在多语言任务中的提示策略效果，发现提示策略不具备跨模型、跨语言的可迁移性，评判模型的选择主导了提示策略的效果，且在低资源语言中稳定性下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示策略（如思维链、角色设定）仅基于英语基准推荐，其在不同评判模型和语言间的有效性未知。提示敏感性研究多以英语为中心，而多语言评判研究通常固定提示模板，缺乏系统性交互分析。

Method: 构建大规模基准：9种提示策略、4个开放权重评判模型、27种语言、4个任务跨越13个数据集，产生超过100万个点对点评分，并以人工判断为金标准验证。

Result: 提示策略不可迁移：评判模型的选择主导提示策略的选择；策略效果在不同评判模型间方向逆转；低资源语言中稳定性下降，且被聚合报告掩盖。提示效果取决于评判模型、任务和语言。

Conclusion: 多语言评估中的提示效果受评判模型、任务和语言制约。因此，提示策略应在预期评估设置中验证，跨研究比较需同时考虑评判模型和提示配置。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation is increasingly applied to multilingual tasks, yet prompting strategies such as Chain-of-Thought and Role Conditioning are recommended based solely on English benchmarks. Whether their effectiveness transfers across judge models and languages remains unknown: prompt-sensitivity studies are largely English-centric, while existing multilingual judge studies fix the prompt template. In this work, we address this gap by measuring this interaction through a benchmark of 9 prompting strategies, 4 open-weight judges, 27 languages, and 4 tasks across 13 datasets, yielding over 1M pointwise scores on gold-reference outputs, validated against human judgments. We find that prompting strategies are not transferable: the choice of the judge model dominates the choice of prompting strategy, strategy effects reverse direction across judges, and stability degrades in low-resource languages, where these effects are concealed by aggregate reporting. These findings show that prompting effects in multilingual evaluation are conditioned on the judge, task, and language. Consequently, prompting strategies should be validated for the intended evaluation setting, and cross-study comparisons should account for both judge and prompt configuration.

</details>


### [54] [When More Metadata Misleads: Probing Field-Level Grounding in LLM-Generated Museum Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31193)
*Clemencia Siro,Laura Hollink*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究博物馆文物描述生成中元数据组成对生成行为的影响，发现单纯的元数据数量无法解释生成行为，字段类型决定模型是否接地、省略或推断信息，图像既能支持视觉接地又会增加无支持的推断，建议评估应关注字段级和声明级的支持情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的元数据条件生成通常被框架为数据到文本任务，假设额外输入字段能改善接地效果。但博物馆藏品记录具有异质性、部分可视化、且不同地理区域文档记录不均衡的特点，挑战了这一假设，因此需要研究元数据组成如何塑造文物描述生成。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段分析大型博物馆藏品中自然的元数据变异，考察字段数量、字段类型、图像可用性和地点的协变关系；第二阶段引入受控字段探针，固定物体身份同时改变元数据数量、字段类型和图像访问权限。

Result: 元数据数量单独无法解释生成行为；字段类型决定模型是接地、省略还是推断信息；图像既能支持视觉接地，也会增加无支持的推断。

Conclusion: 元数据条件的数据到文本评估应评估字段级和声明级的支持情况，而不应仅依赖聚合输入覆盖率或参考相似度。

Abstract: Metadata-conditioned generation is often framed as a data-to-text task in which additional input fields should improve grounding. Museum description generation challenges this assumption because catalogue records are heterogeneous, partially visual, and unevenly documented across geographic regions. We study how metadata composition shapes the generation of artefact descriptions through a two-phase framework. Phase 1 analyses natural metadata variation in a large museum collection, where field count, field type, image availability, and Place co-vary. Phase 2 introduces controlled field probes that hold object identity fixed while varying metadata quantity, field type, and image access. Our results show that metadata quantity alone does not explain generation behaviour. Field type determines whether models ground, omit, or infer information, while images can both support visual grounding and increase unsupported interpretation. More broadly, our findings show that metadata-conditioned data-to-text evaluation should assess field- and claim-level support, rather than relying only on aggregate input coverage or reference similarity.

</details>


### [55] [Investigating Multi-Cultural Value Selection Biases in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31199)
*Siddhesh Pawar,Arnav Arora,Dustin Wright,Dirk Hovy,Isabelle Augenstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大语言模型在同时接收系统提示中的国家人设和用户轮次中的第二身份线索（显性国家命名或隐性查询语言）时的文化表现，发现语言线索在多项选择探测中改变答案幅度约为显性线索的三倍，但偏离关联国家分布；显性线索不能一致地使答案向该国分布靠拢。在开放式生成中，命名国家可缩小30-44%的差距，且显性线索产生更分化的价值观变化。结论指出国家与语言线索应作为不同信号评估，而非文化的可互换代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文化评价常将身份简化为单一标签，忽视用户多层次的文化身份。论文旨在探究当大语言模型同时面临系统提示中的国家人设与用户轮次中的第二身份线索（显性国家命名或隐性查询语言）时，模型如何响应及表现，以揭示不同线索对文化表征的差异性影响。

Method: 采用世界价值观调查（WVS）多项选择探测和开放式社会政治生成两种任务。实验设置系统提示赋予国家人设，用户轮次引入第二身份：显性线索为直接命名国家，隐性线索为使用查询语言。对比模型在不同线索组合下的回答分布与目标国家分布的差异，分析160万开放式回答。

Result: 多项选择探测中，语言线索导致答案偏移幅度约为显性线索的三倍，但方向偏离关联国家分布；显性线索未能稳定地将答案推向命名国家分布。开放式生成中，命名国家可缩小模型回答与该国自身回答之间30-44%的差距，且显性线索引发的价值观变化比语言线索更具分化度。

Conclusion: 国家线索与语言线索在引导大语言模型文化表现上具有本质不同的信号特性，不应被视为文化的可互换代理。评估模型文化能力时需区分并分别考察这两类线索的效果，避免混淆导致的评价偏差。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) serve users whose cultural identities are multi-layered, yet cultural evaluations commonly reduce identity to one label. In this work, we study LLMs under simultaneous identity cues, with a country persona in the system prompt and a second identity signaled in the user turn, either by naming a country (explicit cue) or through the query language (implicit cue). We test World Values Survey (WVS) multiple-choice probing and open-ended sociopolitical generation. In multiple-choice probing, language cues shift answers about three times more than explicit cues but away from the associated country's distribution, and explicit cues do not consistently move answers toward the named country. Across 1.6 million open-ended responses, naming a country closes 30-44% of the gap to the model's own responses for that country, and explicit cues produce more differentiated value changes than language cues. Our results suggest that country and language cues should be evaluated as distinct signals rather than interchangeable proxies for culture.

</details>


### [56] [IndoNutriQA: An Indonesian Clinical Nutrition Question Answering Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31256)
*Tirana Noor Fatyanosa,Ummu Ditya Erliana,Mahendra Data,Muhammad Nurwegiono,Eleazar Tadeo Eman,Alham Fikri Aji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布IndoNutriQA，首个印尼语临床营养问答基准，包含911个专家标注的QA对，基于真实临床营养报告，涵盖营养护理流程八大标准问题类型（含PES诊断），答案为自由文本。数据集定位为高质量评测资源，附带结构化元数据。实验给出微调基线（IndoBERT、mT5、RAG）及八大主流LLM的零/五样本基准，并校准了LLM-as-judge评分体系，发现其在事实性与完整性上接近专家，但在临床安全性上不可靠，且会漏判专家能识别的无依据内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床问答支撑智能决策支持系统，但低资源语言（如印尼语）严重缺乏公开领域数据，阻碍相关研究与应用落地。现有资源多为大规模训练语料，缺乏高质量、临床落地的评测基准，且多为抽取式片段而非自由文本答案，难以反映真实临床决策需求。

Method: 1) 数据构建：由研究生营养实习生撰写报告，三位营养师按营养护理流程（NCP）八大问题类型（含PES诊断）编写自由文本答案，附带人体测量、诊断、文本来源、分块等结构化元数据；专家复核发现6-10%参考答案有争议。2) 基线评测：IndoBERT变体、mT5、RAG，采用五折交叉验证、学习曲线、bootstrap 95%置信区间。3) LLM基准：八大当代LLM零/五样本推理。4) LLM-as-judge校准：三位专家（一注册营养师+两营养师）对比，评估事实性、完整性、临床安全性，并做人工误差分析。

Result: 微调基线与RAG在五折交叉验证下给出性能上界；八大LLM零/五样本表现差异显著；LLM-as-judge在事实性与完整性上与专家评分高度一致，但在临床安全性维度不可靠，人工误差分析显示其会漏判专家能识别的无依据/幻觉内容。

Conclusion: IndoNutriQA填补印尼语临床营养QA评测空白，为低资源语言临床NLP提供高质量基准。实验表明现有模型在该任务仍有提升空间，LLM-as-judge虽可辅助事实性/完整性评估，但不可用于临床安全性把关，需引入专家审核机制。数据集已开源以推动社区研究。

Abstract: Clinical Question Answering (QA) underpins Clinical Intelligent Decision Support Systems, but progress is severely limited for low-resource languages such as Indonesian by the absence of public domain-specific data. We introduce IndoNutriQA, an expert-curated Indonesian clinical QA benchmark of 911 question-answer pairs built from real clinical-nutrition reports. Rather than a large-scale training corpus, it is positioned as a high-quality evaluation resource: every pair is grounded in a clinically relevant context, enriched with structured metadata (anthropometric assessment, medical diagnosis, document provenance, chunk segmentation). Eight standardised question types following the Nutrition Care Process (NCP), including the Problem, Etiology, Sign and Symptom (PES) diagnosis statement, are answered for every report, which was written by a graduate-level dietetic intern, by one of three nutritionists. Answers are free-form clinical prose rather than extractive spans, and an expert validation study finds 6-10% of reference answers contestable. The dataset supports generative, extractive and retrieval-augmented QA as well as zero- and few-shot LLM evaluation. We report fine-tuned baselines (IndoBERT variants, mT5, RAG) with 5-fold cross-validation, learning curves and bootstrap 95% confidence intervals, and benchmark eight contemporary LLMs zero- and five-shot. We further calibrate our LLM-as-judge rubric against three expert raters (one RD, two nutritionists), finding it human-comparable on factuality and completeness but unreliable on clinical safety, and a human error analysis shows it misses unsupported content that the raters identify. IndoNutriQA is released to foster research in Indonesian clinical NLP and nutritional decision support.

</details>


### [57] [A Systematic Comparison of Search Space Complexity and Efficiency for Time-Synchronous End-to-End ASR Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31259)
*Simon Berger,Larissa Kleppel,Ralf Schlüter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比研究了CTC和神经转换器模型在不同剪枝策略和语言模型配置下的搜索空间复杂度与效率，发现端到端模型大多时刻搜索空间较小但在不确定时期假设数量会大幅峰值化，基于分数的动态剪枝能自适应模型不确定性显著降低实时因子(RTF)，揭示了固定小束宽剪枝的局限并给出高效搜索实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索是自动语音识别(ASR)系统的核心组件，在基于隐马尔可夫模型(HMM)的传统ASR中已深入研究，但在当前序列到序列ASR架构中常被简化为不使用外部语言模型的小固定束宽搜索，缺乏对端到端模型搜索行为的系统分析。

Method: 对CTC和神经转换器两类端到端模型进行比较分析，在不同剪枝策略(固定束宽、基于分数的动态剪枝)和语言模型配置(无LM、浅层融合、深度融合)下，测量搜索空间复杂度(假设数量峰值、平均假设数)和效率指标(实时因子RTF)，观察模型不确定性时期的搜索行为动态变化。

Result: 端到端模型大部分时间搜索空间很小，但在不确定时期假设数量会出现大的临时峰值；基于分数的动态剪枝能根据模型不确定性自适应调整搜索宽度，相比固定小束宽剪枝显著降低RTF；固定小束宽剪枝在峰值期会导致搜索错误或效率低下。

Conclusion: 固定小束宽剪枝存在局限性，无法应对端到端ASR模型在不确定时期的搜索空间爆发；基于分数的动态剪枝是更有效的策略，能在保持识别精度的同时大幅提升解码效率，为端到端ASR系统的高效搜索提供了实用指导原则。

Abstract: Search is a central component of Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems and has been deeply investigated for classical ASR modeling based on Hidden Markov Models (HMMs). However, in work on current sequence-to-sequence ASR architectures it is often simplified to a small fixed-size beam search without external Language Model (LM). In this work, we present a comparative analysis of search space complexity and efficiency for Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC) and neural transducer models under different pruning strategies and LM configurations. We show that these end-to-end (E2E) models work with a small search space most of the time but exhibit large temporary peaks in hypothesis count during periods of uncertainty. Score-based pruning dynamically adapts the search to model uncertainty, leading to a significantly lower Real-Time Factor (RTF). Our results highlight the limitations of search with low fixed-size beam pruning and provide practical guidelines for efficient search in E2E ASR.

</details>


### [58] [Memory-Aware Reward Modeling: Learning Human Preferences for LLM Memory Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31272)
*Yijiang River Dong,Zexue He,Cheng Yu,Tiancheng Hu,Michael J Ryan,Jan Batzner,Zheng Hui,Shaolong Wu,Orestis Papakyriakopoulos,Alex Pentland,Nigel Collier,Jiaxin Pei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了语言模型助手中记忆机制对用户偏好的影响，发现基于记忆的回复并不总是被用户偏好，自动评估器与人类判断一致性有限，因此提出了一种基于用户判断的记忆感知奖励模型，实现了72.8%的偏好预测准确率，优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注记忆能力本身，而未直接验证记忆使用是否真正符合用户偏好。作者旨在填补这一空白，探究记忆增强回复是否真正提升用户满意度，并开发更贴合人类偏好的评估与优化目标。

Method: 从87名参与者收集约1000对基于其真实ChatGPT查询和个人记忆的成对偏好判断；对比有记忆和无记忆条件下的回复偏好；分析自动评估器与人类判断的一致性；训练记忆感知奖励模型以预测用户偏好。

Result: 记忆条件回复未被一致偏好，部分情况下用户强烈偏好无记忆回复；自动评估器与用户一致性有限，且更倾向于记忆条件回复；提出的记忆感知奖励模型达到72.8%偏好预测准确率，显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 记忆使用需与用户偏好对齐，而非单纯增加记忆能力。未来评估和优化目标应直接纳入用户判断，确保记忆机制真正服务于被使用信息的用户。

Abstract: Memory enables language model assistants to maintain continuity across sessions and generate personalized responses, but knowing more about a user does not necessarily produce responses they prefer. Existing evaluations assess memory capabilities without directly establishing whether memory use benefits the user. To study this gap, we collect approximately 1,000 pairwise judgments from 87 participants using their own ChatGPT queries and personal memories. We find that memory-conditioned responses are not consistently preferred over responses generated without memory and in some cases, users strongly prefer the latter. Automatic evaluators also show limited agreement with users and favor memory-conditioned responses substantially more often than users do. Motivated by these gaps, we introduce a memory-aware reward model that learns from user judgments, achieving 72.8\% preference prediction accuracy and outperforming the existing baselines. Our findings motivate evaluation and optimization objectives that align memory use with the preferences of the people whose information is being used.

</details>


### [59] [CoWriter: Guiding Methodological and Synergetic Human-LLM Co-Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31277)
*Shmuel Amar,Benjamin Lieber,Ori Shapira,Debanjana Kar,Sebastian Maximilian Dennerlein,Yufang Hou,Ido Dagan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出CoWriter，一种主动代理方案，将认知科学中的专家共同写作方法论应用于整个写作流程，用户研究显示其能显著提升作者表达观点、文档所有权感及文本“人味”质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM聊天和画布界面使人类写作沦为被动监督，导致审查疲劳、过度依赖AI，且忽视人类在长文本、基于来源写作中的角色；先前系统仅针对孤立写作阶段，缺乏端到端人机协作探索。

Method: 引入CoWriter，一种主动代理方案，适配认知科学中的专家（共同）写作方法论，灵活引导整个写作工作流并主动鼓励用户参与；进行探索性用户研究，对比标准LLM基线。

Result: 使用CoWriter的作者更好地表达观点，保持更高文档所有权感，代理成功交互次数增加2.2倍；外部评委评价生成文本在“故事性”、“整合独特个人经历”等人味质量上更高。

Conclusion: 主动通过成熟人类实践吸引用户参与，有利于有效的LLM辅助共同写作，为未来协作代理研究提供方法论、技术和见解。

Abstract: While LLMs have transformed text generation, standard chat-and-canvas interfaces often reduce human authoring to quite passive supervision, causing review fatigue, over-reliance on AI, and the sidelining of the human role in long-form, source-grounded writing. Prior systems typically address isolated writing phases, leaving comprehensive end-to-end human-LLM collaboration largely unexplored. In this paper, we introduce CoWriter, a proactive agentic scheme that adapts expert human (co-)authoring methodologies from cognitive science to flexibly steer the entire writing workflow while actively encouraging the user to engage. In an exploratory user study, compared to a standard LLM baseline, authors using CoWriter better expressed their perspectives and maintained a higher sense of document ownership, supported quantitatively by $2.2\times$ more successful agent engagements. Furthermore, external reader judges rated the resulting texts higher in "human touch" qualities like storytelling and integrating unique personal experiences. Overall, our results demonstrate that proactively engaging users through established human practices is beneficial for effective LLM-supported co-authoring, providing useful methodologies, techniques and insights for future research on collaborative agents.

</details>


### [60] [Cola2: Co-Adaptive Hierarchical Language Modeling with Continuous Semantic Compression and Balanced Discrete Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31305)
*Hongcan Guo,Yian Zhao,Feng Wang,Shen Nie,Guoqiang Wei,Yan Zeng,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Cola2框架，通过动态语义分段、无辅助损失的负反馈压缩率控制、重建与生成联合监督、因果掩码扩散解码器及分类器自由引导，实现连续潜在空间与离散文本生成的协同自适应。在约2100亿训练令牌上验证，3460亿源令牌下未收敛检查点在八项任务平均分60.10，超越预训练预算大43倍的Llama-3-8B，展示了连续潜在层级语言生成的巨大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级语言生成将连续语义建模与离散文本实现分离，但其效果取决于潜在空间压缩内容及离散生成器对其利用方式。现有方法在语义压缩、重建与生成平衡、连续-离散协同自适应三方面存在不足，需要统一框架联合优化。

Method: Cola2包含：1) 动态将文本分段为语义跨度；2) 通过无辅助损失的负反馈动态控制压缩率；3) 结合重建与生成监督学习紧凑、预测性的潜在表示；4) 训练块因果掩码扩散解码器，使用干净、噪声、丢失三种潜在条件；5) 分类器自由引导平衡语义重建与自发生成；6) Token预测梯度通过潜在变量更新编码器，Flow Matching先验学习通过stop-gradient与表示学习解耦；7) 推理时在固定跨度布局下重新编码生成文本，保持潜在历史与实际文本上下文一致。

Result: 在五个下游任务（约2100亿训练令牌）上验证核心设计，展现竞争性且未饱和的扩展性。使用3460.3亿源令牌，未收敛检查点在八项任务平均分达60.10，在HellaSwag和StoryCloze上超越Llama-3-8B，后者报告预训练预算大43倍以上。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了连续潜在层级语言生成的巨大潜力，Cola2通过联合优化语义压缩、重建生成平衡及连续离散协同，在远小于主流大模型的预训练规模下达到甚至超越其性能，为高效语言生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Hierarchical language generation separates continuous semantic modeling from discrete text realization, but its effectiveness depends on what the latent space compresses and how the discrete generator uses it. We propose Cola2, a framework that jointly studies continuous semantic compression, the balance between reconstruction and generation, and continuous-discrete co-adaptation. Cola2 dynamically segments text into semantic spans, controls the compression rate through auxiliary-loss-free negative feedback, and combines reconstruction and generation supervision to learn compact, predictive latent representations. A block-causal masked diffusion decoder is trained with clean, noisy, and dropped latent conditions, while classifier-free guidance adjusts the balance between semantic reconstruction and autonomous generation. Token-prediction gradients update the encoder through the latents, whereas Flow Matching prior learning is decoupled from representation learning by stop-gradient. During inference, generated text is re-encoded under a fixed span layout to keep the latent history consistent with the actual text context. Experiments on five downstream tasks with about 210 billion training tokens validate the core designs and show competitive, unsaturated scaling. With 346.03 billion source tokens, an unconverged checkpoint reaches a 60.10 average across eight tasks and surpasses Llama-3-8B on HellaSwag and StoryCloze despite its reported pretraining budget being over 43 times larger. These results demonstrate the potential of continuous-latent hierarchical language generation.

</details>


### [61] [When LLMs Forget What They Believe: Characterizing State Integrity Collapse in Long-Horizon Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31314)
*Yancheng Zhu,Yifan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型在长对话中出现的“记忆-遵守分离”现象：模型能复述指令却不再执行，定义为“状态完整性崩塌”。引入 StateTrack-Bench 基准，覆盖数值、格式、角色/人设、事实记忆、逻辑规则五类约束，跨 7 个模型快照和 5 种对话深度测试，发现严格联合保持率 62.4%–100%，退化集中于符号约束和某脆弱模型。分叉回忆审计显示 88.6% 失败属于行为漂移而非遗忘（去除异常值后仍为 84.4%）。扩展至 50 轮对话确认分离持续存在，结构化状态锚定可在不可变约束上恢复高达 24.4 分。结论：单轮指令遵循无法推断可靠性，评估必须跟踪约束随对话状态增长是否保持生效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注单轮指令遵循，忽视长对话中模型虽保留约束记忆却停止应用的“回忆-遵守分离”问题，即状态完整性崩塌。需要系统量化这种现象，区分遗忘与行为漂移，并探索缓解方法。

Method: 构建 StateTrack-Bench，包含五类约束的受控对话套件；在七个供应商固定模型快照上进行五种对话深度探测，测量严格联合保持率；引入分叉回忆审计区分普通遗忘与行为漂移；提出结构化状态锚定技术并评估其恢复效果；扩展评估至 50 轮对话验证泛化性。

Result: 严格联合保持率 62.4%–100%，退化集中于符号约束和单一脆弱模型。88.6% 失败为行为漂移（去除异常值后 84.4%）。50 轮对话确认分离持续存在。结构化状态锚定在不可变约束上最高恢复 24.4 分。

Conclusion: 模型可靠性不能仅从单轮指令遵循推断；评估必须跟踪记忆的约束是否随对话状态增长保持操作性。代码与工件已开源：https://github.com/xzzvsxd/StateTrack。

Abstract: Large language models can repeat an instruction while failing to keep obeying it. We study this recall-compliance dissociation in long-horizon dialogue and define State Integrity Collapse: a model preserves a stated constraint in memory but stops applying it to later turns. We introduce StateTrack-Bench, a controlled suite of conversations covering numerical, format, role/persona, factual-memory, and logical-rule constraints. Across seven provider-pinned model snapshots and five dialogue-depth probes, strict joint retention ranges from 62.4% to 100.0%, with degradation concentrated in symbolic constraints and in one especially brittle model. A forked recall audit shows that 88.6% of observed failures are behavioral-drift failures rather than ordinary forgetting; excluding the brittle outlier preserves this diagnosis (84.4% of failures). Extended evaluations at depths up to 50 turns confirm that the dissociation persists beyond the core benchmark, while Structured State Anchoring recovers up to 24.4 points on immutable constraints. The results show that reliability cannot be inferred from single-turn instruction following alone; evaluation must track whether remembered constraints remain operational as dialogue state grows. Code and artifacts are available at: https://github.com/xzzvsxd/StateTrack.

</details>


### [62] [CrisiLangs: A Multilingual Resource for Crisis Warning Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31356)
*Giacomo Gonella,Stefano Menini,Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CrisiLangs，一个多语言危机预警消息数据集，扩展了英语CrisiText至意大利语、保加利亚语、德语和希腊语，包含5,000个事件-消息对。在多语言联合微调实验中，微调模型在所有语言和配置下均优于零样本基线；人类偏好研究显示低解码温度最受偏好，指南效果因语言而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有危机预警消息生成研究仅覆盖英语数据，而欧洲应急通信常需多语言发布，因此需要构建多语言数据集以支持多语言预警消息生成。

Method: 专业母语人士将1,000个事件（来自411个场景）的预警消息翻译成四种语言，构建含5,000个事件-消息对的五语言数据集CrisiLangs。随后在五语言上联合微调多语言模型，分别在提示中包含和不包含应急通信指南的配置下进行实验，并与零样本基线对比，使用ROUGE-L、BLEU、BERTScore评估，并进行人类偏好研究。

Result: 微调模型在所有语言和配置下的ROUGE-L、BLEU和BERTScore均显著优于零样本基线。人类偏好研究表明，低解码温度在所有五种语言和两种配置中均获得最多偏好，而应急指南的效果随语言不同而变化。

Conclusion: CrisiLangs填补了多语言危机预警消息生成的数据空白，联合多语言微调能有效提升生成质量，且低温度解码更符合人类偏好，指南的影响具有语言特异性，为多语言应急通信系统提供了重要资源和实证依据。

Abstract: Communicating with the public during an emergency entails issuing warning messages promptly. In Europe, it often requires producing them in more than one language. Recent work has shown that language models can be trained to generate warning messages as a crisis unfolds, but the available data covers only English. To fill this gap, we present CrisiLangs, a multilingual extension of CrisiText (an English dataset of crisis events paired with warning messages written according to expert communication guidelines) to Italian, Bulgarian, German, and Greek. Expert native speakers translated the warning messages of 1,000 events drawn from 411 scenarios, for a total of 5,000 event-message pairs across five languages. We test the resource to fine-tune a multilingual model on all five languages jointly, with and without the emergency guidelines in the prompt, and compare it with a zero-shot baseline. The fine-tuned models outperform the baseline on ROUGE-L, BLEU and BERTScore in every language and configuration. In a human preference study, low decoding temperatures receive the most preferences in all five languages and in both configurations, while the effect of the guidelines varies by language.

</details>


### [63] [Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of Large Language Models to a Low-Resource Agglutinative Language: A Corpus, Datasets, and a Reproducible Benchmark for the Bashkir Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31397)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov,Svetlana S. Khaybullina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了巴什基尔语（低资源黏着语言）的开放资源套件（Web语料库46.9M tokens、三个分类数据集），并系统基准测试了8种模型（82M-8B）在三种适应方法（全微调、QLoRA、上下文学习）及持续预训练（CPT）下的表现。关键发现：QLoRA在大模型上优于编码器微调；YandexGPT-5-Lite-8B在多任务上显著优于Mistral-7B和Phi-3-mini；CPT效果依赖模型与任务；分词器生育率差异2.67倍且与适应质量相关，俄语预训练的西里尔分词器更优；LLM评判能复现语法/连贯性评分但无法替代母语者对语言正确性的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴什基尔语作为使用西里尔字母的低资源黏着语言，在现代大语言模型中表现不足，缺乏系统性的资源构建与适配方法基准研究，亟需开放语料、标注数据及全面的实证对比来指导该语言的模型适配。

Method: 构建巴什基尔语Web语料库（71,567文档，46.9M tokens，16来源）及二分/多分/多标签三类分类数据集；选取8种架构（DistilGPT2、GPT-2 base/medium、Phi-3-mini、Mistral-7B、YandexGPT-5-Lite-8B、mBERT、XLM-R，参数量82M-8B）；对比全量微调、QLoRA、上下文学习三类适配方法，并引入持续预训练（CPT）；评估指标包括macro-F1、BPB、母语者打分、困惑度、分词器生育率及LLM-as-judge相关性。

Result: 1) QLoRA在3.8-8B模型上超越编码器微调：YandexGPT-5-Lite-8B+CPT在二分类达macro-F1 0.748，显著优于XLM-R(0.653)和mBERT(0.650)，p<0.001经Holm校正。2) YandexGPT-5-Lite-8B在多分类、BPB及母语者评分上显著优于Mistral-7B和Phi-3-mini（均值3.28 vs 2.52/2.22，n=100，alpha=0.69-0.77，p<0.05），但二分类QLoRA优势不显著(p>=0.084)。3) CPT效果模型/任务相关：Phi-3-mini(+0.58)和YandexGPT-5-Lite-8B(+0.33)困惑度改善，Mistral-7B恶化(-1.15，p<0.001)；二分类上CPT对Mistral-7B提升最大(+4.0 macro-F1)。4) 分词器生育率差异2.67倍(2.92-7.80)且追踪适配质量；跨分词器困惑度不可比；俄语预训练的西里尔分词器更优；LLM评判与母语者在语法/连贯性相关(rho=0.33-0.46)，但语言正确性不相关(rho=0.16，p=0.11)，母语者评估不可替代。

Conclusion: 研究为巴什基尔语提供了首个开放资源套件与系统适配基准，证实大模型QLoRA适配优于编码器微调，YandexGPT-5-Lite-8B在该语言表现最优，CPT收益高度依赖模型架构与任务类型，分词器生育率是适配质量的关键指标，且LLM自动评估无法完全替代母语者对语言正确性的判断，所有产物均采用开放许可。

Abstract: Bashkir is a low-resource agglutinative language written in Cyrillic and remains poorly represented in modern LLMs. We present an open resource suite and a systematic benchmark of adaptation methods: the Bashkir Web Corpus (71,567 documents, 46.9M tokens, 16 sources) and three labelled datasets for binary, multiclass and multilabel text classification; the benchmark covers the first two. Experiments use eight architectures (82M-8B: DistilGPT2, GPT-2 base/medium, Phi-3-mini, Mistral-7B, YandexGPT-5-Lite-8B, mBERT, XLM-R) and three adaptation groups (full fine-tuning, QLoRA, in-context learning), plus continual pretraining (CPT). (1) QLoRA on 3.8-8B models outperforms encoder-only fine-tuning on binary classification: YandexGPT-5-Lite-8B with CPT reaches macro-F1 0.748 vs 0.653 (XLM-R) and 0.650 (mBERT); pairwise differences are significant after Holm correction (p<0.001). (2) YandexGPT-5-Lite-8B significantly outperforms Mistral-7B and Phi-3-mini on multiclass classification, BPB and native-speaker ratings (mean 3.28 vs 2.52 and 2.22; n=100; alpha=0.69-0.77; p<0.05); on binary classification its QLoRA advantage is not significant (p>=0.084). (3) CPT effects depend on model and task: perplexity improves for Phi-3-mini (+0.58) and YandexGPT-5-Lite-8B (+0.33) but worsens for Mistral-7B (-1.15, p<0.001); on binary classification CPT helps Mistral-7B most (+4.0 macro-F1). (4) Tokeniser fertility on Bashkir varies 2.67x (2.92-7.80) and tracks adaptation quality, while perplexity across tokenisers is not comparable. Cyrillic tokenisers with Russian pretraining are preferable; LLM judges reproduce native ratings for grammar and coherence (rho=0.33-0.46) but not language correctness (rho=0.16, p=0.11); native evaluation remains necessary. All artefacts are open-licensed.

</details>


### [64] [HERD: Harness-Evolving Recursive Distillation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31401)
*Xun Sun,Thanh Thi Nguyen,Thuy-Trang Vu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HERD 是一种混合持续学习框架，结合非参数化 harness（技能、提示）与参数化蒸馏，通过递归蒸馏在固定内存和计算预算下实现新任务习得、最小遗忘和最强泛化能力保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习需要模型在不损害现有能力的前提下持续获取新技能。参数化方法面临灾难性遗忘和可塑性丧失；非参数化方法（如提示、技能、harness）虽利用上下文学习，但受限于上下文长度和有损纯文本压缩。需融合两者互补优势。

Method: 提出 HERD（Harness-Evolving Recursive Distillation）：harness 侧持续精炼技能以捕捉可复用模式，并将技能无法捕捉的高残差样本保留在有界内存中；参数侧采用双锚点的漂移约束蒸馏，大幅降低长期递归运行中的遗忘。

Result: 在 TRACE+17 的 25 阶段任务流上，固定内存和计算预算下，HERD 在新任务习得上达到最强参数化基线水平，同时表现出最小遗忘和最强泛化能力保持。

Conclusion: HERD 通过混合非参数化 harness 与参数化蒸馏，有效平衡了可塑性与稳定性，为长时程持续学习提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Continual learning requires a model to keep acquiring new skills and knowledge without compromising its existing capabilities. Parametric methods suffer from catastrophic forgetting and loss of plasticity. Non-parametric methods, such as prompts, skills, and harnesses, leverage in-context learning but are limited by context length and lossy plain-text compression. To effectively combine their complementary strengths, we propose HERD (Harness-Evolving Recursive Distillation), a hybrid continual learning framework that learns efficiently in the harness and internalizes experience into cumulative capabilities via distillation. On the harness side, HERD continually refines its skills to capture reusable patterns, while retaining high-residual examples that the skills fail to capture in a bounded memory. On the parameter side, HERD performs drift-constrained distillation with dual anchors, substantially reducing forgetting over long recursive runs. On the 25-stage task stream of TRACE+17, under fixed memory and compute budgets, HERD matches the strongest parametric baseline in new-task acquisition while exhibiting the least forgetting and the strongest retention of general capabilities.

</details>


### [65] [Same Entity, Different Script: Diagnosing Cross-Script Entity Matching Failures in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31407)
*Athira Gopal,Patil Prathamesh Pravin,Ankush Kadu,Arpit Jain,Ashwanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多语言语言模型在不同书写系统（如天城文与拉丁文）下对同一人名实体的身份判断差异。实验发现模型在天城文提示下的同一人准确率大幅下降（35.1%→8.5%），但不同人准确率保持高位。通过探针、校准、激活修补等方法，定位了因果敏感层并提出无需事实知识的干预方法，将举出的马拉地语同一人准确率从6.5%提升至33.5%，揭示了脚本依赖的判别能力与决策准则偏移的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言模型对同一实体在不同书写系统下的表征不一致，导致身份判断出现脚本依赖的偏差。现有研究缺乏对该现象的系统量化、因果定位及有效干预手段。

Method: 构建601个经过审计的天城文-拉丁文同人/异人姓名对，固定问题模板与关系，在马拉地语提示下评估五个模型；复现于374个马拉雅拉姆语对；使用线性探针、标量校准、激活修补等技术分析表征与决策机制；在中间层第二实体位置实施与事实无关的干预。

Result: 同一人准确率在天城文下骤降至8.5%（拉丁文35.1%），马拉雅拉姆语更低至5.3%，但AUROC仍达0.925；拉丁文训练的探针能从天城文状态恢复标签信息；标量校准弥补了87%的平衡准确率差距，表明决策阈值偏移是主因；激活修补定位因果敏感性于Qwen中间层第二实体位置，共享敏感方向能转移约一半修正量；干预使举出马拉地语同一人准确率从6.5%升至33.5%，异人准确率98.5%。

Conclusion: 脚本依赖的身份判断差异主要源于决策准则偏移而非判别能力缺失；模型中间层第二实体表征是因果关键节点；无需外部事实知识的激活干预即可显著缓解跨脚本身份判断偏差，为提升多语言模型一致性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multilingual language models can give different identity judgments when the same people's names are written in different scripts. We study this effect using 601 audited identity pairs with attested Devanagari and Latin forms, holding the question and relation fixed. Qwen2.5-7B's same-person accuracy falls from 35.1% in Latin to 8.5% in Devanagari under Marathi prompts, while different-person accuracy remains above 98%. Evaluation across five models reveals distinct response biases, and reduced tokenization fragmentation does not reliably close the gap. Replicating the design on 374 audited Malayalam pairs built from the same entities reproduces the effect in a second script and language family: same-person accuracy falls to 5.3%, about half the matched Devanagari rate, while the margin still ranks matching above non-matching names at 0.925 AUROC. Latin-trained probes recover label information from Devanagari states, exceeding a string-feature baseline under similarity-matched negatives. Scalar calibration recovers 87% of the raw model-probe balanced-accuracy gap, identifying decision criterion placement as a major contributor. Activation patching localises causal sensitivity to the second entity in Qwen's middle layers; one shared sensitivity direction transfers about half the full-state margin correction. A fact-independent intervention at this site raises held-out Marathi same-person accuracy from 6.5% to 33.5%, with 98.5% different-person accuracy. Together, these results distinguish script-dependent discrimination from criterion placement and identify a causal site for improving identity judgments.

</details>


### [66] [SyRA-CS: Synthetic-to-Real Adaptation for Code-Switched ASR with Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31415)
*Fadel Thior,Ahmer Tabassum,Haania Siddiqui,Hanan Aldarmaki,Thamar Solorio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SyRA-CS框架，利用Reptile元学习在合成代码切换语音上元训练，再用1小时真实标注语音适应，在四种架构和四个语言对上显著优于持续微调基线，WER相对降低5.3%-17.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码切换(CS)自动语音识别(ASR)面临真实CS语音稀缺且合成语音与真实语音分布不匹配的问题。现有方法多采用持续微调，但合成语音是否更适合学习适应友好的初始化而非直接微调仍未充分探索。

Method: 提出SyRA-CS：针对每个语言对和预训练模型，先在对应的合成CS-FLEURS子集上使用Reptile算法进行元训练，学习适应友好的初始化参数；再利用1小时标注的真实目标语音进行快速适应。评估四种架构：Encoder-CTC、FastConformer-RNN-T、Attention Encoder-Decoder(AED)和Speech-LLM。

Result: 在阿拉伯语-英语、法语-英语、德语-英语、印地语-英语四个语言对上，SyRA-CS在所有16个模型-语言对比较中WER均优于持续微调，15/16在CER上优势。XLS-R模型宏平均WER相对降低17.7%，其他三模型降低5.3%-5.8%。增益跨架构一致但幅度随架构、语言对、指标而异。

Conclusion: 合成CS语音通过元学习可作为有效的适应友好初始化来源，优于直接持续微调。SyRA-CS为低资源代码切换ASR提供了一种通用、有效的合成到真实适应范式，适用于多种主流架构。

Abstract: Code-switched (CS) automatic speech recognition (ASR) remains difficult because real CS speech is scarce and synthetic speech does not fully match it. We investigate whether synthetic CS speech is more useful for learning an adaptation-friendly initialization than for ordinary continual fine-tuning. We propose SyRA-CS, a pair-specific synthetic-to-real adaptation framework based on Reptile meta-learning. For each language pair and pretrained model, we meta-train on the corresponding synthetic CS-FLEURS subset and then adapt using one hour of labeled real target speech. We evaluate four architectural settings: Encoder-CTC, FastConformer-RNN-T, Attention Encoder-Decoder (AED) and Speech-LLM. Experiments cover on Arabic-, French-, German-, and Hindi-English, against synthetic-only transfer, real-only fine-tuning, and continual fine-tuning. SyRA-CS improves over continual fine-tuning on WER in all 16 model-pair comparisons and on CER in 15 of 16, with relative macro WER reductions of 17.7% for XLS-R and 5.3-5.8% for the other three models. The gains hold across the tested models but vary in size across architectures, language pairs and metrics.

</details>


### [67] [How Depth Growth and Looping Shape In-Context Learning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31421)
*Ferdinand Kapl,Kaitlin Maile,Stefan Bauer,Johannes von Oswald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究循环和深度增长语言模型如何通过跨深度重用计算来增强上下文学习（ICL）能力，发现这两类模型共享一种倾向于从上下文学习的归纳偏置，且参数重用塑造了该能力的习得、保持及底层注意力机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作已初步证明循环和深度增长模型在ICL上表现更好，但缺乏系统分析：这种跨深度计算重用究竟如何影响ICL的习得时机、保持强度及支撑机制（如诱导头的传播与作用）。

Method: 在高达3B参数的预训练模型与受控任务上实验，对比循环、深度增长与全深度稠密基线；追踪训练过程中注意力头的演化，量化诱导头跨层传播情况；通过消融实验测量诱导头对few-shot性能及后续token预测的贡献。

Result: 循环与深度增长模型更依赖上下文信息，在受控任务中更早习得ICL，且在权重也可学习固定关联的情况下能更强地保持ICL；深度增长会将早期诱导头传播到更多层；消融显示这两类模型对诱导头的依赖度显著高于基线。

Conclusion: 跨深度的（软）参数重用形成了共享的归纳偏置，使模型倾向于从上下文学习，并决定了ICL能力的发展轨迹与支撑该能力的注意力机制（特别是诱导头的分布与作用）。

Abstract: Looped and depth-grown language models reuse computations across depth and have attracted interest for their reasoning capabilities. Building on prior suggestive evidence of improved in-context-learning (ICL), we investigate how such reuse favors ICL, including how it shapes the acquisition, retention, and mechanisms of this ability. Our analysis spans pretrained language models up to 3B parameters and controlled tasks. The experiments provide evidence for a shared inductive bias toward learning from context: grown and looped models rely more on information in context and, compared with a full-depth dense reference, acquire ICL earlier in controlled tasks and retain it more strongly when learning fixed associations in the weights is also possible. Tracing attention heads through training reveals that depth growth propagates early induction heads across layers. Ablations show that grown and looped models rely more strongly on induction-ranked heads for both few-shot performance and later-token prediction. Together, these findings provide evidence for a shared inductive bias toward learning from context and suggest that (soft) parameter reuse shapes how this ability develops and which attention mechanisms support it.

</details>


### [68] [How Well Do Decision Models Transfer to Vietnamese? An Empirical Study of Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31422)
*Phat Huu Ha,Nguyen Xuan Phuc,Nong Nhut Quy,Trinh Tran Tran,Nguyen Minh Triet,Dang Van Thin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估四个专用决策模型在越南语零样本分类中的表现，考察标签描述语言与语言扰动的影响。结果显示：英语标签描述下 JEV 综合性能最优，在 11 个设置中居首；双语描述无稳定提升；去除变音符号导致宏平均 F1 下降 9.51–15.47 点，损害远大于 teencode 或拼写错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用决策模型在零样本分类上近期取得进展，但其在越南语上的有效性尚未充分探索，亟需系统评估模型在越南语分类任务上的零样本性能、对标签描述语言的敏感性以及对越南语正字法变体的鲁棒性。

Method: 在六大任务组的 13 个分类设置上评测四个专用决策模型，分别采用英语、越南语及双语标签描述；并引入 teencode、拼写错误、变音符号移除三类常见语言扰动，量化模型对越南语正字法变体的鲁棒性。

Result: 英语标签描述下 JEV 整体表现最强，11 个设置中排名第一；标签描述语言的影响因模型和数据集而异，双语描述未带来一致性提升；鲁棒性实验表明变音符号移除导致性能下降最剧烈，平均宏 F1 下降 9.51–15.47 点，显著超过 teencode 与拼写错误的影响。

Conclusion: 研究凸显在评估专用决策模型用于越南语零样本文本分类时，标签描述语言的选择与越南语正字法变体（特别是变音符号）的处理至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in specialized decision models have expanded the possibilities for zero-shot classification, yet their effectiveness on Vietnamese remains underexplored. This study evaluates four specialized decision models across 13 classification settings spanning six task groups, examining zero-shot performance, sensitivity to label-description language, and robustness to Vietnamese linguistic variations. The evaluation considers English, Vietnamese, and bilingual label descriptions, alongside common linguistic perturbations involving teencode, typographical errors, and diacritic removal. Under English label descriptions, JEV achieves the strongest overall performance, ranking first in 11 classification settings. The effects of label-description language vary across models and datasets, with bilingual descriptions providing no consistent improvement. Robustness experiments reveal that diacritic removal causes the most substantial performance degradation, reducing mean macro-$F_1$ by 9.51--15.47 points across model configurations, considerably more than teencode or typographical errors. These findings highlight the importance of label-description choices and Vietnamese orthographic variation when evaluating specialized decision models for zero-shot text classification.

</details>


### [69] [From Structured Records to Patient Narratives: Preserving Clinical Alignment in LLM-Generated Health Summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31467)
*Bayram Ayadi,Florian Geissler,Johanna Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于证据的约束生成框架，结合结构化证据条件与检索增强生成（RAG）及提示约束，用于月经健康监测的健康摘要生成。在三个LLM上生成4,500个摘要，通过NLI、原子事实性指标和可读性度量评估，结果显示显式接地约束持续提升临床一致性，改善不确定性保留与忠实性，但增加阅读难度和医学术语，强调需联合评估忠实性、不确定性保留与可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴和数字健康系统产生大量纵向生理记录，但将临床信息转化为患者易懂语言仍具挑战。LLMs虽能生成可读叙述，但可能引入语义漂移，导致临床精确含义在简化过程中失真，因此需要基于证据的约束生成方法以平衡准确性与可读性。

Method: 采用约束生成框架，融合结构化证据条件与检索增强生成（RAG）及基于提示的约束生成。在月经健康监测场景下，使用三个LLM生成4,500个摘要，利用自然语言推理（NLI）、原子事实性指标和可读性度量评估语义对齐、事实一致性及可读性。

Result: 显式接地约束在三个LLM上一致提升临床对齐度，显著改善不确定性保留，且在基于检索证据评估忠实性时普遍保持或提升忠实性；遗漏率影响因模型而异。但临床对齐度提升伴随阅读难度增加和医学术语增多。

Conclusion: 研究表明开发面向患者的医疗LLM系统时，必须联合评估忠实性、不确定性保留和可读性，在临床准确性与患者可理解性之间寻求平衡。

Abstract: As wearable and digital health systems increasingly generate longitudinal physiological records, communicating these observations in patient-accessible language remains a significant challenge. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising mechanism for translating structured clinical information into readable narratives, but this process may introduce semantic drift, where clinically precise meaning is distorted during simplification. We investigate evidence-grounded health summarization for menstrual health monitoring using a constrained generation framework that combines structured evidence conditioning with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) using a prompt-based constrained generation framework. We evaluate semantic alignment using Natural Language Inference (NLI), atomic factuality metrics, and readability measures. Across three LLMs and 4,500 generated summaries, explicit grounding constraints consistently improve clinical alignment, particularly by improving uncertainty preservation and, once faithfulness is evaluated against retrieved evidence, generally preserving or improving faithfulness, while effects on omission remain model-dependent. We also find that improved clinical alignment comes at the cost of increased reading difficulty and medical terminology. These findings highlight the need to jointly evaluate faithfulness, uncertainty preservation, and readability when developing patient-facing medical LLM systems.

</details>


### [70] [Towards User Simulators That Stay On Task And Still Sound Natural](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31512)
*Mustafa Omer Gul,Philippe Laban,Tanya Goyal,Jennifer Neville*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的用户模拟器训练方法（UserLM-2.0），通过联合奖励任务意图遵循度和行为自然度，并利用轻量级判别器进行对抗训练，显著提升了模拟对话的有效性（46.2% vs 14.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟器面临权衡：提示式助手能遵循复杂意图但缺乏行为保真度；专用模拟器行为更自然但难以忠实执行复杂意图。长轮次交互中LLM性能下降，亟需既能坚持复杂意图又能表现自然对话行为的用户模拟器来评估和改进AI助手。

Method: 提出强化学习配方，联合奖励任务意图遵循和行为自然度。关键创新是用轻量级判别器将自然度运算化，并与用户模拟器交替进行对抗训练循环。

Result: 发布的UserLM-2.0模型生成有效模拟对话（同时满足任务忠实性和自然性标准）的比例达到46.2%，是最强基线模拟器（14.1%）的三倍以上。

Conclusion: 通过对抗式强化学习联合优化意图遵循与行为自然度，可显著提升用户模拟器在复杂多轮任务中的模拟质量，为长程交互AI助手的评估提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Users increasingly rely on AI systems for extended, multi-turn tasks, yet evidence indicates that LLM-based system performance degrades over prolonged interaction. To evaluate and improve AI assistants in such settings, we need user simulators that faithfully follow complex user intents while exhibiting realistic conversational behavior. Existing approaches manifest a trade-off: prompted assistants can follow complex user intents but lack behavioral fidelity, while specialized user simulators better approximate conversational behavior but struggle to remain faithful to complex intents. In this paper, we propose a reinforcement learning (RL) recipe that jointly rewards task intent adherence and behavioral naturalness. A crucial contribution of the method is operationalizing naturalness with a lightweight discriminator trained in an alternating, adversarial loop with the user simulator. We release the resulting model, UserLM-2.0, which produces valid simulated conversations (i.e., meeting both task-faithfulness and naturalness criteria) 46.2% of the time, over three times the rate of the strongest prior user simulator (14.1%).

</details>


### [71] [The Geometry of Metaphor: Probing and Steering Metaphor Representations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31520)
*Shihui Li,Mette Andersen,Martha Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究运用机制可解释性技术，在多种架构和规模的大语言模型中识别因果子空间，实现了对分类任务和生成任务中隐喻输出的引导控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐喻是人类认知的关键方面，研究者旨在理解隐喻在大语言模型中的表征方式，并探索如何控制模型输出的隐喻性。

Method: 采用机制可解释性技术，在覆盖不同架构和规模的一系列大语言模型中识别因果子空间，并在此基础上进行输出引导实验。

Result: 成功在不同词性的分类任务中引导模型输出，同时也在生成任务中实现了对输出隐喻程度的控制。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性技术能够有效识别大语言模型中隐喻的表征机制，并可用于控制模型输出的隐喻性，为理解和干预模型认知行为提供了新途径。

Abstract: Metaphor is a crucial aspect of human cognition. As such, we would like to understand how it is represented in large language models (LLMs) and how it might be controlled. We use mechanistic interpretability techniques to identify causal subspaces in a range of LLMs covering different architectures and sizes. We show that we are able to steer output on classification tasks across different parts of speech. We are also able to steer output on generation tasks, indicating that these techniques can be used to control the metaphoricity of LLM output.

</details>


### [72] [An Empirical Study of Recent Large Language Models for Knowledge Graph to Text Generation on Standard Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31529)
*Priyanka,Renaud Delbru,Souhail Bakkali,Mickaël Coustaty,Antoine Doucet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比了大语言模型（LLMs）与微调的编码器-解码器模型在知识图谱到文本（KG-to-text）生成任务上的表现，使用基于参考的指标和基于图的无参考LLM评估方法，发现LLMs在基于参考的指标上显著缩小甚至消除了与微调模型的差距，且在基于图的指标上表现更优；两类评估指标相关性有限，捕捉的质量维度不同。图接地评估显示，提示式LLMs产生的矛盾和三元组遗漏更少，且随图规模增长误差增加较慢，揭示了参考指标未能完全捕捉的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLMs）在自然语言生成任务中的竞争力增强，需要系统地将其与长期作为标准方法的微调编码器-解码器模型在KG-to-text任务上进行基准测试，以了解二者在不同评估维度上的优劣势及差异。

Method: 采用基于参考的自动评估指标（如BLEU、METEOR等）和基于图的无参考LLM评估方法（经人工标注验证），对比近期LLMs与微调编码器-解码器模型在KG-to-text生成任务上的性能；分析两类指标的相关性，并考察随知识图谱规模变化时矛盾和三元组遗漏错误的增长趋势。

Result: 近期LLMs在基于参考的指标上大幅缩小与微调模型的差距，部分设置下甚至持平；在基于图的无参考评估中，LLMs表现更优。两类评估分数相关性有限，表明它们捕捉不同质量维度。图接地评估显示，提示式LLMs产生的矛盾和三元组遗漏更少，且随图规模增长误差增加较慢，而微调模型的错误随图规模增长更快。

Conclusion: LLMs已成为KG-to-text生成的有力竞争者，在保真度（减少矛盾和遗漏）方面优于微调模型，且随输入规模扩展表现更稳健。参考指标与无参考图接地指标捕捉互补的质量维度，未来评估应结合两者以全面衡量生成质量。

Abstract: Knowledge graph-to-text (KG-to-text) generation verbalizes a set of knowledge graph triples into fluent natural language. While fine-tuned encoder-decoder models have long been the standard approach, recent LLMs are becoming increasingly competitive. We benchmark recent LLMs against fine-tuned encoder-decoder models, using reference-based metrics and graph-grounded reference-free LLM-based evaluation validated against human annotations. We observed that recent LLMs substantially narrow, and in several settings close, the gap with fine-tuned models on reference-based metrics, while performing better on graph-grounded metrics. LLM-based evaluation scores show limited correlation with reference-based metrics, suggesting that the two approaches capture different aspects of generation quality. Graph-grounded evaluation reveals that prompted models produce fewer contradictions and triple omissions than fine-tuned models, and these errors increase more rapidly with graph size for fine-tuned models, highlighting differences between the two approaches that reference-based metrics may not fully capture.

</details>


### [73] [Would an LLM Match into a Residency? Uncertainty, Reasoning and Image Use of Open Language Models on the Spanish MIR Examination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31586)
*Miguel Fernandez-de-Retana,Unai Zulaika,Ruben Sanchez-Corcuera,Aritz Bilbao-Jayo,Aitor Almeida*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文评估了四个开源大语言模型（8-27B参数）在西班牙MIR住院医师资格考试上的表现，发现模型虽能达到前600-3000名水平，但拒答机制无效、医学微调虽降低过度自信却牺牲准确率且未改善置信度校准、正确答案中13-38%基于临床不合理理由排除干扰项、且模型未真正利用提供的医学影像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床使用语言模型常以医学考试准确率为依据，但医疗系统若要在自有硬件运行开源模型，需了解更多关键能力：模型是否知道自己何时可能出错、医学微调是否改善该能力、正确答案是否基于合理临床推理、以及模型是否真正使用了输入图像。

Method: 在西班牙MIR考试八届共1552道题上，按考试官方评分规则评测四个开源模型（8-27B参数），分析准确率、排名、拒答策略、医学微调对过度自信和置信度校准的影响、正确答案的临床推理合理性，以及移除或交换上下文图像对准确率的影响。

Result: 三个较大模型答完所有题可排前600-3000名，仍有41-43个专科可选；拒答从不划算，因即使最低置信度答案正确率仍超25%盈亏平衡点；医学微调降低过度自信但损失准确率，未提升置信度区分对错能力；13-38%正确答案以临床不合理理由排除干扰项且置信度未反映此问题；移除或交换上下文图像不改变准确率。

Conclusion: 开源模型虽在医学考试上分数不俗，但存在关键缺陷：缺乏可靠的拒答机制、微调未根本解决置信度校准、推理过程存在临床不合理现象、且忽视多模态输入。临床部署前需解决这些可靠性与可解释性问题。

Abstract: Accuracy on medical examinations is common evidence offered for the clinical use of language models, yet a health system that intends to run an open model on its own hardware needs to know considerably more: whether the model recognizes when it is likely to be wrong, whether medical fine-tuning improves this ability, whether its correct answers rest on sound clinical reasoning, or whether it actually uses the images it is given. We examine these questions for four open models of 8 to 27 billion parameters on the Spanish MIR residency examination, scoring 1,552 questions from eight editions under the exam's own rules. Answering every question, the three larger models would rank among roughly the first 600 to 3,000 candidates, with 41 to 43 specialties still open to them. Abstention, however, never pays, because even their least confident answers are right more often than the 25% break-even. Medical fine-tuning reduced overconfidence at the cost of accuracy, without improving how well confidence separates right from wrong answers. Moreover, 13-38% of correct answers rejected a distractor for a clinically unsound reason that confidence did not reflect, and removing or swapping the provided contextual images left accuracy unchanged.

</details>


### [74] [How Ground Truth Shapes Retrieval Scores: Evidence-Aware Evaluation of a Retrieval-Augmented Generation System for Breast Lesions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31606)
*Miguel Mochizuki Silva,Samuel Ismael,Mateus C. Dias,Thaís Gaudêncio,Yuri Malheiros,Barufaldi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种证据感知的检索评估协议，用于乳腺病变管理文献的临床RAG系统，解决检索分数对标注覆盖度的依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决临床RAG系统中检索评估依赖标注覆盖度的问题，建立证据感知的评估协议以更准确衡量检索性能。

Method: 使用18文档语料库和18查询基准（每文档一条Yes/No或WH问题），检索融合稠密嵌入、BM25和交叉编码器重排序；评估128-1024 tokens块大小、0-20%重叠及四种检索器配置；相关性由证据跨度映射到所有包含块定义，重叠等效证据块视为相关；采用R-Precision、Recall@K、MRR、nDCG@K评估检索；生成评估在512-token索引K=1-5下用DeepEval声明级指标（deepseek-v4-pro作为LLM裁判）及BLEU/ROUGE指标。

Result: 选定混合检索器（384-token块，20%重叠）在17/18查询中前三名检索到接受证据；Recall@3、R-Precision、MRR@3、nDCG@3均达0.94；真值策展使证据跨度从25增至37、接受块目标从36增至51，但未改变检索排名。

Conclusion: 交叉编码器重排序获得最高检索分数；扩展相关性标注增加识别证据而不改变排名，凸显标注敏感性；小规模开发集和检索告知的策展需独立验证，包括放射科医生对证据充分性的评估。

Abstract: Purpose: To describe an evidence-aware retrieval evaluation protocol for a clinical retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system over breast lesion management literature, addressing the dependence of retrieval scores on annotation coverage. Methods: An 18-document corpus and an 18-query development benchmark, with one Yes/No or WH-question per document, were used. Retrieval combined dense embeddings, BM25, and cross-encoder reranking. Chunk sizes of 128 to 1024 tokens, overlaps of 0 to 20%, and four retriever configurations were evaluated. Relevance was defined by evidence spans mapped to all containing chunks, treating overlapping chunks with equivalent evidence as relevant. Retrieval was evaluated using R-Precision, Recall@K, MRR, and nDCG@K. Separately, generation was assessed on a 512-token index at K=1 to 5 using DeepEval claim-level metrics, with deepseek-v4-pro as the LLM judge, and BLEU/ROUGE metrics. Results: The selected hybrid retriever, using 384-token chunks and 20% overlap, retrieved accepted evidence in the top three results for 17 of 18 queries. Recall@3, R-Precision, MRR@3, and nDCG@3 each reached 0.94. Ground-truth curation increased evidence spans from 25 to 37 and accepted chunk targets from 36 to 51 without changing retrieval rankings. Conclusion: Cross-encoder reranking achieved the highest retrieval scores. Expanded relevance annotations increased recognized evidence without changing rankings, highlighting annotation sensitivity. The small development set and retrieval-informed curation require independent validation, including radiologist assessment of evidence sufficiency.

</details>


### [75] [Bilingual Language Models Capture Cross-Linguistic Influence in Second-Language Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31620)
*Abderrahmane Issam,Irene E. Winther,Stefan L. Frank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究双语语言模型中的跨语言迁移是否与人类L2阅读中的L1驱动偏好一致，通过对比匹配读者L1的双语模型、单语英语基线及其他L1训练的双语模型，在12种不同L1背景读者的英语阅读眼动数据上评估，发现匹配L1的双语模型能更好预测L2阅读时间，且该优势集中在晚期眼动指标、受L1-L2文字重叠调节、在中等熟练度读者中最明显，证明双语LM捕捉了跨语言影响且该影响存在于L2阅读行为中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单语语言模型的惊讶度虽能预测阅读时间，但无法捕捉由读者母语（L1）塑造的非母语（L2）阅读动态。需研究双语模型中的跨语言迁移是否与人类L2阅读中的L1驱动偏好一致。

Method: 在12种不同L1背景读者阅读英语文本的眼动数据上评估双语模型。通过对比三类模型隔离L1特异性迁移：1）训练L1与读者L1匹配的双语模型，2）单语英语基线模型，3）训练其他L1的双语模型。

Result: 匹配读者L1的双语模型显著提升L2阅读时间预测效果。预测优势集中在晚期眼动指标（如总阅读时间、回视），受L1-L2文字重叠度调节，且在中等熟练度读者群体中达到峰值。

Conclusion: 双语语言模型能捕捉跨语言影响的某些方面，且此类影响确实存在于L2阅读行为中，为计算心理语言学提供了新证据。

Abstract: While monolingual language model (LM) surprisal is widely established as a predictor of reading times, it cannot capture the non-native (L2) reading dynamics that are shaped by the reader's first language (L1). In this work, we investigate whether the cross-lingual transfer within bilingual models aligns with human L1-driven biases during L2 reading. We evaluate bilingual models on eye-tracking data of readers from 12 diverse L1 backgrounds reading English texts. To isolate L1-specific transfer, we compare bilingual models trained on an L1 that matches the readers' L1 background to both a monolingual English baseline and bilingual models trained on other L1s. We demonstrate that matching the readers' L1 improves the prediction of L2 reading times. This predictive advantage is concentrated in late-stage eye-tracking measures, modulated by L1-L2 script overlap, and peaks among intermediate-proficiency readers. These findings provide evidence that bilingual LMs capture aspects of cross-linguistic influence, and that such influence is present in L2 reading behavior.

</details>


### [76] [Can an Agent Debug a Web Corpus? Agentic Auditing of Slovak FineWeb2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31636)
*Marek Šuppa,Ivan Vykopal,Jaroslav Kopčan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种使用LLM作为语料库调试器而非分类器的方法，通过工具使用代理探索多语言语料库（以FineWeb2的斯洛伐克语部分为例），发现并验证了七个问题现象，其中最大的是一个包含140万文档的机器生成内容农场，且编译的测试套件能在0.7秒内无需GPU扫描全语料库，成本远低于LLM分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言处理管线（如FineWeb2）对上千种语言应用统一配方，导致个别语言的失效模式难以预判，且传统LLM分类成本高昂（$12k-$90k/遍），需要一种低成本、可解释且可复用的语料库调试方法。

Method: 采用LLM作为只读、预算受限API的工具使用代理探索语料库，将发现编译为可执行测试，再由母语者验证。在FineWeb2的2650万斯洛伐克语文档上进行三次运行（每次$2.21-$2.84），生成测试套件并与静态采样基线对比。

Result: 发现七个问题现象（四大类），最大为机器生成内容农场占5.32%（1408437文档），86.6%通过更严格阈值。盲标验证支持6/7发现（缺陷率.53-1.00 vs 对照.08）。编译套件0.7秒全语料扫描无需GPU，成本远低于LLM分类。静态采样基线验证选择器更多且成本减半。代理提供精确流行率、精度估计、管线遗漏原因及可复用测试。

Conclusion: LLM作为语料库调试器而非分类器，能以极低成本发现多语言管线的系统性失效，生成的可执行测试套件高效、可复用且可解释。该框架、斯洛伐克测试套件、验证数据及代理轨迹已开源，为大规模多语言语料库质量控制提供新范式。

Abstract: Multilingual pipelines such as FineWeb2 apply one recipe across a thousand languages, leaving the failure modes of individual languages unanticipated. We use an LLM once, as a corpus debugger rather than a classifier: a tool-using agent explores the corpus through a read-only, budgeted API and compiles each finding into an executable test, which a native speaker then validates. On the 26.5M-document Slovak portion of FineWeb2, three runs costing \$2.21-\$2.84 each found seven findings over four phenomena. The largest is a machine-generated content farm of 1,408,437 documents (5.32%) that passed the pipeline's filters, 86.6% of it passing even stricter thresholds. Blinded labels support 6 of the 7 findings (defect rates .53-1.00 against .08 in a random control) and do not support one. The compiled suite scans the full corpus in 0.7 s of wall-clock time without a GPU, against \$12k-\$90k per LLM classification pass. The best static sampling baseline validates more selectors than any agent run, at under half the cost. The agent contributes exact prevalence, a precision estimate checked against sampled documents, an account of why the pipeline missed each slice, and a reusable test. We release the framework, Slovak test suite, validation data and agent trajectories.

</details>


### [77] [Low-Bit N-Gram Embeddings for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31646)
*Stephen Chung,Wenyu Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将 n-gram 嵌入表量化为低比特精度，可大幅节省内存并保持性能：4-bit 量化节省约 75% 内存且保留 >99% 验证损失提升，微调可恢复部分损失，重新投资内存可进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: n-gram 嵌入表虽能提升模型性能，但消耗大量内存（如 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 分配 1960 亿参数），需要在保持性能提升的同时降低内存成本。

Method: 系统研究不同量化方法和比特分配，在 nanochat、Llama、GPT-2 系列从头训练的模型上测试低比特 n-gram 表，并评估微调恢复及重新投资内存扩大表的效果；同时在 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 和 Qwen3.8-Flash-Next 等大模型上验证。

Result: 4-bit 表节省约 75% 表内存，保留 >99% 验证损失提升；更低比特性能下降但短微调可恢复大部分；将节省内存重投资于更大表可超越全精度表性能；在大模型上节省 47-55% 表内存，困惑度增加 <1.2%，下游任务性能基本保持。

Conclusion: 低比特精度是减少 n-gram 嵌入内存需求的实用方法，节省的内存可用于扩大表进一步提升模型性能。

Abstract: Many recent open-weight language models use n-gram embedding tables to improve performance. These tables store learned representations of short token sequences, such as pairs or triples of consecutive tokens, which the model retrieves during processing. However, they can require substantial memory: DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, for example, allocates 196 billion parameters to its Engram n-gram memory. We therefore investigate whether this memory cost can be reduced while preserving the performance improvement that the tables provide. We propose storing n-gram embeddings at low precision and systematically investigate different quantization methods and bit allocations across tables. Across nanochat-, Llama-, and GPT-2-family models trained from scratch, four-bit tables save approximately 75% of table memory while preserving more than 99% of the validation-loss improvement from adding the tables. Using fewer bits leads to a larger drop in performance, although we find that a short fine-tuning phase recovers a significant part of this loss. In addition, reinvesting the saved memory in larger tables can improve performance beyond that of the original full-precision tables while still using less memory. These table memory savings also extend to large existing models: on DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash and Qwen3.8-Flash-Next, low-bit tables save 47-55% of table memory, with perplexity increases of less than 1.2% and largely preserved downstream task performance. These findings establish low-bit precision as a practical way to reduce the memory requirements of n-gram embeddings and use the saved capacity to improve model performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [78] [MoEZip: Routing-Aware KV Cache Compression for Sparse Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30206)
*Minsang Kim,Seungjun Baek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MoEZip，一种针对稀疏MoE语言模型的路由感知KV缓存压缩方法，解决现有压缩方法忽略专家路由敏感性导致生成质量下降的问题。通过基于Fisher信息矩阵的路由敏感度度量结合注意力权重进行上下文评分，并融合稠密与稀疏MoE架构的驱逐策略，在激进压缩下显著提升长文本基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长上下文推理中，KV缓存随序列长度线性增长成为内存瓶颈。现有针对稠密Transformer的KV缓存压缩方法忽略了MoE模型特有的问题：KV驱逐会改变回答生成时的专家选择，导致生成质量下降。

Method: 1) 提出基于路由分布Fisher信息矩阵的新度量，估计专家路由对扰动的敏感度。2) 将该度量与注意力权重结合，实现路由感知的上下文评分。3) 设计互补评分系统，融合稠密与稀疏MoE架构的驱逐方法。

Result: 在多个长文本基准上，MoEZip在激进缓压缩下表现鲁棒。KV保留率10%时，在LongBench和RULER上分别比SOTA基线平均提升+24和+20分，对应得分分别高出2.3倍和4.9倍。

Conclusion: MoEZip有效解决了MoE模型KV缓存压缩中的路由敏感性问题，通过路由感知的压缩策略显著提升了长上下文推理的生成质量，为稀疏MoE模型的高效部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models scale model capacity efficiently by activating a small subset of parameters per token. However, in long-context inference, memory remains a major bottleneck because the KV cache grows linearly with sequence length. Existing KV cache compression methods reduce this cost for dense transformers, but overlook a problem specific to MoE: KV eviction can change which experts are selected during answer generation, degrading the generation quality. We propose MoEZip, a routing-aware KV cache compression for sparse MoE language models. To estimate the sensitivity of expert routing to perturbation, we propose a novel metric based on the Fisher information matrix of routing distributions. MoEZip combines this metric with attention weights for routing-aware scoring of contexts. Finally, we compose a complementary scoring system that combines eviction methods for dense and sparse-MoE architectures. Experiments show that MoEZip achieves robust performance under aggressive cache compression on various benchmarks. At a KV retention ratio of 10\%, MoEZip improves over the SoTA baseline by +24 and +20 points on average on LongBench and RULER, corresponding to 2.3$\times$ and 4.9$\times$ higher scores, respectively.

</details>


### [79] [Spectral Uncertainty Decomposition for Sparse Bayesian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30207)
*Ismail Huseynov,Stefan Haufe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于先验预条件化前向算子奇异值结构的谱框架，将后验协方差精确分解为数据知情和本质不确定性成分，并推导出减法分解，实现M/EEG源成像中可解释的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决M/EEG逆问题中后验高斯协方差无法显性区分测量可见方向与不可见方向不确定性的问题，区分可通过更好测量减少的不确定性与由感知几何根本不可减的不确定性。

Method: 基于先验预条件化前向算子的奇异值分解（SVD）建立谱框架，将后验协方差分解为数据知情成分和本质不确定性（认知）成分；并推导等价的减法分解，将先验协方差表示为后验协方差与数据驱动缩减项之和。

Result: 提供了后验协方差的精确可解释分解，识别出可归约与不可归约的不确定性贡献；给出先验协方差的减法分解，精确量化贝叶斯学习带来的不确定性减少；为线性高斯贝叶斯逆求解器提供理论洞见和实用工具。

Conclusion: 该互补表示框架实现了M/EEG源成像中可解释的不确定性量化，区分了可通过改进测量减少的不确定性与固有不可减的不确定性，为线性高斯贝叶斯逆问题提供了闭式解下的理论与实践工具。

Abstract: Estimating distributed neural sources from M/EEG measurements is an ill-posed inverse problem where principled uncertainty quantification remains challenging. The usual conditional Gaussian posterior covariance does not explicitly distinguish uncertainty along likelihood-visible source directions from uncertainty that is invisible to the measurements. We propose a spectral framework based on the singular value structure of the prior-preconditioned forward operator, which provides an exact, interpretable decomposition of the posterior covariance into data-informed and indeterminacy epistemic components. This formulation identifies uncertainty contributions that are reducible by better measurements from those that are fundamentally irreducible by sensing geometry. We further derive an equivalent subtractive decomposition that expresses the prior covariance as the sum of posterior and data-driven reduction terms, enabling precise accounting of uncertainty reduction through Bayesian learning. Together, these complementary representations offer both theoretical insight and practical tools for interpretable uncertainty quantification in M/EEG source imaging for linear-Gaussian Bayesian inverse solvers where the posterior covariance is available in closed form.

</details>


### [80] [Prediction Is Not Calibration: Item Parameter Recovery and Representation Coupling in Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30211)
*Wenrui Yuan,Stéphanie M. van den Berg,Johannes Steinrücke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究知识追踪模型中项目参数恢复的问题，发现预测准确性不保证参数恢复。通过分离序列项目表示宽度和项目参数键宽度，提出SK(64,8)架构，在不增加模型大小和牺牲测试准确性的前提下，改善固定顺序下的能力扭曲，提升参数恢复和跨顺序一致性。实验表明区分度和选项斜率等参数必须单独评估，不能仅依赖响应预测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识追踪模型虽能通过仅用于下一响应预测的项目响应解码器估计项目参数，但预测准确性不保证参数恢复质量。特别是区分度和选项斜率等关键参数的恢复依赖于估计能力的尺度和分布，且受固定项目顺序影响大。现有方法将序列表示和参数键共享相同宽度，限制了参数恢复性能。

Method: 研究2PL、GPCM、NRM三种解码器搭配LSTM、Transformer、DKVMN三种编码器。通过响应模型导数分析区分度和选项斜率更新机制，利用仿射Logit恒等式揭示项目依赖的偏移和缩放如何被吸收进项目参数。提出将序列项目表示宽度(d_S)与项目参数键宽度(d_M)分离，采用宽键窄序列的SK(64,8)架构对比共享宽度64的SH(64)基线。在模拟数据、TIMSS和EdNet真实数据上评估参数恢复、跨顺序一致性及与离线IRT参考的一致性。

Result: 模拟实验显示：难度、均值阈值、正确选项截距恢复在乱序下表现良好，不随表示宽度变化；区分度和键入选项斜率对比恢复需要更宽的项目表示，且在固定顺序下显著下降。SK(64,8)架构减少固定顺序能力扭曲，提升恢复和跨顺序一致性，不损失测试准确性且模型大小不变。真实数据上，增大d_M持续提升与离线IRT参考一致性，缩小d_S的权衡取决于编码器对序列宽度的预测依赖程度。

Conclusion: 项目参数恢复（尤其是区分度和选项斜率）必须独立于响应预测指标进行评估。分离序列表示与参数键宽度的架构设计能有效缓解固定顺序带来的能力估计偏差，在保持预测性能的同时显著改善参数恢复质量和跨顺序一致性。这为知识追踪模型的参数可解释性提供了重要设计原则。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) models can estimate item parameters through an item response decoder trained solely for next-response prediction, yet predictive accuracy does not guarantee parameter recovery. We study 2PL, GPCM, and NRM decoders paired with LSTM, Transformer, and DKVMN encoders. Response-model derivatives show why discrimination and option slope updates depend directly on the scale and spread of estimated ability. Affine logit identities show how item-dependent shifts and rescaling in estimated ability are absorbed into item parameters without changing response probabilities. In simulations, difficulty, mean threshold, and correct-option intercept recovery remain high under shuffled order across representation widths, whereas discrimination and keyed option slope contrast recovery require wider item representations and degrade further under fixed item order. Separating the sequence item representation (width $d_S$) from the item parameter key (width $d_M$), and pairing a wide key with a thin sequence representation ($\mathrm{SK}(64,8)$), reduces fixed-order ability distortions and improves recovery and cross-order consistency without sacrificing test accuracy or increasing model size relative to a shared width-64 baseline ($\mathrm{SH}(64)$). On TIMSS and EdNet, widening $d_M$ consistently improves agreement with an offline IRT reference, while the trade-off from narrowing $d_S$ depends on the encoder's reliance on sequence width for prediction. These findings show why item parameters, especially discrimination and option slopes, must be evaluated separately from response prediction.

</details>


### [81] [AI Mindmeld: A Latent Interconnect Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30215)
*Siavash Golkar,Jake Kovalic,Shirley Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在互联协议（LIP），通过学习连接器将专家模型（蛋白质、小分子）的表示映射到大语言模型（LLM）内部状态，实现跨领域零样本迁移和组合性，无需联合训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM作为科学代理需通过文本、图像等模态获取专家信息，存在高维表示瓶颈；主张直接表示交换，支持扩展性、灵活性和组合性，避免LLM主动请求信息的局限。

Method: 训练连接器将三个蛋白质和三个小分子专家的表示映射到Gemma 3（4B/12B）和Kimi K2.6（1T总参数，32B激活）的内部状态，冻结所有预训练模型；独立训练的连接器提供有用信息，支持熟悉和新问题类型及未见对象。

Result: 实现零样本跨域任务迁移，任务性能和组合性随LLM能力提升而改善，零样本任务迁移增益尤为显著；但当前LLM未针对专家表示训练，接入不保证可靠解释、整合和推理。

Conclusion: 呼吁专家开发者提供一致表示接口，LLM开发者训练模型接收、组合和推理专家输入，以扩展科学代理信息获取同时保留传统工具灵活性。

Abstract: LLMs can act as scientific agents using tools to consult specialists and run experiments. Yet specialist information must enter their context, generally as text, images, audio or video: a bottleneck between models with rich, high-dimensional representations. We advocate direct representation exchange: a mindmeld conveying many properties, including information an LLM would not think to request. It should be extensible to new specialists, flexible across questions and tasks, and compositional across independently connected specialists without joint training.
  The Latent Interconnect Protocol (LIP) learns connectors mapping specialist representations into LLM internal states. Training only connectors keeps this practical as LLMs grow and evolve. We connect three protein and three small molecule specialists to Gemma 3 (4B and 12B parameters) and Kimi K2.6 (1T total, 32B active), freezing all pretrained models. Independently trained connectors provide useful information without changing the LLM or existing connectors, supporting familiar and new question types about unseen objects. We demonstrate zero-shot transfer to cross-domain tasks without joint training. Task performance and compositionality generally improve with LLM capability, with especially strong gains in zero-shot task transfer. Limitations remain: current LLMs were not trained for specialist representations as native inputs; access does not ensure reliable interpretation, integration or use in reasoning. We call on specialist developers to provide consistent representation interfaces and, especially, on LLM developers to train models to receive, combine and reason with these inputs. These efforts could expand scientific agents' information while preserving conventional tools' flexibility.
  Data, pretrained connectors, and code for training, evaluation and interactive inference: https://github.com/PolymathicAI/AI-Mindmeld

</details>


### [82] [When Reconstruction Is Not Enough: Doubly Robust Counterfactual and Interventional Mediation Analysis with Learned Multimodal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30220)
*Cong Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高维多模态健康记录的学习表征在因果推断中的应用，指出以重构数据为目标训练的表征不足以保证因果识别，证明仅靠协变量训练目标无法认证表征的充分性，并提出结合变分自编码器、双重稳健估计、锁定表型、选择后确认通路分析等的工作流，以及针对表征不足、暴露泄露和敏感性的诊断方法。仿真实验显示丢弃罕见强混杂因子导致间接效应区间覆盖率仅2%，重构损失未能发现问题，而预测性诊断每次都能标记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维多模态健康记录的学习表征常用于调整混杂，但现有方法多以重构数据为训练目标，而非保留因果识别所需的信息，导致反事实结果和中介效应估计出现偏差。需要明确表征保持识别的条件，并开发能够诊断和缓解表征不足的工作流。

Method: 1. 理论分析：给出表征保持因果识别的条件，证明协变量仅训练目标无法认证表征充分性，线性结构模型下重构精度损失微小却可导致任意大偏差；分解交叉拟合双重稳健估计误差为一阶表征偏差和二阶剩余项。2. 实践工作流：模态特定变分自编码器在估计样本外训练、交叉拟合双重稳健估计器、锁定表型、选择后确认通路分析、可移植表征、以及针对表征不足、暴露泄露、敏感性的诊断工具。3. 仿真验证：丢弃罕见中等强度混杂因子，评估间接效应区间覆盖率，对比重构损失与预测性诊断的检测能力。

Result: 理论上证明中介效应识别比反事实比较更苛刻；表征不足时，双重稳健性和交叉拟合无法消除一阶偏差，样本量增大反而使覆盖率恶化。仿真中丢弃罕见混杂因子导致间接效应区间覆盖率仅2%（n=3000），重构损失未检出，预测性诊断每次复现均标记出问题。

Conclusion: 仅靠重构、对比或物理信息等协变量训练目标无法保证表征对因果识别的充分性。需采用因果感知的表征学习与诊断工作流，结合外部训练的模态特定VAE、双重稳健估计、锁定表型等，并引入预测性诊断以检测表征不足、暴露泄露和敏感性，从而获得可靠的反事实与中介效应估计。

Abstract: Learned representations of high-dimensional multimodal health records are increasingly used to adjust for confounding, yet they are usually trained to reconstruct the data rather than preserve the information that causal identification requires. We study counterfactual outcomes under alternative treatments, including phenotype-specific effects and patient-level counterfactual risk profiles of the kind digital twins provide, and interventional direct and indirect effects under exposure-induced intermediate confounding. We give conditions under which a representation preserves identification and show that interventional mediation is more demanding than counterfactual comparison. We prove that no covariate-only training objective, whether reconstruction, contrastive, or physics-informed, can certify that a representation is sufficient, and that, in a linear structural model, a representation can lose arbitrarily little reconstruction accuracy yet induce arbitrarily large bias. We decompose the error of cross-fitted doubly robust estimators into a first-order representation bias, which neither double robustness nor cross-fitting removes, and a second-order remainder of nuisance-estimation errors, so coverage worsens as the sample size grows when the representation is insufficient. We propose a workflow combining modality-specific variational autoencoders trained outside the estimation sample, cross-fitted doubly robust estimators, locked phenotypes, select-then-confirm pathway analysis, portable representations, and diagnostics for representation insufficiency, exposure leakage, and sensitivity. In simulations, discarding a rare, moderately strong confounder gave indirect-effect intervals with 2% coverage at n = 3,000; reconstruction loss missed the problem, and a simple predictive diagnostic flagged it in every replicate.

</details>


### [83] [How Prompt Style Affects AI Accuracy and Speed in Competitive Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30223)
*Saad B. Alarifi,Mohammed A. Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在 GPT-5.5 上对比了九种提示风格在三个 IOI 准确性任务和两个 NP-hard 速度任务上的表现，发现不存在通用的最佳提示：最优提示取决于任务类型和汇总指标（均值还是中位数）。与人类 IOI 参赛者的描述性对比显示，AI 在某项任务上超越平均人类水平，但在另外两项任务上落后。研究主张提示风格应与目标（准确性 vs 运行时间、均值 vs 中位数）相匹配，而非视为通用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅对比提示策略在竞技编程准确性上的效果，未涉及运行时间速度，也未将结果置于人类表现背景下。本研究旨在将提示对比扩展到运行时速度，并以人类 IOI 选手表现为基准，提供更全面的图景。

Method: 探索性研究：在 GPT-5.5 上分别采用九种提示风格各一条，测试三个来自 IOI 的准确性任务和两个 NP-hard 速度任务。使用均值和中位数汇总结果，并与人类 IOI 参赛者（在不同条件下）进行描述性对比。

Result: 无单一提示在所有任务和指标上均最优；最强提示随任务类型和汇总方式（均值/中位数）而异。均值差异主要源于少数异常运行，单一指标评估会掩盖提示有效性的波动。与人类相比，AI 在一项任务上超越平均选手，在另外两项任务上表现较差，整体呈现非严格优劣关系。

Conclusion: 提示风格应根据具体目标（准确性或运行时间、均值或中位数）选择，而非假设存在通用最佳提示。单一指标评估会遮蔽提示效果的变异性。通过引入运行时间速度对比并参照人类表现，研究为竞技编程中提示风格的影响提供了更宽广的视角。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in competitive programming, and their outputs are sensitive to how a prompt is phrased. Prior work has compared prompt strategies for accuracy on competitive programming, but not for runtime speed, and has not situated the results against human performance. In an exploratory study of one prompt in each of nine styles on GPT-5.5 across three accuracy tasks from the International Olympiad in Informatics (IOI) and two NP-hard speed tasks, we found that no single prompt was best: the strongest prompt depended on the task type and on whether results were summarized by the mean or the median, suggesting that, at least for these tasks, prompt style should be matched to the objective rather than treated as universal. Many differences in mean scores came from a few outlier runs, showing that evaluating prompting by a single metric can obscure how much its effectiveness varies. Compared descriptively with human IOI contestants, who worked under different conditions, the AI was neither strictly better nor strictly worse, exceeding the average contestant on one task but falling short on the other two. By extending prompt comparison from accuracy to runtime speed and grounding it against human performance, this study gives a broader picture of how prompt style affects competitive programming performance.

</details>


### [84] [GANet: A Lightweight Group Attention Network for Plant Disease Identification on Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30225)
*Anubhab Tripathi,Bibek Das,Asfak Ali,Sheli Shinha Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出轻量级Group Equivariance Attention Network (GANet)，用于38种植物病害预测监测，仅0.001M参数，在PlantVillage数据集上达99.99%准确率，并在树莓派4B和Jetson Nano上实现5/11 FPS实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球变暖加剧植物病害，威胁粮食生产；现有深度学习模型计算量大、难在低端硬件部署，且多针对特定作物病害，泛化性差，亟需轻量化、通用的实时监测方案。

Method: 设计Group Attention块和Group-Wise Feature Fusion块，构建稀疏高效的GANet网络，实现组等变注意力机制，大幅降低参数量至0.001M，并针对边缘设备优化部署。

Result: 在PlantVillage数据集（38类病害）上达到99.99%准确率；在树莓派4B上运行5 FPS，Jetson Nano上11 FPS，验证了低功耗设备上实时监测的可行性。

Conclusion: GANet以极小参数量实现高精度多病害识别，并成功部署于边缘计算平台，为大规模、实时、低成本的植物病害监测提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Global warming and climate change are major catalysts for plant diseases, significantly affecting food production worldwide. Addressing this issue requires continuous monitoring and early detection of plant diseases. While recent advancements in deep learning offer potential solutions, most models demand high processing power and struggle to perform efficiently on low-end electronic hardware. Furthermore, many existing works focus on specific crop diseases, limiting their broader applicability. This paper presents a lightweight Group Equivariance Attention Network (GANet) designed for the prediction and monitoring of 38 types of plant diseases. GANet introduces a novel Group Attention block and Group-Wise Feature Fusion block, making it both sparse and efficient. With only 0.001M parameters, the model achieves an impressive 99.99% accuracy on the PlantVillage dataset. Additionally, GANet is optimized for deployment on edge devices such as the Raspberry Pi 4b and Jetson Nano, where it reaches 5 FPS and 11 FPS, respectively, demonstrating its potential for real-time plant disease monitoring on low-power devices.

</details>


### [85] [Benchmarking Model Compression Techniques Across Heterogeneous Edge Platforms: A Predictive Maintenance Use Case for Rotating Machinery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30228)
*Matheus Calheira Guimarães de Oliveira,Marco Aurélio da Silva Cruz,Regis Cardoso,Lucas Cambuim,Rafael Macieira,Arthur Paim,Helaine Neves,Alex Santos,Lilian Guarieiro,Jean Schmith,Prashant Kumar,Erick G. Sperandio Nascimento*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对工业5.0边缘计算场景，对比了四种最先进的模型压缩技术在预测性维护CNN模型上的效果，发现在知识蒸馏压缩下，模型在九种异构边缘设备上实现90%以上加速、98.9%体积压缩，且仅微小牺牲预测性能（平衡F1分数91.22%），为工业边缘部署提供了明确的压缩策略指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业5.0要求自主系统与人协作促进可持续发展，需将复杂深度学习模型高效部署在资源受限的边缘硬件上。但工业边缘环境存在硬件异构性高、模型压缩导致性能下降、缺乏针对预测性维护等应用的实用压缩技术指导等挑战。

Method: 选取一个高性能卷积神经网络作为基础模型，使用真实旋转机械数据集进行预测性维护任务；系统对比四种最先进的模型压缩技术（包括知识蒸馏等）；在九种从高度受限边缘设备到高性能处理器的异构计算平台上部署评估；联合考量分类性能、推理速度和压缩比三个指标进行综合分析。

Result: 知识蒸馏压缩方法表现最优：在所有九种设备上平均推理加速超过90%，模型体积减少98.9%，平衡F1分数保持91.22%，相对未压缩模型预测性能下降极小；成功验证了在多边缘平台上的可行部署路径。

Conclusion: 通过系统性压缩策略对比与多平台部署验证，证明知识蒸馏是实现工业5.0边缘推理目标的有效途径，能在不牺牲预测性能前提下大幅降低计算成本与能耗，为工业预测性维护的边缘化部署提供了清晰的技术路线图。
