Abstract: To enable Industry 5.0, where autonomous systems are widely adopted and integrated with people to promote sustainable development in industries, it is imperative to adapt complex deep learning models to operate efficiently on resource-constrained hardware for edge inference. However, adapting these models remains challenging due to the hardware heterogeneity of edge computing in industrial settings, performance degradation caused by model compression, and the lack of clear guidance on which compression techniques are practical and effective for applications such as predictive maintenance. To address these challenges, this paper presents a comprehensive comparative analysis of four state-of-the-art model compression techniques applied to a high-performance convolutional neural network for predictive maintenance using a real-world dataset of rotating machinery, equipment typically used in industry. The compressed models were evaluated across a heterogeneous set of nine computing platforms, ranging from highly constrained edge computing devices to general purpose and high-performance processors, reflecting the diversity and fragmentation typical of industrial edge environments. By jointly prioritizing classification performance, inference speed, and compression ratio, we found that compression using knowledge distillation emerged as the most effective approach. The model had more than 90% speedup across all devices while maintaining a 91.22% balanced F-score, with a very small drop in predictive performance relative to the uncompressed model, along with a 98.9% size reduction. Through successful deployment across multiple edge platforms and a systematic comparison of compression strategies, we demonstrated a clear path to achieving Industry 5.0 targets, drastically cutting computational costs and, consequently, energy consumption without sacrificing predictive performance.

</details>


### [86] [Automated screening of privacy risks in medical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30229)
*Konstantinos Vrettos,Eleftherios Tzanis,Haridimos Kondylakis,Evangelia E. Vassalou,Florence X. Doo,Michail E. Klontzas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了医疗AI模型中患者数据对成员推断攻击的脆弱性，发现跨不同训练配置下高风险患者保持一致，并开发了轻量级筛查工具在生产模型训练前识别高风险患者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗AI模型常在不共享训练数据的情况下发布，假设这能保护患者隐私。但模型可能泄露患者记录是否被用于训练（成员推断攻击）。现有风险评估多基于队列平均值，可能掩盖个体患者的高暴露风险。需要在开发生产模型前识别潜在脆弱记录，使隐私评估更实用。

Method: 在四种模态的六个临床数据集上训练了7,789个模型（包括两个适配的预训练医疗基础模型），测量每个患者对成员推断攻击的脆弱性。分析训练选择（权重衰减、架构、初始化等）对高风险患者数量和身份的影响。开发了一个使用四个小模型的轻量级筛查工具，在生产模型训练前识别高风险患者。

Result: 训练选择改变了高攻击分数患者的数量，但风险排名最高的患者在测试配置中基本保持不变。更强的权重衰减将暴露队列减少了多达三倍，但相同患者在不同训练配方、架构和初始化（包括基础模型）中仍面临最高风险。筛查工具能有效识别高风险患者。

Conclusion: 患者对成员推断攻击的脆弱性在不同模型配置中具有稳定性。隐私筛查应纳入临床AI的数据准备流程，可在不移除数据的情况下保护被标记患者。轻量级筛查工具使生产模型训练前的隐私风险评估成为可能。

Abstract: Medical artificial intelligence (AI) models are often shared without their training data, assuming this protects the patients who contributed them. However, a model can reveal whether a patient's records were used in training, a privacy breach known as a membership inference attack. This risk is usually reported as a cohort average, which can appear negligible while individual patients are almost certainly exposed. Identifying potentially vulnerable records before developing a production model could make privacy assessment more practical. We measured each patient's vulnerability to membership inference attack, across 7,789 models trained on six clinical datasets in four modalities, including two adapted pretrained medical models. Training choices changed how many patients had high attack scores, but the patients who ranked highest by risk largely stayed the same across the configurations we tested. Stronger weight decay reduced the exposed cohort up to threefold, yet largely the same patients remained most at risk across training recipes, architectures and initializations, including foundation models. Therefore, we developed a lightweight screening tool that uses four small models to identify patients with high risk of exposure before production-model training. We also assessed if flagged patients could be protected without removing their data. Privacy screening should become incorporated into data preparation for clinical AI.

</details>


### [87] [Edit, Don't Imitate: Minimal-Edit On-Policy Distillation for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30231)
*Wen Ye,Ailin Deng,Zirui Wang,Shixing Yu,Han Zhang,Tian Yun,Nikhil Mehta,Tarun Kalluri,Xuefei Cao,Xuezhe Ma,Hengduo Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出最小编辑修正的在线策略蒸馏方法，解决视觉语言模型在数学推理中因图表感知错误导致的推理失败，无需测试时提供图表描述即可达到或超越有描述辅助的基准模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉数学推理常因模型误读图表而失败，小模型感知瓶颈尤为明显；虽准确文本描述能缩小与大模型差距，但推理时获取描述需额外标注或模型调用，因此需要一种无需测试时描述即可改善感知的方法。

Method: 提出最小编辑修正在线策略蒸馏：仅在训练时使用参考图表描述和验证过的参考解，教师模型对错误学生轨迹按最小编辑目标打分，仅教师不偏好的token计入修正损失；正确轨迹用任务奖励强化学习。保留在线策略监督，避免全响应模仿。

Result: 跨模型规模，方法无需测试时描述即达到或超越提供参考描述的基准模型；提升感知导向性能，尤其低级几何感知；迁移到外部数学和感知基准；冻结同规模教师也有效，但增益小于大教师。

Conclusion: 最小编辑修正蒸馏有效解决视觉数学推理的感知瓶颈，无需推理时额外描述即可显著提升性能，且具备迁移性，同规模教师也能工作但大教师增益更大。

Abstract: Visual mathematical reasoning can fail before reasoning begins: when a vision-language model (VLM) misreads a diagram, subsequent reasoning proceeds from false premises. This perceptual bottleneck is especially pronounced in smaller models, while accurate textual descriptions can substantially narrow the gap to larger models. Because obtaining such descriptions at inference requires additional annotation or model calls, we propose minimal-edit corrective on-policy distillation. Using reference figure descriptions and verified reference solutions as training-only information, a teacher scores incorrect student rollouts under a minimal-edit objective, and only teacher-disfavored tokens contribute to the corrective loss. Correct rollouts receive task-reward reinforcement learning. This preserves on-policy supervision while avoiding full-response imitation. Across model scales, the method requires no descriptions at test time and meets or exceeds the corresponding base model supplied with a reference figure description at inference. It also improves perception-oriented performance, particularly low-level geometric perception, and transfers to external mathematics and perception benchmarks. Because the minimal-edit teacher evaluates corrections rather than generating its own solution, the method remains effective with a frozen same-size teacher, although its gains are smaller than with a larger teacher on most metrics.

</details>


### [88] [A Quadratic Lens on Muon's Polar Update: Benefits, Pitfalls, and the Role of Newton-Schulz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30280)
*Egor Shulgin,Sam Laing,Antonio Orvieto,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文深入分析了Muon优化器核心PolarGD的正交化更新机制，通过矩阵二次函数的闭形式分析揭示了正交化加速的条件与原理：当梯度远离低秩且最大奇异方向曲率高时正交化有效；PolarGD在误差行向量正交且等范数时一步收敛，不受问题旋转影响；长期表现为由当前迭代点预条件的梯度下降，速度取决于迭代点而非Hessian的条件数；微小奇异值导致慢速收敛，而Muon中使用的少量Newton-Schulz迭代恰好像梯度下降一样处理这些微小奇异值，反而比精确正交化收敛更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon通过近似正交化方向更新矩阵参数，在大语言模型训练中往往比AdamW更快，但何时以及为何正交化有帮助仍不清楚。本文旨在通过理论分析揭示PolarGD（Muon核心正交化更新）的加速机制与适用条件。

Method: 在矩阵二次函数上研究PolarGD，利用闭形式分析优化器行为，并通过精确线性搜索比较更新方向以消除学习率调节的影响。分析单步收敛条件、多步动态下的预条件效应、以及Newton-Schulz近似正交化的影响。

Result: 1) 正交化在梯度远离低秩且最大奇异方向曲率高时有效；2) 当误差行向量正交且等范数时，PolarGD对任意Hessian一步收敛，而GD需所有Hessian特征值相等；3) PolarGD不受问题旋转影响（区别于sign descent）；4) 多步下PolarGD表现为由当前迭代点预条件的GD，速度取决于迭代点条件数而非Hessian条件数；5) 迭代点的微小奇异值导致慢速收敛；6) Muon中少量Newton-Schulz迭代将微小奇异值视为GD处理，比精确正交化收敛更快；7) Transformer注意力矩阵约1/4奇异值落入此慢速收敛区间。

Conclusion: 正交化加速源于将优化几何从Hessian条件数转移到迭代点条件数，解释了快慢两种收敛模式。Muon采用的不完全Newton-Schulz正交化反而通过保留类GD的微小奇异值处理而获得更快收敛，为矩阵参数优化器设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Muon updates matrix-valued parameters along approximately orthogonalized directions and often trains large language models faster than AdamW, yet it remains unclear when and why orthogonalization helps. We study PolarGD, the orthogonalized update at the core of Muon, on matrix quadratics, where optimizers can be analyzed in closed form, and compare update directions under line search to remove the effect of learning-rate tuning. Orthogonalization pays off when the gradient is far from low rank and its largest singular directions see high curvature. PolarGD reaches the minimizer in one step whenever the rows of the error are orthogonal with equal norms, for any Hessian, whereas gradient descent needs all Hessian eigenvalues to be equal; and unlike sign descent, PolarGD is unaffected by rotations of the problem. Over many steps, PolarGD acts as gradient descent preconditioned by the current iterate, so its speed is governed by the conditioning of the iterate rather than of the Hessian. This explains fast regimes as well as a slow one, caused by tiny singular values of the iterate, in which PolarGD is much slower than gradient descent. Finally, the few Newton-Schulz iterations used in Muon treat these tiny singular values as gradient descent does, so fewer iterations can converge faster than exact orthogonalization; in a transformer trained with Muon, about a quarter of the singular values of the attention matrices fall in this regime.

</details>


### [89] [SWE-VERIFIER: Selective Verification for Cost-Efficient Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30284)
*Kaijian Zou,Vaibhav Chennareddy,Yunxiang Zhang,Naihao Deng,Longju Bai,Jiaxin Pei,Rada Mihalcea,Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SWE-Verifier 是一个轻量级生成模型，用于选择性地触发候选动作采样和验证，在极低开销下显著提升编码智能体的任务解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在生成动作和处理长交互历史时消耗大量计算；糟糕的动作选择会导致低价值状态，浪费计算并增加 API 成本。虽然采样和验证备选动作能改善决策，但在每一步都应用会带来巨大开销。

Method: 训练一个基于 Qwen3.5-4B 的轻量级验证模型 SWE-Verifier，利用偏好数据（将对比动作与其环境观察和基于评分标准的教师分数分组）。该模型对智能体提出的动作打分，决定是否需要额外采样候选动作，随后对采样得到的动作进行排序再执行。

Result: 在 mini-swe-agent 测试平台上，SWE-Verifier 将 MiniMax-M2.7 的解决率从 75.47% 提升至 77.20%，成本仅为 1.09 倍；零样本泛化到 DeepSeek-V4-Flash，解决率从 72.60% 提升至 74.27%，成本 1.13 倍。两模型上均仅验证 <4% 的步骤，在大幅更低成本下达到与轨迹级和全步骤基线相当或更高的解决率。

Conclusion: 选择性候选采样与轻量级验证在编码智能体性能与推理效率上提供互补增益，证明了按需验证的有效性。模型、代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Coding agents spend substantial computation generating actions and processing long interaction histories. Poor action choices can steer agents into low-value states, wasting computation and increasing API costs. Sampling and verifying alternative actions can improve the agent's decisions, but applying this procedure at every step adds substantial overhead. We introduce SWE-Verifier, a lightweight generative model that selectively triggers candidate sampling and verification. It scores the coding agent's proposed action to determine when additional candidate sampling is needed, then ranks the resulting actions before execution. We train SWE-Verifier from Qwen3.5-4B using preference data that groups contrasting actions with their resulting environment observations and rubric-guided teacher scores. Using the mini-swe-agent harness, SWE-Verifier improves resolution from 75.47% to 77.20% on MiniMax-M2.7 at 1.09 times cost and generalizes to DeepSeek-V4-Flash without training on its actions, improving resolution from 72.60% to 74.27% at 1.13 times cost. Across both models, SWE-Verifier verifies fewer than 4% of steps and achieves comparable or higher resolution than the trajectory and all-step baselines at substantially lower cost. These results demonstrate that selective candidate sampling and lightweight verification provide complementary gains in coding-agent performance and inference efficiency. Our model, code, and data are available at https://github.com/zkjzou/SWE-Verifier.

</details>


### [90] [Generalizing Metropolis-Hastings for DAG Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30291)
*Daniele Nikzad,Mikko Koivisto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法——duet sampling（二重采样），用于贝叶斯网络结构学习中的有向无环图（DAG）后验分布近似。该方法通过同时重采样多个节点的父节点，并从提议分布池中选择两个不同的提议分布（一个用于生成候选状态，另一个用于评估返回当前状态），构造了更高效的接受准则，显著提升了混合效率，且无额外计算开销。实验表明，该方法在单独使用或组合使用时均优于现有最先进方法，即使让竞争方法也使用duet sampling增强后依然保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯网络结构学习的核心挑战在于高效近似DAG的后验分布，特别是在大规模、高维数据集上，现有MCMC方法因移动操作受限（如单节点父节点重采样）导致混合缓慢、难以扩展。需要一种能同时更新多节点结构、且不增加计算成本的更灵活的采样策略。

Method: 提出duet sampling，作为Metropolis-Hastings算法的推广：每次迭代从提议分布池中选择两个不同的提议分布，分别用于生成候选状态和评估反向转移概率，构造对应的接受准则。基于此设计了新的MCMC移动操作，可同时重采样多个节点的父节点集合。该框架允许配对不同提议分布，提供额外自由度，构建混合更快的马尔可夫链，且无额外计算开销。

Result: 实验表明，新提出的移动操作（单独或组合使用）在多个基准数据集上均优于现有最先进方法。即使允许竞争方法引入duet sampling进行增强，本文方法仍保持显著性能优势，体现了其在混合效率和结构探索能力上的根本改进。

Conclusion: Duet sampling通过双提议分布机制有效扩展了MCMC在DAG空间的探索能力，实现了多节点父节点的联合重采样，以零额外计算成本显著提升混合效率。该方法为大规模贝叶斯网络结构学习提供了可扩展、高效的新范式，优于现有最先进技术。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in Bayesian network structure learning lies in efficiently approximating the posterior distribution of directed acyclic graphs (DAGs). For large, high-dimensional datasets, the task remains beyond the capabilities of current methods. This work advances Markov chain Monte Carlo algorithms by introducing novel moves that simultaneously resample the parents for multiple nodes of the DAG. At the core of these moves is duet sampling, a new generalization of the Metropolis-Hastings algorithm that selects two proposal distributions from a pool at each iteration. One is used for generating a candidate state of the Markov chain and another for evaluating the return to the current state; both are ingredients of an appropriately crafted acceptance criterion. Pairing different proposal distributions provides additional freedom and enables constructing Markov chains that mix more efficiently than their basic Metropolis-Hastings counterparts, at no additional computational cost. Experiments show that our new moves, both individually and in combination, outperform the previous state of the art, which relies on a more restrictive pool of moves. This advantage persists even when letting the previous methods leverage duet sampling to enhance their performance.

</details>


### [91] [GAV: A scientific experimentation environment for PDE agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30292)
*Muheng Li,Wenlong Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAV是一个用于PDE求解代理开发的科学实验环境，通过生成器学习连续参考解分布，利用制造解方法反向构造PDE问题，使验证无需额外数值求解，从而大幅降低开发成本并提高评估可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PDE求解代理开发面临两大瓶颈：1) 新PDE问题评估需要昂贵的参考计算；2) 固定数据集限制了实验范围。需要一种能按构造生成可验证新PDE问题的环境，以支持代理的迭代改进和可靠评估。

Method: GAV包含三个核心组件：Generator从数值模拟或实验数据学习连续参考解分布，利用制造解方法（MMS）反向构造匹配的PDE问题（无需额外数值求解）；Agent设计实验、调查失败并迭代改进程序；Verifier基于参考解评估候选程序，提供精度和计算成本的数值反馈，并可指导生成针对当前程序弱点的新问题。提供了评估可靠性和引导开发的理论分析。

Result: 在Navier-Stokes和Vlasov-Poisson类方程的不同数据源和物理机制下验证有效性。数值Navier-Stokes任务上，GAV将三个现有PDE代理的平均测试误差相较其原始设置降低了一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: GAV通过生成-验证闭环解决了PDE代理开发中的评估瓶颈，制造解方法使新问题构造高效可靠，验证器反馈驱动的针对性问题生成显著加速代理迭代改进，为科学机器学习代理开发提供了可扩展的实验范式。

Abstract: Reliable feedback is essential for coding agents to develop programs for partial differential equations (PDEs). Yet evaluating these programs on new PDE problems often requires expensive reference computations, while fixed datasets restrict the range of experiments. We introduce Generator-Agent-Verifier (GAV), a scientific experimentation environment that makes new PDE problems verifiable by construction. The Generator learns a distribution of continuous reference solutions using data from numerical simulations or experimental measurements to represent a target physical regime. Using the method of manufactured solutions, it works backward from the generated reference solutions to construct matching PDE problems without a separate numerical solve. The Agent designs experiments, investigates failures, and iteratively improves its program. The Verifier evaluates candidate programs against the reference solutions and provides numerical feedback on accuracy and computational cost. This feedback can also guide the generation of new problems that probe the current program's weaknesses. We provide a theoretical analysis of evaluation reliability and guided development. Experiments on Navier-Stokes and Vlasov-Poisson-type equations across different data sources and regimes demonstrate the effectiveness of GAV. In particular, on the numerical Navier-Stokes task, GAV reduces the mean test error of three existing PDE agents by more than an order of magnitude relative to their original setups. Code is available at https://github.com/PotatoJnny/PDE-GAV.

</details>


### [92] [Spike-Aware Gradient-Based Mixed-Precision Quantization of Spiking Neural Networks for Vision Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30339)
*Rajshekhar Rakshit,Devgokul B.,Rakshitha Kalkura,Gopalakrishnan Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向脉冲神经网络（SNN）的脉冲感知梯度混合精度量化框架，利用一阶梯度信息自适应分配位宽，避免了昂贵的二阶海森矩阵计算，在保持精度的同时显著降低内存消耗和位宽分配运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN因其事件驱动和稀疏计算特性而备受关注，但在低精度约束下保持精度尚未充分探索。现有基于海森矩阵的量化方法（如HAWQ-V2）因跨多个SNN时间步的时间展开导致巨大的内存和计算开销。

Method: 提出脉冲感知梯度混合精度量化框架，利用一阶梯度信息自适应分配位宽，避免二阶海森矩阵计算。在ANN-to-SNN转换和直接训练得到的VGG、ResNet、Vision Transformer等SNN架构上进行评估。

Result: 在ImageNet上ResNet-34达到73.30%精度，压缩率4.78×，与HAWQ-V2相当，但内存消耗降低2.31×，位宽分配运行时间加速100×。在CIFAR10/100、DVS-CIFAR-10上也表现优异。能量分析显示该量化策略实现了节能部署且不损失性能。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了SNN低精度量化的空白，提供了一种高效的混合精度量化方法，显著降低了计算和内存开销，为SNN在资源受限设备上的部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: In recent years, Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have gained massive attention as an energy-efficient neural computing paradigm due to their event-driven and sparse computing capability. However, maintaining accuracy under low-precision constraints, necessary for practical deployment, has been largely unexplored for SNN models. In addition, existing Hessian-based approaches for quantization, such as HAWQ-V2, incur significant memory and computational overhead due to temporal unrolling across multiple SNN timesteps. We propose a spike-aware gradient-based mixed-precision quantization framework for SNNs that adaptively assigns bit-widths using first-order gradient information, avoiding costly second-order Hessian computations. We evaluate our method across various SNN-based architectures, including VGG, ResNet, and Vision Transformer models obtained from both ANN-to-SNN conversion and direct training frameworks, on CIFAR10/100, ImageNet, and DVS-CIFAR-10 datasets. Notably, on ResNet-34 evaluated on ImageNet, our method achieves 73.30% accuracy at 4.78$\times$ compression, which is comparable to that provided by HAWQ-V2, while reducing memory consumption by 2.31$\times$ and bit-allocation runtime by 100$\times$. We also present an energy analysis for mixed-precision SNNs showing that the proposed quantization strategy enables energy-efficient deployment without compromising performance.

</details>


### [93] [CTRL-FM: Inference-Time Structural Control for Flow-Based Virtual Sensing under Long-Horizon Node Outages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30362)
*Boshra Khalili,Andrew Smyth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CTRL-FM 是一种基于流匹配的观测保留框架，用于长时程节点故障下的传感器数据插补，通过推理时自适应选择结构引导策略，实现优于固定先验的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程节点故障比传统缺失值插补更难，因为完整传感器轨迹长期未观测，且时空依赖的有效性随故障位置和运行模式变化。现有方法通常施加固定结构偏置，可能过度平滑突变或传播不可靠邻居的误差。

Method: CTRL-FM 包含仅缺失流匹配主干网络，保持观测测量固定；为每个实际故障构建自监督代理故障，利用已知值在无控制、图主导、时间主导、平衡四种控制策略中选择；选定策略由仅从存活观测估计的图和时间可靠性局部调制，边界锚定趋势提供结构引导；控制仅施加于缺失条目，当代理证据不足以改进无控制采样时拒绝控制。

Result: 在 PeMS08、METR-LA、Air-36 和 AQI 数据集上，CTRL-FM 在 30% 和 60% 节点故障下取得最强整体性能；高达 75% 传感器损失的压力测试显示优雅降级，并持续优于训练时固定结构控制。

Conclusion: 针对每个实际故障验证、选择并局部自适应结构引导，比施加单一全局结构先验更有效。

Abstract: Long-horizon node outages are harder than conventional missing-value imputation because complete sensor trajectories remain unobserved for extended periods, while the usefulness of spatial and temporal dependencies varies across outage locations and operating regimes. Existing graph, diffusion, and flow-based methods typically impose a fixed structural bias, which can oversmooth abrupt changes or propagate errors from unreliable neighbors. We introduce CTRL-FM, an observation-preserving flow-matching framework that treats structural guidance as an outage-conditioned inference-time decision. A missing-only flow-matching backbone learns a conditional velocity field while keeping observed measurements exactly fixed. For each realized outage, CTRL-FM constructs self-supervised proxy outages from surviving measurements and uses their known values to select among uncontrolled, graph-dominant, temporal-dominant, and balanced control policies. The selected policy is locally modulated by graph and temporal reliability estimated exclusively from surviving observations, while boundary-anchored trends provide structural guidance. Control is applied only to missing entries and is rejected when proxy evidence does not sufficiently improve over uncontrolled sampling. Across PeMS08, METR-LA, Air-36, and AQI, CTRL-FM achieves the strongest overall performance under 30% and 60% node outages. Stress tests with up to 75% sensor loss further show graceful degradation and consistent gains over training-time and fixed structural control. These results demonstrate that validating, selecting, and locally adapting structural guidance for each realized outage is more effective than imposing a single global structural prior.

</details>


### [94] [Probing and Steering for Safety-Relevant Behaviours in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30363)
*Nimun Bajwa,Massimo Poesio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统探讨了激活探针在 LLM 中检测和引导三种行为（欺骗、高风险意识、讨好）的设计选择，对比了两种估计器和三种池化方案，发现“读/写分离”现象在欺骗和高风险任务中表现为逻辑回归检测最优、均值差引导最优，但在讨好任务中反转；最佳池化和层选择随任务和数据变化，引导最优层可早于检测最优层，且引导指标随任务不同甚至相互矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作零散地研究探针设计选择，缺乏在统一流程、同一模型、多行为下的系统对比，且“读/写分离”假说未在多样化行为上验证，需明确估计器、池化、层选择及评估指标如何相互作用。

Method: 在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上构建统一流程：交叉两种估计器（均值差、逻辑回归）与三种池化（最后 token、均值、学习注意力），为欺骗、高风险意识、讨好三行为分别选取检测层与引导层，将每个方向同时作为检测器和引导向量评估，使用检测准确率、引导成功率等指标，并导出探针供交互界面使用。

Result: 1) 读/写分离在欺骗和高风险任务中成立：逻辑回归检测最优，均值差引导最优；讨好任务反转。2) 反转部分源于数据分布而非估计器本身。3) 最佳池化方式和层选择强依赖行为和数据。4) 引导最优层可显著早于检测最优层。5) 改变模型感知与诱发行为的引导指标随任务不同，甚至给出相反建议。

Conclusion: 探针设计需针对具体行为和数据定制，不存在通用最优配置；读/写分离非普适规律，数据分布影响估计器相对性能；层选择应分离检测与引导目标；引导评估需根据任务目标（感知变化 vs 行为诱发）选取指标。开源探针与交互界面为后续研究和应用提供工具。

Abstract: Activation probes are computed directions in a language model's residual stream, used both to detect safety-relevant behaviours and, by adding their direction back into the stream, to steer them. We explore how the main design choices behind such probes interact, within one pipeline, on one model (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct), and across three behaviours: deception, high-stakes awareness and sycophancy. The pipeline crosses two estimators (difference-of-means and logistic regression) with three token-pooling schemes (last, mean and learned attention), selects appropriate layers to detect or steer a behaviour, and evaluates every direction twice: as a detector of the behaviour, and as a steering vector to push outputs towards the behaviour. Prior work read together suggests a read/write dissociation, in which logistic regression reads (detects) best but difference-of-means directions write (steer) best. We find that it holds for deception and high-stakes but not for sycophancy, where the pattern reverses. The reversal appears to follow partially from the data rather than from the estimators. The best pooling technique and the layer choice depend on the behaviour and data, and the layer that steers best can sit well before the layer that detects best. The right steering metric to use between changing a model's perception and eliciting behaviour depends on the task - and the two can disagree outright. The exported probes and directions power an interactive interface through which a user can score and steer text directly.

</details>


### [95] [Lean Finder V2: Semantic Search for Mathlib That Disambiguates User Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30843)
*Jialin Lu,Xiaoyu Liu,Hengrui Luo,Rodrigo Stehling,Linxi Liu,Weiran Sun,Yuepeng Wang,Wuyang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Lean Finder V2 是一个针对 Mathlib 的语义搜索框架，通过模型级对抗训练和输入级查询重写来消除用户查询的歧义，在 MathlibQR 基准测试上达到最先进水平，相对改进至少 11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Mathlib 搜索引擎假设用户提供上下文丰富的查询，但实际中数学家和编码代理通常只提供简短的性质、符号、别名或假设，留下大量隐含数学意图。缺乏带有真值 Mathlib 语句的真实短查询数据，使得这种歧义消除问题更加棘手。作者认为这本质上是一个数据问题。

Method: 通过对抗训练大规模生成带标签的短查询和歧义查询，并在模型级微调和输入级查询重写两个层面学习消除用户查询歧义。结合互补的模型级对抗训练和输入级查询重写，实现查询消歧。

Result: 在人工策划的 MathlibQR 基准上，Lean Finder V2 设立新的 SOTA，相对改进至少 11%。在最优检索器基础上，优化的查询重写进一步提升检索效果。在来自人类和编码代理交互的无标签查询上，Lean Finder V2 被评为最强系统。集成到 Claude Code 后，在下游定理证明任务上提升证明成功率。

Conclusion: Lean Finder V2 通过将查询歧义消除视为数据问题，利用对抗训练扩充训练数据，结合模型级微调和输入级查询重写，有效解决了 Mathlib 语义搜索中短查询歧义问题，实现了最先进的检索性能，并验证了在实际定理证明工作流中的实用价值。

Abstract: We introduce Lean Finder V2, a semantic search framework for Mathlib that disambiguates user queries through complementary model-level adversarial training and input-level query rewriting. Human mathematicians and coding agents rarely search Mathlib by providing detailed context. In practice, they query short properties, notation, aliases, or hypotheses, leaving much of the intended mathematics implicit. In contrast, existing Mathlib search engines unrealistically assume context-rich queries. The scarcity of real-world short queries paired with ground-truth Mathlib statements makes this ambiguity even more intractable. We argue that this is essentially a data problem. Through adversarial training, we scale up the generation of labeled short and ambiguous queries, and learn to disambiguate user queries at both model-level fine-tuning and input-level query rewriting. On the human-curated MathlibQR benchmark, our Lean Finder V2 sets a new state of the art, with at least 11% relative improvement over the previous best Mathlib semantic search engines. On top of our state-of-the-art retriever, optimized query rewriting further improves MathlibQR retrieval. On unlabeled queries from human and coding-agent interactions, Lean Finder V2 is ranked as the strongest system. Integrated with Claude Code, Lean Finder V2 improves proof success on downstream theorem-proving task. Lean Finder V2 is available at: https://leanfinder.github.io/

</details>


### [96] [Bayesian Fixed-Confidence Best-Arm Identification with Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30384)
*Yuqi Huang,Yunlong Hou,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带有弃权选项的贝叶斯固定置信度最优臂识别问题，提出了Screen-and-Confirm (S&C) 算法并分析其期望停止时间。在高斯先验和奖励假设下，建立了匹配的上下界，揭示了近似平局实例主导采样成本；当不允许弃权时，S&C 实现了最优的 Θ(1/δ) 依赖，移除了先前工作中的额外 log(1/δ) 因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最优臂识别问题通常要求必须给出推荐，但实际场景中允许学习者在不确定时弃权。本文旨在研究在目标错误概率 δ 和弃权预算 α 约束下，如何设计算法最小化期望停止时间，并刻画基本极限。

Method: 提出 Screen-and-Confirm (S&C) 算法，包含筛选阶段和确认阶段。通过理论分析其期望停止时间，并推导满足约束条件的任意算法的最小期望停止时间下界。重点分析高斯先验和奖励设置下的渐近行为。

Result: 1. 对于高斯先验和奖励，当 (δ,α)↓(0,0) 时，建立了匹配的上下界，速率为 κ_ν^2[1+log((α+δ)/δ)]/(α+δ)，其中 κ_ν 是前两名差距在零处的先验密度，表明近似平局实例主导采样成本。2. 当 α=0（不允许弃权）时，S&C 达到最优的 Θ(1/δ) 依赖，移除了 Jang et al. (2024) 上界中的额外 log(1/δ) 因子。3. 刻画了固定 α∈(0,1) 和 α/δ→∞ 两种实际制度下的精确乘法常数，S&C 均能达到。

Conclusion: S&C 算法在带有弃权选项的贝叶斯固定置信度最优臂识别问题中是最优的（在乘法常数意义上），其性能由近似平局实例决定。该工作完善了该问题的理论理解，并为实际应用提供了精确的采样复杂度表征。

Abstract: We study the Bayesian fixed-confidence best-arm identification problem in which a learner can abstain from making a terminal recommendation. Subject to a target error probability $δ$ and an abstention budget $α$, we propose Screen-and-Confirm (S&C) and analyze its expected stopping time. We further derive the minimum expected stopping time for any algorithm that satisfies these constraints. For Gaussian priors and rewards, as $(δ,α)\downarrow(0,0)$, we establish matching upper and lower bounds up to multiplicative constants, with rate $κ_ν^2[1+\log((α+δ)/δ)]/(α+δ)$, where $κ_ν$ is the prior density of the top-two gap at zero, highlighting that nearly tied instances govern the sampling cost. When abstention is disallowed ($α=0$), S&C yields the optimal $Θ(1/δ)$ dependence for fixed model parameters, removing the additional $\log(1/δ)$ factor in the upper bound of Jang et al. (2024) under our Gaussian assumptions. We further characterize the exact multiplicative constant of the minimum expected stopping time for practical regimes including (i) fixed $α\in (0,1)$ and (ii) $α/δ\to\infty$, which are also achieved by S&C.

</details>


### [97] [Escaping the Phylogenetic Trap: Persistent Homology Enables Cross-Species Ion Channel Classification via Multimodal Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30394)
*Arezoo Bybordi,Favour Achimba,Mariam Gelashvili,Jessy Ramirez,Weigang Qiu,Mandë Holford,Anita Raja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究蛋白质分类器在随机划分训练/测试集时的系统发育泄漏问题，提出用拓扑持久同伦表示作为几何先验，在留一分类单元评估协议下显著优于其他结构归纳偏置，证明拓扑表示能有效应对结构化生物分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质分类器在随机划分评估下报告80-90%高准确率，但存在严重的系统发育泄漏：模型利用分类单元特有的相关性而非结构特征，导致对未见分类单元的泛化能力被高估。毒液化合物按离子通道靶点（Ca2+、K+、Na+）分类尤为典型，需要能捕捉跨系统发育不变结构特征的表示。

Method: 构建共享多模态主干网络融合ESM-2序列嵌入与二硫键感知图注意力网络，系统比较五种结构归纳偏置（谱、黎曼、SE(3)不变、统计流形、持久同伦）在1373个ColabFold预测毒液化合物（跨五个分类单元）上的留一分类单元表现。持久同伦表示通过拓扑描述符概括主链几何，对局部结构扰动鲁棒，提供系统发育不变学习的归纳偏置。

Result: 随机i.i.d.划分下所有模型准确率相近（81-86%），掩盖了归纳偏置差异。分类单元分布偏移下：仅序列基线跌至约30%，其他几何先验聚集在54-55%，持久同伦模型（PH-GNN）达63.4%，比次优方法高出8个百分点以上。

Conclusion: 随机划分评估掩盖了表示质量的实质差异；拓扑持久同伦表示作为有效归纳偏置，能学习跨系统发育不变的结构特征，显著提升结构化生物分布偏移下的泛化性能。

Abstract: Protein classifiers are highly sensitive to evaluation protocol: random train/test splits induce phylogenetic leakage, allowing models to exploit taxon-specific correlations and substantially overestimate out-of-distribution performance. This issue is particularly acute for venom compound classification by ion-channel target (Ca2+, K+, Na+), where reported accuracies of 80-90% do not reflect generalization to unseen taxa. We formulate cross-species prediction as a structured distribution shift in which taxonomic identity acts as a confounder, requiring representations that capture structural features invariant across phylogeny. Using a shared multimodal backbone that fuses ESM-2 sequence embeddings with a disulfide-aware graph attention network, we systematically compare five structural inductive biases (spectral, Riemannian, SE(3)-invariant, statistical-manifold, and persistent-homology representations) under a leave-one-taxon-out evaluation protocol on a benchmark of 1,373 ColabFold-predicted venom compounds spanning five taxa. The persistent-homology representation summarizes backbone geometry through topological descriptors that are robust to local structural perturbations, providing an inductive bias toward phylogenetically invariant learning. Under conventional i.i.d. splits, all models achieve similar accuracy (81-86%), masking differences in inductive bias. Under taxonomic shift, this parity collapses: a sequence-only baseline drops to approximately 30%, the remaining geometric priors cluster at 54-55%, while our persistent-homology model (PH-GNN) achieves 63.4%, outperforming the next-best method by over 8 percentage points. These results demonstrate that random-split evaluation obscures meaningful differences in representation quality and identify topological representations as an effective inductive bias for learning under structured biological distribution shift.

</details>


### [98] [AcoustiPrint: Physics-Guided Neural Design of Manufacturable Acoustic Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30411)
*Shanmuka Sadhu,Harshvardhan C. Takawale,Yehia Zakaria,Ahmed Allam,Nirupam Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AcoustiPrint是一种物理信息神经架构，用于受约束的逆向设计，通过隐式神经表示(INR)和可打印性算子实现可制造的3D声学结构设计，解决了非可微制造约束与可微物理仿真之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增材制造和神经逆向设计方法在处理离散、非可微制造约束时，要么使用软约束损失导致设计空间受限，要么依赖后处理导致优化几何与最终制造结果存在差距，无法同时保证物理性能优化和制造可行性。

Method: 提出AcoustiPrint架构：1) 使用隐式神经表示(INR)参数化3D几何；2) 引入可打印性算子，执行逐层自支撑、离散材料选择和整体连通性检查，保证制造可行性；3) 采用代理梯度实现非可微操作的反向传播；4) 设计自监督可打印性目标，逐步引导INR向有效设计域收敛；5) 结合可微波动物理仿真，联合优化几何和材料组成。

Result: 在个性化经颅聚焦超声应用中，AcoustiPrint生成的可制造声学结构相比基于密度和基于相位的基线方法，实现了更高的聚焦精度，验证了该方法在非可微制造约束下的神经设计泛化能力。

Conclusion: AcoustiPrint通过将可打印性约束显式集成到优化循环中，弥合了可微物理仿真与非可微制造约束之间的鸿沟，为个性化医疗器械设计等领域提供了通用的神经逆向设计方法论。

Abstract: Recent work in additive manufacturing and neural inverse design enables the creation of complex three-dimensional structures to manipulate physical fields, yet these designs must also adhere to discrete, non-differentiable fabrication constraints. Current methods limit the design space by imposing differentiable soft manufacturability loss penalties, or by fixing structures through post-processing, causing a discrepancy between the geometry optimized by the physics model and the final fabricated result. Thus, we present AcoustiPrint, a physics-informed neural architecture for constrained inverse design that represents three-dimensional geometries with an implicit neural representation (INR) and passes each candidate through a printability operator before evaluating its final response. The printability operator executes layer-wise self-support, discrete material selection, and whole-body connectivity, assuring fabrication feasibility. To allow for backpropagation through the discrete non-differentiable operations, we use surrogate gradients for the backward pass and a self-supervised printability objective that progressively drives the INR toward a valid design domain. We demonstrate AcoustiPrint for personalized transcranial focused ultrasound, where inter-patient differences in acoustic properties and head geometry deform wave propagation and requires patient-specific acoustic focusing. AcoustiPrint jointly optimizes three-dimensional geometry and material composition through differentiable wave physics while ensuring manufacturability. Experiments show manufacturable acoustic structures with enhanced focal accuracy and over density-based and phase-based baselines, demonstrating a generalized methodology for neural design through non-differentiable manufacturing constraints and differentiable physics.

</details>


### [99] [Memory-Augmented Online Test-Time Adaptation for EEG Motor Imagery via Perturbation Consistency and Confidence Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30422)
*Kunkun Zhang,Kehao Wu,Zhitao Chen,Haigen Hu,Qianwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOPC框架，一种无需源数据的在线测试时自适应方法，通过随机掩码扰动一致性、置信度校准重加权损失和轻量级记忆模块，实现了EEG-BCI的实时、鲁棒解码，在两个公开数据集上达到SOTA性能且延迟极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG-BCI系统面临非平稳性和跨被试分布偏移导致的被试间不一致问题；现有自适应方法通常需离线访问源或聚合目标数据，不适用于实时测量场景，亟需一种无源、单次通过、低延迟的在线自适应方案。

Method: MOPC包含三大核心组件：(1) 随机掩码扰动机制，强制模型对EEG固有噪声和伪影保持预测一致性；(2) 置信度校准重加权损失，融合特征空间相似度以抑制不可靠伪标签并缓解类别不平衡；(3) 轻量级记忆模块，在严格延迟预算内正则化参数更新，防止已建立神经模式的灾难性遗忘。整体框架为源自由、单次通过的在线测试时自适应。

Result: 在BCI IV IIb和OpenBMI数据集上分别比先前最佳方法提升2.22%和3.67%，单次试验自适应延迟约40-60 ms，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为实时EEG解码提供了一种鲁棒、免重新校准的解决方案，显著推进了实际BCI测量仪器的精度与易用性。

Abstract: Recently, electroencephalography (EEG)-based brain--computer interfaces (BCIs) have gained traction as portable neural measurement systems. However, the practical deployment of these instruments is often hindered by nonstationarity and cross-subject distribution shifts, which lead to significant intersubject inconsistency. Existing adaptation methods remain impractical for real-time measurement scenarios, as they typically require offline access to source or aggregated target data. To address these challenges, this article proposes memory-augmented online test-time adaptation (OTTA) with perturbation consistency and confidence calibration (MOPC), a source-free framework designed to enhance the reliability of BCI instrumentation. MOPC enables the measurement system to update its decoding parameters on the fly during the inference stage, facilitating single-pass adaptation without accessing sensitive source data. We introduce random-mask perturbations to enforce prediction consistency against inherent EEG signal noise and acquisition artifacts. Furthermore, a confidence-calibrated reweighting loss is designed to integrate feature-space similarity, effectively mitigating unreliable pseudolabels and class imbalance during continuous signal acquisition. To ensure system stability and prevent the catastrophic forgetting of established neural patterns, a lightweight memory module is incorporated to regularize parameter updates within a tight latency budget. Evaluated on the BCI IV IIb and OpenBMI datasets, MOPC achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming prior methods by 2.22~\% and 3.67~\%, respectively, while keeping the per-trial adaptation latency to approximately 40--60 ms. This study provides a robust, recalibration-free solution for real-time EEG decoding, marking a significant advancement in the precision and usability of practical BCI measurement instruments.

</details>


### [100] [Towards Faithful Data Selection: Statistical Limits and Scalable Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30442)
*Ahmed Mehdi Inane,Mahdi Haghifam,Gintare Karolina Dziugaite,Ioannis Mitliagkas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在没有极大验证集的情况下，最优数据选择（最小化子集训练分类器的总体误差）在信息论上是不可能的。因此提出了“忠实性”概念：提供一个可估计的代理指标，上界全量数据训练与子集训练参数分布的差异。基于无调整朗之万算法建模训练过程，通过轨迹累积梯度差异界定KL散度，并提出基于交换的局部搜索算法最小化该界，可在不恶化界的前提下改进任意选择器的输出。实验表明该方法在CIFAR10/100上与现有基线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优数据选择目标（最小化子集训练模型的总体风险）在无超大验证集时信息论上不可行，需要从不可验证的总体风险最小化转向可验证的单侧保证——忠实性，即为任意候选子集提供可估计的上界，量化其与全量数据训练的差距。

Method: 采用无调整朗之万算法（ULA）建模训练动态，将子集训练与全量训练参数分布的KL散度上界为轨迹累积梯度差异。最小化该差异即为原则性子集选择目标。虽该问题NP-hard，但提出基于交换的局部搜索：每步接受严格降低界的交换操作，可细化任意选择器输出且保证界不增。

Result: 在CIFAR10和CIFAR100上实验表明，该交换局部搜索方法能有效降低理论界，且最终测试性能与现有数据选择基线（如随机选择、核心集、影响函数等）相当，验证了忠实性代理的有效性与算法的实用性。

Conclusion: 论文从信息论角度揭示了最优数据选择的根本局限，提出忠实性作为可行替代目标，并给出基于ULA轨迹梯度差异的理论界与可优化的交换局部搜索算法，实现了在不恶化理论保证下改进任意选择器，为数据选择提供了新的原则性框架。

Abstract: What does it mean to have an optimal data selection algorithm? Optimality is conventionally framed as finding a subset that minimizes the population error of the classifier trained only on that subset. Our starting point in this work is first to show that this goal is information-theoretically impossible unless the data selection algorithm has access to a validation set order-wise larger than the training set. This impossibility result motivates shifting from unverifiable population risk minimization to a one-sided guarantee that we refer to as faithfulness: for any candidate subset, we provide an estimable proxy that upper-bounds the difference between training on the full training data and on only the subset. We model training with the Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm and bound the Kullback--Leibler divergence between subset-trained and full-data-trained parameter distributions by the trajectory-accumulated gradient discrepancy. Minimizing this discrepancy gives a principled way to select a subset. Even though the resulting problem is NP-hard, we propose a swap-based local search that decreases the bound at every accepted step. It can therefore refine the output of any selector without worsening its bound. Finally, we provide experimental results on CIFAR10 and CIFAR100 showing that it matches existing data selection baselines. Implementation of our algorithm and the code for reproducing the results are available at: https://github.com/Mehdi-Inane/data_selection_swap.

</details>


### [101] [Stability Analysis of Signed Graphs under Structural Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30444)
*Ning Zhang,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究签名图滤波器在边扰动（添加、删除、符号翻转）下的稳定性，提出理论指导的扰动策略，实验表明该策略能显著引起嵌入漂移、极性改变和下游链接预测性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图滤波器稳定性分析主要针对无符号图，未考虑符号翻转扰动，而签名图学习中正负交互并存，需要针对符号感知和分布感知的稳定性分析。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示有符号边扰动与节点特征相关性如何共同决定表示稳定性，基于此提出理论指导的扰动策略，选择结构上有影响力的有符号边修改。

Result: 在合成和真实签名图上实验显示，提出的方法比基线持续产生更大的嵌入漂移、极性变化和下游链接预测性能退化。

Conclusion: 结果强调了符号感知和分布感知稳定性分析对签名图学习的重要性，为鲁棒签名图学习提供了理论依据和实用扰动策略。

Abstract: Signed graph learning has emerged as a powerful framework for modeling networks with both positive and negative interactions. While graph-filter-based methods have achieved promising empirical performance on signed networks, their stability under edge perturbations remains less well understood. Existing stability analyses primarily focus on unsigned graphs and do not account for sign-flip perturbations. In this paper, we study the stability of signed graph filters under edge additions, deletions, and sign flips. Our theoretical analysis reveals how signed edge perturbations and node feature correlations jointly determine the learned representation stability. Based on this analysis, we propose a theory-guided perturbation strategy that selects structurally influential signed-edge modifications. Experiments on synthetic and real signed graphs show that the proposed method consistently induces larger embedding drift, polarity change, and downstream link prediction degradation than baselines. These results highlight the importance of \textit{sign-aware} and \textit{distribution-aware} stability analysis for signed graph learning.

</details>


### [102] [aggmetrics: Aggregating Classification Performance Measures for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30456)
*Ronaldo Cristiano Prati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: aggmetrics 是一个 Python 包，将基于混淆矩阵四元计数的二分类性能度量扩展到多类、多标签、序数、成本敏感、软标签和交叉验证等场景，通过四种聚合算子替代传统平均开关，并提供结构性质存储、推断与警告功能，测试套件将理论结果编码为可执行测试，仅依赖 NumPy，采用 BSD-3 许可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有库通常仅提供简单的平均开关（如 macro、micro、weighted），无法统一处理多类、多标签、序数、成本敏感、软标签和交叉验证等复杂场景，且缺乏对度量结构性质的系统管理，导致在不适用场景下使用度量时不易察觉。

Method: 提出四种聚合算子（宏观、微观、加权、样本级）替代平均开关，将任意基于混淆矩阵四元计数的二分类度量推广到上述六种设置；为每个度量存储决定恒等式适用性的结构性质，支持从用户提供的公式推断性质，并在度量用于不符合常规假设的场景时发出警告；将配套理论结果编码为可执行测试套件。

Result: 实现了聚合算子框架，支持多类、多标签、序数、成本敏感、软标签和交叉验证六种扩展场景；提供结构性质自动推断与误用警告机制；测试套件覆盖理论结果；包仅依赖 NumPy，BSD-3 开源，文档完善。

Conclusion: aggmetrics 为分类性能评估提供了统一、理论严谨且可扩展的工具，解决了现有库在复杂场景下度量扩展不一致、缺乏结构性质管理的问题，适合研究与生产环境中严格的模型评估需求。

Abstract: aggmetrics is a Python package that extends any binary performance measure expressed in the four confusion-matrix counts to multiclass, multilabel, ordinal, cost-sensitive, soft-label and cross-validation settings, by substituting one of four aggregation operators for what libraries usually expose as an average switch. The package also stores, for every measure, the structural properties that decide which identities apply to it, can infer those properties from a user-supplied formula, and uses them to warn when a measure is used where it does not behave as commonly assumed. Its test suite encodes the results of the companion theory as executable tests. The package is BSD-3 licensed, depends only on NumPy, and is available at https://github.com/rcprati/aggmetrics; documentation at https://aggmetrics.readthedocs.io.

</details>


### [103] [Short-to-Long: Detached Features for Long-Horizon Value Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30461)
*Tong Che,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Short-to-Long (S2L)方法，通过将小折扣源网络学习的特征迁移给长时域行为学习器，并利用梯度隔离防止行为价值损失更新源网络，解决了深度强化学习中持续变化的价值目标导致的表示学习不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习中从持续变化的价值目标学习有用表示仍具挑战性。较小折扣因子使目标对远期自举变化不敏感，但直接用于行为会改变控制目标。需要一种方法在保持长时域行为目标的同时，利用小折扣的稳定性学习鲁棒特征。

Method: S2L架构包含多个小折扣源网络和一个长时域行为网络。源网络独立优化各自的价值目标学习特征，通过分离连接将特征传递给行为网络，阻断行为价值损失对源网络的梯度回传，实现特征迁移与目标解耦。

Result: 在Atari、在线连续控制和离线连续控制任务上均取得综合性能提升。Atari 57个游戏中40个游戏的配对终端中位数性能得到改进。消融实验验证了特征迁移、小折扣源网络和梯度隔离的关键作用。固定轨迹测量显示混合折扣下源价值目标的漂移显著减少。

Conclusion: S2L通过解耦表示学习与长时域控制目标，成功利用小折扣的稳定性训练鲁棒特征，同时保持原始行为目标不变。该方法为深度强化学习中的价值目标不稳定性提供了一个通用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning useful representations from continually changing value targets remains a challenge in deep reinforcement learning. We study the temporal horizon of this supervision: smaller discounts make these targets less sensitive to distant bootstrap changes, but using them for behavior changes the control objective. We introduce Short-to-Long (S2L), which supplies a long-horizon behavior learner with features trained by smaller-discount source networks. Detached connections prevent the behavior value loss from updating the sources, allowing each source to follow its own value objective while the behavior discount remains unchanged. Across Atari, online continuous control, and offline continuous control, the approach yields aggregate performance gains, improving the paired terminal median on 40 of 57 Atari games. Matched Atari ablations support the roles of feature transfer, smaller source discounts, and gradient isolation. Fixed-trajectory measurements further show reduced drift in source value targets with mixed discounts.

</details>


### [104] [DiffLTM: Continuous Diffusions Large Tabular Models for Conditional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30472)
*Alankrita Bhatt,Mukur Gupta,Andrea Montanari,Pulkit Tandon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于大型表格模型的连续扩散模型，解决了混合数值和类别数据（尤其是高基数、不平衡类别数据）的条件生成难题，引入交叉熵损失处理类别变量并给出理论证明，设计关注稀有子群体的评估指标，扩展条件生成技术，并在劳动力数据上验证了模型能捕捉计量经济学经典洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型表格模型的条件生成面临两大挑战：(i) 数据包含数值和类别混合特征，类别特征常具有高基数和严重不平衡比例；(ii) 对稀有子群体的条件生成难度大。现有最优模型多采用连续与离散扩散混合方法，但本文旨在设计纯连续扩散模型并取得更强生成质量。

Method: 设计连续扩散模型，核心创新是使用交叉熵损失处理类别变量并提供首个理论证明；引入关注稀有子群体的评估指标；将条件生成技术泛化以支持新型条件设定；在劳动力数据集上训练并验证模型性能。

Result: 模型在条件生成质量上表现优异，尤其在稀有子群体上表现更好；理论证明了交叉熵损失对类别变量的有效性；新指标能更好评估稀有子群体生成质量；泛化的条件生成技术支持更多条件类型；在劳动力数据上成功捕捉计量经济学经典洞见。

Conclusion: 纯连续扩散模型结合交叉熵损失可有效处理混合类型表格数据的条件生成，尤其擅长稀有子群体生成。该方法不仅理论扎实，还在实际经济数据上验证了实用价值，为大型表格模型的条件生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Conditional generation is a key capability to be supported by Large Tabular Models, enabling a number of applications. Accurate conditional generation has to contend with two key challenges: $(i)$ Data comprise a mixture of numerical and categorical entries, the latter often with high cardinality and heavily unbalanced proportions; $(ii)$ Conditional generation becomes increasingly difficult for rare sub-populations. The best models in this class use a hybrid of continuous and discrete diffusions. In this paper, we design a continuous diffusion model with strong generation quality. An important element is the use of cross-entropy loss for categoricals, for which we provide a first theoretical justification. We introduce metrics that focus on rare subpopulations. Further, we generalize conditional generation techniques to enable new types of conditioning. Finally, we show that our model (trained on labor data) can capture classical insights from econometrics.
  Code is available at https://github.com/granica-ai/DiffLTM

</details>


### [105] [Rethinking SAE Evaluation: An Atomic Interpretable Unit Framework Reveals Hidden Polysemanticity and Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30474)
*Riteng Zhang,Xiaoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的稀疏自编码器（SAE）评估范式，要求SAE特征形成原子可解释单元基础，并引入DAME等代理指标和基准测试框架。实验发现现有SAE在主流指标下看似单语义，但主导特征却系统性地更难解释且冗余，揭示了功能性多语义失效模式，呼吁在部署前进行主导性感知评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE最初旨在发现单语义特征，但随着其在归因、电路发现和干预中的应用，研究者开始关注稀疏性、死特征率、重构保真度和解缠等全局属性。然而，这种从单语义性向整体属性的隐性转变缺乏明确的、定义良好的目标，导致现有评估指标零散且必要性未获论证。

Method: 形式化终极目标：要求SAE特征及基于字典的模型表示分解构成原子可解释单元基础，即语义有意义解释的坐标系。在此范式下，将现有及新提出的测量（如DAME——主导性感知单语义性评估）组织为代理指标，并建立全面的基准测试框架。在多架构、多模型族上评估数百个SAE。

Result: 揭示失效模式：部分SAE在现有指标下呈现单语义，实则功能上多语义——对模型计算贡献最大的主导特征系统性地更难解释且冗余度更高。

Conclusion: 这些失效模式应成为SAE研究者改进训练和架构的目标，并激励从业者在将SAE特征用于归因、电路追踪或干预前，将主导性感知评估作为标准检查。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) were originally motivated by discovering monosemantic features, a property that characterizes individual features but does not specify what the learned feature set should look like as a whole. As SAEs have increasingly been adopted for attribution, circuit discovery, and intervention, researchers have begun evaluating metrics that describe global properties of the feature set, such as sparsity, dead feature rates, reconstruction fidelity, and disentanglement, a diverse set that extends far beyond monosemanticity. However, this implicit shift beyond monosemanticity has not been formalized as a clear, well-defined objective that comes before these evaluation metrics. Without such an objective, existing metrics remain ad hoc, with their necessity unjustified. In this work, we formalize this ultimate objective by introducing a new paradigm for SAE evaluation: requiring that SAE features, and more generally dictionary-based decompositions of model representations, form an atomic interpretable unit basis, a coordinate system for semantically meaningful explanation. Under this paradigm, we organize existing and new measurements such as DAME (Dominance-Aware Monosemanticity Evaluation) as proxy metrics and introduce a comprehensive benchmarking framework. Evaluating hundreds of SAEs across multiple architectures and model families, we uncover failure modes in which some SAEs appear monosemantic under existing evaluation metrics while remaining functionally polysemantic: the most dominant features, those contributing most to model computation, are systematically less interpretable and more redundant. These failure modes warrant attention from SAE researchers as targets for improved training and architectures, and motivate dominance-aware evaluation as a standard check for practitioners before SAE features are deployed for attribution, circuit tracing, or intervention.

</details>


### [106] [Optimal Multitask Representations Under a Rate Constraint: An Information Bottleneck Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30493)
*Nicholas Stiles,Gregory W. Wornell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在信息预算下哪种预训练目标能产生最佳下游任务表示，形式化为多任务信息瓶颈，发现弱任务对齐的专家在低速率下优于重建通才，但在高速率下劣势反转；提出平均任务信息矩阵目标具有统一相对遗憾界，实验验证压缩会改变最优预训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限信息预算（如压缩表示）下，如何选择预训练目标以最大化下游任务性能？现有工作缺乏理论指导，特别是不同目标在不同速率下的表现差异及任务感知训练的动态变化。

Method: 将问题形式化为多任务信息瓶颈，引入互信息约束建模不完整信息保留；针对高斯输入和任务，推导最优表示的闭式解；构造任务族分析固定高斯预测目标的遗憾；提出基于平均任务信息矩阵的目标并证明其均匀相对遗憾界；在CelebA和dSprites上进行实验验证。

Result: 理论上证明弱任务对齐专家在低速率下优于重建通才，高速率下反转；存在任务族使任意固定高斯预测目标遭受任意大的相对遗憾；平均任务信息矩阵目标提供由最大任务SNR控制的均匀相对遗憾界。实验显示压缩率改变最优预训练目标，任务感知训练甚至会随速率增加反转哪个下游任务保留更好。

Conclusion: 预训练目标的最优选择强烈依赖于信息预算（速率）；没有单一目标在所有速率下均占优；平均任务信息矩阵提供了一个具有理论保证的鲁棒目标；实践中需根据压缩率和任务分布自适应选择或设计预训练目标。

Abstract: Which pretraining objective yields the best representation for downstream tasks under an information budget? We formalize this problem as a multitask information bottleneck, using a mutual information constraint to model incomplete information retention. For Gaussian inputs and tasks, we characterize optimal representations and show that a weakly task-aligned specialist can outperform a reconstruction generalist at low rates but lose at high rates. We also construct task families for which every fixed Gaussian prediction objective incurs arbitrarily large relative regret, driven by reversals in optimal allocation priorities. Conversely, the average task information matrix defines an objective with a uniform relative-regret bound controlled by maximum task SNR. CelebA and dSprites experiments illustrate that compression can change which pretraining objective performs best, and even task-aware training can reverse which downstream task is better preserved as rate increases.

</details>


### [107] [NEST: Nested Embedding Representation for Multiscale Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30520)
*Zhanqi Zhang,Mikio Aoi,Gal Mishne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NEST 是一个无监督框架，受 Matryoshka Representation Learning 启发，将粗到细的多层次聚类结构编码在单一嵌入中，通过嵌套前缀子空间的联合优化实现多尺度结构发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据常在多个分辨率下展现簇结构，但现有方法通常只学习单一划分或构建显式层级。需要一种方法能在单一嵌入中内化多粒度聚类能力，无需观测层级标签即可发现多尺度结构。

Method: NEST 在嵌套前缀子空间上联合优化聚类与分离目标，随着嵌入维度增加实现逐步细化的区分。引入秩正则化和簇内正则化防止嵌入坍塌。仅需每层簇数作为先验，无需层级监督信号。

Result: 学习到的嵌入距离比输入更贴近层级距离。在合成层级上能仅给定每层簇数就恢复多尺度结构。在视觉、文本、单细胞数据上均展现出多尺度组织能力。

Conclusion: NEST 将聚类粒度作为表征的内在属性，提供了统一的多尺度结构发现方法，无需显式层级构建即可在单一嵌入中编码粗到细的簇结构。

Abstract: Real-world data often exhibits cluster structure at multiple resolutions, yet existing methods typically learn a single partition or construct an explicit hierarchy. Inspired by Matryoshka Representation Learning, we introduce NEST, an unsupervised framework that encodes coarse-to-fine structure within a single embedding. NEST jointly optimizes clustering and separation across nested prefix subspaces, enabling progressively finer distinctions as embedding dimensionality increases, with rank and within-cluster regularization that keep the embedding from collapsing. Empirically, distances in the learned embedding follow the hierarchy distance more closely than those of the input, although the hierarchy is never observed during training. On synthetic hierarchies, NEST recovers multiscale structure given only the number of clusters per level. Experiments on vision, text, and single-cell data further demonstrate multiscale organization across real-world representations. By making clustering granularity an intrinsic property of the representation, NEST provides a unified approach to multiscale structure discovery.

</details>


### [108] [When News Gets Old: Portfolio Allocation with Reinforcement Learning and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30564)
*Bolin Song,Surui Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究如何处理陈旧金融新闻信号，比较保留信号并提供时机信息给RL策略 vs 过滤陈旧新闻后直接由LLM选股。实验显示LLM过滤旧新闻后年化净收益达18.43%（新闻延迟四周），优于所有使用新闻的RL策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融新闻发布后长期影响投资决策，及时新闻可能在价格充分调整前到达投资者，而旧新闻可能已反映在价格中。需要研究如何处理不同时效性的新闻信号以优化组合选择。

Method: 使用固定大语言模型(LLM)从公开SEC披露中提取信号，对比两种处理陈旧信号的方式：1) 保留信号并为强化学习(RL)策略提供时机信息；2) 在组合选择前过滤陈旧新闻。在2023-2025年历史数据上测试扣除交易成本后的收益，评估RL策略和直接选股的LLM。

Result: RL策略在受控测试中对时机信息有反应，但在历史回测中添加时机信息无稳定收益提升。LLM过滤旧新闻后获得最高平均净收益，新闻延迟四周时年化收益达18.43%，超越所有使用新闻的RL策略。

Conclusion: 对于基于LLM的直接组合选择，过滤陈旧新闻优于保留时机信息；RL策略虽能利用时机信息但在实战中无显著优势。研究表明新闻时效性处理方式对收益影响显著，LLM直接决策配合新闻过滤效果最佳。

Abstract: Financial news can influence investment decisions long after publication. Timely news may reach an investor before prices fully adjust, whereas older news may already be reflected in prices. We examine two ways to handle aging signals: retain them and give a portfolio policy timing information, or filter them out before portfolio selection. A fixed large language model (LLM) extracts signals from public SEC disclosures. We compare these treatments in reinforcement learning (RL) policies and LLMs that choose portfolios directly. We evaluate returns after trading costs in historical tests over 2023-2025. RL choices respond to timing information in a controlled test, but historical returns show no consistent benefit from adding it. For the LLM, filtering old news produced the highest average net return, reaching 18.43% per year when news delivery was delayed by four weeks. This exceeded the returns of all RL strategies that used news.

</details>


### [109] [Evaluating Type-1 Diabetes Digital Twins by Decision Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30577)
*Victor Li,Owen Tucker,Michael S. Hughes,Temiloluwa Prioleau,Shengpu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出直接基于下游治疗排序的决策质量评估1型糖尿病数字孪生（DT），而非传统的血糖轨迹预测准确性。实验表明预测准确性与决策质量不一致：群体平均模型预测准确性最低（RMSE 63.6 mg/dL），但治疗排序表现第二好（平均Spearman 0.766）；而黑盒神经网络预测准确性高出近一倍（RMSE 32.9 mg/dL），排序却不优于随机（Spearman 0.049）。因此，仅凭预测准确性选择DT可能导致推荐治疗接近随机猜测，必须直接评估决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前数字孪生的质量通常通过预测血糖轨迹的准确性来衡量，但这可能无法反映其指导下游治疗决策的实际质量。需要直接评估DT在治疗排序任务上的决策质量，即DT预测的血糖轨迹能否像真实患者那样对候选治疗进行排序。

Method: 基于FDA认可的UVA/Padova模型，构建30名虚拟患者，每位患者模拟21种不同的基础-餐时胰岛素治疗方案。对每位患者，在一种治疗下拟合DT，再利用该DT预测其余20种治疗下的血糖轨迹。对比六种方法，计算预测准确性（RMSE）和治疗排序决策质量（Spearman等级相关系数）。

Result: 预测准确性与决策质量存在显著分歧。从未见过个体血糖数据的群体平均基线模型，预测准确性最差（RMSE 63.6 mg/dL），但治疗排序平均Spearman高达0.766，居第二位；而预测准确性近乎翻倍的黑盒神经网络（RMSE 32.9 mg/dL），排序表现仅相当于随机排序（Spearman 0.049）。

Conclusion: 仅依据预测准确性选择数字孪生可能导致推荐治疗方案的效果不优于随机选择。必须直接评估决策质量，而不能假设其随预测准确性提升而改善。

Abstract: A digital twin (DT) for type 1 diabetes (T1D) can predict how an individual's blood glucose will respond to different insulin treatments without actually trying them. For each treatment, downstream statistics of predicted glucose trajectories, such as glycemic risk, can be computed to guide clinical decisions. However, the quality of DTs is usually measured by the accuracy of their predicted glucose trajectories, which may not reflect the quality of the downstream treatment decisions they inform. In this work, we directly evaluate DTs by this decision quality on downstream treatment ranking, i.e., whether a DT predicts glucose trajectories that can be used to rank candidate treatments as the real patient would. We present an evaluation protocol over 30 virtual patients from the FDA-accepted UVA/Padova model, each simulated under 21 different basal-bolus treatments. For each patient, a DT is fitted under one treatment and then produces glucose trajectories for the remaining 20 treatments. Our results show that prediction accuracy and decision quality disagree. A baseline that models the average patient, never seeing the individual's glucose data, has the lowest prediction accuracy of all six methods (RMSE: 63.6 mg/dL) but ranks treatments with the second-highest mean Spearman (0.766), while a black-box neural network that predicts glucose nearly twice as accurately (RMSE: 32.9 mg/dL) ranks them no better than a random ordering of the 20 treatments (0.049 Spearman). Therefore, selecting a DT based on prediction accuracy alone can prefer one that recommends treatments at chance, and decision quality must be evaluated directly rather than assumed to follow from prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [110] [Future of Time Series Anomaly Detection: From Historical Review to Unified Outlook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30628)
*Zhijie Zhong,Zhiwen Yu,Pengyu Li,Liujian Zhang,Qiaoli Ni,Jiang Jun,Jianming Lv,Kaixiang Yang,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于时间序列异常检测（TSAD）的综述论文，系统梳理了扩散模型、状态空间模型、大语言模型等新兴架构，建立了细化的异常分类与范式分类体系，构建了开源基准库并评估指标保真度，指出了动态阈值、可解释性、零样本迁移等关键挑战，为未来研究提供统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述对扩散模型、状态空间模型、大语言模型等新兴架构覆盖不足，缺乏对多变量异常、跨域统一建模、指标可靠性及当代评估挑战的统一处理，亟需填补这些理论与实证空白。

Method: 建立细化异常分类与结构化范式分类体系，对比检测机制并梳理发展轨迹；深入分析评估方法并开展大规模实验验证指标保真度；构建包含算法、数据集、评估工具的开源基准库；梳理关键开放挑战并提供前瞻视角。

Result: 形成了反映现代进展的统一分类框架，完成了指标保真度的综合实证评估，发布了开源基准仓库，明确了动态阈值、模型可解释性、零样本迁移、多模态对齐及时间序列基础模型五大核心挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述通过解决现有理论与实证局限，建立了连贯框架，将指导未来时间序列异常检测的研究方向与实际部署，推动领域向统一建模、可靠评估与基础模型方向发展。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) is a foundational problem in data mining, aiming to identify observations that deviate significantly from nominal temporal patterns. Driven by escalating data volumes and application complexity, recent research has yielded diverse architectures, including diffusion models, state space models, and large language models (LLMs). However, existing surveys provide limited coverage of these emerging designs and lack a unified treatment of multivariate anomalies, cross-domain unified modeling, metric reliability, and contemporary evaluation challenges. To bridge these gaps, this survey delivers three core contributions: (1) We establish a refined anomaly categorization and a structured taxonomy of paradigms reflecting modern advances, comparing detection mechanisms and charting developmental trajectories across paradigms. (2) We provide an extensive analysis of evaluation methodologies and conduct comprehensive experiments to assess metric fidelity, complemented by an open-source benchmark repository encompassing algorithms, datasets, and evaluation tools. (3) We outline key open challenges, offering forward-looking perspectives on dynamic thresholding, model interpretability, zero-shot transfer, multi-modal alignment, and foundational models for time series. By addressing existing theoretical and empirical limitations, this survey establishes a coherent framework to guide future TSAD research and practical deployments.

</details>


### [111] [Interpreting Model Reasoning through Behavioral Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30630)
*Shawn Im,James Oldfield,Adel Bibi,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行为反事实的方法，通过最小化固定回答下源提示与反事实目标提示输出分布的KL散度来定位和分析干预对模型推理的影响，并在Gemma-4-E4B-it和DeepSeek-R1-0528-Qwen3-8B上验证了建议如何在未被口头承认的情况下影响推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型能力提升，忠实理解模型形成思维链的内部过程变得重要，但由于采样回答的方差大，难以隔离干预效果并系统比较模型推理。

Method: 提出行为反事实方法：给定固定回答，研究干预如何改变下一token分布。训练模块编辑残差流，最小化源提示与反事实目标提示下固定回答输出分布的KL散度。利用训练模块输出的归因图识别反事实恢复的因果关键位置，并通过激活补丁验证假设。

Result: 匹配输出分布比标准激活重构目标更有效恢复反事实行为。归因图和下游效应可视化为生成原始模型计算假设提供接口。案例研究发现：问题陈述和填充token的表示中介了建议影响的很大一部分，下游效应包括改变论证框架甚至最终答案。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效定位和分析干预对模型推理的影响，为理解模型内部计算提供了可验证的工具。

Abstract: Faithfully understanding the internal process that occurs as models form their chain of thought is an important problem with the rise in model capabilities. However, this remains challenging due to the variance in sampled responses, making it difficult to isolate the effects of interventions and compare model reasoning systematically. We propose a method for localizing and analyzing how a given intervention affects the model's reasoning through behavioral counterfactuals: given a fixed response, how does introducing an intervention to the prompt change the next-token distributions? We train a module to edit the residual stream such that it minimizes the KL divergence between the output distributions for a fixed response under a source prompt and its counterfactual target prompt. We find that matching the output distributions recovers the counterfactual behavior more effectively than standard activation reconstruction objectives and validate that attribution of the trained module outputs is effective for identifying causally important locations for counterfactual recovery. The attribution maps and visualizations of the downstream effects of model edits provide an interface for generating hypotheses about the original model's computation, which can then be tested with activation patching. In a case study on Gemma-4-E4B-it and DeepSeek-R1-0528-Qwen3-8B, we investigate how a suggestion influences model reasoning even when it is not verbally acknowledged. Our analysis identifies representations at the question statement and filler tokens that mediate a substantial part of the suggestion's influence, with downstream effects such as changing the framing of the justification or even the final answer.

</details>


### [112] [RiboBridge: protein-conditioned de novo RNA binder structure design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30668)
*Zhou Zhang,Hanqun Cao,Minghao Sun,Odin Zhang,Yang Zhang,Pheng Ann Heng,Wujun Li,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RiboBridge 是一种基于结构的框架，用于设计结合目标蛋白的 RNA 分子。它将 RNA 构象生成与蛋白-RNA 组装解耦：首先在蛋白表面提出候选结合界面，然后在预训练的 RNA 结构潜在空间 (RiboSphere) 中生成规范 RNA 构象，再预测跨坐标系的相互作用约束，最后通过刚性放置得到几何一致的复合物。在 124 个蛋白-RNA 复合物基准测试中，RiboBridge 在 fNAT、DockQ、界面和配体 RMSD 指标上均优于现有方法。消融实验进一步揭示了各阶段对复合物恢复误差的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RNA-蛋白复合物设计方法通常在统一表示中建模 RNA 构象生成与蛋白相对放置，导致两者耦合，难以在不预设结合位点的情况下生成几何一致的复合物。需要一种能够分离 RNA 构象生成与蛋白-RNA 组装的方法。

Method: RiboBridge 采用三阶段流程：(1) 蛋白表面候选结合界面预测；(2) 在预训练的 RNA 结构潜在空间 RiboSphere 中生成规范 RNA 构象；(3) 预测蛋白-RNA 相互作用约束以桥接独立的 RNA 和蛋白坐标系，并通过刚性变换将其转化为几何一致的蛋白相对构象。

Result: 在时间分割、序列过滤、去冗余的 124 复合物基准上，采用事后界面对应评估（仅在生成后使用天然信息选择候选口袋），RiboBridge 实现了最高的 fNAT 和 DockQ 值，以及最低的界面 RMSD 和配体 RMSD。消融实验和分阶段诊断量化了 RNA 构象生成、相互作用预测和刚性放置各环节对复合物恢复误差的贡献。

Conclusion: RiboBridge 通过结构优先、解耦生成策略，显著提升了从头设计 RNA-蛋白复合物的几何精度。其模块化设计使得各阶段误差来源可被独立诊断，为后续改进 RNA 构象采样、界面预测及跨坐标系几何约束提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Designing RNA molecules that bind to target protein structures requires generating both an RNA conformation and its bound complex geometry, without assuming a predefined binding site. Existing approaches generally model these coupled processes within a unified representation, entangling RNA conformation generation with protein-relative placement. In this work, we introduce RiboBridge, a structure-first framework that separates RNA conformational generation from protein--RNA assembly through cross-frame interaction geometry. RiboBridge first proposes multiple candidate RNA-binding interfaces on the protein surface, generates canonical RNA conformations within a pretrained RNA structural latent space (RiboSphere), and then predicts protein--RNA interaction constraints to bridge the independently defined RNA and protein coordinate frames. The resulting interaction geometry is converted into a geometrically consistent protein-relative pose through rigid placement. We evaluate RiboBridge on a temporally split, sequence-filtered, and redundancy-controlled benchmark of 124 protein--RNA complexes. Under post-hoc interface-correspondence evaluation, where native information is used only after generation to select among already generated predicted-pocket candidates, RiboBridge achieves the highest fNAT and DockQ and the lowest interface and ligand RMSDs among compared methods. Component ablations and stage-wise diagnostics further characterize the contributions of RNA conformational generation, interaction prediction, and rigid placement, identifying the main sources of complex-recovery error.

</details>


### [113] [Learning Domain Trajectories with Flow Matching for Gradual Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30685)
*Yubo Huang,Yushe Cao,Zixi Wang,Jingzehua Xu,Xin Lai,Xiongyu Hu,Shuai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FlowGDA，一种基于流匹配的连续渐进域适应框架，在统计流形上建模连续传输，用仿真自由回归目标替代对抗训练，结合源锚定传输和语义一致性损失防止语义漂移，通过两阶段顺序优化稳定训练，实验超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大分布偏移是域泛化和无监督适应的主要障碍。渐进域适应虽引入有序中间域缓解难度，但现有方法仍采用离散步进方式，易累积误差且依赖不稳定的极大极小优化，亟需连续、稳定且无对抗的建模范式。

Method: FlowGDA将渐进适应建模为表示空间统计流形上的连续传输，学习时变速度场诱导平滑域轨迹；采用仿真自由回归目标替代对抗训练；耦合相邻域的左向右传输与源锚定传输，并引入语义一致性损失防止语义漂移；采用两阶段顺序优化方案稳定学习过程。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，FlowGDA性能优于最先进的渐进域适应方法，验证了流匹配作为域适应有效替代范式的可行性。

Conclusion: FlowGDA通过连续流匹配建模渐进域适应，避免了离散步进的误差累积和对抗训练的不稳定性，结合源锚定与语义一致性保证语义保真，两阶段优化保证收敛稳定，为域适应提供了新的理论与实践范式。

Abstract: Large distribution shifts remain a primary obstacle for domain generalization and unsupervised adaptation. Although gradual domain adaptation alleviates this difficulty by introducing ordered intermediate domains, most existing approaches still operate in a discrete, step-wise manner, which can accumulate errors and often relies on unstable Min--Max optimization. We propose \textbf{FlowGDA}, a flow-matching framework that models gradual adaptation as \emph{continuous} transport on a statistical manifold in a representation space. FlowGDA learns time-dependent velocity fields that induce smooth domain trajectories, using a simulation-free regression objective rather than adversarial training. To prevent semantic drift, we couple left-to-right transport between consecutive domains with a source-anchored transport and a semantic consistency loss, and we further stabilize learning via a two-stage sequential optimization scheme. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art techniques, validating flow matching as an effective alternative for domain adaptation.

</details>


### [114] [An Agent Architecture with Tools and Memory for Selective Correction of Wrist PPG Heart Rate Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30688)
*Xu Wang,Shuhan Yi,Zhen Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于代理架构的手腕PPG心率估计修正框架，利用脉搏、运动和历史证据生成候选估计，通过独立风险门控决定保留、修正或拒绝输出，在四个公开数据集上将十估计器宏平均MAE降低高达44.5%，并实现了实时可穿戴设备部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手腕光容积脉搏波（PPG）心率估计极易受运动伪影干扰，现有修正方法虽能降低平均误差，但可能恶化个别时间窗口的估计精度，亟需一种能够量化修正收益与风险、平衡准确性与覆盖率的自适应修正框架。

Method: 构建围绕固定心率估计器的代理架构，包含工具模块与记忆模块。输入脉搏信号、运动数据及有限历史证据生成多个候选估计，依据预测的收益与危害指导选择，引入独立风险门控对每个输出决定保留、数值修正或拒绝。在PPG-DaLiA数据集训练，冻结权重后在Wrist Exercise、GalaxyPPG、WildPPG三数据集测试；并实现实时可穿戴采集、蓝牙传输与智能手机端部署。

Result: 跨四数据集十估计器宏平均MAE最大降低44.5%（GalaxyPPG降幅最大）。有益修正使绝对误差减少>1 bpm，占比16.06%-36.64%；有害修正使绝对误差增加>1 bpm，占比1.68%-6.77%。在60%和80%目标覆盖率下，数值修正对总误差降低贡献大于窗口选择。单受试者四次原型录制后端分析显示，十估计器宏平均MAE从9.09 bpm降至4.45 bpm且无额外拒绝。

Conclusion: 该架构在评估条件下显著提升平均准确性，同时量化修正的收益与风险，为兼顾估计误差、修正风险与数据覆盖率的可穿戴心率分析提供了可落地的解决方案。

Abstract: Wrist photoplethysmography (PPG) heart rate estimates are vulnerable to motion artifacts, and corrections that reduce mean error can worsen individual windows. We propose an agent architecture with tools and memory around a fixed heart rate estimator. Pulse, motion, and limited historical evidence generate candidate estimates, whose predicted benefits and harms guide selection. An independent risk gate determines whether to retain, correct, or reject each output. We trained the architecture on PPG-DaLiA and tested it on Wrist Exercise, GalaxyPPG, and WildPPG with frozen weights. Across these four public datasets, mean absolute error (MAE), macro-averaged over ten estimators, decreased by up to 44.5%, with the largest reduction on GalaxyPPG. Beneficial and harmful corrections decreased and increased absolute error by more than 1 bpm, respectively. Their ten-estimator macro-averaged proportions ranged from 16.06% to 36.64% and from 1.68% to 6.77%, respectively, across the four datasets. At 60% and 80% target coverage, numerical correction on the same accepted windows contributed more to total error reduction than window selection. We implemented real-time wearable acquisition, Bluetooth reception, and smartphone deployment of a selected estimator and the framework. In backend analysis of four prototype recordings from one participant, ten-estimator macro-averaged MAE decreased from 9.09 to 4.45 bpm without additional rejection. Under the evaluated conditions, the architecture improved average accuracy while quantifying correction benefits and harms, supporting wearable heart rate analysis that considers error, correction risk, and coverage.

</details>


### [115] [Solvable models of learning to pursue a moving target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30721)
*John J. Vastola,Kanaka Rajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个连续时间线性-二次模型研究开环目标追踪，推导出最优控制策略、策略学习动力学和价值学习动力学的闭式解，发现学习时间尺度对应特征值谱，且特征值间隙揭示智能体先学会到达目标再优化运动轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解智能体如何学习高效拦截移动目标的策略，特别是介于反应式（朝向目标当前位置）与预测式（朝向目标未来位置）两极之间的最优策略如何被学习。

Method: 构建连续时间线性-二次开环追踪模型，利用线性结构求解最优控制、策略梯度学习动力学和时序差分价值学习动力学的闭式解，通过特征值分析学习时间尺度。

Result: 最优策略由少量可解释参数决定；学习动力学的时间尺度精确对应特定矩阵的特征值，特征值谱普遍存在间隙，表明智能体先学会到达目标（快尺度），后优化运动效率（慢尺度）。

Conclusion: 该模型为追踪行为及其学习动力学提供了详细数学刻画，可作为实证研究的基准，也为更复杂的追踪理论奠定基础。

Abstract: Agents tasked with intercepting a moving target must learn how to reach it, ideally as efficiently as possible. As observers of animal pursuit behavior have noticed, different strategies are possible: on one extreme, the agent reactively moves toward the target's current location; on the other, the agent predicts the target's future location and moves directly there. Motivated by the desire to understand how such strategies might be learned, we introduce a continuous-time linear-quadratic model of open-loop target pursuit whose optimal control strategies interpolate between these possibilities. Usefully, a small number of interpretable parameters control which type of strategy is optimal, and it is possible to derive closed-form solutions for optimal strategies, policy learning dynamics, and value learning dynamics. Exploiting our model's linear structure, we find that the time scales of learning precisely correspond to the eigenvalues of certain matrices, and that relevant eigenvalue spectra generically have a gap. We show that this gap indicates that agents tend to learn how to reach their target before optimizing their movement. Our results provide a detailed mathematical characterization of pursuit behavior and its learning dynamics, which can both serve as a benchmark for empirical work, and as a foundation for more elaborate theoretical treatments of pursuit.

</details>


### [116] [Federated Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30728)
*Xiaobao Wang,Renda Han,Zhiliang Xia,Meng Liu,Renxiang Guan,Di Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedGRAG是一个联邦图检索增强生成框架，解决了分布式知识图谱环境下的隐私保护与统一检索问题，通过联邦知识路由和可控披露证据构建实现跨客户端的知识利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的知识天然分布在多个组织中，而非中心化存储。隐私约束禁止共享完整的本地知识图谱，导致现有GraphRAG无法有效检索和利用分布式知识。

Method: 提出两大核心机制：(1) 联邦知识路由 - 通过轻量级知识索引高效识别相关客户端，不暴露本地图结构；(2) 可控披露证据构建 - 提取检索到的证据子图并转换为证据摘要，支持服务端的证据转换与重建。

Result: 大量实验表明，Federated GraphRAG在检索和下游生成任务上均持续超越现有基线，为在联邦环境中部署GraphRAG确立了新范式。

Conclusion: FedGRAG成功解决了联邦环境下分布式知识检索与隐私保护证据共享的根本挑战，实现了在不共享本地图谱的前提下跨组织的知识增强生成。}

Abstract: Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) leverages centralized knowledge graphs to enhance large language models with structured knowledge retrieval. However, real-world knowledge is inherently distributed across multiple organizations rather than being centrally accessible. Privacy constraints further prohibit sharing entire local knowledge graphs, preventing GraphRAG from effectively retrieving and utilizing distributed knowledge. To this end, we propose Federated GraphRAG (FedGRAG), a unified framework for distributed graph retrieval and knowledge-enhanced generation over multiple clients with knowledge graphs. Specifically, we address two fundamental challenges in federated GraphRAG. First, the absence of a global knowledge graph makes unified retrieval impossible. To address this, we develop a Federated Knowledge Routing mechanism that efficiently identifies relevant clients through lightweight knowledge indices without exposing local graph structures. Second, local graph memory cannot be shared directly because it contains sensitive entities and knowledge information. We therefore propose a controlled-disclosure evidence construction method. It extracts retrieved evidence subgraphs and converts them into an evidence abstract, enabling evidence conversion and reconstruction on the server. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Federated GraphRAG consistently outperforms existing baselines in both retrieval and downstream generation tasks, establishing a new paradigm for deploying GraphRAG in federated environments.

</details>


### [117] [Width and Depth Limits Commute During Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30729)
*Ruijia Zhang,Soufiane Hayou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了在线性残差网络中，采用Depth-μP缩放（残差分支按1/√depth缩放）和μP宽度参数化，若使用梯度裁剪，网络输出在有限训练步数下沿任意宽度-深度联合增长路径以L²收敛，从而无限宽度与无限深度极限在整个有限训练期内可交换；无梯度裁剪时L²收敛一般不成立，但可得依概率收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作已建立残差网络在初始化时无限宽度与无限深度极限的可交换性（当残差分支按1/√depth缩放时），本文旨将该可交换性结果推广至训练阶段，特别是线性残差网络在梯度下降训练下的极限行为。

Method: 采用Depth-μP参数化（残差分支缩放1/√depth）结合μP宽度参数化，分析固定步数梯度下降过程；引入梯度裁剪以控制梯度范数，证明网络输出沿任意宽度-深度联合发散路径的L²收敛；无裁剪情况下建立依概率收敛。

Result: 有梯度裁剪时，网络输出在任意有限训练步数下沿所有宽度-深度联合增长路径L²收敛，无限宽度与无限深度极限在整个有限训练期内可交换；无梯度裁剪时L²收敛一般失效，但可证明依概率收敛。

Conclusion: Depth-μP与μP参数化下，梯度裁剪是保证线性残差网络训练过程中无限宽度与无限深度极限可交换的关键条件；该结果为深度残差网络的无限宽深理论分析提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Recent work established that the infinite-width and infinite-depth limits of residual networks commute at initialization when residual branches are scaled by $1/\sqrt{depth}$. In particular, the network output converges to the same limit whether width and depth tend to infinity sequentially or jointly. We extend this commutativity result to training in linear residual networks. Under the same $1/\sqrt{depth}$ residual-branch scaling (Depth-$μ$P) and the $μ$P width parameterization, we prove that, for any fixed number of gradient-descent steps with gradient clipping, the network output converges in $L^2$ along every joint width--depth growth path as both width and depth tend to infinity. Consequently, the infinite-width and infinite-depth limits commute throughout any finite training horizon. Without gradient clipping, the corresponding $L^2$ convergence does not hold in general, but we establish the weaker convergence in probability.

</details>


### [118] [Random Remasking Scales Masked Diffusion Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30744)
*Zhaoyi Zhou,Frank Wu,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文重新表述了掩码扩散模型（MDM）的推理过程为马尔可夫链，提出了随机重掩码-去噪（R²D）推理和轨迹校正训练（TCT），在逻辑任务和语言任务上显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MDM推理中错误不可逆，迭代校正虽能提升质量但缺乏理论解释，尤其是在不完美的去噪器模型下。

Method: 将MDM推理重构为干净序列空间上的马尔可夫链，定义重掩码-去噪核；提出R²D推理（纯测试时、无需微调、无需奖励）和TCT训练（让去噪器暴露于自身生成轨迹的错误中）。

Result: 在逻辑任务上实现近对数线性测试时缩放，硬数独准确率>97%，3-SAT准确率>99%；在预训练语言模型上超越迭代校正基线和LLaDA2.1-mini的官方Q-mode准确率。

Conclusion: 通过马尔可夫链分析和轨迹校正训练，有效解决了MDM推理中的错误累积问题，实现了强大的测试时计算扩展能力，且无需额外微调或奖励模型。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) generate sequences by progressively unmasking tokens, but errors introduced during decoding are typically irreversible. Recent work has shown that iterative correction can improve sample quality, yet existing theory fails to explain this given a learned, imperfect denoiser model. We re-formulate MDM inference as a Markov chain on the clean sequence space, defined by a remask-denoise kernel that randomly re-introduces masks and then denoises. This helps us analyze the actual denoising trajectory towards the data distribution. The analysis motivates Random Remask-Denoise (R$^2$D) inference, a purely test-time, finetuning-free, reward-free procedure. To further improve performance, we propose Trajectory Correction Training (TCT), a denoiser-training procedure that exposes the model to its own errors along the generation trajectory. On logical tasks, our approach exhibits a striking near-log-linear test-time scaling, achieving over 97% accuracy on hard Sudoku puzzles and over 99% accuracy on 3-SAT. Our approach also applies to pretrained models on actual language tasks, beating iterative correction baselines and the official Q-mode accuracy of LLaDA2.1-mini on HumanEval+.

</details>


### [119] [Wavenumber-Aware Deep Learning for Robust Analysis of Raman Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30746)
*Jihwan Kim,Jaeseong Heo,Yujin Kim,Sang Bin Yoon,Chanjoong Kim,Kwang-Im Oh,Hyon Bin Na,Kyungwon Kwak,Myeongkee Park,Joonyoung F. Joung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于波数的显式物理坐标编码方法，将光谱点表示为强度-波数对，并利用波数计算Transformer位置编码，实现了对采样网格、分辨率和光谱范围变化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光谱机器学习模型将光谱视为固定网格强度向量，物理坐标隐含在数组索引中，导致模型绑定单一采样网格、光谱范围和分辨率，难以适应测量条件变化。

Method: 将每个光谱点表示为强度和波数的配对，直接从波数计算Transformer位置编码，而非使用索引或学习编码；在卷积、残差和Transformer网络上验证，使用三种细菌物种的拉曼光谱及其合成混合物，测试噪声、稀疏采样和部分光谱覆盖下的鲁棒性。

Result: 波数编码在部分观测下优于索引编码和学习编码，在训练未见的采样网格上保持性能，而索引编码性能下降；在公共30株细菌基准上复现了结果。

Conclusion: 显式物理坐标为变化测量条件下的鲁棒光谱分析提供了简单有效的途径。

Abstract: Machine-learning models for spectroscopy usually treat a spectrum as a fixed-grid intensity vector, leaving the physical coordinate implicit in the array index. Such models are tied to a single sampling grid, spectral range and resolution, which limits their use when measurement conditions change. Here we represent each spectral point as a pair of intensity and wavenumber and compute Transformer positional encodings from the wavenumber itself. On Raman spectra of three bacterial species and their synthetic mixtures, this representation remained robust to noise, sparser sampling and partial spectral coverage across convolutional, residual and Transformer networks. In controlled comparisons, wavenumber-based encoding outperformed index-based and learned encodings under partial observation and retained its performance on sampling grids never seen during training, where index-based encoding degraded. These findings were reproduced on a public 30-isolate bacterial benchmark. Explicit physical coordinates offer a simple route to robust spectral analysis under variable measurement conditions.

</details>


### [120] [Visualizing and Interpreting Temporal Care Team Dynamics for Cancer Survival Risk Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30748)
*Yuhua Huang,Hsiao-Ying Lu,Kwan-Liu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个可视分析框架，利用电子健康记录中的通信数据构建动态协作网络，结合时序图神经网络(TGNN)预测患者风险，并通过多视图交互系统探索护理团队协作模式与患者风险演变的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症护理是一个纵向、时间敏感的过程，需要多专业医护人员协作，但现有研究缺乏对协作时间动态及其与患者风险演变关系的理解。

Method: 从EHR通信数据构建动态协作网络，使用时序图神经网络(TGNN)进行时间序列风险预测，结合代理逻辑回归模型提供特征级解释，开发多视图交互系统支持协作网络可视化、风险演变分析和反事实假设分析。

Result: 通过505例乳腺癌、结直肠癌和肺癌患者的专家引导分析，系统揭示了临床上有意义的协作模式，展示了护理团队组成变化与风险估计演变的关联。

Conclusion: 该工作提升了时序图模型在纵向团队医疗中的可解释性，为其他演化协作场景提供了潜在应用框架。

Abstract: Cancer care is a longitudinal, time-sensitive process requiring coordination among diverse healthcare professionals (HCPs), whose collaboration evolves throughout treatment. Prior studies link care team structure to patient outcomes, but the temporal dynamics of collaboration and their relationship to evolving patient risk remain poorly understood. We introduce a visual analytics framework for modeling and interpreting time-varying care team interactions in oncology.
