*Felix Saretzky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PriMa-Causa，一种将因果推理嵌入预测性维护的框架，通过合成先验和图条件因果基础模型，在单次前向传播中回答干预查询，实现了比关联基线更低的处方误差，且在弱重叠和稀疏上下文中优势最大，部分因果图披露可进一步降低误差，为自主自愈维护的自动化因果推理迈出关键一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化制造需要在不确定性和稀疏观测记录下做数据驱动决策，非计划停机是决策失误的主要成本。预测性维护仅预测故障，而处方性维护需推荐针对即将发生故障的维护动作。现有参考架构将预测模型与基于规则或优化的推荐逻辑耦合，但选择纠正动作需要干预量（即若执行该动作生产线行为如何变化）。制造数据是观测性的，拟合预测结果的模型估计的是关联量，可能在预测误差不暴露的情况下误导动作选择。因果估计器针对干预量，但需要专家主导的逐案因果研究，而在有限的运行决策窗口内无法进行。

Method: 论文分析如何将因果推理嵌入处方性维护，使动作按估计的干预效应排序。构建了 PriMa-Causa，包含三个组件：(1) 因果推理层，将干预估计集成到 PriMa 参考模型中；(2) 参数化模拟器，提供制造数据中缺失的干预真值；(3) 图条件表格因果基础模型，在合成生产线先验上一次性预训练，单次前向传播即可回答干预查询。

Result: PriMa-Causa 实现了比关联基线更低的处方误差，在弱重叠和稀疏上下文条件下优势最大；部分披露的因果图可进一步降低该误差。

Conclusion: 合成先验的因果归纳偏置和图条件化可替代逐案估计器拟合，这是迈向自主和自愈维护的自动化因果推理的重要一步。

Abstract: Automated manufacturing requires data-driven decisions under uncertainty and sparse observational records, and unplanned downtime is a dominant cost of getting them wrong. Prescriptive maintenance recommends a maintenance action against an impending failure rather than merely predicting it. Established reference architectures couple predictive models with rule- or optimisation-based recommendation logic. Selecting a corrective action, however, requires an interventional quantity: how the line behaviour would change if the action were set. Manufacturing data is observational, so a model fitted to predict the outcome estimates an associational quantity and may misdirect the choice of action without the prediction error revealing it. Causal estimators target the interventional quantity but require an expert-led causal study per case, for which the bounded decision window of operation leaves no room. This dissertation analyses how causal reasoning can be embedded into prescriptive maintenance so that actions are ranked by their estimated interventional effect. The result is PriMa-Causa, built from three components: a causal reasoning layer that integrates the estimate into the PriMa reference model, a parametric simulator that supplies the interventional ground truth absent from manufacturing data, and a graph-conditioned tabular causal foundation model that, pre-trained once on this synthetic production-line prior, answers interventional queries in a single forward pass. PriMa-Causa attains a lower prescription error than associational baselines, with the largest margin under weak overlap and sparse context, and a partially disclosed causal graph reduces this error further. The causal inductive bias of the synthetic prior and the graph conditioning can thus replace per-case estimator fitting, a step toward automating causal inference for autonomous and self-healing maintenance.

</details>


### [154] [Towards Field-Deployable Wearable IMU Systems for Occupational Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31160)
*Ihsan Ul Haq,James Brusey,Farnad Nasirzadeh,Chandan Karmakar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对VTT-ConIoT基准数据集进行了首次系统性配置敏感性分析，评估了六种分类器、五种传感器布局和三种窗口大小在留一被试交叉验证（LOSO）协议下的表现，发现多传感器融合和更长窗口能提升准确率，标准数据增强有小幅稳定增益，而CTGAN表格生成对抗网络增强反而损害性能，个性化校准仅用30%目标被试数据即可为最弱被试恢复16.8个百分点准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有建筑工人HAR研究常报告>95%的聚合准确率，但很少在真正的被试独立（LOSO）评估下验证，掩盖了决定实际部署性能的个体间差异，缺乏系统性的配置敏感性分析和基于证据的部署指导。

Method: 使用VTT-ConIoT基准，采用单一LOSO协议，系统评估6种分类器×5种传感器布局×3种窗口大小的组合；对比标准时间序列增强与条件表格生成对抗网络（CTGAN）增强；开展个性化校准试点，用目标被试30%数据微调模型。

Result: 主多传感器16分类配置下，被试级LOSO准确率分布为61.9%-88.7%（均值77.5%），跨度27个百分点；多传感器融合和更长窗口均提升准确率；标准增强带来小而稳定的增益；CTGAN增强因分布保真度问题主动损害准确率；个性化校准仅用30%目标数据即为最弱被试恢复16.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究提供了基于证据的配置指导：应优先采用多传感器融合与较长窗口，使用标准时间序列增强而非CTGAN，并引入轻量级个性化校准（仅需30%目标数据），以弥合实验室高聚合准确率与真实被试独立部署性能之间的鸿沟，推动职业身体传感器网络走向现场应用。

Abstract: Wearable inertial measurement unit (IMU) systems for construction worker human activity recognition (HAR) routinely report aggregate accuracies above 95 %, rarely under genuine subject-independent (leave-one-subject-out (LOSO)) evaluation, masking the per-individual variance that determines real-world deployment performance. Using the VTT-ConIoT benchmark, we conduct, to our knowledge, the first systematic configuration sensitivity analysis of this dataset: six classifiers across five sensor placements and three window sizes under a single LOSO protocol, a personalised-calibration pilot, and a validated comparison of tabular GAN-based and standard time-series augmentation. Per-subject LOSO accuracy for the main multi-sensor, 16-class configuration ranges from 61.9 % to 88.7 % (mean 77.5 %), a 27 pp spread, though all data are laboratory-collected. Multi-sensor fusion and longer windows both improve accuracy; standard augmentation gives small, consistent gains, while Conditional Tabular GAN (CTGAN) augmentation actively harms accuracy with a demonstrated distributional-fidelity mechanism; and personalised calibration recovers 16.8 pp for the weakest participants from just 30 % of their own data. These findings provide evidence-grounded configuration guidance for scaling occupational body sensor networks towards field deployment

</details>


### [155] [Inside-JEPA: From Prediction to Relational Grounding in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31194)
*Hossein Resani,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Inside-JEPA 在 JEPA 潜在状态中引入关系通道，通过逆动力学损失无需关系标签即可定位这些通道，在多个基准上显著提升关系推理和规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合嵌入预测架构（JEPAs）在潜在空间学习世界模型，虽能高效规划但其状态不透明，难以直接提取实体间关系。现有方法要么需要关系标签，要么无法将关系建模融入潜在状态。

Method: Inside-JEPA 在 JEPA 潜在状态中为有序实体对预留关系通道，并设计逆动力学头读取这些通道（单独或结合完整状态）以预测动作，通过动作监督隐式定位关系通道，无需显式关系标签。

Result: 在 Something-Something V2 上，逆动力学监督使关系 AUROC 提升 .065-.103（十种设置）；在 RH20T 机器人任务上提升 .081；使用现成检测追踪提升 .050-.061。逆动力学头在实体潜在变量上解码动作更准确，但仅靠潜在预测、打乱标签、无标签目标或仅 5% 动作标签均无法定位通道。预测关系变化接近观测未来关系质量（AUROC .599 vs .621）。一步关系目标下，16 通道监督读出能在 65 个动作中将正确动作排前五的 43% 情况，优于仅预测潜在变量的 24% 和频率先验的 17%。

Conclusion: Inside-JEPA 证明了通过逆动力学损失可在无关系标签下将关系建模内化到 JEPA 潜在状态中，显著增强关系推理与下游规划能力，为不透明潜在世界模型提供了可解释的关系接口。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn world models in latent space, enabling efficient planning but leaving their states opaque. We introduce Inside-JEPA, which places relational channels for ordered entity pairs inside the JEPA latent state and shows that an inverse-dynamics loss can ground these channels without relation labels when its head reads them, either alone or as part of the full state. On Something-Something V2, latent prediction alone performs no better than a random relation head, while inverse-dynamics supervision that recovers actions from channel changes improves relational AUROC by .065-.103 across ten settings with annotated boxes, by .081 on RH20T robot episodes with held-out tasks, and by .050-.061 across five relations using off-the-shelf detection and tracking. An inverse head on entity latents decodes actions more accurately but barely improves relational grounding; shuffled labels, two label-free objectives, and action labels on only 5% of the pairs also fail to ground the channels. Given an action, predicted relational changes approach the quality of observed future relations (AUROC .599 vs .621). For one-step relational goals, a supervised readout of the 16 channels ranks the correct action among the top five of 65 in 43% of cases, compared with 24% for prediction-only latents and 17% for a frequency prior.

</details>


### [156] [ChemBART: Pre-training a Reaction Language Model for Comprehensive Synthesis Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02915)
*Kenan Li,Yijian Zhang,Jin Wang,Haipeng Gan,Zeying Sun,Xiaoguang Lei,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ChemBART，一种基于 BART 的反应中心预训练语言模型，通过在整体反应表达式（反应物 > 试剂 > 产物）上进行掩码填充预训练，统一解决前体/试剂生成、温度-收率回归、分子性质分类等多种化学任务，并结合蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）实现多步合成路线设计。湿实验验证发现的合成路线比文献基准缩短且收率提升 >20%，实现了从 silico 预测到湿实验合成的闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在计算机辅助合成规划（CASP）中多局限于单分子表示，仅能处理孤立的单任务场景（如前体预测），缺乏对完整反应上下文的建模能力，无法支撑多步合成路线设计与反应条件优化等复杂任务。

Method: 采用 BART 架构，以“反应物 > 试剂 > 产物”的整体反应表达式为输入，设计掩码填充预训练任务，学习反应级别的化学知识。微调阶段覆盖前体/试剂生成、温度-收率回归、分子性质分类等多任务。进一步将 ChemBART 作为策略网络与价值网络嵌入蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）强化学习框架，指导多步逆合成路线搜索与反应条件推荐。

Result: ChemBART 在单任务基准上达到或超越现有专用模型性能；结合 MCTS 发现的多步合成路线经湿实验验证，相比文献已知路线实现路径缩短且收率提升 >20%，证实了模型预测的实用合成价值。

Conclusion: 反应中心的预训练策略能有效统一多种化学任务，ChemBART 展示了从单分子预测向完整合成规划闭环的跨越，为大语言模型在化学合成中的深度应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated transformative potential across diverse fields. However, in computer-aided synthesis planning (CASP), current LLM methodologies are often restricted to single-molecule representations, limiting their application to isolated, single-task scenarios such as precursor prediction. Addressing this gap, we present ChemBART, a BART-based language model that adopts a reaction-centric pre-training strategy. By leveraging a mask-filling pre-training task on holistic reaction expressions (Reactant > Reagent > Product), ChemBART effectively solves a variety of chemical problems, including precursor/reagent generation, temperature-yield regression, and molecular property classification. Building on these capabilities, we demonstrate the integration of ChemBART with Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) for multi-step synthesis route design, where the model serves to optimize the policy and value functions within a reinforcement learning framework. Beyond single-molecule pre-trained LLMs that are constrained to specific applications, ChemBART unifies diverse chemical tasks to facilitate synthesis planning with comprehensive implementation information. Most significantly, the proposed multi-step synthesis routes and reaction conditions were validated in the wet lab, confirming the discovery of shorter synthetic pathways with a >20% yield improvement over established literature benchmarks. Our work highlights the potential of reaction-focused pre-training and showcases the broad utility of ChemBART in closing the loop between in silico prediction and wet-lab synthesis.

</details>


### [157] [Evolving Federated Density Peak Clustering with Shuffler-Based Anonymization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31211)
*Miha Ožbot,Seiichi Ozawa,Igor Škrjanc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的一次性联邦聚类方法 eFDPC，结合演化模糊数据流聚类与密度峰值聚类，通过仅传输选定的峰值而非完整本地模型并引入洗牌器匿名层，实现了单轮通信下的高效、隐私保护联邦聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦聚类需要多轮客户端-服务器交互，每轮都会向服务器泄露额外模型信息，增加本地数据集属性被推断的风险。因此，减少通信轮次并实现不可关联性（即传输信息无法归因于单个客户端）至关重要。数据流聚类算法单次遍历数据即可学习，契合一次性联邦学习范式。

Method: 提出演化联邦密度峰值聚类 (eFDPC)：各客户端独立从私有数据小批量演化微簇（通过簇添加与合并）；对学习到的微簇应用密度峰值聚类，仅传输选定的峰值而非完整本地模型；在客户端与服务器间引入基于洗牌器的匿名层，对贡献进行匿名化后再重建并进行全局峰值选择。提供两种变体：仅峰值的 eFDPC-P 与服务端标签传播的 eFDPC-G。

Result: 在合成与真实基准数据集上的实验表明，eFDPC 在一次性设置下优于近期联邦聚类基线，并能扩展至高维、非 IID 数据场景。

Conclusion: eFDPC 通过演化模糊聚类与密度峰值聚类的结合，配合洗牌器匿名机制，实现了单轮通信、强隐私保护（不可关联性）且可扩展的联邦聚类，为一次性联邦学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a novel one-shot federated clustering approach based on evolving fuzzy clustering and shuffler-based anonymization. In federated clustering, each round reveals additional model information to the server and can increase the opportunity to infer properties of local datasets, even when raw samples are never shared. Consequently, fewer client-server exchanges and unlinkability are important, so transmitted information cannot be attributed to any single client. Data-stream clustering algorithms, which learn in a single pass over the data, align well with the one-shot federated learning paradigm, where a single communication round can be sufficient. The proposed Evolving Federated Density Peak Clustering (eFDPC) method combines evolving fuzzy data-stream clustering and density peak clustering in a federated setting. Each client independently evolves micro-clusters from private data mini-batches through cluster addition and merging. To limit communication, density peak clustering is applied to the learned micro-clusters and only the selected peaks are transmitted, rather than the full local models. A shuffler-based layer is introduced between the clients and the server to anonymize contributions prior to reconstruction and global peak selection. We consider a peak-only variant, eFDPC-P, as well as eFDPC-G with server-side label propagation. Experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmark datasets show that eFDPC outperforms recent federated clustering baselines in one-shot settings and scales to higher-dimensional, non-IID data.

</details>


### [158] [Local Discrimination of Binary Classifiers via Localized AUC Functional](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31216)
*Wenye Ren,Anna Győrffy-Kerekes,Tancredi Dorsi,Olga Demler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为LOCATOR的方法，利用局部化AUC（LAUC）识别风险预测模型在哪些患者亚群中表现不佳，解决了传统AUC无法捕捉性能异质性的问题，并在真实数据和模拟实验中验证了其有效性，同时为LAUC提供了统计学理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统风险预测模型评估主要依赖AUC，但AUC掩盖了不同患者亚群间的性能差异（性能异质性），可能导致临床决策失误。因此需要一种方法来识别模型预测不准确的特定患者队列，以便进行针对性改进或替代建模。

Method: 引入加权AUC的变体——局部化AUC（LAUC），并基于此构建LOCATOR方法。LAUC通过赋予不同样本不同权重，聚焦于局部区域的模型性能；LOCATOR利用LAUC系统地搜索并识别模型表现显著下降的患者亚群。同时建立了LAUC的统计推断框架，包括方差估计和假设检验。

Result: 在真实临床数据集和模拟实验中，LOCATOR成功识别出多个模型预测准确性显著低于整体水平的患者队列。实验表明，LAUC能敏感捕捉局部性能下降，且统计推断框架有效控制了假阳性率。

Conclusion: LOCATOR结合LAUC为风险预测模型的局部性能评估提供了实用工具和理论基础，使临床医生和模型开发者能发现并解决特定人群中的预测偏差，提升模型在异质性人群中的可靠性和临床适用性。

Abstract: Risk prediction models are frequently evaluated with AUC, which means that during evaluation we are blind to performance heterogeneity, which could lead to bad decision-making in clinics. In this work, we aim to change that by identifying patient cohorts for whom the risk prediction model might not be an accurate predictor. To separate these cohorts, we use a version of the weighted AUC, which we call Localized AUC or LAUC. Using this metric, we build a method that we coin LOCATOR. This method is able to identify cohorts of patients for whom the risk prediction model is inaccurate. We test this method both in real-world data and in simulations. We also build a statistical background for the use of Localized AUC for our purposes.

</details>


### [159] [QuaNAS: Uncertainty-Aware Reuse of Earlier Searches for Query-Efficient Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31251)
*Brama Seta Putra Satria,Dwi Sunaryono,Misbakhul Munir Irfan Subakti,Irzal Ahmad Sabilla,Laras Suciningtyas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuaNAS 利用同一搜索空间中以往神经架构搜索（NAS）的历史库作为显性先验知识，通过学习设计决策的主效应和零成本代理构建架构先验，结合高斯过程在极少评估下高效搜索新数据集，显著降低即时遗憾并匹配或超越基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在新数据集上进行 NAS 通常只能负担极少量的短训练运行，搜索前的先验知识决定了效率。现有方法大多从零开始，仅依赖跨任务不可靠的零成本代理，缺乏可靠的迁移机制来利用历史搜索经验。

Method: QuaNAS 将同一搜索空间内的历史搜索库视为不确定可靠性的显性知识。从库中学习设计决策的主效应，结合零成本代理作为次要信息，构建无需新数据集标签的候选架构先验。高斯过程从先验最优点启动，并在每次评估后重新估计对各先验的信任程度，动态调整搜索策略。

Result: 在冻结协议下，QuaNAS 于 12 个新图像、时间序列、文本和表格数据集上单次评估。相较于五个从零开始的 NAS 基线，即时遗憾降低 2.7 至 3.9 倍（Holm 校正 p=0.0012）。最终模型在九个数据集上达到非劣效性（0.5 个百分点边界）或超越匹配配方的参考模型，且零评估的先验最优点表现相当。消融实验显示：无库时同高斯过程遗憾增加逾两倍；主效应池化最适合弱迁移的表格任务，任务级代理模型适合图像、时间序列、文本任务及小规模/失配库。主效应使迁移知识可解释。

Conclusion: QuaNAS 通过显性利用历史搜索库，构建可解释的架构先验，在极少评估下实现跨多模态任务的高效 NAS。其核心优势在于将迁移知识显性化（主效应可检视），并自适应地平衡先验与零成本代理，显著优于从零开始的基线及无库的迁移方法，为小样本 NAS 提供了实用的迁移学习范式。

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) on a new dataset often affords only tens of short training runs, so what the search knows before its first evaluation decides its efficiency. Yet most NAS methods start from scratch, guided at best by zero-cost proxies that are unreliable across tasks. QuaNAS treats a library of earlier searches in the same search space as explicit knowledge of uncertain reliability. Main effects of design decisions learned from this library and, secondarily, zero-cost proxies become priors over candidate architectures without any label of the new dataset. A Gaussian process starts at the prior optimum and re-estimates after every evaluation how far each prior is trusted. Under a protocol frozen before any confirmation data were seen, QuaNAS was evaluated once on 12 new image, time-series, text and tabular datasets. It lowered anytime regret on the short-training objective 2.7- to 3.9-fold relative to five pre-specified NAS baselines that start from scratch (Holm-adjusted p = 0.0012 each). Its final models matched (non-inferiority margin 0.5 pp) or exceeded matched-recipe reference models on nine datasets, as did the prior optimum, selected without a single evaluation. Post-hoc comparisons with established transfer methods given the same library trace the gain to the library: without it, the same Gaussian process had over twice the regret. They also show where each transfer mechanism works best: the pooled main effects of QuaNAS for weakly transferable tabular tasks, per-task surrogates for image, time-series and text tasks and for small or mismatched libraries. Finally, the main effects make the transferred knowledge inspectable.

</details>


### [160] [Deep ReLU Networks Can Fail the Best Approximation Property for Continuous Target Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31264)
*Sebastian Kassing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个连续函数，证明对于具有两隐藏层架构 (2,2,1,1) 的 ReLU 网络，L² 逼近误差的下确界不可达，即深层 ReLU 网络一般不满足最佳逼近性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究深层 ReLU 网络是否满足最佳逼近性质（即全局最小值是否存在），与浅层 ReLU 网络形成对比，因为浅层 ReLU 网络是少数保证典型回归任务中全局最小值存在的激活函数之一。

Method: 构造反例：在 [-1,1]² 上定义一个特定连续函数，利用第二隐藏层神经元折线可以弯曲的特性，打破了一隐藏层情况下使用的对称性论证，证明下确界不可达。

Result: 存在连续目标函数，使得两隐藏层 ReLU 网络 (2,2,1,1) 的 L² 逼近误差下确界不可达，表明深层 ReLU 网络一般不满足最佳逼近性质。

Conclusion: 深层 ReLU 网络不保证最佳逼近性质，即使对于连续目标函数；这与浅层 ReLU 网络形成鲜明对比，后者在典型回归任务中保证全局最小值存在。证明关键在于第二隐藏层神经元折线的弯曲破坏了对称性。

Abstract: We construct a continuous function on $[-1,1]^2$ for which the infimum of the $L^2$-approximation error over the class of ReLU networks with two hidden layers and architecture $(2,2,1,1)$ is not attained. This counterexample shows that deep ReLU networks do not satisfy the best approximation property in general, even for continuous target functions. Our findings contrast with existing results for shallow neural networks, where ReLU is one of the few activation functions that guarantee the existence of global minima in typical regression tasks. The proof is based on the fact that the breaklines for neurons in the second hidden layer can bend, which breaks the symmetry arguments used in the one-hidden-layer case.

</details>


### [161] [DISC: Domain-Integrated Similarity Cost for Time-Series Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31288)
*Yen-Ku Liu,Hongjie Chen,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究时间序列数据集相似度度量与下游预测任务表现的相关性，提出可学习的聚合框架 DISC，通过校准迁移观测来选择、加权和组合多个相似度度量，比单一指标更好地预测迁移学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据集和基础模型的增长使得数据集比较成为核心环节。现有相似度度量捕捉不同的数据集关系，且与下游任务效用的对齐程度各异。需要一种方法自动整合互补度量，以更准确地预测源-目标迁移性能，避免穷举评估每个候选对。

Method: 提出 Domain-Integrated Similarity Cost (DISC)，一种可学习的聚合框架。利用校准迁移观测（即已知的源-目标对及其下游预测误差）来训练模型，选择、加权并组合多个现有相似度度量。校准后，DISC 仅使用相似度度量值即可为新的源-目标对打分。

Result: 实验表明，DISC 在报告的数据划分上，其评分与下游预测误差的对齐程度显著高于单一指标基线。说明时间序列数据集相似度是任务依赖的，学习到的指标整合比手工选择单一指标更能作为下游迁移行为的代理。

Conclusion: 时间序列数据集相似度度量应针对具体下游任务进行自适应整合。DISC 通过学习聚合多个互补度量，提供了更强的下游迁移行为代理，为迁移学习中的源数据集选择提供了实用工具。

Abstract: The growth of time-series datasets and foundation models has made dataset comparison a central part of time-series workflows. Assessing source-target transfer before model adaptation requires similarity estimates that can summarize transfer-related dataset relationships without exhaustive source-target evaluation over every candidate pair. Many metrics have been proposed for measuring similarity between time-series datasets, but they capture different dataset relationships and vary in how well they align with downstream utility across tasks. We evaluate this gap by measuring the correlation between source-target similarity scores and downstream forecasting errors in out-of-distribution and transfer-learning settings. We propose Domain-Integrated Similarity Cost (DISC), a learnable aggregation framework that uses calibration transfer observations to select, weight, and combine similarity metrics for different downstream tasks. By integrating complementary metric values, DISC better distinguishes source-target dataset differences that matter for downstream behavior. After calibration, DISC scores source-target pairs using only their similarity-metric values and yields higher alignment than single-metric references on the reported split. These results show that time-series dataset similarity is task-dependent, and that learned metric integration provides a stronger proxy for downstream transfer behavior than manually selecting one similarity metric.

</details>


### [162] [The Bayesian Learning Rule Beyond KL Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31308)
*Sophia Sklaviadis,Wu Lin,Andre Martins,Mario Figueiredo,Thomas Moellenhoff,Emtiyaz Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出广义贝叶斯学习规则（generalized BLR），基于混合空间Bregman或Fenchel-Young差异，推广了经典BLR，使得可以使用非KL几何，实现可加性更新，并导出有界支持和重尾的q-高斯后验，在CIFAR-100上表现竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典BLR依赖KL散度几何，但KL几何在参数相关支持的后验族上可能导致未定义目标；先前工作认为仅KL散度能保持可加性BLR计算，本文旨在探索非KL几何下保持可加性BLR的可能性。

Method: 利用混合空间Bregman或Fenchel-Young差异，诱导广义最大熵族、对偶坐标和广义BLR规范梯度更新，恢复经典BLR；以Tsallis负熵配合二次统计量为例，推导出有界支持和重尾q-高斯后验，并给出类牛顿实现。

Result: 广义BLR导出无法从KL-based BLR得到的新算法，实现可控制的支持集和尾部行为，在CIFAR-100等实验中展现竞争力性能。

Conclusion: 广义BLR扩展了贝叶斯学习规则的适用几何范围，提供了更灵活的后验族选择（如q-高斯），并保持了计算效率，为变分推断提供了新工具。

Abstract: The Bayesian learning rule (BLR) exploits the relation between Bayesian updating and the Kullback-Leibler divergence (KLD) to derive efficient additive updates for exponential family variational posteriors. The KL geometry is powerful but restrictive: combined with posterior families with parameter-dependent support it can lead to undefined objectives. We ask which non-KL geometries preserve the additive BLR computation. Previous works that use $f$-divergences suggest that this is only possible with the KLD. Here, we show that this is not true. We show that additive information-geometric optimization algorithms can be derived for the class of mixed-space Bregman or Fenchel-Young discrepancies. These discrepancies induce generalized maximum-entropy families, dual coordinates, and generalized-BLR canonical gradient updates, recovering the classical BLR. The generalized BLR leads to new algorithms that \textit{cannot} be derived from the KL-based BLR. For the Tsallis negentropy with quadratic statistics, the generalized BLR yields bounded-support and heavy-tailed $q$-Gaussian posteriors and an easy Newton-like implementation. Experiments up to CIFAR-100 illustrate controllable support and tail behavior and competitive performance.

</details>


### [163] [Extreme Compression of Per-Layer Embeddings via Cross-Layer Latent Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31309)
*Jiacheng Liu,Yaxin Luo,Xiaohan Zhao,Peng Sun,Xinyi Shang,Jiacheng Cui,Yonina C. Eldar,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OPTIC 方法，用于语言模型中逐层嵌入（PLE）的极端压缩，在约 100× 压缩率下保持 97.8% 性能，显著优于传统量化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着嵌入容量随 Transformer 计算规模扩大成为趋势，高压缩率变得愈发重要。PLE 占 Gemma 4 E4B 参数的 35.2%，在极端压缩率下，传统量化器为每个 token-layer 向量仅分配几字节的独立码本，效果急剧下降。

Method: OPTIC（输出路径拟合、Token 共享索引码）为每个 token 仅存储一组量化码元组，并在每层使用逐层的仿射解码器进行解码。码本与解码器直接拟合 PLE 的输出路径，而非存储的向量本身。

Result: 在约 100× BF16 E4B PLE 压缩下，OPTIC 保持 97.8% 综合性能（七项基准平均），而乘积量化（最强基线）仅 66.3%。594× 和 1,002× 压缩下分别保持 90.5% 和 84.1%。官方 W4A16 E4B 上 100× 压缩保持 96.9%。推理时显存减少 29.7%，速度最多仅慢 0.40%。

Conclusion: OPTIC 通过 Token 共享码本与逐层仿射解码、面向输出路径拟合，实现了 PLE 的极端压缩，在极高压缩率下仍能保持模型性能，并带来实际推理显存与延迟收益。

Abstract: Scaling embedding capacity alongside Transformer computation is an emerging trend in language models. This trend makes high compression rates increasingly useful. Hence, we study extreme compression of Per-Layer Embeddings (PLE), which follow this trend and account for 35.2% of the parameters of Gemma 4 E4B. At such rates, conventional quantizers give each token-layer vector its own code of only a few bytes. We instead develop OPTIC (Output-Path-fitted, Token-shared Indexed Codes), which stores one quantized code tuple per token and decodes it at every layer with a layer-specific affine decoder. Codes and decoders are fitted to the PLE output path rather than to the stored vectors. At about $100\times$ PLE compression of bfloat16 (BF16) E4B, OPTIC retains an aggregate 97.8% of the uncompressed model's performance across seven benchmarks, whereas product quantization, the strongest of six conventional baselines, retains 66.3%. At $594\times$ and $1,002\times$ PLE compression of BF16 E4B, OPTIC retains 90.5% and 84.1%. On the official E4B release with 4-bit linear weights and 16-bit activations (W4A16), OPTIC retains 96.9% at about $100\times$ PLE compression. During inference at about $100\times$ PLE compression, BF16 E4B uses 29.7% less GPU memory than with uncompressed PLE at the same key-value cache size, while running at most 0.40% slower.

</details>


### [164] [Identifiability from Stationary Covariance in Linear SDEs with Reciprocal Interactions and Latent Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31310)
*Ana Leticia Garcez Vicente,Saber Salehkaleybar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了新的图形条件，用于在可能包含有向2-环（互惠交互）的线性SDE系统中通用识别因果效应，并开发了工具变量框架以在部分观测（存在潜在变量）的情况下识别目标直接因果效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性SDE因果识别结果主要限于完全观测且无有向2-环的系统，而实际系统常包含互惠交互（有向2-环）和潜在变量，因此需要扩展识别条件和处理部分观测的方法。

Method: 首先建立新的图形条件，用于在可能存在有向2-环的系统中实现因果效应的通用识别；其次开发工具变量框架，在存在潜在变量的部分观测设定下识别目标直接因果效应。

Result: 提出了允许有向2-环的新图形识别条件，并给出了利用工具变量在部分观测下识别直接因果效应的方法框架。

Conclusion: 该工作为连续时间SDE系统中的因果识别提供了更一般的理论基础，涵盖了互惠交互和潜在变量两个重要但此前未充分解决的场景。

Abstract: Continuous-time stochastic differential equations (SDEs) provide a natural framework for modeling interacting dynamical systems. A central causal question is whether causal effects among observed variables can be identified from observational data drawn from the stationary distribution, given the underlying causal graph. For linear SDEs, existing identifiability results are largely restricted to fully observed systems whose causal graphs contain no directed 2-cycles. We first establish new graphical conditions for the generic identifiability of causal effects in systems that may contain directed 2-cycles, thereby allowing for reciprocal interactions. Next, we address the previously unexplored setting of latent variables by developing an instrumental-variable framework for identifying a target direct causal effect under partial observability.

</details>


### [165] [The Value of System 1 Models in Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31315)
*Xiufeng Yang,Konstantinos Vrettos,Zeineb Ben Chaaben,Fabian Drexel,Jannik Lübberstedt,Johannes Moll,Anirudh Narayanan,Frederike Stumpf,Cosmin I. Bercea,Jiawen Wang,Evangelia E. Vassalou,Krischan Braitsch,Sebastian Ziegelmayer,Markus Graf,Marcus R. Makowski,Lisa Adams,Nassir Navab,Michail E. Klontzas,Keno Bressem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了四个System 1模型（两个专有、两个开放权重）在86个临床任务上的零样本表现，涵盖14个临床领域、20种语言、179,111个案例。结果显示System 1模型在医学知识回忆和文本理解上表现优异（AUROC 0.93-0.94），但在临床判断、图像感知和体积影像发现上性能下降（AUROC 0.69-0.89）。影像任务比文本任务低0.09 AUROC。最佳模型在13/26文本任务和8/35影像任务上达到或超越已发表最佳结果。分诊护士准确率显著高于模型（0.85 vs 0.56）。推理速度快（0.34-0.44秒/决策）、能耗低（24-81焦耳）。语言差异大：从英语到约鲁巴语准确率下降高达39个百分点。同家族生成模型慢11-76倍，平衡准确率仅高3-5个百分点。结论：System 1模型适合快速、低成本、带可用置信度的文本知识回忆任务，不适合需要判断或读取影像的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策常需在固定选项中快速、大量选择，且需要不确定性估计以知晓何时移交临床医生。生成式语言模型回答慢、自由文本输出、置信度校准差。相比之下，System 1模型能零样本给出各选项概率，但其在临床任务上的整体表现未知，因此需要系统评估。

Method: 选取四个System 1模型（两专有、两开放权重），在86个公开临床任务上零样本评估，任务覆盖14临床领域、20语言、考试题与医学影像。任务预先划分为六个能力层级。主要指标为AUROC，次要指标包括校准、语言效应、与已发表结果及生成式模型的对比。共计179,111个案例。

Result: 最佳模型各层级平均AUROC：医学知识0.94、文本理解0.93、文本临床判断0.83、图像感知0.89、2D影像发现0.84、体积影像发现0.69。影像任务比文本任务低0.09（95% CI 0.06-0.11，P<0.001）。最佳模型在13/26文本任务和8/35影像任务上匹配或超越已发表最佳结果。分诊护士准确率0.85显著优于模型0.56。开放权重模型本地推理单次决策耗时0.34-0.44秒、能耗24-81焦耳。同题目从英语到约鲁巴语准确率下降高达39百分点。同家族生成模型推理慢11-76倍，平衡准确率仅高3-5百分点。

Conclusion: System 1模型能快速、低成本、带可用置信度地完成答案明确陈述在文本中或需从医学知识回忆的决策，但不适用于需要临床判断或从影像读取答案的任务。

Abstract: Many clinical decisions are a choice among a few fixed options, taken quickly and in large numbers. Such decisions also need an uncertainty estimate to know when to defer to a clinician. Generative language models answer slowly in free text and state their confidence poorly. In contrast, System 1 models answer zero-shot with a probability for each answer. However, their performance across clinical tasks is unknown. Four System 1 models, two proprietary and two with open weights, were evaluated zero-shot on 86 public tasks from 14 clinical domains in 20 languages, spanning examination questions and medical imaging. Tasks were assigned to six capability tiers before analysis. The primary outcome was the area under the receiver-operating-characteristic curve (AUROC). Secondary outcomes included calibration, language effects and comparisons with published results and generative models. Across 179,111 cases, the mean AUROC of the best model per task was 0.94 for medical knowledge, 0.93 for text understanding, 0.83 for clinical judgement from text, 0.89 for image perception, 0.84 for 2D image findings and 0.69 for volumetric findings. Imaging tasks scored 0.09 (95% CI, 0.06 to 0.11) lower than text tasks (P < 0.001). The best model matched or exceeded the best published result on 13 of 26 text tasks and on 8 of 35 imaging tasks. Triage nurses outperformed the best model (accuracy, 0.85 vs. 0.56). One decision took 0.34 to 0.44 seconds and 24 to 81 joules on open-weight models run locally. Accuracy on identical questions fell by up to 39 percentage points from English to Yoruba. Generative models of the same family were 11 to 76 times slower, with 3 to 5 percentage points higher balanced accuracy. System 1 models decide quickly, cheaply and with usable confidence when the answer is stated in a text or recalled from medical knowledge, but not when it must be judged or read from an image.

</details>


### [166] [Bridging the Data Gap: Source-Informed Task-Space Transport for Few-Shot Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31323)
*Jinjian Jiang,Jing Peng,Rehan Deen,Yu Cao,Ziquan Liu,Muhammad Salman Haleem,David Mguni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Meta-Diff提出了一种在紧凑任务空间中学习可复用的源-目标迁移关系的方法，通过共享编码器将分布映射到低维坐标，学习的关系将源坐标传输到预测目标坐标，再由共享扩散骨干生成目标域样本，无需目标域特定微调，实现极少样本适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决数据丰富源域向数据稀疏目标域的快速适应问题，特别是在目标数据难以收集（如医疗场景）的情况下，避免对大容量生成模型进行昂贵且统计上不可靠的重新训练或微调。

Method: 1) 共享编码器将源/目标分布编码为低维任务坐标；2) 在任务空间中学习一个可复用的源-目标迁移关系，将新源坐标映射到预测目标坐标；3) 共享扩散骨干网络从预测的目标坐标生成样本，无需目标域特定微调。方法在高斯混合、CIFAR-100、Office-Home上验证。

Result: 在CIFAR-100保留验证任务上，正确源+迁移比错误源迁移平均SW降低13.9%；最终测试单目标对比中，比仅目标建模降低14.7%，比完全微调降低32.7%。实验还揭示成功条件：任务身份需在表示中保留、关系需跨任务泛化、源-目标偏移需留有传输余地。Office-Home显示迁移可在无可靠物体身份时对齐目标域统计。

Conclusion: 将极少样本适应重构为任务空间中的可复用传输，并使成功/失败条件可直接度量。Meta-Diff展示了在不使用目标图像且假设已知适用关系的情况下，通过任务空间传输实现高效适应的可能性。

Abstract: Many deployments require a model to move rapidly from a data-rich source to a related target observed only sparsely. This is especially consequential where target data are difficult to collect, as in healthcare settings with persistent population or acquisition imbalances. Retraining or fine-tuning a high-capacity generator for every target is costly and can be statistically unreliable when target data are scarce. We introduce Meta-Diff, which instead learns a recurring source--target relation once in a compact task space. A shared encoder maps each distribution to a low-dimensional coordinate; a learned relation transports a new source coordinate to its predicted target, and a shared diffusion backbone generates from that prediction without target-specific retraining. Controlled Gaussian-mixture experiments identify when this works: task identity must survive the representation, the relation must generalise across tasks, and the source--target shift must leave useful headroom for transport. On held-out CIFAR-100 validation tasks, correct source $+$ transport achieves 13.9% lower mean SW than transport from the wrong source. With validation-selected adaptation settings in the final-test one-target comparison, its mean SW is 14.7% lower than target-only modelling and 32.7% lower than full fine-tuning; the main route itself uses no target image and assumes the applicable relation is known. On matched CIFAR test episodes, generating from the clean-source coordinate and then applying the known corruption yields lower SW than task-space transport. Office-Home exposes a complementary boundary: transport can align target-domain statistics without reliable object identity. Together, these results recast extreme-few-shot adaptation as reusable transport in task space, while making the conditions for its success and failure directly measurable.

</details>


### [167] [Harmful Responses Are Jailbreak Templates: Transferring Autoregressive Jailbreaks to Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31331)
*Jinwei Gan,Zifeng Cheng,Zhiwei Jiang,Ran Sun,Shiping Ge,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RMT-Jailbreak 框架，利用扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）的双向重构特性，将自回归模型生成的有害响应转化为高掩码率模板，实现跨范式越狱攻击的有效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型通过双向上下文建模实现灵活的掩码生成，但这也引入了独特的越狱攻击面。研究发现，针对查询的有害响应天然可作为越狱模板：仅掩盖部分 token 即可诱导 dLLM 重构有害内容。

Method: RMT-Jailbreak 将自回归模型生成的有害响应转化为高掩码率模板（均匀 token 保留），再通过基于提示的安全过滤掩盖残余可见有害内容，最后将精炼模板提供给目标 dLLM 进行双向重构。

Result: 跨多种自回归越狱方法和目标 dLLMs 的大量实验验证了 RMT-Jailbreak 的有效性与通用性，揭示了越狱攻击在不同生成范式间的强跨范式迁移能力。

Conclusion: 扩散大语言模型的双向重构机制使其易受基于模板的越狱攻击；RMT-Jailbreak 通过自动化模板构建与安全过滤，实现了从自回归模型到扩散模型的越狱攻击高效迁移，提示需针对扩散模型设计专门防御机制。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) enable flexible masked generation through bidirectional context modeling, but this capability also introduces a distinct jailbreak attack surface. We find that a harmful response to a given query naturally serves as an effective jailbreak template: simply masking a subset of its tokens can induce a dLLM to reconstruct harmful content. Based on this observation, we propose RMT-Jailbreak, a general framework that transfers existing autoregressive (AR) jailbreak attacks to dLLMs through their induced harmful responses. RMT-Jailbreak converts an AR-generated harmful response into a high-mask-ratio template using uniform token retention, and further applies prompt-based safety filtering to mask any remaining visible harmful content. The refined template is then provided to the target dLLM for bidirectional reconstruction. Extensive experiments across multiple AR jailbreak methods and target dLLMs demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of RMT-Jailbreak, revealing strong cross-paradigm transferability of jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [168] [SQuAT: Spherical Lattice Vector Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31412)
*Sebastian Loeschcke,Ivan Koryakovskiy,Tycho F. A. van der Ouderaa,Riccardo Del Chiaro,Mart van Baalen,Paul N. Whatmough,Markus Nagel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SQuAT，一种基于形状-增益分解的矢量量化感知训练方法，将权重分解为方向和幅度，分别使用E8球面码本和标量量化器进行量化，并设计了独立的直通估计器(STE)，实现了隐式的球面黎曼优化，在多个LLM模型上达到SOTA VQ-QAT精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于格点的矢量量化(VQ)在低比特大语言模型中具有优秀的率失真权衡，但在量化感知训练(VQ-QAT)下的行为理解不足。标准STE应用于完整量化器时忽略了形状-增益结构，无法充分利用球面几何特性。

Method: 1. 形状-增益分解：将权重分解为归一化方向(形状)和幅度(增益)；2. 方向使用E8导出的球面码本量化，增益使用低比特标量量化器独立量化；3. 为形状和增益设计独立的STE，诱导切空间更新，实现隐式球面黎曼优化；4. 开发分布式精确最近邻分配方案，工作节点量化不相交权重块，仅通信离散索引，将训练时间减少12.8倍。

Result: 在指令微调的LLaMA-3.2-1B/3B、Qwen3-4B和基座Qwen3-1.7B上，SQuAT在相同量化器和初始化下持续优于标准VQ-QAT STE，达到SOTA VQ-QAT精度；分布式方案使其能扩展到10B tokens训练规模。

Conclusion: SQuAT通过形状-增益分解和独立STE设计，成功利用了球面几何结构进行隐式黎曼优化，配合分布式最近邻分配实现了大规模VQ-QAT的高效训练，为低比特LLM量化提供了新的SOTA基准。

Abstract: Vector quantization (VQ), in particular lattice-based methods, offer strong rate-distortion trade-offs for low-bit large language models (LLMs), yet their behavior under quantization-aware training (VQ-QAT) remains poorly understood. We scale lattice VQ-QAT to billion-parameter LLMs and multi-billion-token training regimes, and introduce SQuAT, a shape-gain VQ-QAT approach trained with a separate straight-through estimator (STE) for the shape and gain. SQuAT decomposes weights into direction and magnitude: normalized directions are quantized using an $E_8$-derived spherical codebook, while gains are quantized independently with low-bit scalar quantizers. We show that, unlike a standard STE applied to the full quantizer, which ignores the shape-gain structure, these separate STEs induce tangent-space updates for directions, yielding an implicit form of spherical (Riemannian) optimization. To enable scalability, we develop a distributed exact nearest-neighbor assignment scheme, in which workers quantize disjoint weight blocks and communicate only discrete indices, reducing VQ-QAT training time by 12.8$\times$ and allowing us to scale it to 10B tokens. Across instruction-tuned LLaMA-3.2-1B/3B, Qwen3-4B, and base Qwen3-1.7B, SQuAT consistently outperforms standard VQ-QAT STE under identical quantizers and initialization, and achieves SOTA VQ-QAT accuracy.

</details>


### [169] [Post-Hoc Feature Selection Layer for Neural Networks Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31420)
*João Kenji Suwa,João Pedro Silveira e Silva,Bruno Iochins Grisci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 post-hoc FSL，一种非侵入式的后训练特征选择层，将 FSL 改造为轻量级可训练模块接入冻结的预训练模型，通过微调特征权重识别重要特征，在保持原模型预测性能的同时增强可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂神经网络的可解释性是关键挑战，尤其针对已部署在高风险领域的模型，需要一种不改变原模型架构和参数的非侵入式方法来提升可解释性。

Method: 将 FSL 重构为轻量级可训练模块，集成到冻结的预训练模型中，基于原模型输出微调特征权重以学习特征相关性。实验采用准确率、F1 分数、召回率、精确率、加权 t-SNE 和轮廓系数等统计与可视化指标，在高维合成及真实表格数据集上评估稳定性，并与嵌入式 FSL、Integrated Gradients、Noise Tunnel、DeepLIFT、Gradient SHAP、Feature Ablation 等后验解释方法对比。

Result: post-hoc FSL 在不同数据集上均能成功识别相关特征，保持了原神经网络的预测能力，同时显著增强了模型的可解释性。

Conclusion: post-hoc FSL 是一种有效的非侵入式后训练特征加权方法，可为已部署模型提供可解释性，且不损害其预测性能。

Abstract: The interpretability of complex neural networks remains a critical challenge, especially for models already deployed in high-stakes domains. To address this, we adapted the Feature Selection Layer (FSL) to optimize feature weights post-training, a variant we term post-hoc FSL. Our approach reframes the FSL as a lightweight, trainable module that integrates with already frozen pre-trained models on tabular datasets to highlight the features the original model considers most important. This post-hoc FSL learns feature relevance by fine-tuning its weights based on the original model's learned outputs. Crucially, this process is non-invasive, operating without altering the original model's architecture or its learned parameters. We conducted our experiments using both statistical and visual metrics, including accuracy, F1 score, recall, precision, weighted t-SNE and silhouette score, and also analyzed the stability of the post-hoc FSL on high-dimensional synthetic and real-world tabular datasets. We compared the post-hoc FSL feature weighting method using these metrics against the original embedded FSL and other post-hoc interpretability methods, such as Integrated Gradients, Noise Tunnel, DeepLIFT, Gradient SHAP, and Feature Ablation. Experimental results demonstrate that the post-hoc FSL feature weighting method successfully identified relevant features across the different datasets, maintaining the predictive power of the original neural network while enhancing its interpretability.

</details>


### [170] [Predictable performance is suboptimal, and what it means for model routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31428)
*Guanhua Zhang,Moritz Hardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型路由中的性能预测方法，发现仅当候选模型在任务上次优或预测器增加显著容量时，性能预测才优于模型自信心基线。理论上证明预测器优势可通过重校准精确转移到对数损失，且其优势受限于模型次优性与额外参数增益。实验验证：预测器对自信心的优势随模型次优性增大而增加，微调后减小；级联路由呈现相同模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能力与成本差异大，激发自适应路由方法为每个查询选择模型。现有路由方法依赖性能预测器，但缺乏对何时性能预测优于简单基线（模型自信心）的理论理解。本文旨在建立性能预测器相对于自信心基线的理论界限，并揭示其与模型次优性、预测器容量的关系。

Method: 以模型自信心（对所选答案的概率）为基线，理论分析性能预测器的优势：1) 证明任何超越自信心的预测器可通过重校准将优势精确转移到对数损失；2) 考虑预测器额外参数，将其优势上界为模型次优性加参数增益；3) 扩展至双模型级联路由。实验使用16个语言模型在四个基准上验证理论预测。

Result: 实验表明：性能预测器相对于自信心的优势在更次优模型上更大，且随模型微调改进而收缩。双模型级联路由表现出相同模式：基于预测器的路由相对于基于自信心的路由增益随模型次优性增加而增大，微调后增益减小。

Conclusion: 性能预测在路由中仅当候选模型次优或预测器显著增加容量时才有效。自信心基线已捕获模型大部分不确定性；预测器额外增益受限于模型次优性与参数预算。这为路由系统设计提供理论指导：优先改进模型本身而非训练复杂预测器。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) differ in capabilities and costs, motivating routing methods that adaptively choose a model for each query. These methods rely on performance prediction: given a query, estimate whether a model will answer it correctly. Most routing methods train separate predictors of candidate models' performance to guide model selection. Our work shows that performance prediction only beats a simple baseline when the candidate model is suboptimal on the task or the performance predictor adds significant capacity relative to the candidate model. Specifically, we take a model's self-confidence, the probability it assigns to its chosen answer, as a baseline and study how much a performance predictor can improve on it. First, we show that any performance predictor that outperforms a model's self-confidence can be used to improve the model's loss: the predictor's advantage transfers exactly in log loss through recalibration. Second, accounting for the predictor's additional parameters, we bound its advantage by the model's suboptimality plus the gain from those parameters. Third, we extend our theoretical results to two-model cascades, which route queries to a more capable model when the first model is predicted to fail. Experiments with 16 language models on four benchmarks show that predictors gain a larger advantage over self-confidence on more suboptimal models. This advantage shrinks as models improve through finetuning. Cascading exhibits the same two patterns: predictor-based routing offers larger gains over confidence-based routing for more suboptimal models, and these gains diminish after finetuning.

</details>


### [171] [Drift Says When, Budget Says How Much: Rate-Controlled KV Re-selection for Long-Form Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31433)
*Bin Hu,Yingbo Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Rate-Controlled Refresh (RCR) 方法，用于多模态大语言模型的 KV 缓存压缩，通过自适应控制每样本的视觉 token 重选频率，解决了长文本生成中注意力漂移导致的一次性选择失效问题，在多个基准上超越现有方法且开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态 LLM 的 KV 缓存压缩通常在预填充阶段一次性选择视觉 token 并复用，但在长文本生成（如详细图像描述）中，模型关注的证据会随着生成过程移动，导致一次性选择严重受限。即使使用未压缩输出的注意力作为 oracle 选择，ROUGE-L 与未压缩输出的一致性也仅为 0.44-0.52。固定漂移阈值会系统性失效，因为漂移幅度在不同样本间不可比，低漂移样本几乎从不刷新尽管它们受益最大。

Method: 提出 Rate-Controlled Refresh (RCR)：让注意力漂移决定何时、何地重新选择 token，同时引入逐样本控制器调节刷新频率。结合低秩漂移检测器、候选受限重选策略，且决策全程留在 GPU 上。统一超参数适用所有模型和任务。

Result: 在六个模型-任务设置中的五个上，RCR 比逐步检索方法 InfiniGen 高出 1.6-3.8 ROUGE-L，第六个无显著差距；与精确逐步重选仅差 1.7 点以内；每步估算数据移动量相当，GPU 驻留检测器体积缩小 4 倍。在 DetailCaps 上，漂移驱动的时机优于同刷新率的随机时机和更高频率的周期时机 2.0-5.0 点。在预注册视频描述任务中（漂移均质），RCR 与 InfiniGen 持平（Qwen2-VL）或超越（Qwen3-VL），但不再优于最近测试速率下的固定阈值，与诊断一致。目标级评估显示，RCR 在三个模型中的两个上对未压缩输出的目标 F1 高于 InfiniGen。

Conclusion: RCR 通过自适应的逐样本刷新率控制，有效解决了长文本多模态生成中注意力漂移的动态性问题，在保持极低开销的同时实现了接近精确逐步重选的性能，验证了漂移驱动的自适应刷新优于固定阈值或周期性刷新。

Abstract: KV cache compression for multimodal LLMs usually selects the visual tokens to keep once, at prefill, and reuses that selection for the whole generation. We show that this one-shot selection is severely limited on long-form generation: on detailed image description, even an oracle that selects with the attention of the uncompressed answer reaches only 0.44-0.52 ROUGE-L agreement with the uncompressed output, because the evidence the model reads moves as the description unfolds. Re-selecting whenever attention drifts out of the kept set is the natural remedy, but we find that a fixed drift threshold fails systematically: drift magnitudes are not comparable across samples, so low-drift samples are almost never refreshed although they benefit the most. We propose Rate-Controlled Refresh (RCR), which lets drift decide when and where to re-select while a per-sample controller regulates how often, combined with a low-rank drift detector, candidate-restricted re-selection, and a decision that stays on the GPU. With one setting for all models and tasks, RCR outperforms the per-step retrieval method InfiniGen by 1.6-3.8 ROUGE-L points on five of six model-task settings with no significant difference on the sixth, while staying within 1.7 points of exact per-step re-selection, with comparable estimated data movement per step and a 4x smaller GPU-resident detector. On DetailCaps, drift-chosen timing beats random timing at the same refresh rate and periodic timing even at a higher rate, by 2.0-5.0 points. On pre-registered video description, where drift is homogeneous across samples, RCR is on par with InfiniGen on Qwen2-VL and exceeds it on Qwen3-VL but no longer beats a fixed threshold at the nearest tested rates, consistent with the diagnosis; object-level evaluation shows that RCR achieves higher object F1 against the uncompressed output than InfiniGen on two of three models.

</details>


### [172] [Multi-Token Prediction and Alternative Tokenization Improve Learning Latent Multi-Hop Graph Search at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31439)
*Arvid Frydenlund,FuTe Wong,Katherine Lambert,Frank Rudzicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究语言模型在未见图上生成最短路径的能力，发现失败的两个核心原因是监督信号和分词方式，提出多token预测（MTP）和替代分词方法，成功突破了扩展性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作发现语言模型在图规模扩大时无法学习最短路径任务，论文旨在理论和实证上揭示失败根因，并提出通用解决方案以实现大规模稳定泛化。

Method: 1. 理论建立并实验验证多token预测（MTP）的有效性，证明其收益与拓扑结构无关；2. 引入替代分词策略，证明监督信号与分词是正交的两个因素；3. 结合两者突破扩展性墙。

Result: 在远超以往极限的图规模上，模型能一致且稳定地学会最短路径任务，验证了方法的有效性和拓扑无关性。

Conclusion: 通过改进监督信号（MTP）和分词方式，语言模型可在未见大规模图上可靠生成最短路径，解决了长期存在的扩展性难题。

Abstract: Given an unseen graph, a start node, $s$, and an end node, $t$, can a language model generate the shortest path from $s$ to $t$? Prior work has found that models fail to learn this task as the graphs scale. We show that this failure has two causes: the supervision signal and the tokenization. First, we theoretically establish and empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of multi-token prediction (MTP), and show that the benefits of MTP are topologically agnostic. Second, we introduce alternative tokenizations and show that the two causes are orthogonal. Using these methods, we break through the scaling wall observed by prior work, consistently and stably learning to solve the task at scales far beyond previous limits.

</details>


### [173] [RheOPT: Few-Shot Non-Newtonian Flow Prediction via Newtonian Pretraining of RheOFormer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31465)
*Maedeh Saberi,Amir Barati Farimani,Safa Jamali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出RheOPT（流变学算子预训练Transformer），通过在廉价的牛顿流体模拟上预训练神经算子，再在极少量非牛顿流体样本上微调，实现了数据高效的非牛顿流体流动预测。预训练复用了几何条件传输结构而非本构响应，在收缩流道和尾流两个基准测试中，仅用4个目标样本即可匹敌或超越从头训练16个样本的模型，且能恢复涡脱落频率等关键物理特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非牛顿流体模拟因速度与应力需通过率依赖和历史依赖的本构动力学耦合推进，计算成本极高。神经算子代理虽能降低推理成本，但训练数据仍需昂贵模拟生成，导致数据高效学习成为核心瓶颈。现有方法难以在仅有极少非牛顿样本时实现准确预测。

Method: RheOPT采用基于Transformer的神经算子架构。首先在大量廉价的牛顿流体模拟数据上预训练，学习几何条件下的通用传输结构（如速度场的对流扩散模式），而非迁移牛顿本构响应本身。随后在少量（如4个）目标非牛顿流体样本上微调。引入可逆实例归一化（RevIN）处理不同流体速度和应力场的动态范围差异，对比全局归一化的效果。

Result: 在4:1收缩流道中针对Giesekus、Oldroyd-B、Bingham三种流体，预训练显著提升少样本预测精度：微调4样本模型匹敌或超越从头训练16样本模型。RevIN下收益稳健，全局归一化下依赖数据集，归因于各流体场动态范围差异。在三角形障碍物后的Oldroyd-B振荡尾流中，相位不敏感诊断显示预训练恢复了从头训练在少样本下抑制的涡脱落频率和尾流波动振幅。

Conclusion: RheOPT确立了牛顿流体模拟作为非牛顿流体神经算子学习的物理合理预训练源，通过复用几何条件传输结构实现跨本构模型的知识迁移，为数据稀缺场景下的非牛顿流体高保真模拟提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Despite advances in numerical methods, non-Newtonian flow simulations remain computationally expensive because velocity and stress must be advanced together through rate- and history-dependent constitutive dynamics. Neural-operator surrogates reduce inference cost after training, but their training data require the same expensive simulations they aim to replace, making data-efficient learning essential when only a few non-Newtonian simulations can be afforded. Here, we introduce the Rheological Operator Pretrained Transformer (RheOPT), which pretrains a transformer-based neural operator on inexpensive Newtonian simulations and fine-tunes it on a small number of non-Newtonian samples. Rather than transferring the Newtonian constitutive response itself, RheOPT reuses geometry-conditioned transport structure that remains useful after the stress model changes. We evaluate RheOPT in two geometry-matched settings: a $4{:}1$ contraction channel for Giesekus, Oldroyd-B, and Bingham fluids, and the oscillatory wake behind a triangular obstacle for Oldroyd-B flow. In the contraction, Newtonian pretraining substantially improves few-shot prediction, and a model fine-tuned on four target samples can match or exceed a from-scratch model trained on four times as many. This benefit is reliable under reversible instance normalization (RevIN) but dataset dependent under Global normalization, a difference we trace to the dynamic range of each fluid's velocity and stress fields. In the wake, where relative $L_2$ error alone cannot verify that vortex shedding is recovered, phase-insensitive diagnostics show that pretraining recovers the shedding frequency and wake fluctuation amplitude that are suppressed by the from-scratch model in the few-shot regime. Overall, RheOPT establishes Newtonian simulation as a physically grounded pretraining source for data-efficient non-Newtonian operator learning.

</details>


### [174] [Evaluating the Applicability of LLM Inference Optimizations on Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31479)
*Kyrylo Yemets,Andrii Yaroshevych,Victor Muryn,Ostap Khomenko,Pavlo Kryven,Maksym Kmet,Maksym Shamrai,Mariya Hirna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了在Apple Silicon上进行本地LLM推理时，四类常见优化技术（KV缓存剪枝、推测性解码、移植GPU内核、训练后量化）的实际效果，发现这些优化在服务器级NVIDIA GPU上的加速效果并不直接迁移到Apple Silicon，性能增益取决于技术、运行时、硬件契约和工作负载模式的联合作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apple Silicon是广泛使用的本地LLM推理平台，但几乎所有推理优化都是在服务器级NVIDIA GPU上针对批量服务工作负载设计和验证的。需要明确哪些优化在迁移到Apple Silicon时仍能保持收益，以及决定其适用性的因素。

Method: 在四台Apple机器上进行单请求解码工作负载的实验评估，并尽可能与RTX 4090对比。评估四类优化家族：KV缓存剪枝、推测性解码、移植的GPU内核、训练后量化。

Result: KV缓存剪枝在Apple上最高可达1.75倍端到端吞吐，但在桌面级NVIDIA GPU上可能降低吞吐；所有评估的推测性解码方法在Apple上均降低吞吐，尽管在服务器GPU上报告有2-6倍增益；CUDA内核设计仅部分迁移：公开Metal缺乏异步拷贝，单批次解码使多个面向服务的机制不适用，但可迁移组件仍带来高达1.20倍解码吞吐提升；量化收益因机器而异。

Conclusion: 报告的加速比不是技术本身的可移植属性，而是技术、运行时、硬件契约和工作负载模式的联合函数。优化效果高度依赖于硬件平台、软件栈和负载特征的具体组合。

Abstract: Apple Silicon is among the most widely used platforms for local LLM inference, yet nearly all inference optimizations are designed and validated on server-class NVIDIA GPUs under batched serving workloads. Which optimizations retain their benefits when moved to Apple Silicon, and what determines their applicability? We evaluate four optimization families: KV-cache pruning, speculative decoding, ported GPU kernels, and post-training quantization. Experiments span four Apple machines under a common single-request decode workload, with matched RTX 4090 comparisons where possible. KV-cache pruning can reach up to 1.75 times end-to-end throughput on Apple but can reduce throughput on desktop NVIDIA GPUs. Under the common benchmark, all evaluated speculative decoding methods reduce throughput on Apple, despite reported 2-6 times gains on server GPUs. CUDA kernel designs transfer only partially: public Metal lacks asynchronous copy, and batch-one decode makes several serving-oriented mechanisms inapplicable, although transferable components yield up to 1.20 times decode throughput. Quantization gains vary by machine. Across four families, a reported speedup is not a portable property of a technique alone but a joint function of technique, runtime, hardware contract, and workload regime.

</details>


### [175] [Diffusion Language Models for Perplexity-Aware Black-Box Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31558)
*Ali Al-Lawati,Elsayed Eshra,Dongwon Lee,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将连续潜在空间攻击框架与扩散语言模型（dLLM）相结合，替代原本基于随机映射的解码方式，使生成的对抗提示词更自然、更难被检测，并在RareTrap和ThinkTrap两种设置下显著降低了字符级困惑度，同时揭示了不同映射方式下风险估计可相差一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续空间攻击使用未从语言中学习的随机映射将高斯潜变量解码为文本，导致生成的提示词极不自然，易被输入护栏检测，且风险评估不具备自然提示词的代表性。

Method: 将标准高斯潜在空间框架直接耦合到一个学习到的语言生成分布——连续扩散语言模型（dLLM）上，移除人工随机映射，保留标准高斯潜在公式及下游采样/优化流程。在采样基础的RareTrap和优化基础的ThinkTrap两种设置下评估。

Result: 字符级困惑度在RareTrap中从10^3–10^7降至3–5，在ThinkTrap中从20–28降至4.3–5.4。两种映射方式下的模型风险估计相差可达一个数量级。

Conclusion: 引入学习型扩散语言模型作为解码器，能使连续空间攻击生成更自然的对抗提示词，显著提升隐蔽性，并获得更真实的风险评估，揭示了映射选择对风险量化的关键影响。

Abstract: \emph{Continuous-space} attacks find prompts that trigger malicious behaviors, such as overthinking, in black-box LLMs by sampling or optimizing over a low-dimensional Gaussian latent space and decoding it to text through a \emph{random mapping} and nearest-token lookup. Because the random mapping is not learned from language, its prompts can be highly unnatural, and as such easily detectable through input guardrails, and the risk estimates they yield are not grounded in natural prompts. In this paper, we demonstrate that the same continuous latent-space framework can be coupled directly to a learned generative distribution over language, a continuous diffusion language model (dLLM), which removes the artificial random mapping while preserving the standard-Gaussian latent formulation and the downstream sampling or optimization procedure. We evaluate this approach under two settings: sampling-based estimation using \emph{RareTrap} and optimization-based search using \emph{ThinkTrap}. We find that character-level perplexity drops from $10^3$--$10^7$ to 3--5 in RareTrap and from 20--28 to 4.3--5.4 in ThinkTrap. We also find that model risk estimates can differ by an order of magnitude across the two mappings.

</details>


### [176] [Training Small Language Models for Transferable Tool Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31483)
*Zhaoyi Zhou,Akshayaa Magesh,Jeongyeol Kwon,Artem Kuriksha,Daniel Jiang,Andrea Zanette,Ayush Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出通过强化学习训练小型开源模型（Qwen3-8B）来执行工具选择任务，在ToolBench基准上超越了402B参数的Llama-4-Maverick，且模型能直接迁移到不同检索器和新工具领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM Agent面对超万规模工具库时，工具描述无法全放入上下文，基于相关性的检索器只能排序而不知实际所需工具；现有方法依赖Claude/ChatGPT等专有商业模型驱动检索器分解指令并选择候选工具，存在成本高、闭源、难以定制等问题。

Method: 采用强化学习对Qwen3-8B进行后训练，使其学会分解复杂多步用户指令、在检索候选工具中选择所需工具，从而替代专有商业LLM驱动检索器的功能。

Result: 在ToolBench基准上，训练后的Qwen3-8B模型性能超过参数量大50倍的Llama-4-Maverick（402B）；该模型还能直接迁移到不同检索器，并泛化到不同领域的新工具语料库。

Conclusion: 小型开源模型经强化学习训练即可实现高性能工具选择，打破对专有大模型的依赖，展示了开源小模型在复杂工具使用任务中的巨大潜力和迁移能力。

Abstract: As LLM agents operate over corpora of more than ten thousand tools, selecting the right tool for a complex multi-step user instruction becomes a central bottleneck. Tool descriptions cannot all fit in context, and relevance-based retrievers only rank tools without knowing which are actually needed. Recent work instead leverages proprietary commercial LLMs like Claude or ChatGPT to drive a retriever, decomposing the instruction and selecting over retrieved candidates. We show that a small, open model can be trained via reinforcement learning to perform the same functionality. On ToolBench, a Qwen3-8B model post-trained with our recipe outperforms Llama-4-Maverick (402B). In addition, the trained model transfers directly to different retrievers and generalizes to new tool corpora in different domains.

</details>


### [177] [Cost-Aware Abstention for LLM-Based IT Ticket Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31657)
*Sachin Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一种成本感知弃权框架，用于IT支持工单路由：在校准数据上根据显式成本选择路由策略，再在保留测试集上冻结评估。实验对比四种非LLM分类器与四种LLM（零/少样本提示），在公开47,837条、八类工单语料上，TF-IDF+逻辑回归配合0.60阈值将测试成本从0.763降至0.472（相对降低38.1%），错误路由率从15.3%降至3.5%。冻结校准策略在16种成本设置中14种优于全路由/全弃权基线。LLM提示配置在分层1000条测试集上准确率0.477，远低于微调DistilBERT的0.879。等渗回归虽降低ECE但不稳定提升判别力；GPT-4o-mini少样本仅有一个置信值且AUROC=0.5。文中报告正确率AUROC和tie-aware风险覆盖面积。在理论盈亏平衡概率0.80下，七个提示配置全弃权，Haiku少样本仅路由19/1000。包含LLM一致性对照及margin/entropy拒绝基线。结论限定于该数据集、提示设计、模型端点和示例成本；这是评估框架而非新弃权算法或通用部署认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动工单路由错误成本高于人工分拣，需在显式成本下评估何时应弃权（转人工）而非强行路由。现有工作缺乏离线、成本条件化的就绪度评估框架，且LLM与传统分类器在真实成本下的表现对比不足。

Method: 采用离线成本条件化评估框架：在校准集上根据成本模型(c_ok=0, c_triage=1, c_wrong=5)选择阈值/策略，冻结后在保留测试集单次评估。对比四种非LLM分类器（TF-IDF+LR等）与四种LLM（GPT-4o-mini、Haiku等）的零/少样本提示。引入等渗回归校准、正确率AUROC、tie-aware风险覆盖面积等指标。设置LLM一致性对照、margin/entropy拒绝基线。分层抽样1000条测试子集对比微调DistilBERT。事后成本参数分析16种设置。

Result: TF-IDF+LR阈值0.60将测试成本从0.763降至0.472（相对降38.1%，95%CI[35.7%,40.4%]），错误路由率15.3%→3.5%。冻结校准策略在16种成本设置中14种优于全路由/全弃权。LLM最强提示配置准确率0.477，微调DistilBERT达0.879。等渗回归降低ECE但不稳定提升判别力：GPT-4o-mini少样本仅有一个置信值且AUROC=0.5。盈亏平衡概率0.80下，七提示配置全弃权，Haiku少样本仅路由19/1000。

Conclusion: 成本感知弃权框架在该数据集上有效降低路由成本与错误率，传统分类器经校准阈值优于LLM提示方法。等渗校准改善校准度但不保证判别力提升。LLM在高弃权成本下倾向全弃权，实际部署需针对特定数据、提示、模型端点和成本模型重新评估。本工作为评估框架，非通用弃权算法或部署认证。

Abstract: Wrong automatic routing of an IT support ticket can cost more than human triage. We evaluate cost-aware abstention as an offline, cost-conditional readiness assessment: select a routing policy on calibration data under explicit costs, then evaluate that frozen policy once on held-out test data. On a public 47,837-ticket, eight-class corpus, we compare four trained non-LLM classifiers with four large language models under zero- and few-shot prompting. Under the illustrative cost model (c_ok, c_triage, c_wrong) = (0, 1, 5), TF-IDF with logistic regression and a threshold of 0.60 selected on calibration reduces test cost from 0.763 to 0.472, a 38.1% relative reduction (paired ratio-bootstrap 95% CI [35.7%, 40.4%]), while lowering wrong routes from 15.3% to 3.5%. Frozen calibration-selected policies also beat both always-route and always-defer on held-out test data in 14 of 16 settings in a post-hoc cost-parameter analysis. The strongest prompted configuration reaches 0.477 accuracy on a stratified 1,000-ticket test subset, versus 0.879 for fine-tuned DistilBERT on the same tickets. Isotonic lowers expected calibration error without reliably improving discrimination: GPT-4o-mini few-shot has one confidence value before and after recalibration and correctness AUROC 0.500. We therefore report correctness AUROC and tie-aware risk-coverage area alongside ECE. At the analytically derived break-even probability 0.80, seven prompted configurations defer all tickets; Haiku few-shot routes only 19/1,000. An LLM-only agreement control and margin/entropy rejection baselines further separate calibration from discrimination. The findings are scoped to this dataset, prompt design, model endpoints, and illustrative costs; the work is an evaluation framework, not a new abstention algorithm or universal deployment certification.

</details>


### [178] [SABLE: Scalable Affine Bayesian Latent-space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31495)
*Erik Bodin,Lisa Maria Kreusser,Donney Fan,Henry Moss,Geoff Pleiss,Nicolas Durrande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 SABLE，一种适用于高维潜在空间且评估预算有限的贝叶斯优化方法。SABLE 使用贝叶斯仿射代理模型，将查询分解为在观测张成空间内的分量（用期望改进优化）和张成空间外的分量（用标量范数控制），并通过有限时域调度避免欠探索，同时利用重压缩方案将复杂度降至 O(M²D + M³)，在合成基准和潜在空间优化任务上实现了高样本效率和极快的决策速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计、蛋白质等复杂对象的优化面临高维设计空间和昂贵目标评估的挑战。生成模型虽提供连续潜在表示，但潜在空间维度常达数千至数万，远超评估预算，现有方法难以在高维、有限预算下高效优化。

Method: SABLE 采用贝叶斯仿射代理模型进行预测和采集，将高阶结构视为代理差异。在 N 次观测后，斜率更新维度至多 N-1，将每次查询分解为张成空间内分量（在至多 N 维中用期望改进优化）和张成空间外分量（由标量范数控制）。针对仿射 EI 隐式选择的张成空间外范数可能导致欠探索，SABLE 用有限时域调度显式规定该范数，不限制搜索于学习子空间。重压缩方案将信息压缩为 O(M) 个伪观测，将重拟合成本限制在 O(M²D + M³)，使参数推断、预测和采集决策均在数十毫秒内完成。

Result: 在合成基准和潜在空间优化任务中，SABLE 表现出高样本效率，且在大预算下将决策时间降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: SABLE 通过仿射代理分解、有限时域调度和重压缩机制，有效解决了高维潜在空间下预算受限的贝叶斯优化问题，兼顾了样本效率和计算效率。

Abstract: Complex objects such as engineering designs and proteins are often difficult to optimise because their design spaces exhibit high-dimensional structure and objective evaluations are expensive. Generative models provide continuous latent representations, but their latent spaces can have thousands to tens of thousands of dimensions, creating a regime where the latent dimension $D$ greatly exceeds the evaluation budget. We introduce SABLE, a Bayesian optimisation method for high-dimensional latent spaces with an isotropic distribution and limited budget. SABLE uses a Bayesian affine surrogate for prediction and acquisition, treating higher-order structure as surrogate discrepancy. After $N$ observations, the slope update has dimension at most $N-1$, yielding a decomposition of each query into an in-span component optimised by expected improvement (EI) in at most $N$ dimensions and an off-span component governed by a scalar norm. We show that the off-span norm implicitly selected by affine EI can lead to underexploration, and SABLE instead prescribes it using a finite-horizon schedule, without restricting search to a learned subspace. A recompression scheme reduces information to $\mathcal O(M)$ pseudo-observations, bounding refitting cost by $\mathcal O(M^2D+M^3)$ while keeping parameter inference, prediction, and acquisition decisions to tens of milliseconds. Across synthetic benchmarks and latent-space optimisation tasks, SABLE achieves high sample efficiency and reduces decision time by several orders of magnitude at large budgets.

</details>


### [179] [Piano2Pop: Expanding Piano Demos into Pop Productions Without Aligned Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31510)
*Yun-Chen Cheng,Chih-Pin Tan,Tzu-Hung Huang,Yi-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Piano2Pop 任务，将钢琴 MIDI 扩展为流行乐器伴奏，采用伪监督流匹配模型，在无对齐钢琴-流行音乐监督下训练，合成数据训练，对比配对自由与配对监督方法，配对监督效果最佳，FST 缓解钢琴主导输出，所有微调系统显著优于零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决钢琴 MIDI 到流行乐器编曲的自动扩展任务，缺乏成对监督数据，利用现有音乐生成和多音音乐转录进展，构建伪监督训练框架。

Method: 基于预训练流匹配模型，条件化钢琴卷帘表示；从流行录音合成钢琴-器乐对；对比配对自由转录（含功能选择性 FST 和寄存器密度 RDA 增强）与配对监督钢琴翻弹生成器作为伪演示源；在 476 真实钢琴翻弹/流行对上评估。

Result: 配对监督方法在真实钢琴输入上取得最佳 Frechet Audio Distance、伴奏提示遵循度和频谱形状；FST 缓解配对自由训练中的钢琴主导输出，仍具潜力；所有微调系统显著优于零样本基线。

Conclusion: 任务特定建模对钢琴到流行音乐扩展有效；伪监督流匹配结合合成数据可缓解数据稀缺；配对监督优于配对自由，但 FST 为有前景的替代方向。

Abstract: We introduce Piano2Pop, a music-expansion task that transforms piano MIDI into instrumental pop productions. Enabled by recent advances in music generation and polyphonic music transcription, our pseudo-supervised approach conditions a pretrained flow-matching model on piano rolls without aligned piano-pop supervision. We train on synthetic piano-instrumental pairs derived from pop recordings, comparing pair-free transcription, including function-selective (FST) and register-density (RDA) augmentations, with a pair-supervised piano-cover generator as pseudo-demo sources. On 476 testing real-world piano-cover/pop pairs, the pair-supervised approach achieves the best Frechet Audio Distance, Accompaniment Prompt Adherence, and spectral shape on real-piano inputs, while FST mitigates piano-heavy outputs in pair-free training and remains a promising alternative. All fine-tuned systems significantly outperform the zero-shot baselines, which suggests a role for task-specific modeling.

</details>


### [180] [PYTHIA, a transferable machine learning framework for simultaneous defect and adsorption site optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31539)
*Ioannis Kouroudis,Zhaosu Gu,Poonam,Leon Muller,Alessio Gagliardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PYTHIA框架，结合基础模型与迁移学习，仅需少量数据即可实现对新型和研究不足材料的高效优化，并结合贝叶斯优化用于吸附位点和缺陷优化任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在材料优化中虽具高价值，但严重依赖大量样本，限制了其在探索较少材料家族中的应用。需开发能在小样本条件下利用已有化学知识的方法。

Method: PYTHIA框架：1) 利用在化学相似分子大数据集上预训练的复杂基础模型（目标物理接近预测目标）；2) 冻结权重，通过Transformer层进行子空间对齐；3) 引入高斯过程进行不确定性量化；4) 仅需少量数据训练后两层即可迁移知识；5) 将最终模型作为代理用于贝叶斯优化。

Result: 在两个不同体系的吸附与缺陷优化任务中验证了框架的鲁棒性，能正确且高效地识别甚至复杂系统的最优吸附位点。

Conclusion: PYTHIA通过基础模型迁移学习和不确定性量化，成功将机器学习扩展到数据稀缺的新型材料优化，为小样本材料发现提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Machine learning has permeated material optimization, providing high value. Nevertheless, its re- liance on a high number of samples limits its applicability to relatively explored material families. In this paper we extend the applicability of machine learning to novel and understudied materials through the PYTHIA (Probabilistic Yet Transferable Hierarchical Interaction Architecture) framework. Pythia utilizes two of the most powerful machine learning concepts, foundational models and transfer learning. We employ a complex foundational model trained on a large dataset of chemically similar molecules, on a target that is physically close to our prediction target. Then, we freeze the weights and perform subspace alignment with the use of a transformer layer and add uncertainty quantification with gaussian processes. These two layers can be trained on a limited dataset yet allow us to leverage the gained knowl- edge and the studied materials and complex architectures. The resulting model is used as a surrogate for a Bayesian Optimization scheme. To showcase the robustness of the framework, we applied it to the investigation of adsorption and defect optimization of two distinct systems. Not only were we able to correctly and efficiently identify the optimal adsorption sites of even complex a

</details>


### [181] [Same Luck, Different Choice: Twin-World Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31544)
*Zeropy Surio,Tobias Engel,Mei-Lin Chou,Anika Raman,Samuel Okafor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TWICE (TWIn-world Credit Estimation) 方法，解决智能体强化学习中长回合的信用分配问题。通过利用检索器、语料库和评分器的确定性特性，构建共享下游采样噪声的孪生世界反事实轨迹，将配对结果差异转化为逐轮优势估计，显著降低方差并减少所需采样次数。实验在七个问答基准上显著提升 GRPO 性能，且辅助 token 开销大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体强化学习仅对完整回合给予奖励，但策略需要为每个决步分配信用。现有反事实分支方法独立重采样下游噪声，导致结果差异混淆了决策效应与后续运气。作者观察到智能体搜索中的检索器、语料库和评分器均为确定性组件，因此回合轨迹是策略决策与采样噪声的确定性函数，使得构建共享噪声的孪生世界成为可能。

Method: TWICE 将每个 token 的 Gumbel 噪声按轮次和行号键控，在生成反事实孪生轨迹时继承事实轨迹的下游噪声。通过配对的孪生世界结果差异计算逐轮优势，用于 Actor 采样轨迹的策略梯度更新；仅 Actor 被部署。理论上证明孪生信用无偏，方差为独立反事实的 (1-ρ) 倍（ρ 为孪生结果相关性），解析优势大小 Δ 所需采样数从 Θ(Δ⁻²) 降至 Θ(Δ⁻¹)；并提出平方根规则按预测分歧程度分配孪生预算。

Result: 在七个问答基准上，TWICE 使 GRPO 平均精确匹配分别在 Qwen2.5-3B 和 7B 上提升 5.3 和 4.5 个百分点，优于树搜索和反事实分支基线，且比 VinePPO 减少 68% 的辅助 token 开销。

Conclusion: TWICE 通过确定性环境构建共享噪声的孪生世界，实现了低方差、无偏的逐步信用分配，在理论样本复杂度和实际性能上均显著优于现有方法，为长回合智能体 RL 提供了高效的信用分配范式。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning rewards a complete episode, yet the policy needs credit for every decision inside it. Counterfactual branching estimates this credit by re-running the episode after an alternative decision, but the branch draws its sampling noise independently of the factual episode, so the outcome difference mixes the effect of the decision with the luck of everything that follows. We observe that in agentic search the retriever, the corpus, and the grader are deterministic, so an episode is a deterministic function of the policy's decisions and its sampling noise. Twin worlds are therefore realizable: the same noise, a different decision. We introduce TWICE (TWIn-world Credit Estimation), which keys the Gumbel noise of every token by turn and line, rolls out counterfactual twins that inherit the factual episode's downstream noise, and turns their paired outcome differences into turn-level advantages for policy-gradient updates on actor-sampled trajectories; only the actor is deployed. Twin credit is unbiased for any coupling quality, its variance is that of an independent counterfactual scaled by $1-ρ$, where $ρ$ is the twins' outcome correlation, and under coupling the number of rollouts needed to resolve an advantage of size $Δ$ falls from $Θ(Δ^{-2})$ to $Θ(Δ^{-1})$. A square-root rule spends the twin budget where outcomes are predicted to disagree per unit cost. On seven question-answering benchmarks, TWICE improves the average exact match of GRPO by 5.3 and 4.5 points on Qwen2.5-3B and 7B, outperforms tree-search and counterfactual-branching baselines, and uses 68% fewer auxiliary tokens than VinePPO.

</details>


### [182] [Shift-aware score transport for privacy auditing without retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31549)
*Yaohong Yang,Gauri Pradhan,Qi Chen,Samuel Kaski,Antti Honkela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在观测性审计设置下，利用分布外非成员数据估计反事实分布内非成员得分分布的方法，并引入保守校正以缓解迁移误差，从而在分布偏移下获得更稳定且更紧的隐私损失下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在观测性隐私审计中，审计员无法插入金丝雀、控制数据纳入或重新训练模型，只能依赖固定的已知成员和非成员集合。由于可用的非成员数据可能与训练数据分布不同，导致成员推理得分因分布偏移而失真，需要解决这一问题以获得可靠的隐私损失下界。

Method: 从分布外非成员学习数据表示与成员推理得分的关系，并将其迁移至目标协变量分布以估计反事实分布内非成员得分分布。针对迁移可能因外推或条件不变性不完美而不可靠的问题，构建受控校准任务，利用可用非成员数据估计表示空间位移对审计的膨胀效应，并据此引入保守校正以修正迁移引入的审计误差。

Result: 在多个存在分布偏移的数据集上，该方法产生的隐私估计显著更稳定，且比基线方法保留了更紧的隐私损失下界。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了观测性隐私审计中非成员数据分布偏移导致的得分偏差问题，通过反事实估计和保守校正，在保持紧下界的同时提高了估计的稳定性，适用于大规模部署模型或受计算/访问限制无法重训练的场景。

Abstract: Privacy auditing empirically assesses privacy leakage by using membership inference attacks to establish lower bounds on privacy loss. We consider an observational auditing setting in which the auditor cannot insert canaries, control data inclusion, or retrain the target model, and must instead rely on fixed sets of known members and non-members. This setting is relevant for large deployed models or when retraining is restricted by computational or access constraints. In this case, non-members available for auditing may differ in distribution from the training data, causing membership-inference scores to be skewed due to the distribution shift. We address this problem by estimating the counterfactual in-distribution non-member score distribution using the available out-of-distribution (OOD) non-members. Our proposed method learns the relationship between data representations and membership-inference scores from OOD non-members and transports it to the target covariate distribution. Because this transport may become unreliable under extrapolation or imperfect conditional invariance, we further introduce a conservative correction calibrated directly to the nominal transported privacy level. We construct controlled calibration tasks from the available non-member data to estimate how representation-space displacement inflates the audit, and use this estimate to correct the transport-induced audit error. Across multiple datasets with distribution shifts, our method yields substantially more stable privacy estimates while retaining tighter lower bounds than baselines.

</details>


### [183] [Fine-Grained Analysis of Catastrophic Forgetting in Continual Learning via Algorithmic Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31551)
*Guodong Zheng,Yan Sun,Peng Wang,Enneng Yang,Jiafei Lyu,Xiaochun Cao,Dacheng Tao,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于算法稳定性的理论框架，用于分析随机梯度下降（SGD）在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，无需预设特定模型架构。将遗忘分解为任务内泛化间隙、跨任务泛化间隙和经验遗忘，分析了三种学习率调度策略（常数、任务内衰减、持续衰减）对遗忘的影响，推导出包含训练集大小、每任务更新次数、总任务数、学习率调度和累积梯度对齐的遗忘上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于灾难性遗忘的理论分析往往依赖特定的模型或任务结构，难以揭示迭代优化过程如何影响遗忘。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立一个不依赖特定架构的通用理论框架，从算法稳定性角度理解SGD在持续学习中的预期总体遗忘行为。

Method: 1. 将预期遗忘分解为任务内泛化间隙、跨任务泛化间隙和经验遗忘三部分。2. 通过追踪样本扰动在任务内的更新影响及其跨任务传播来界定泛化间隙。3. 分析三种学习率调度：常数、任务内衰减、持续衰减。4. 对比两种衰减调度，刻画在任务边界重启学习率衰减如何影响历史样本扰动的传播。5. 利用优化轨迹上的累积梯度对齐界定经验遗忘，无需凸性假设。

Result: 推导出预期遗忘的上界，显式捕捉了训练集大小、每任务更新次数、总任务数、学习率调度策略和累积梯度对齐等因素的影响。实验在Split CIFAR-100和Split MiniImageNet上验证了理论发现的实证意义。

Conclusion: 该理论框架为理解持续学习中的灾难性遗忘提供了统一视角，揭示了学习率调度策略（特别是任务边界是否重启衰减）通过控制历史扰动传播来影响遗忘的机制，且累积梯度对齐是界定经验遗忘的关键因素。

Abstract: Understanding catastrophic forgetting, in which learning new tasks degrades performance on previously learned tasks, remains a fundamental challenge in continual learning (CL). Existing theoretical analyses often rely on specific model or task structures and offer limited insight into how iterative optimization affects forgetting. To address this gap, we develop an algorithmic stability framework for expected population forgetting under SGD without prescribing a particular model architecture. Specifically, we decompose forgetting into intra-task and cross-task generalization gaps and empirical forgetting. To bound the generalization gaps, we track how a sample perturbation affects updates within a task and propagates through subsequent tasks. We consider constant, intra-task decaying, and continuously decaying learning-rate schedules. Comparing the two decaying schedules allows us to characterize how restarting learning-rate decay at task boundaries affects the propagation of historical sample perturbations. For empirical forgetting, we derive bounds for all three schedules by retaining cumulative gradient alignment along the optimization trajectory, without requiring convexity. Combining these results yields upper bounds on expected forgetting that capture the effects of training set size, the number of updates per task, the total number of tasks, learning-rate schedules, and cumulative gradient alignment. Finally, we conduct experiments on Split CIFAR-100 and Split MiniImageNet to examine the empirical implications of our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [184] [When to Follow the Teacher: Advantage-Based On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31563)
*Mahdi Sabbaghi,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani,Adel Javanmard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Advantage-Based On-Policy Distillation (AOPD)，通过基于优势的加权方式利用教师动作，仅在教师动作能提升学生预期结果时进行强化，避免了标准 OPD 盲目跟随教师的问题，且无需额外 rollout 即可无偏估计优势，实验在数学、编码、MedQA 任务上优于蒸馏和 RL 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线策略蒸馏（OPD）假设教师指导总是有益的，强制学生在每个状态跟随教师动作，但教师偏好不一定适合当前学生，可能引入有害监督。

Method: AOPD 引入反事实优势加权教师动作：计算教师动作在学生策略下的优势，仅强化能提升学生预期回报的动作。提出无偏估计器，利用教师重要性权重对学生采样动作进行加权，仅需标准学生 rollout 和终端奖励，无需额外 rollout 或奖励评估。

Result: 在数学、编码、MedQA 任务上，AOPD 在外部教师蒸馏和自我蒸馏设置下均优于蒸馏和强化学习基线。

Conclusion: AOPD 通过优势加权机制实现了更高效的在线策略蒸馏，在保持 rollout 效率的同时聚焦于对当前学生有帮助的教师监督，显著提升了蒸馏性能。

Abstract: Online Policy Distillation (OPD) often outperforms offline methods such as Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) by training on trajectories sampled directly from the student. However, standard OPD trains the student to follow the teacher at every state of the trajectory, assuming that teacher guidance is always beneficial. We instead distinguish between what the teacher prefers and what actually helps the current student succeed. We introduce Advantage-Based On-Policy Distillation (AOPD), which weights teacher actions by their counterfactual advantage under the student. Teacher actions that improve the student's expected outcome are reinforced, while those that do not are downweighted.
  Directly computing these advantages requires trying alternative teacher actions at each step and running new rollouts from each. Instead, we show that the AOPD objective can be estimated using only standard student rollouts and their terminal rewards. Our estimator uses teacher importance weights on the student-sampled actions to provide unbiased advantage-weighted supervision, without any additional rollouts or reward evaluations. This allows AOPD to focus teacher supervision on actions that help the current student while preserving the rollout efficiency of standard OPD. Experiments on math, coding, and Medqa show gains over distillation and RL baselines in both external-teacher and self-distillation settings.

</details>


### [185] [On the Token Collapse Phenomenon in Textual Graph Soft Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31572)
*Andrea Giuseppe Di Francesco,Rishabh Jain,Franco Maria Nardini,Nicola Tonellotto,Pietro Liò,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现 G-Retriever 等方法在将图结构注入 LLM 时存在“token collapse”现象：图 tokens 在不同输入间变得几乎不可区分，本质上退化为通用 soft prompt，而非编码图内容。实验表明，替换 token 甚至使用恒定 prompt 不影响准确率；提出两阶段方法缓解 collapse，但需通过干预和解码探针验证 token 是否真正被 LLM 利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图-LLM 方法（如 G-Retriever）声称通过 GNN 学习图特定 tokens 来捕捉实体间关系，但缺乏对 token 实际行为的深入分析。作者旨在揭示这些 tokens 是否真正编码图结构信息，还是仅作为通用提示起作用。

Method: 在三个基准、三种图编码器、两个 LLM 家族及冻结/LoRA 微调设置下系统评估 G-Retriever；通过 token 互换、损坏图、恒定 prompt 对照实验量化 collapse 程度；提出两阶段训练（先对比学习分离 token，再联合微调）获取非崩塌 token；引入干预实验和解码探针区分 token 反映的是问题、答案先验还是图信息。

Result: 跨所有配置观测到严重 token collapse：用数据集均值、他例 token 或损坏图 token 替换原 token，答案级准确率无显著变化；恒定 prompt 性能持平或超越 G-Retriever。两阶段方法能生成可变 token 且在多数情况下保持竞争力，但 token 可变性不等于图信息被利用：token 可能编码问题、答案先验或图内容，且 LLM 未必使用图信息。

Conclusion: 任务准确率不足以证明 graph-token 对齐；需通过 token 变异性分析、干预实验和解码探针直接评估 token 内容与 LLM 使用情况。提出的两阶段训练是缓解 collapse 的实用方案，但真正的图推理仍需显式验证 token 语义与模型行为的一致性。

Abstract: Recent methods for injecting graph structure into LLM prompts include graph soft prompting, in which a graph neural network (GNN) learns graph-specific tokens that capture relational information among entities in textual prompts. G-Retriever combines graph soft prompts with a verbalized description of the same graph to strengthen the textual prompt signal. We conduct an in-depth analysis of G-Retriever and uncover a pathological behavior we term token collapse. Across three benchmarks, three graph encoders, two LLM families, and both frozen and LoRA-adapted models, G-Retriever's graph tokens become almost indistinguishable across inputs. We provide evidence that the same failure is inherited by subsequent methods, even with multi-token pooling. Under the standard setup, swapping a token with the dataset mean, a token from another example, or one derived from a corrupted graph produces no resolved answer-level effect in accuracy. Moreover, a learned constant prompt that never accesses the graph matches or surpasses G-Retriever on all three benchmarks. These findings indicate that, rather than conveying graph content as intended, the collapsed token functions primarily as a generic soft prompt. We also examine conditions linked to this collapse and propose a two-stage procedure that yields non-collapsed tokens while maintaining competitive performance in most cases. However, token variability alone does not prove that the model uses graph information: via interventions and decoding probes, we show that depending on the configuration, a token can reflect the question, an answer prior, or graph-derived content that the LLM may or may not use. Overall, our results offer a practical approach to evaluating graph-token alignment rather than inferring it from task accuracy alone. Code to reproduce our experiments is available at https://github.com/difra100/Token_Collapse_KGQA.

</details>


### [186] [Rethinking Sequence Compression with Test-time Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31574)
*Sihan Yang,Jie Huang,Hanxuan Li,Yifan Guo,Weigao Sun,Yiran Zhong,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TTR-Compressor，一种基于测试时回归的序列压缩方法，将输入 token 视为回归问题，通过闭式解计算拟合系数作为压缩表示，在稀疏注意力和多模态视觉 token 合并任务上显著优于池化和投影等传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列压缩是高效语言模型和多模态模型中的基础操作和关键信息瓶颈，但基于池化或学习投影的主流方法性能不足。

Method: 从测试时回归视角重新审视序列压缩：输入 token 定义一个回归问题，其拟合系数构成压缩表示。回归问题通过反向传播端到端学习，而系数在前向传播中以闭式解高效计算，形成 TTR-Compressor 作为传统池化和投影压缩的通用替代方案。

Result: 在分块稀疏注意力任务中，TTR-Compressor 在 RULER 基准上大幅超越基于注意力的压缩器；在视觉 token 合并任务中，持续优于标准 MLP 合并器，且仅用 44.4% 的视觉 token 即可达到同等性能。

Conclusion: 测试时回归被确立为高效语言模型和多模态模型中序列压缩的有效基元。

Abstract: Sequence compression is a fundamental operation and a critical information bottleneck in efficient language and multimodal models, and yet prevailing approaches based on pooling or learned projections remain underperforming. We revisit sequence compression through the lens of test-time regression: input tokens define a regression problem whose fitted coefficients form the compressed representation. Importantly, the regression problem is learned end-to-end via backpropagation, while its coefficients are computed efficiently in closed form during the forward pass. This leads to TTR-Compressor, a general alternative to conventional pooling and projection-based compression. We evaluate TTR-Compressor as the compressor for block-wise sparse attention and the visual token merger for multimodal large language models. On block-wise sparse attention, TTR-Compressor improves RULER performance over attention-based compressors by a large margin. For visual token merging, it consistently outperforms the standard MLP merger and matches its performance with 55.6% fewer visual tokens. These results establish test-time regression as an effective primitive for sequence compression in efficient language and multimodal models.

</details>


### [187] [Action Learning for Scientific Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31585)
*Sarah Liaw,Yuanqi Du,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LEAP（Learned Energy on Action Paths），一种动作学习框架，通过将未知动作锚定到可处理的参考过程并学习修正项，从轨迹观测中恢复随机动作，得到一个在路径空间上可微的目标函数，可同时捕捉局部动力学结构和非加性的跨时间依赖，并支持路径排序、推理、机制引导、约束优化和采样等多种下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
