Motivation: 现有方法要么学习局部动力学（基于单步转移的目标会丢失跨时间依赖），要么使用生成模型（难以提供对任意路径进行评估和优化的可处理目标）。需要一种能从轨迹观测中恢复可复用的随机动作目标的统一框架。

Method: LEAP 将未知动作分解为可处理的参考过程与可学习的修正项。通过对比观测轨迹与参考轨迹，学习修正项（能量函数），从而在路径空间上构建可微目标。该目标同时编码局部动力学结构和非加性的长程时间依赖。

Result: 在随机物理系统、多体轨道动力学和分子模拟等任务上，LEAP 能准确恢复底层动力学、捕捉跨时间依赖，并能在不同推理目标和物理条件间迁移复用。

Conclusion: 动作学习是一个将轨迹观测转化为可复用的科学推理与控制目标的有前景框架。

Abstract: Understanding and controlling how systems evolve is central to science and engineering, where many problems require reasoning about complete trajectories. Existing approaches typically learn local dynamics or generate trajectories, but objectives constructed from individual transitions can miss dependencies across time, while generative models do not necessarily provide a tractable objective for evaluating and optimizing arbitrary paths. We study $\textit{action learning}$, the problem of recovering a stochastic action from trajectory observations, yielding a common objective for reasoning about dynamical systems. We introduce $\textbf{LEAP}$ ($\textbf{L}$earned $\textbf{E}$nergy on $\textbf{A}$ction $\textbf{P}$aths), which anchors the unknown action to a tractable reference process and learns the remaining correction by comparing observed trajectories with reference trajectories. This construction yields a differentiable objective on path space that can capture both local dynamical structure and nonadditive dependencies across time. Once learned, the same action can be flexibly reused for path ranking and inference, mechanism steering, constrained optimization and sampling. Across stochastic physical systems, many-body orbital dynamics and molecular simulations, LEAP recovers underlying dynamics, captures dependencies across time and enables transfer across inference objectives and physical conditions. We show that $\textit{action learning}$ is a promising framework for turning trajectory observations into reusable objectives for scientific inference and control.

</details>


### [188] [CRANE: Post-Hoc Counterfactual Retrieval for Multimodal Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31594)
*Franco Rugolon,Ioanna Miliou,Panagiotis Papapetrou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRANE是首个面向多模态分类器的事后模型无关反事实解释框架，通过多模态共识检索（MC-R）在联合训练分布中检索真实训练样本作为解释，确保合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单模态反事实方法无法直接迁移到多模态分类器；现有多模态解释方法仅独立对各模态做归因，无法产生连贯解释，且不适用于任意预训练多模态架构。

Method: 提出CRANE框架，采用最近邻检索策略，从目标类别中检索真实训练样本作为候选解释；核心策略为多模态共识检索（MC-R），通过求各模态最近邻集合的交集，识别在所有模态上均与查询样本接近的实例；支持任意融合策略，模型无关。

Result: 在四个异构数据集和三种融合策略上实验，MC-R在三个数据集上达到最优或并列最优，所有解释均植根于联合训练分布；单模态策略在单一模态主导决策边界时仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: CRANE填补了多模态分类器事后反事实解释的空白，MC-R通过多模态共识机制提供合理且连贯的解释，为多模态模型可解释性提供了通用、即插即用的解决方案。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanation methods have been introduced for unimodal settings, with dedicated frameworks for tabular, timeseries, text, and image data, yet no post-hoc method has been designed for classifiers that learn from multimodal inputs. Existing explainability methods for multimodal classifiers apply modality-specific attribution independently to each input, which fails to yield coherent explanations and does not transfer to arbitrary pre-trained multimodal architectures. To address these shortcomings, we present CRANE (Counterfactual Retrieval via Agnostic Nearest-neighbour Explanations), the first post-hoc, model-agnostic framework for nearest-neighbour multimodal counterfactual generation that retrieves real training examples from the target class as candidate explanations, ensuring plausibility by construction. Across four heterogeneous datasets and three fusion strategies, we show that Multimodal Consensus Retrieval (MC-R), which intersects per-modality neighbour sets to identify instances jointly close to the query in every modality, emerges as the strongest multimodal strategy, achieving the best or joint-best outcome on three of four datasets while grounding every explanation in the joint training distribution. Unimodal strategies remain competitive when a single modality dominates the decision boundary.

</details>


### [189] [Efficient and Optimal Learning of Discrete Distributions with Person-Level Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31612)
*Gautam Kamath,Mahbod Majid,Ankur Moitra,Argyris Mouzakis,Jonathan Ullman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究在批次设置下的人级差分隐私离散分布学习问题，提出了首个多项式时间纯DP算法，其样本复杂度匹配信息论最优界（对数因子内）；同时给出了近似DP算法，并在δ=2^{-Θ(k)}时实现Õ(k)样本复杂度。核心技术是基于度-6和平方（SOS）估计器的鲁棒均值估计，利用迹有界对角主导矩阵和Grothendieck不等式的对称形式证明了纯净数据的证书，保留了多项式批次均值的均值依赖方差，实现近似最优鲁棒率。此外证明了统计查询下界，排除了固定多项式SQ资源下一致更小的误差阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在差分隐私框架下学习离散分布是基础问题。现有工作多针对单样本每用户设置，而批次设置（每用户贡献m个i.i.d.样本）更具实用性，但纯DP下多项式时间算法的最优样本复杂度尚未解决。论文旨在填补这一空白，实现同时满足纯DP/近似DP、多项式时间、最优样本复杂度的算法。

Method: 提出基于度-6和平方（SOS）规划的鲁棒均值估计器，用于从不可信批次中学习。关键技术：构造迹有界对角主导矩阵作为经验协方差与多项式预测协方差差异的主导量；通过半定规划对偶性和Friedland-Lim的Grothendieck不等式强对称形式，证明纯净数据满足该证书。该证书保留了批次均值的均值依赖方差，产生近似最优鲁棒率O(η√(log(1/η)/m))，并为隐私归约提供所需的评分函数。

Result: 纯(ε,0)-DP下：n = Õ(k/(α²m) + k/(α√m ε) + k/ε)，首个匹配信息论最优界的多项式时间纯DP算法。近似(ε,δ)-DP下：n = Õ(k/(α²m) + k/(α√m ε) + k + log(1/δ)/ε)；当δ=2^{-Θ(k)}时，固定α,ε,m下样本复杂度为Õ(k)。同时证明统计查询下界，排除固定多项式SQ资源下一致更小的误差阶。

Conclusion: 论文解决了批次设置下离散分布学习在纯DP和近似DP下的最优样本复杂度问题，引入了新的SOS鲁棒估计技术，结合Grothendieck不等式的对称形式实现了方差感知的证书，达到了近似最优的鲁棒率并支撑了隐私归约。SQ下界表明该结果在统计查询模型下近似最优。

Abstract: We study learning a discrete distribution under person-level differential privacy in the batched setting. Given $n$ users, each contributing $m$ i.i.d. samples from an unknown distribution $μ$ over $[k]$, we seek an estimate of $μ$ with total-variation error $α$. We give polynomial-time algorithms under both pure and approximate differential privacy. Under pure $(\varepsilon,0)$-DP, we achieve $n = \widetilde O(k/(α^2 m)+k/(α\sqrt{m}\varepsilon)+k/\varepsilon)$, the first polynomial-time pure-DP guarantee matching the information-theoretic optimum up to logarithmic factors. Under approximate $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP, we achieve $n = \widetilde O(k/(α^2 m)+k/(α\sqrt{m}\varepsilon)+k+\log(1/δ)/\varepsilon)$. At $δ=2^{-Θ(k)}$, this is $\widetilde O(k)$ for fixed $α,\varepsilon,m$ and failure probability. Our algorithms use a new degree-6 sum-of-squares estimator for learning from untrusted batches. Its key ingredient is a trace-bounded diagonal majorizer for the difference between the empirical covariance of the repaired batch averages and the multinomial covariance predicted by their mean. We prove that clean data admit this certificate through semidefinite-programming duality and a new application of the stronger symmetric form of Grothendieck's inequality due to Friedland and Lim. The certificate preserves the mean-dependent variance of multinomial batch averages, yielding the near-optimal robust rate $O(η\sqrt{\log(1/η)/m})$ and providing the score required by the privacy reduction. We also prove a statistical query lower bound ruling out a uniformly smaller order of error under fixed polynomial SQ resource bounds.

</details>


### [190] [OP-Print: Tracing Membership Imprints in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31616)
*Qi Li,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了在线策略自蒸馏下的成员推理攻击，提出统一攻击框架 OP-Print。发现监督印记稀疏且结构化，集中在不确定性敏感的修正点。灰盒设置下直接聚合教师-学生残差修正，黑盒设置下利用重启输出盆地稳定性作为代理信号。实验表明基线攻击基本处于随机猜测水平，而 OP-Print 在灰盒和黑盒均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略自蒸馏通过使用学生自身输出（经额外上下文或演示增强）作为监督，移除了对外部教师的依赖，使在线策略蒸馏更实用。但其隐私风险鲜有探索：同一训练样本的两个耦合视图可能强化记忆，带来成员推理攻击风险。

Method: 提出统一攻击框架 OP-Print，针对灰盒和黑盒两种威胁模型：1) 灰盒：直接聚合同一轨迹上教师-学生残差修正信号；2) 黑盒：测量基于重启的输出盆地稳定性，作为隐藏修正过程的可观测代理。核心发现是监督印记稀疏且结构化，集中在不确定性敏感的修正点而非整个轨迹。

Result: 在多种模型和蒸馏方案上，所有基线攻击即使在灰盒访问下也基本维持随机猜测水平；OP-Print 在灰盒和黑盒设置下均被广泛验证有效。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了在线策略自蒸馏存在显著的成员推理隐私风险，OP-Print 攻击框架在两种访问级别下均表现出强有效性，呼吁在部署此类技术时需考虑隐私防护。

Abstract: On-policy distillation has been widely adopted for alleviating the distribution mismatch of off-policy distillation. Self-distillation further removes the need for an external teacher by using the student's own outputs, enhanced with extra context or demonstrations, as supervision. This teacher-free design makes on-policy self-distillation practical, yet its privacy risks remain largely unexplored. In particular, using two coupled views of the same training example may strengthen its memorization. Motivated by this, we present the first study of membership inference attacks against on-policy self-distillation. We investigate both gray-box and black-box threat models and propose a unified attack framework, named OP-Print. OP-Print builds on our finding that the supervision imprint in on-policy self-distillation is sparse and structured, concentrating on uncertainty-sensitive correction sites rather than across the entire rollout. Exploiting this property, we construct attack signals tailored to the two access levels: in the gray-box setting, OP-Print directly aggregates the privileged teacher-student residual correction along the same rollout; in the black-box setting, it measures restart-based output-basin stability as an observable surrogate for the hidden correction process. Across diverse models and distillation schemes, all baseline attacks remain essentially at random guessing even under gray-box access, whereas OP-Print is broadly validated to be effective in both gray-box and black-box settings.

</details>


### [191] [Questioning Instrumental Convergence in RLVR: Checkpoint Evidence from OLMo-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31624)
*Barış Alparslan,Sandy Tanwisuth,Caleb Petterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）是否会增加大语言模型中的工具性收敛行为。通过在OLMo-3-7B-Think模型上跨SFT、DPO、13个中间RLVR检查点及最终RLVR模型进行InstrumentalEval评估，发现工具性行为率呈非单调轨迹：从SFT的25.0%上升至RLVR步骤200附近的46.1%，随后下降至最终检查点的11.8%。配对项目级测试和逐步回归支持SFT到最终RLVR的下降。提示框架效应仅解释约15%的检查点内方差，INLP移除框架方向后不改变SFT到最终RLVR的差距。初步32B结果显示类似趋势（35.53% → 22.37%）。结论：RLVR在此设置下不单调增加工具性行为，而是早期上升后降至SFT基线以下。结果特定于该模型家族和基准，评判者一致性中等，绝对比率需谨慎解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具性收敛理论认为强智能体倾向于发展求权子目标（自我保存、资源获取、欺骗、抗拒监管），对社会构成威胁。对LLM而言，强化学习是后训练中首个直接针对目标优化行为的阶段，因此按工具性收敛理由，该阶段最可能出现收敛工具性行为。核心问题是：RLVR是否使模型更倾向表达工具性收敛行为，还是说这取决于训练机制、评估框架和后训练阶段？

Method: 采用检查点条件行为轨迹法而非单一前后安全分数。使用InstrumentalEval基准评估OLMo-3-7B-Think在SFT、DPO、13个中间RLVR检查点及最终RLVR模型上的表现。使用配对项目级测试和逐步回归验证SFT到最终RLVR的变化。引入有害提示指示器结合其他框架效应分析提示框架影响，并应用迭代零空间投影（INLP）移除可检测的框架方向。同时报告32B模型的初步端点结果。

Result: 工具性行为率呈非单调轨迹：SFT 25.0% → RLVR步骤200附近峰值46.1% → 最终检查点11.8%。配对项目级测试和逐步回归均支持SFT到最终RLVR的显著下降。提示框架效应（有害提示指示器+其他框架）平均仅解释15%的检查点内分数方差；INLP移除框架方向后SFT到最终RLVR的差距保持不变。32B模型初步结果显示一致趋势：SFT 35.53% → 最终RLVR 22.37%。

Conclusion: 在该模型家族和基准设置下，RLVR不会单调增加工具性收敛行为：行为率早期上升后下降至SFT基线以下。结果具有模型家族和基准特异性，评判者一致性中等，因此绝对比率应谨慎解读。

Abstract: Instrumental convergence is often invoked as a general reason to expect capable agents to acquire or express power-seeking subgoals: self-preservation, resource acquisition, deception, or resistance to oversight which poses an important threat to the society. For LLMs, reinforcement learning is the first stage of post-training that directly optimizes behavior against an objective; therefore, it is the stage where convergent instrumental behavior, on IC grounds, likely to appear. Does reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) make a model more likely to express instrumental-convergence behavior, or does this depend on the training regime, evaluation frame, and stage of post-training? We study this question as a checkpoint-conditioned behavioral trajectory rather than as a single before/after safety score. Using InstrumentalEval, we evaluate OLMo-3-7B-Think across SFT, DPO, 13 intermediate RLVR checkpoints, and the final RLVR model. The trajectory is nonmonotonic: instrumental rate rises from 25.0% at SFT to 46.1% near RLVR step 200, then falls to 11.8% at the final checkpoint. Paired item-level tests and step-wise regression support the final SFT-to-RLVR reduction. Prompt framing does not explain this pattern: a harmful-prompt indicator combined with other framing effects explains only 15% of within-checkpoint score variance on average, and Iterative Null-space Projection (INLP) removes a detectable framing direction without changing the SFT-to-final-RLVR gap. Together with preliminary 32B endpoint results (35.53% SFT to 22.37% RLVR final), these findings indicate that RLVR does not monotonically increase instrumental behavior in this setting: the rate rises early, then falls below the SFT baseline. The result is specific to this model family and benchmark, and judge agreement is moderate, so absolute rates should be read cautiously.

</details>


### [192] [BeNice! Closing Looping's Inference-Memory Gap via KV-Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31633)
*Hayden Prairie,Jacob M. Ryan,Taylor Berg-Kirkpatrick,Daniel Y. Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种两阶段方法（预训练随机深度 + BeNice! 后训练），实现循环架构中的 KV-cache 共享，显著降低内存占用并提升推理效率，同时兼容其他 KV 压缩技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环架构在推理时每层循环都会生成独立的 KV-cache，导致内存随循环次数线性增长。虽然先前工作提出跨深度共享 KV-cache（KV-shareability），但其机制不明，且相同训练的模型表现不一致，需要系统性方法启用该特性。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一阶段，预训练时使用随机深度，诱导收缩残差动力学，使表示跨循环收敛到共同极限；第二阶段，提出 BeNice! 后训练方法，将最后一层循环的 KV-cache 广播到第一层，训练其产生兼容残差同时保持模型质量。

Result: BeNice! 成功启用 KV-shareability，验证困惑度与标准微调无共享版本持平。KV 共享与其他压缩方法互补，可与高效序列混合器（如多潜在注意力、Kimi delta attention）及低精度量化（fp8, mxfp8, nvfp4）组合。在模拟低内存长上下文推理中，缓存命中率提升 31.4%，吞吐率提升 23.6%，p90 首 token 时间降低 83.6%。

Conclusion: 通过预训练随机深度和 BeNice! 后训练，循环模型可实现可靠的 KV-cache 共享，大幅降低内存占用并提升推理效率，且与现有压缩技术正交兼容，为长上下文、低资源部署提供实用方案。

Abstract: Looped architectures scale quality while keeping parameter count fixed by repeatedly passing activations through the same block of layers. During inference, each recurrence generates independent KV-caches, causing memory to grow linearly. To address this challenge, prior work has proposed KV-sharing (i.e., sharing KV-caches across depth), finding that, in some cases, the KV-cache of the final recurrence can be shared with the earlier recurrences without quality loss, a property we denote as KV-shareability. However, the mechanisms underlying KV-shareability remain poorly understood, with even identically trained looped architectures not consistently exhibiting it. In this paper, we study how KV-shareability can be enabled in looped models. We propose a two-stage method: in the first stage, we use stochastic depth during pretraining, which induces contractive residual dynamics that drive representations toward a common limit across recurrences. In the second stage, we propose BeNice!, a post-training method that broadcasts the final recurrence's KV-cache into the first recurrence, training it to produce compatible residuals while preserving the original model's quality. As a result, BeNice! enables KV-shareability while matching standard finetuning's unshared validation perplexity. Using BeNice!, we show that KV-sharing is complementary to alternative KV-compression methods, composing with memory-efficient sequence mixers (e.g., multi-latent attention, Kimi delta attention) and low-precision KV-cache quantization (e.g., fp8, mxfp8, nvfp4). Finally, in simulated low-memory, long-context inference, KV-sharing improves cache hit rates by up to 31.4%, increases throughput by up to 23.6%, and reduces p90 time-to-first-token by up to 83.6%.

</details>


### [193] [Causal Chain of Thought: Mitigating Attention Leakage via Context Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31639)
*Gabriele Dominici,Teague McMillan,Fazl Barez,Marc Langheinrich,Martin Gjoreski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalCoT框架，通过将推理问题分解为有向无环图中的原子子问题，并在仅包含直接因果父节点的隔离上下文中评估每个节点，强制实现因果透明度，解决了标准CoT中推理步骤可能非因果驱动最终答案的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CoT提示虽能生成逐步推理，但稠密注意力机制允许模型通过直接查找上下文得出答案，同时生成合理但事后诸葛亮式的解释，无法保证推理步骤因果驱动最终预测。

Method: CausalCoT将问题分解为原子子问题的有向无环图(DAG)，每个节点仅在包含其直接因果父节点的隔离上下文中评估。这种马尔可夫结构切断了对非父节点内容的注意力路径，使下游预测对局部推理敏感而非全局上下文泄露。

Result: 在多样化推理基准上，CausalCoT保持了具有竞争力的预测性能，同时显著提升解释忠实度，因果敏感性平均提升12个百分点，最高达21个百分点。结构化依赖图还支持测试时干预，局部修正可可预测地传播至下游推理。

Conclusion: CausalCoT通过因果图结构解耦推理过程，有效解决了CoT的事后合理化问题，在保持性能的同时大幅提升推理的因果忠实性和可干预性，为可信AI推理提供了新范式。}

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting elicits step-by-step rationales that appear to explain model predictions, but there is no guarantee these steps causally drive the final answer. In standard autoregressive generation, dense attention allows each reasoning step to attend to the full context, enabling the model to derive answers through direct lookup while generating plausible-sounding but post-hoc justifications. We introduce CausalCoT, an inference framework that enforces causal transparency by decomposing problems into a directed acyclic graph of atomic sub-questions, evaluating each node in an isolated context containing only its direct causal parents. This Markovian structure removes attention pathways to non-parent content, making downstream predictions sensitive to local reasoning rather than global context leakage. Across a diverse suite of reasoning benchmarks, CausalCoT preserves competitive predictive performance while improving explanation faithfulness, increasing causal sensitivity of intermediate reasoning steps by an average of 12 and up to 21 percentage points over the strongest baseline. The structured dependency graph additionally enables test-time interventions in which local corrections propagate predictably through downstream reasoning.

</details>


### [194] [A Fix for Softmax and Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31642)
*Jonathan T. Barron,Dan Gnanapragasam,Michael Tschannen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出标准softmax、log-softmax和cross-entropy实现存在两个数值bug：Softmax Collapse（目标logit梯度归零）和Softmax Cancellation（师生logit相等时仍产生非零梯度）。作者提出简单修复实现，消除了Slingshot现象，加速了带权重衰减的grokking，防止了无权重衰减时的精度崩塌，阻止了蒸馏过程中学生网络偏离教师，并揭示了记忆化后logit增长受权重衰减和Adam的ε超参数平衡约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准softmax、log-softmax和cross-entropy的实现存在数值稳定性问题，导致梯度计算错误：当某个logit远大于其他logit时，目标logit的梯度会被舍入为零（Softmax Collapse），破坏梯度和为零的性质；当学生logit接近教师logit时，即使两者相等也会产生非零梯度（Softmax Cancellation）。这些bug影响训练动态，导致Slingshot现象、grokking减速、精度崩塌和蒸馏偏移等问题。

Method: 作者提出了简单的数值稳定实现来修复两个bug：对于log-softmax，使用稳定的log-sum-exp计算避免减法抵消；对于softmax，采用数值稳定的指数归一化；对于cross-entropy，结合修正后的log-softmax直接计算损失和梯度。核心思想是避免大数吃小数导致的精度丢失，以及避免相近数值相减产生的灾难性抵消。

Result: 修复后的实现：1）消除了之前观察到的Slingshot现象（训练中突然的损失尖峰）；2）在带权重衰减时加速了grokking（泛化能力的突发式提升）；3）在无权重衰减时防止了精度崩塌；4）阻止了知识蒸馏中学生网络偏离教师网络；5）揭示记忆化阶段后，logit增长受权重衰减与Adam优化器ε超参数之间平衡机制的约束，不再无界增长。

Conclusion: softmax家族函数的标准实现存在根本性数值bug，通过简单的数值稳定重写可同时修复Softmax Collapse和Softmax Cancellation。这不仅解决了多个已观察到的训练异常现象，还揭示了记忆化后logit动态的理论边界——由权重衰减和Adam的ε共同决定的平衡点。该修复具有广泛适用性，建议作为深度学习框架的默认实现。

Abstract: Standard implementations of softmax, log-softmax, and cross-entropy have two numerical bugs. The first bug, Softmax Collapse, rounds the target logit's gradient to zero if that one logit is much larger than the others, resulting in gradients that no longer sum to zero. The second bug, Softmax Cancellation, occurs when student logits are close to teacher logits, and produces non-zero gradients even when the student and teacher logits are equal. We present simple implementations of log-softmax, softmax, and cross-entropy that fix both bugs. Our fix eliminates the previously-observed "Slingshot" phenomenon, speeds up grokking with weight decay, prevents accuracy collapse without weight decay, stops student networks from drifting away from the teacher during distillation, and shows that after memorization, logit growth is bounded by an equilibrium between weight decay and Adam's $ε$ hyperparameter.

</details>


### [195] [METTA: Memory-Enhanced Test-Time Adaptation for Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31656)
*Ali Najar,Hosein Hasani,Amir Kooshan Fattah,Yasamin Rajabi,Mahdieh Soleymani Baghshah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: METTA是一种用于元强化学习的记忆增强测试时适应方法，将快速适应分解为因果神经记忆进行精细的单集适应和紧凑的回合级摘要进行长跨度跨回合任务推理，实现了线性序列复杂度和高效的长上下文适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的元强化学习方法要么使用循环神经网络压缩经验到隐藏状态，要么使用Transformer以二次方内存和计算代价检索过去轨迹，难以在长交互历史中高效表示任务证据并快速适应未见任务。

Method: METTA将快速适应阶段分解为两个时间尺度：因果神经记忆执行精细的单集内适应，具有线性序列复杂度；紧凑的回合级摘要支持长距离的跨回合任务推理，无需对完整交互历史进行注意力计算。神经记忆提供了足够的表达能力和测试时训练能力。

Result: 在未见的Meta-World ML10/45测试环境中，METTA达到超过50%的成功率，同时保持低训练预算和短墙钟时间。在长上下文稀疏奖励PointMaze任务中，其表现优于基于Transformer的基线，展现出更结构化的探索行为。

Conclusion: METTA为更自适应和可扩展的长上下文元强化学习提供了一条高效路径。

Abstract: Meta-reinforcement learning aims to train agents that adapt quickly to unseen tasks from limited interaction, but this requires representing task evidence over long interaction histories. Classical recurrent agents compress experience into hidden states, while transformer-based agents retrieve from past trajectories at quadratic memory and computation cost. In this work, we propose Memory-Enhanced Test-Time Adaptation (METTA) for meta-reinforcement learning. It decomposes the fast adaptation phase into two temporal scales: a causal neural memory performs fine-grained within-episode adaptation with linear sequence complexity, while compact episode-level summaries support long-range cross-episode task inference without attending over the full interaction history. Moreover, equipping agents with neural memory provides sufficient expressive capacity and test-time training capability, enabling effective adaptation to unseen held-out tasks and task families at meta-test time. On unseen held-out Meta-World ML10/45 test environments, METTA achieves a success rate over $50\%$ while maintaining a low training budget and short wall-clock time. On long-context sparse-reward PointMaze tasks, it outperforms transformer-based baselines by exhibiting more structured exploration behavior. These results show that METTA provides an efficient path toward more adaptive and scalable long-context meta-RL.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [IRAH: Hierarchical Agentic Tree Search for Retrieval Pipeline Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30167)
*Maria Ana Cardei,Aniket Singh,Amir Moghadam,Srividhya Baikadi,Rashmi Shetty*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了信息检索代理式框架（IRAH），结合了用于信息检索的语言代理树搜索（LATS-IR）与离线元学习初始化，用于在有限评估预算下联合优化检索增强生成（RAG）管线的结构组件选择与数值超参数。LATS-IR采用UCT引导的层次化搜索，先探索宏观结构选择再细化数值参数，并利用自然语言反思指导后续变异并提供可解释的搜索轨迹。实验表明，在相同元学习初始化下，LATS-IR在三个分布内基准中有两个超越TPE（最高提升6.4%），在分布外基准上保持竞争力。预热启动的IRAH在四个基准上实现最高平均保留nDCG@10（0.4816），并优于冷启动版本，确立了其在受限评估预算下检索管线优化的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化用于检索增强生成（RAG）的信息检索（IR）管线需要在有限评估预算下联合选择结构组件（如检索器类型、重排序器）和数值超参数（如top-k、相似度阈值）。现有方法难以同时高效探索离散结构空间和连续参数空间，且缺乏可解释的搜索过程记录。

Method: 提出IRAH框架，核心为LATS-IR（Language Agent Tree Search for IR）：采用UCT（Upper Confidence bounds applied to Trees）引导的层次化树搜索，第一阶段探索宏观结构管线选择（离散组合空间），第二阶段细化数值超参数（连续空间）；引入自然语言反思机制，基于历史评估结果生成文本推理以指导后续变异，同时产生可审查的搜索轨迹；结合离线元学习初始化（warm-start），利用历史任务经验提供高质量初始候选。

Result: 在三个分布内基准和一个分布外基准上评估：相同元学习初始化下，LATS-IR在三个分布内基准中有两个优于TPE，最大提升6.4%，在分布外基准上表现竞争；预热启动IRAH在所有四个基准上达到最高平均保留nDCG@10（0.4816），显著优于冷启动版本。

Conclusion: IRAH通过层次化树搜索与元学习初始化的结合，在受限评估预算下实现了对RAG检索管线结构与参数的联合高效优化，自然语言反思提供了可解释性，且在分布内外均展现出强泛化能力，为自动化检索管线优化提供了有竞争力的解决方案。

Abstract: Optimizing Information Retrieval (IR) pipelines for Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) requires jointly selecting structural components and numerical hyperparameters under limited evaluation budgets. We introduce the Information Retrieval Agentic Harness (IRAH), which combines Language Agent Tree Search for IR (LATS-IR) with offline meta-learned initialization. LATS-IR performs UCT-guided hierarchical search, first exploring macro-structural pipeline choices and then refining numerical parameters, with natural-language reflections to guide subsequent mutations and provide inspectable search traces. We evaluate IRAH on three in-distribution benchmarks and one out of-distribution benchmark to assess generalization under distribution shift. Under the same meta-learned initialization, LATS-IR outperforms TPE on two of three in-distribution benchmarks, with gains of up to 6.4%, and remains competitive on the out-of-distribution benchmark. Across all four benchmarks, warm-started IRAH achieves the highest average held-out nDCG@10 (0.4816) and improves over its cold-start counterpart. These results establish IRAH as a competitive approach to retrieval pipeline optimization under constrained evaluation budgets.

</details>


### [197] [FormBench: A Benchmark for Fine-Grained Retrieval in Formulation Patents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30175)
*Vineeth Venugopal,Elsa Olivetti,Jonathan Welch*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出FormBench基准，用于评估模型从技术文档中精准检索化学信息的能力。基准包含55,352个查询和两个约100万段落的语料库变体（随机干扰项C0和结构相近干扰项C1），每个查询配有锚点段落、难负例和软负例。评估显示商业API（如OpenAI）在检索任务上表现极差（C1仅0.009），但经任务自适应微调（TAPT）的Qwen3-Embed大幅提升（C1达0.4085），且BM25仍具竞争力。API失效归因于域内几何崩溃，但API在分类/聚类任务上优于TAPT模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索基准缺乏针对化学等专业领域精确信息检索的评估，且商业嵌入模型在专业技术文档上的检索能力未被系统测试。需要一个细粒度、基于本体的分级负例构建机制，以深入考察模型的判别能力，并揭示商业API在特定领域检索失效的原因。

Method: 构建FormBench基准：从材料本体通过实体重叠评分生成查询-锚点对及难/软负例，提供分级判别信号。提供两种语料库变体：FormBench-Random (C0) 随机采样干扰项，FormBench-Structured (C1) 结构相近干扰项。评估10种模型配置：商业API编码器、开源编码器及其TAPT变体。进行语料库规模消融实验，分析几何崩溃现象，并对比检索、分类、聚类任务表现。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 商业API检索表现极差（OpenAI-3-large在C1仅0.009，C0为0.213）；(2) TAPT微调显著提升Qwen3-Embed（C1达0.4085，C0达0.4835）；(3) BM25仍具竞争力（C1 0.375，C0 0.467），仅逊于TAPT-Qwen3；(4) 语料库规模显著影响检索行为；(5) API失效源于域内几何崩溃；(6) API在分类/聚类任务上优于TAPT变体。

Conclusion: FormBench揭示了商业嵌入模型在专业化学文档检索中的严重失效，并指出几何崩溃为核心原因。任务自适应微调能有效弥补开源模型不足，使其超越BM25和商业API。基准的分级负例设计和双语料库变体为诊断模型判别能力提供了细粒度工具。尽管API在检索任务失效，但其在分类/聚类等下游任务仍保持优势，提示检索与语义理解能力的解耦。FormBench已开源，将推动专业领域检索模型的研发。

Abstract: We introduce FormBench, a platform for testing the ability of models to retrieve precise chemical information from technical documents. FormBench contains 55,352 queries and two retrieval corpus variants each with ~1M passages: FormBench-Random (C0), with randomly sampled distractors, and FormBench-Structured (C1), with structurally proximate distractors. Each query is paired with an anchor passage, a hard negative, and a soft negative - derived from a materials ontology through entity overlap scoring, providing a deeper graded signal on a model's discriminatory power. We evaluate 10 model configurations spanning commercial API encoders (OpenAI, Voyage), open-source encoders (Nomic, GTE-large, Qwen3-Embed), and their task-adaptive fine-tuned (TAPT) variants. We find: (1) commercial APIs score very poorly across all configurations (OpenAI-3-large: 0.009 on C1, 0.213 on C0); (2) TAPT on FormBench triplets lifts Qwen3-Embed to 0.4085 on C1, 0.4835 on C0; (3) BM25 remains competitive (0.375 on C1, 0.467 on C0), exceeded only by TAPT-Qwen3; (4) corpus size has a significant impact on retrieval behavior, as demonstrated through a corpus-size ablation; (5) the API failure traces to within-domain geometric collapse; (6) APIs outperform even the TAPT variants on classification and clustering tasks. FormBench is released in BEIR-compatible format at huggingface.co/datasets/vinven7/FormBench under CC-BY-4.0.

</details>


### [198] [FlexVDR: Learning to Allocate Matryoshka Representation Budgets for Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30177)
*Haowen Xiang,Yibo Yan,Xin Zou,Shuliang Liu,Yi Cao,Mingdong Ou,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FlexVDR 是一个面向视觉文档检索的查询自适应预算分配框架，通过在编码器深度、嵌入宽度和 token 数量上构建联合路由族，并利用预算分配器根据早期查询特征预测各路由的检索效用，结合可配置的资源偏好动态选择紧凑路由，在保持原生检索质量的同时大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉文档检索依赖丰富的多向量表示捕获细粒度证据，但编码和匹配这些表示计算昂贵。现有的压缩和弹性表示方法虽提供更小配置，却无法决定某个查询应使用哪种配置。分配决策需在承担完整计算前估计不同表示的检索价值，同时适应不同的资源偏好。

Method: 1. 构建覆盖编码器深度、嵌入宽度和 token 数量的联合路由族。2. 通过检索监督和蒸馏训练紧凑路由，保留原生旁路作为全预算参考。3. 设计预算分配器，从早期查询特征预测各路由的检索效用，并结合可配置的资源偏好选择路由。

Result: 在 ViDoRe V1、V2、V3 公开基准上，跨五种骨干网络的实验表明，FlexVDR 在为每个查询选择紧凑路由的同时，保留了大部分原生检索质量。可配置的资源偏好使同一检索器能在无需重新训练或切换模型的情况下调整计算量。

Conclusion: FlexVDR 实现了查询级的自适应预算分配，解决了多向量视觉文档检索中的计算效率与检索质量权衡问题，提供了一种灵活、无需重训练即可调节资源消耗的统一检索器框架。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval relies on rich multi-vector representations to capture fine-grained evidence in document pages, but encoding and matching these representations can be computationally expensive. Existing compression and elastic representation methods expose smaller configurations, yet making multiple budgets available does not determine which configuration a query should use. The allocation decision must estimate the retrieval value of alternative representations before incurring their full computation, while accommodating different resource preferences. We introduce FlexVDR, a framework for query-adaptive budget allocation within a single retriever. We construct a joint family of routes over encoder depth, embedding width, and token count. Retrieval supervision and distillation train the compact routes, while a native bypass preserves the full-budget reference. A Budget Allocator predicts route-specific retrieval utility from early query features and combines these predictions with a configurable resource preference to select a route. Experiments across five backbones on ViDoRe V1, V2, and V3 public show that FlexVDR retains much of the native retrieval quality while selecting compact routes for individual queries. A configurable resource preference allows the same retriever to adjust its computation without retraining or switching models.

</details>


### [199] [Association between Player Perception and AI Disclosure on Steam: An Exploratory Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30178)
*Fabian Wiesinger,Johanna Pirker,Denis Helic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文分析Steam评论，探讨AI披露游戏的评论模式差异，发现AI披露游戏在负面评论中存在更多AI相关批评。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在游戏开发中应用迅速，玩家反应是争论核心，需了解AI披露如何影响玩家感知。

Method: 使用跨多个披露组的数据集，应用关键词、词频、特征词等语言学分析方法，考察Steam上AI相关讨论。

Result: AI披露游戏在负面评论中关联更高水平的AI相关批评。

Conclusion: 研究为理解玩家对AI披露的感知提供了首步探索性证据。

Abstract: The use of generative AI in game development is growing rapidly and is the subject of intense debate in which player reactions play a central role. This paper contributes to this discussion by analyzing Steam reviews to examine whether games with AI disclosure are associated with different review patterns. Using a dataset across multiple disclosure groups, we apply linguistic analysis including keyword-based, word frequency, and characteristic word analysis to investigate the AI-related discussion on Steam. Our results show that AI-disclosed games are associated with higher levels of AI-related criticism in negative reviews. Our findings provide a first exploratory step towards understanding how players perceive AI disclosure.

</details>


### [200] [QProtFuse: Query-Guided Layer Fusion for Retrieval-Augmented Protein Function Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30180)
*Mianzhi Pan,Peishuo Liu,Yawen Ouyang,Botian Wang,Yiming Rong,Hao Zhou,Jianbing Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 QProtFuse，一种查询引导的多层蛋白特征融合方法，通过联合蛋白与注释检索的跨模态对比学习，显著提升蛋白功能预测的检索性能和下游 RAG 生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白 RAG 方法主要依赖外部检索，忽视了单一蛋白语言模型 (PLM) 内部蕴含的丰富知识。研究发现不同 PLM 层在不同生物任务上的检索性能差异大，传统仅使用最后一层的方式次优。

Method: QProtFuse 利用查询自适应融合 PLM 多层特征，并通过模态内和跨模态对比目标联合学习蛋白检索与注释检索，实现查询引导的特征融合与互补训练目标。

Result: 在 Prot-Inst-OOD 基准上，QProtFuse 达到最优蛋白和注释检索分数，注释重排后 Recall@1 達 16.4%；下游 RAG 功能描述在 LLM-as-a-judge 评分中 Qwen3-8B 得 6.22/10，DeepSeek-V4-Pro 得 6.68/10，均为最高。消融实验与权重分析验证了查询引导融合与互补目标的有效性。

Conclusion: QProtFuse 通过查询自适应的多层特征融合与联合对比学习，充分挖掘 PLM 内部知识，显著提升蛋白功能预测的检索与生成性能，为蛋白 RAG 提供了新范式。

Abstract: Predicting protein functions from amino acid sequences is essential to understanding biological processes. Large language models (LLMs) offer flexible function descriptions to natural-language queries, yet their limited protein understanding motivates retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Existing protein RAG methods primarily expand external retrieval signals while overlooking the rich knowledge encoded within a single protein language model (PLM). We show that retrieval performance varies across PLM layers and biological tasks, making conventional final-layer selection suboptimal. To this end, we propose QProtFuse, which uses queries to adaptively fuse multi-layer protein features and jointly learns protein and annotation retrieval through intra-modal and cross-modal contrastive objectives. On Prot-Inst-OOD, QProtFuse achieves the best protein and annotation retrieval scores, reaching 16.4\% Recall@1 with annotation reranking. These gains improve downstream RAG-based function descriptions, yielding the highest LLM-as-a-judge scores for both Qwen3-8B (6.22/10) and DeepSeek-V4-Pro (6.68/10). Ablations and weight analyses demonstrate the value of query-guided fusion and complementary training objectives for exploiting a PLM's internal knowledge.

</details>


### [201] [Deep Search in Real-World Web Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30181)
*Xiaomin Yu,Hongliang Chen,Zhe Huang,Shilin Yan,Shao-Rong Su,Xuqiao Ma,Feiyang Wang,Jiayu Chen,Jiwei Fang,Yu Yan,Yini Huang,Chen Chen,Ruoxi Cheng,Yuzhi He,Yuhui Zhang,Xiaoxing Hu,Chen Liu,Jianbo Lin,Yijiang Li,Xiaobin Hu,Menglong Li,Zhuoran Lu,Weishi Wang,Zhongqi Yue,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 OpenDS 任务与 OpenDS-World 基准，首次在真实开放网络环境中系统评估智能体的端到端深度搜索能力，测试 12 个前沿模型发现最佳模型仅达 39.0% 准确率，人类为 21.1%，揭示工具协调与长程搜索管理的持久挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度搜索智能体主要在静态检索设置下评估，缺乏对真实开放网络环境中跨模态、异构格式、复杂交互机制的端到端科研能力评估，亟需一个真实、动态、多源的评测平台。

Method: 构建 OpenDS 任务定义，要求智能体自主探索实时网络、通过搜索、浏览器交互、证据处理发现并关联多源证据，最终给出唯一事实答案。基于此建立 OpenDS-World 基准，包含 200 个涵盖多模态、异构格式、交互式访问的高难度任务，经人工独立验证保证证据有效性与答案唯一性。在统一工具环境与执行协议下评测 12 个前沿模型，并分析工具使用、搜索成本、终止行为、失败恢复等策略差异。

Result: 最佳模型准确率 39.0%，人类独立尝试 1000 次准确率 21.1%。分析显示不同模型在研究策略上存在显著差异，工具协调与长程搜索管理仍为核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: OpenDS-World 为系统评估和推进智能体在真实开放网络环境中的自主深度搜索能力提供了统一评测平台，填补了现有评测在真实性、多源性、交互性上的空白。

Abstract: Despite substantial progress in Deep Search agents, their ability to conduct end-to-end research in real-world open Web environments remains insufficiently evaluated. Unlike static retrieval settings, information on the real-world Web is distributed across diverse sources, modalities, and heterogeneous formats, often involving complex interaction mechanisms that require agents to autonomously search for, access, and integrate relevant evidence during execution. To address this gap, we introduce the OpenDS task, designed to evaluate agents' ability to perform multi-step deep search in the live, open Web. This task requires agents to autonomously explore the real Web, discover and connect relevant evidence from multiple independent sources through Web search, browser interaction, and evidence processing, and ultimately determine a unique factual answer. Building on this task formulation, we construct OpenDS-World, a benchmark comprising 200 challenging tasks spanning diverse information modalities, heterogeneous file formats, and interactive access requirements, with independent human verification to ensure evidence validity and answer uniqueness. We evaluate 12 frontier models under a unified tool environment and execution protocol. The best-performing model achieves 39.0% accuracy, while human evaluation across 1,000 independent attempts yields 21.1% accuracy. Further analyses of tool usage, search cost, termination behavior, and failure recovery reveal substantial differences in research strategies across models, as well as persistent challenges in tool coordination and long-horizon search management. OpenDS-World provides a unified evaluation platform for systematically assessing and advancing agents' autonomous deep search capabilities in real-world open Web environments.

</details>


### [202] [FAIRGate: Agentic Runtime Policy Selection and Verified Repair for Fairer Recommendation Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30359)
*Navkar Jain,Enock Ayiku,Kenneth Fletcher*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FAIRGate 是一个模型无关的交付控制器，在基础排序器和最终 Top-K 列表之间引入了一个代理策略选择层。代理从预定义策略库（长尾曝光、物品组曝光平衡、集中度控制、校准保护、弃权）中选择策略，确定性执行器通过约束修复应用策略，验证器在满足相关性、可行性、元数据、校准和审计约束时才接受结果，最后由语言模型基于验证的审计轨迹生成证据链接的叙述。该设计使代理在运行时承担公平性责任，同时保持公平性执行的可度量、有界和可重放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公平性感知推荐系统通常在训练中编码单一公平目标或在评分后应用固定的重排序规则，但在实际部署中，候选池、物品元数据、曝光债务、用户偏好历史和效用预算都在运行时动态变化，导致合适的公平性行动需随请求而变。因此需要一种能在运行时根据上下文灵活选择公平性策略、且保证执行过程可验证、可重放的机制。

Method: FAIRGate 架构包含四个核心组件：1) 代理策略选择层：从预定义策略库（长尾曝光、物品组曝光平衡、集中度控制、校准保护、弃权）中选择策略；2) 确定性执行器：通过约束修复应用选定策略；3) 验证器：检查相关性、可行性、元数据、校准和审计约束，仅在全部满足时接受结果；4) 语言模型叙述组件：仅接收验证后的审计轨迹，在排序决策固定后生成证据链接的解释。整个流程模型无关，置于基础排序器之后。

Result: 论文提出了 FAIRGate 这一完整架构设计，实现了运行时代理策略选择、确定性执行、约束验证和事后可解释叙述的端到端流程。该设计使公平性执行具有可度量性（通过验证器约束）、有界性（通过确定性执行器和策略库边界）、可重放性（通过审计轨迹记录），解决了动态部署环境下公平性目标多变且需严格保证的问题。

Conclusion: FAIRGate 通过引入代理策略选择层、确定性执行器、多约束验证器和基于审计轨迹的语言模型叙述，提供了一种模型无关、运行时自适应、可验证且可解释的公平性交付控制方案。该架构将公平性决策的责任分配给运行时代理，同时通过结构化约束和审计机制保证了公平性执行的严格性、透明度和可追溯性，为实际部署中的动态公平性需求提供了可落地的技术路径。

Abstract: Fairness-aware recommender systems often encode one fairness objective during training or apply a fixed re-ranking rule after scoring. In deployment, however, the appropriate fairness action can vary across requests because the candidate pool, item metadata, exposure debt, user preference history, and utility budget all change at runtime. We propose FAIRGate, a model-agnostic delivery controller that places an agentic policy-selection layer between a base ranker and the final top-K list. The agent does not define fairness objectives from scratch. It chooses from a predefined policy library covering long-tail exposure, item-group exposure balance, concentration control, calibration protection, and abstention. A deterministic executor then applies the selected policy through constrained repair, and a validator accepts the result only when relevance, feasibility, metadata, calibration, and audit constraints are satisfied. The final language-model component receives only the verified audit trace and produces evidence-linked narration after the ranking decision is fixed. This design gives the agent runtime responsibility while keeping fairness enforcement measurable, bounded, and replayable.

</details>


### [203] [When Agentic Retrieval Fails Silently: Framework and Production Evidence from a Creative AI Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30423)
*Yan,Liang,Anuj Agarwal,Aditya Reddy Cheruku*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了Layered Agentic Retrieval Assessment (LARA)框架，解决智能体助手中检索归因断裂问题，将检索质量、用户参与与结果、产品成功三层解耦，通过四字段谱系模式恢复检索归因，并在Adobe Express上验证：聊天用户重构查询比例（44.4%）显著高于画廊搜索用户（33.1%），因聊天重构旨在优化生成输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索指标（CTR、MRR、NDCG）假设用户直接查看排序结果并采取行动，但在智能体助手场景中，生成层先消费检索输出，导致三重假设失效：1) 谁先阅读结果——智能体先于用户阅读；2) 用户何时行动——可能在多轮对话或多会话后才行动；3) 结果由何引起——结果融合了检索内容与模型自有知识。这种归因鸿沟使得无法准确评估检索质量。

Method: 提出LARA框架，将评估拆解为三个独立层级：检索质量层、参与与结果层、产品成功层，通过四字段谱系模式（检索ID、生成ID、用户动作ID、业务结果ID）串联，无需额外建模即可实现跨层归因。在Adobe Express部署实验，同一检索基础设施同时服务画廊搜索与智能体助手，对比两类用户群体的查询重构行为。

Result: 聊天用户查询重构率（44.4%）显著高于画廊搜索用户（33.1%），原因在于聊天重构旨在细化生成输出而非直接优化检索。LARA成功恢复了检索归因，使检索质量可在智能体流程中被独立度量，验证了三层解耦设计的有效性。

Conclusion: LARA通过分层评估与谱系追踪，填补了智能体助手场景下的检索归因鸿沟，为检索系统在生成式AI流程中的质量监控提供了可落地的度量范式，且无需引入新模型即可部署。

Abstract: CTR, MRR, and behavioral NDCG assume the user sees ranked results and acts on them. In an agentic assistant, a generation layer consumes retrieval output first, and that assumption breaks in three ways. The agent reads the results before the user does (who). The user's action can come several turns or sessions after retrieval (when). Outcomes blend retrieved content with the model's own knowledge (what caused it). We call this the attribution gap. We propose Layered Agentic Retrieval Assessment (LARA), which measures retrieval quality, engagement and outcome, and product success as three independent layers joined by a four-field lineage schema that requires no new modeling. We examine the motivation on Adobe Express, where one retrieval infrastructure serves a gallery search surface and an agentic assistant at roughly one-eighth its session volume for the same user population. Chat users reformulate more than gallery users (44.4% vs. 33.1%) because chat reformulation refines generated output. LARA restores attribution for the retrieval.

</details>


### [204] [Getting a GRIP on Graph-Based Search for Learned Sparse Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30425)
*Vihan Lakshman,Franco Maria Nardini,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文挑战了倒排索引在学习稀疏检索（LSR）中优于图索引的传统认知，提出了结合倒排索引优势的图近似最近邻搜索系统GRIP，通过三项技术（术语入口点种子、稀疏草图路由、稀疏分区图构建）在MS MARCO v2基准上实现比最先进倒排索引Seismic快2.5倍的检索延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为针对学习稀疏嵌入改进的倒排索引在LSR任务中达到最先进的召回-延迟权衡，远超图索引，导致社区默认选择倒排索引部署LSR。作者指出直接将标准图ANN索引应用于稀疏向量性能不佳，但可通过融入倒排索引的优势来克服这一短板。

Method: 提出GRIP系统，包含三项核心技术：1) 基于术语的入口点种子，利用倒排索引快速定位相关图节点；2) 基于稀疏草图的近似路由，通过稀疏向量草图加速图遍历中的距离计算；3) 基于稀疏分区的图构建，根据稀疏向量的非零维度划分子图以减少搜索空间。

Result: 在MS MARCO v2（1.38亿段落）基准上使用SPLADE嵌入，GRIP在95% recall@10下比专为LSR设计的最先进倒排索引Seismic实现2.5倍延迟降低，显著超越现有图索引和倒排索引基线。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，经过适当优化的图ANN索引可在LSR任务中超越倒排索引，质疑了部署学习稀疏检索时默认选择倒排索引的做法，为大规模稀疏向量检索提供了新的高效范式。

Abstract: Learned sparse retrieval (LSR) has recently gained traction in the information retrieval community for the ability to combine the generalization capabilities of neural representations with the interpretability of keyword-based search. A number of recent works have demonstrated that inverted indexes, when appropriately modified for learned sparse embeddings, achieve state-of-the-art recall-latency tradeoffs for LSR, often outperforming graph-based indexes by substantial margins in latency at a fixed recall target. As a result, the community has generally concluded that graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search is inferior to inverted indexes for LSR. This paper challenges this conventional wisdom. Our key insight is that, while applying standard graph-based ANN indexes out-of-the-box to sparse vectors leads to uncompetitive performance, we can overcome these shortcomings by infusing the strengths of inverted indexes into graph-based search. In particular, we introduce three techniques to accelerate graph-based LSR: (i) term-based entry point seeding, (ii) approximate routing via sparse sketching, and (iii) sparse partition-based graph construction. We combine these techniques into a new graph-based ANN system for LSR we call GRIP. On the MS MARCO v2 benchmark (138M passages) with SPLADE embeddings, GRIP achieves a $2.5\times$ latency improvement over Seismic, a state-of-the-art inverted index tailored for LSR, at 95\% recall@10. Ultimately, our results call into question the default choice of inverted indexes for deploying learned sparse retrieval.

</details>


### [205] [The Fusion Tax: A Diagnostic Analysis of When Hybrid Retrieval Hurts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30612)
*Aaditya Chauhan,Arun Agarwal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了混合检索（稀疏+稠密）在单个查询层面的“融合税”现象：即使最佳融合方法在聚合指标上优于单一检索器，但在13-48%的查询上会降低相关文档排名，且在6/7语料库上表现出显著的风险厌恶损失。融合失败主要表现为排序效应而非召回损失，且跨语料库泛化性差。作者提出基于稀疏-稠密平衡度量的融合决策指导原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管混合检索（如BM25+稠密编码器+RRF）在工业界被广泛推荐，但现有研究多关注聚合增益，忽视了融合在单个查询上的代价。本文旨在量化融合对单查询性能的负面影响（融合税），并探究其成因、预测性及缓解策略。

Method: 在7个BEIR数据集上实验：Anserini BM25作为稀疏基线，5个稠密编码器（22M-596M参数，跨3代模型），SPLADE作为学习稀疏替代方案，交叉编码器作为二阶段重排。对比3种融合函数（RRF、加权分数插值等），采用留一语料库交叉验证选择权重，并引入风险厌恶评估指标。

Result: 最佳融合方法（稠密加权分数插值）在聚合层面击败纯稠密检索，但：1) 13-48%查询上相关文档排名低于两个组件中的较好者；2) 8-21%查询上低于可部署的纯稠密检索器；3) 6/7语料库上风险厌恶评估显著为负。融合税主要表现为排序重排而非召回损失，最易发生于仅单一检索器覆盖的查询，跨编码器规模和SPLADE替换均持续存在。RRF仅在稀疏稠密平衡时优于纯稠密，且融合失败跨语料库预测性差（特征符号随平衡翻转）。交叉编码器仅在其自身可靠时能部分回收融合税。

Conclusion: 融合并非无成本，存在显著的查询级融合税。决策应基于目标语料库的实测稀疏-稠密平衡：当稠密检索器明显更强时避免融合；仅在组件能力均衡时考虑RRF；交叉编码器重排不能普遍修正融合税。需建立风险敏感的部署评估标准。

Abstract: Hybrid retrieval, which fuses a sparse lexical retriever with a dense neural one, is widely recommended in the software documentation surveyed in September 2026, usually with reciprocal rank fusion (RRF), on the strength of aggregate gains. We ask what fusion costs on individual queries, treating "do not fuse" as a first-class outcome, in the setting in which hybrid retrieval is deployed: a standard Anserini BM25, five dense encoders spanning 22M-596M parameters and three model generations, a learned-sparse (SPLADE) alternative, and a cross-encoder second stage, on seven BEIR datasets and three fusion functions. Even the best method, dense-weighted score interpolation, ranks relevant documents below the better of its two components on 13-48% of queries and below the deployable dense-only retriever on 8-21% (14-48% and 11-25% at a weight selected under leave-one-corpus-out validation), while beating dense-only in aggregate on every corpus with the primary encoder; the downside is significant under loss-averse risk-sensitive evaluation on six of the seven corpora. We call this loss the fusion tax. It is mostly a ranking effect that preserves recall in aggregate, it is most likely on the queries that only one retriever covers, and it persists across the encoder ladder and the SPLADE substitution. RRF carries the higher tax wherever the dense retriever is clearly stronger and only beats dense-only where the two components are balanced. Fusion failure is weakly predictable within a corpus but transfers unreliably across corpora, because the features that predict it change sign with the sparse-dense balance. A cross-encoder recovers part of the tax only where it is itself reliable. We distill these findings into guidance that keys the fusion decision to the measured sparse-dense balance of the target corpus.

</details>


### [206] [OneLatent: Latent Reasoning for Efficient Foundation Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30675)
*Hao Jiang,Peiru Du,Pengfei Yao,Mengting Li,Siyuan Lou,Kuo Cai,Sheng Yu,Qiang Luo,Jian Liang,Ruiming Tang,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: OneLatent 是一个高效的潜在推理框架，将显式 CoT 推理压缩为可学习的潜在 token，实现无需生成冗长推理轨迹的 Latent-Reason-then-Answer 推理，显著提升推荐性能和推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于基础推荐模型（FRM）的显式 CoT 推理方法存在推理开销大、依赖人工模板难以捕捉多样动态用户兴趣的问题。

Method: 提出 OneLatent 框架，包含三大核心组件：1) 多视角自适应 CoT (MV-ACoT) 自动生成多样化高质量教师监督；2) 三阶段潜在 token 对齐范式，逐步将 CoT 轨迹内化为可学习潜在 token；3) 多阶段课程式后训练策略激活潜在 token 推理能力。

Result: 在工业级快手数据集和公开基准上，OneLatent 持续优于显式 CoT 方法和传统基线；相比 FRM 的 Think 和 No-Think 变体，SID@64 分别提升 17.44% 和 9.33%，在线推理吞吐量提升 17 倍以上。在线 A/B 测试显示部署 OneLatent 带来 9.6% 的收入提升。

Conclusion: OneLatent 通过潜在推理机制有效解决了显式 CoT 的效率与表达力瓶颈，结合自适应多视角监督和课程式对齐训练，实现了推荐性能与推理效率的双重提升，已成功落地快手本地生活广告场景并显著提升商业指标。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning capabilities, motivating their use as the backbone of foundation recommendation models (FRMs). Existing methods enhance recommendations through explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning under a Think-then-Answer paradigm. However, explicit CoT incurs substantial inference overhead by generating lengthy reasoning traces and relies on manually designed templates that struggle to capture diverse, dynamic user interests. We propose OneLatent, an efficient latent reasoning framework that compresses explicit reasoning traces into several learnable latent tokens, enabling Latent-Reason-then-Answer inference without generating verbose traces. OneLatent first introduces Multi-View Adaptive CoT (MV-ACoT), which creates diverse, high-quality teacher-generated supervision by exploring user interests from multiple perspectives and automatically adapting reasoning complexity to each instance. Building on pretrained FRMs, it then uses a three-stage latent-token alignment paradigm to progressively internalize CoT traces into learnable latent tokens. Finally, a multistage curriculum-based post-training strategy activates latent-token reasoning for downstream recommendation tasks. Experiments on an industrial-scale Kuaishou dataset and the public Kuaishou LLM-Rec benchmark show that OneLatent consistently outperforms explicit CoT-based methods and traditional baselines. Compared with the Think and No-Think variants of FRMs, OneLatent improves SID@64 by 17.44% and 9.33%, respectively, while achieving over 17x higher online inference throughput. We further develop a production serving system for scalable, real-time FRM inference. An online A/B test in Kuaishou's local-services advertising scenario shows that deploying OneLatent with this system yields an estimated 9.6% revenue lift over strong online baselines, including OneRec and OneReason.

</details>


### [207] [Lychee: Certified Screening of Inverted Lists for High-Recall Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30697)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Lychee，一种基于GPU的倒排文件（IVF）近邻搜索方法，通过张量核心对探测列表进行筛选，仅对极少数候选向量计算精确距离，实现了在极高召回率（0.999-0.9999）下对十亿级向量的高效搜索，速度比IVF-Flat快9.7-117倍，且达到图索引无法企及的召回水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近邻搜索应用（人脸识别、欺诈检测、医学影像诊断）要求极高召回率（0.999-0.9999），但近似索引无法报告哪些查询丢失了真实邻居。倒排文件索引通过探测更多列表提高召回，但需对所有探测向量进行全精度评分，吞吐率随探测列表数线性下降；图索引在目标召回下饱和；量化列表需重排序。核心痛点是如何在不读取全量向量的情况下，以极低开销实现精确搜索等价的高召回。

Method: Lychee利用认证排除的洞察：通过同列表锚点向量计算认证下界，在张量核心上对探测列表进行快速筛选，仅对极少数幸存向量（每查询几百个）计算精确距离。理论证明Lychee返回的正是探测列表中的精确k近邻，等同于全精度IVF搜索结果，且读取的全精度向量数与探测列表数无关。

Result: 在11个数据集（100万到10亿向量）上对比10个GPU/CPU基线：Lychee在召回率相同时比IVF-Flat快9.7-117倍（探测200列表，数据集≤1亿向量）；达到CAGRA和HNSW无法达到的召回水平；比认证排除精确搜索快5-18倍（>1000万向量）。在4张GPU上搜索10亿向量（BIGANN-1B），召回0.9999时比IVF-Flat快44.5倍。

Conclusion: Lychee通过认证排除实现了倒排文件索引的高召回与高吞吐的统一，证明了可以在不牺牲精度的前提下大幅减少全精度向量读取，为十亿级向量的极高召回近邻搜索提供了实用解决方案，显著优于现有IVF、图索引及量化方法。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor search over embedding vectors matches faces against watchlists, transactions against known fraud patterns, and medical images against confirmed cases, where a missed neighbor is a missed threat, fraud, or diagnosis. Because an approximate index cannot report which queries lost a true neighbor, these applications require recall of 0.999 to 0.9999, at a cost far below that of exact search over billions of vectors. Inverted-file indexes reach high recall by probing more lists, but they read and score every probed vector at full precision, so that throughput falls in inverse proportion to the number of probed lists. Graph indexes saturate below these targets, whereas quantized lists must rerank their candidates with the full vectors. Our insight is to decide for almost every probed vector that it cannot enter the top k of the probed lists without reading the vector, through the certified lower bound of exact search by certified exclusion. This paper turns this insight into Lychee, a GPU inverted-file search that screens the probed lists on tensor cores under a threshold from anchors of the same lists and computes exact distances only for the few survivors. We prove that Lychee returns exactly the k nearest vectors of the probed lists, the answer of an inverted-file search at full precision, while it reads a few hundred full vectors per query regardless of the number of probed lists. On eleven datasets from one million to one billion vectors and against ten GPU and CPU baselines, Lychee is 9.7 to 117 times faster than IVF-Flat at equal recall with 200 probed lists on datasets of up to 100 million vectors. It also reaches recall levels that CAGRA and HNSW do not reach and outruns exact search by certified exclusion by 5 to 18 times above ten million vectors. On four GPUs, Lychee searches one billion vectors at recall 0.9999, 44.5 times faster than IVF-Flat on BIGANN-1B.

</details>


### [208] [Automated Enrichment Analysis with GraphRAG: Efficient Multi-Database ORA Extraction and Biological Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30710)
*Guangcan Li,Shuyuan Zheng,Jiayao Liu,Shigeto Seno,Makoto Onizuka*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于图的过度表达检索增强生成（GBOR-RAG）框架，用于解释基因表达研究。它将异构基因集数据库表示为统一的术语-基因知识图谱，通过图检索紧凑的证据子图，并利用LLM生成生物学解释，同时引入声明级忠实度指标评估生成内容的证据支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的过度表达分析（ORA）输出在基因集数据库间分散，且呈现为扁平术语列表，掩盖了相关富集信号间的基因级联系。需要一种整合且可追溯的框架来跨数据库关联术语、保留基因级证据，并生成有依据的生物学解释。

Method: 1) 将异构基因集数据库构建为统一的术语-基因知识图谱，共享基因连接跨数据库的相关术语；2) 基于图的ORA评分；3) 检索紧凑连通的证据子图，保留跨数据库关系和基因级支持；4) 以子图为条件让LLM生成生物学解释；5) 引入声明级忠实度指标评估生成声明是否由显性证据支持；6) 可选添加PubMed文献层进一步提升解释质量。

Result: 图基ORA与传统ORA高度一致（平均Pearson r=0.997），查询时间中位数减少37倍（最高48倍）；检索子图平均36.8节点，获得最高整体整合得分（4.12）；GBOR-RAG在比较系统中达到最高声明级忠实度（0.90）；添加PubMed文献层将整体解释质量从4.17提升至4.75。

Conclusion: GBOR-RAG框架通过知识图谱整合异构基因集数据库，实现了高效、可追溯的ORA解释，显著降低计算开销并保持高一致性，同时通过声明级忠实度指标确保生成解释的证据支持，引入文献层可进一步提升解释质量。

Abstract: Over-representation analysis (ORA) is widely used to interpret gene-expression studies, but its outputs are often fragmented across gene-set databases and presented as flat term lists that obscure gene-level links among related enrichment signals. We propose Graph-Based Over-Representation Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GBOR-RAG), an integrated and traceable framework for ORA interpretation. GBOR-RAG represents heterogeneous gene-set databases as a unified term--gene knowledge graph, where shared genes connect related terms across sources. After graph-based ORA scoring, the framework retrieves a compact connected evidence subgraph that preserves both cross-database relationships and gene-level support. The subgraph conditions an LLM to generate biological interpretations, and we introduce a claim-level faithfulness metric to assess whether generated claims are supported by explicit evidence. Experiments show that graph-based ORA preserves conventional ORA results with high consistency (mean Pearson $r=0.997$) while reducing query time by a median of $37\times$ (up to $48\times$). The retrieved subgraphs average 36.8 nodes and achieve the highest overall integration score (4.12), and GBOR-RAG attains the highest claim-level faithfulness (0.90) among the compared systems. Adding a PubMed literature layer further improves the overall interpretation quality from 4.17 to 4.75.

</details>


### [209] [Bezier-Guided Adaptive Flow for Cross-domain Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30825)
*Ziang Lu,Yu Zhang,Xinzhe Jiang,Lei Sang,Yiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BGAFlow 是一个跨域序列推荐框架，通过个性化迁移控制与状态自适应语义演化解决用户跨域偏好一致性差异问题，利用 LLM 推断用户画像建立共享语义空间，基于潜在兴趣重叠估计个性化迁移范围，并通过条件贝塞尔曲线与残差流实现动态语义适应，在真实数据集上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域序列推荐方法多采用全局对齐目标或粗粒度迁移策略，忽视用户跨域偏好一致性的个体差异，易引入无关信息且缺乏灵活性，难以针对个体用户调整语义表示。

Method: 1. 统一双域预训练结合 LLM 推断用户画像，建立共享语义空间。2. 基于潜在兴趣重叠估计个性化迁移范围，决定每个用户的迁移程度。3. 构建条件贝塞尔曲线作为几何参考，学习残差流根据中间偏好状态逐步细化轨迹。4. 融合动态偏好反馈调整迁移方向，配合推荐监督与轨迹正则化实现有效平滑的语义适应。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，BGAFlow 持续优于最先进的基线方法，验证了个性化迁移控制与状态自适应语义演化的有效性。

Conclusion: BGAFlow 通过个性化迁移范围估计、条件贝塞尔曲线引导的残差流学习及动态偏好反馈机制，实现了跨域序列推荐中细粒度、自适应的语义迁移，有效缓解了数据稀疏与冷启动问题。

Abstract: Cross-domain sequential recommendation aims to alleviate data sparsity and cold-start problems by transferring useful preference knowledge across domains. However, users exhibit varying degrees of preference consistency across domains, while existing methods often rely on global alignment objectives or coarse-grained transfer strategies. Such approaches may introduce irrelevant information and provide limited flexibility in adapting semantic representations to individual user preferences. To address these challenges, we propose BGAFlow, a personalized framework that combines state-adaptive semantic evolution with user-specific transfer control. BGAFlow first establishes a shared semantic space through unified dual-domain pretraining with user profiles inferred by a large language model (LLM). Based on latent interest overlap, it then estimates a personalized horizon to determine the appropriate transfer extent for each user. To enable semantic adaptation, BGAFlow constructs a conditional Bezier curve as a geometric reference and learns a residual flow that progressively refines the trajectory based on intermediate preference states. The learned flow incorporates dynamic preference feedback to adjust transfer directions, while recommendation supervision and trajectory regularization encourage effective and smooth adaptation. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that BGAFlow consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, validating the benefits of personalized transfer control and state-adaptive semantic evolution.

</details>


### [210] [UserArena: Benchmarking Interactive User Simulation for Long-Horizon Shopping Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30932)
*Jujia Zhao,Jiakai Tang,Jing Yu,Wen Chen,Jian Wu,Han Zhu,Fei Sun,Suzan Verberne,Zhaochun Ren*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出UserArena基准和OPTD方法，用于长时程推荐交互环境的建模与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源缺乏长时程、细粒度的真实用户轨迹，且不支持轨迹级评估和离线验证生产A/B测试结果，阻碍了智能体推荐策略的开发与评估。

Method: 构建UserArena基准，包含64,729个会话轨迹，11,010用户，566,102商品，13种动作类型，配备交互环境并支持五级评估；提出On-Policy Trajectory Distillation (OPTD)，利用轨迹级意图在学生生成的rollouts上提供稠密教师目标以提升模拟器保真度。

Result: 实验表明OPTD在UserArena评估协议下达到最强整体保真度，且在离线模拟中最接近生产策略效果。

Conclusion: UserArena填补了长时程推荐交互基准的空白，OPTD有效提升了模拟器保真度，为智能体推荐策略的离线开发与验证提供了可靠平台。

Abstract: Agentic recommendation pursues shopper goals through sequences of context-dependent decisions. Developing and evaluating such policies requires faithful recommendation world models that capture both the interactive shopping environment and users' behavioral responses over long interactions. Existing resources rarely provide long-horizon, fine-grained real-user trajectories or support trajectory-level evaluation and offline validation against production A/B results. We introduce UserArena, a real-world e-commerce interactive benchmark containing 64,729 session trajectories from 11,010 users, spanning 566,102 items and 13 action types. UserArena pairs these trajectories with an interactive environment and supports offline validation against outcomes from a controlled production A/B test, enabling five-level evaluation from output validity and local decisions to sequential behavior, personalization, and outcomes. To improve simulator fidelity over these long-horizon interactions, we further propose On-Policy Trajectory Distillation (OPTD), which uses trajectory-level intent to provide dense teacher targets on student-generated rollouts. Experiments show that OPTD achieves the strongest overall fidelity among the evaluated simulators under UserArena's evaluation protocol and most closely reproduces the production policy effect in offline simulation.

</details>


### [211] [PAIR: Preference-Aligned Intent Retrieval for Premium People Recommendation at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30941)
*Zhongkai Shangguan,Yanbin Kang,Yihan Cao,Ruijie Jiang,Qiannan Yin,Chang Yue,Zijian Zhou,Girish Kathalagiri,Changshuai Wei,Zhengming Xing*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Preference-Aligned Intent Retrieval (PAIR)，一种针对专业社交网络（如 LinkedIn）的检索框架，通过建模用户意图偏好而非档案相似性，解决职场互动中“互补性”匹配需求。方法采用基于曝光条件的多负样本对比目标实现偏好学习，并结合位置编码的点击历史聚合实现高效增量个性化更新。在 LinkedIn Premium 检索任务上，0.6B 参数模型达到 MAR@100 0.45。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于档案相似度或社交信号（如共同好友）的推荐系统适用于熟人社交网络，但在专业平台（如 LinkedIn）中，用户互动具有目标驱动、跨角色特点，相关性本质上是互补而非对称的（如求职者与招聘者互动而非同类人群），导致通用嵌入严重误排。因此需要一种建模互补意图偏好的检索框架。

Method: 提出 PAIR 框架：1) 受 Direct Preference Optimization 启发，将偏好学习实例化为曝光条件的多负样本对比目标，简化训练并高效扩展至大规模候选集，无需显式参考模型；2) 利用位置编码聚合点击历史捕获时序，支持固定窗口下用户表示的常数成本增量更新，新增交互无需重编码历史；3) 服务阶段仅需轻量 CPU 操作即可更新个性化，避免重复 LLM 推理。

Result: 在 LinkedIn Premium 检索任务上实验，0.6B 参数模型达到 MAR@100 0.45，验证了方法在专业网络互补意图匹配上的有效性与高效性。

Conclusion: PAIR 通过偏好对齐的意图检索与高效历史增量更新，成功解决了专业社交网络中互补性匹配与实时个性化的双重挑战，为大规模工业级推荐系统提供了可落地的轻量化方案。

Abstract: General-purpose embeddings trained on profile similarity often misrank candidates in professional networks, where relevance is inherently complementary rather than symmetric. For example, job seekers usually engage with recruiters rather than peers with similar credentials. Current user recommendation systems are often built on profile similarity or social signals such as shared connections, which are effective in friend-oriented social networks where users tend to connect with similar people or within existing social circles. However, this does not hold in professional platforms like LinkedIn, where interactions are often goal-driven and cross-role interactions, requiring matching based on complementary intents rather than similarity or social proximity. In this paper, we propose Preference-Aligned Intent Retrieval, a retrieval framework that is designed to model user intent preference instead of profile similarity. Our approach is inspired by the preference-learning perspective of Direct Preference Optimization, but instantiates preference learning as an exposure-conditioned multi-negative contrastive objective over embeddings. This formulation simplifies training and scales efficiently to large candidate sets without requiring an explicit reference model. Furthermore, we incorporate user interaction history by aggregating click histories with positional encoding to capture temporal order, which enables constant-cost updates of user representations over a fixed history window, allowing new interactions to be incorporated without re-encoding history. As a result, personalization can be updated efficiently using lightweight CPU operations without repeated LLM inference during serving. We evaluate PAIR on LinkedIn's Premium retrieval task. A 0.6B parameter model achieves a MAR@100 of 0.45.

</details>


### [212] [Formal-Informal Mathematical Alignment through and beyond Human Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31011)
*Noah Gießing,Ankit Satpute,Hamid Rahkooy,André Greiner-Petter,Moritz Schubotz*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个连接非正式数学（Stacks Project）与形式化数学的数据集，利用专家标注、检索、基于LLM的重排序和LLM辅助验证发现新的非正式到形式的链接，并将结果集成到MaRDI知识图谱中，讨论了LLM在该任务中的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化数学库（如Lean的mathlib）提供了可机器验证的数学知识表示，但目前非正式与形式化数学库之间的连接稀疏且非系统化，需要更好的形式化集成以挖掘隐含概念联系并改善对现有形式化的访问。

Method: 利用专家标注构建Stacks Project非正式陈述到mathlib形式化的数据集；采用检索、基于LLM的重排序和LLM辅助验证相结合的方法发现未标注的非正式到形式链接；将新发现的对齐集成到MaRDI知识图谱中。

Result: 成功构建了链接数据集，通过检索、重排序和验证发现了新的未标注对齐，并将这些对齐集成到MaRDI知识图谱中，使数学家能找到非正式数学内容的形式化对应物。

Conclusion: 该工作为非正式与形式化数学的更好集成迈出了第一步，展示了LLM在链接任务中的潜力，但也指出了使用LLM进行此类链接的困难和挑战，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Formalized mathematics libraries such as Lean's \texttt{mathlib} provide machine-verifiable representations of mathematical knowledge, including definitions, theorems, and their relationships. If effectively connected to informal mathematical resources, formal mathematics could help uncover previously implicit links between mathematical concepts and improve access to existing formalizations. However, connections between informal and formal mathematics libraries are currently sparse and unsystematic. In this paper, we make first strides towards better formal integration. We construct a dataset linking informal statements from the Stacks Project to their formalizations in \texttt{mathlib} by exploiting expert annotations. We evaluate retrieval, LLM-based reranking, and LLM-assisted verification to discover new unannotated informal-to-formal links. To facilitate exploration and reuse of formalized knowledge, we integrated the newly discovered alignments into the MaRDI knowledge graph, enabling mathematicians to find formal counterparts to informal mathematical content. We discuss the difficulties and challenges in using LLMs for linking formal and informal mathematical content. All code and data necessary for reproducing this paper can be found at: https://github.com/AnkitSatpute/Formal-Informal-Mathematical-Alignment

</details>


### [213] [The Delayed Countdown Method for Validating Review-Oriented AI Workflows in eDiscovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31311)
*David D. Lewis,Tara Emory,Lenora Gray,Mark Noel,Jeremy Pickens,Eugene Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个名为“延迟倒计数法”的评估方法，用于验证电子取证中面向审查工作流的有效性，解决了技术辅助审查中的争议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向审查的工作流已成为AI辅助文档审查的主流方法，但如何验证其有效性在技术辅助审查（TAR）法律领域仍是一个混淆和有争议的话题。需要一种简单实用的评估方法。

Method: 提出“延迟倒计数法”，可轻松集成到现有工作流和软件中，兼容任意停止规则，并产生有效的频率学派置信区间。

Result: 该方法提供了概念基础、示例计算，讨论了逻辑上不可能的召回值处理，并建议未来工作降低审查成本。

Conclusion: 延迟倒计数法为面向审查工作流提供了一种有效、实用的评估方法，解决了TAR中的争议，并为未来降低审查成本指明了方向。

Abstract: Review-oriented workflows have become the dominant approach to AI-assisted document review in electronic discovery (eDiscovery). How to validate the effectiveness of review-oriented workflows has, however, remained a confusing and controversial topic in technology-assisted review (TAR) in the law. We examine the sources of this controversy, and propose a simple and practical evaluation approach for review-oriented workflows. Our Delayed Countdown method is easily incorporated into existing review-oriented workflows and software, is compatible with arbitrary stopping rules, and produces valid frequentist confidence intervals on effectiveness. We explain the conceptual foundations of the method, provide example computations, discuss the handling of logically impossible recall values, and suggest future work on decreasing review costs.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [The Cost of Saying Hello: An Empirical Study of Handshake Overhead in AI Agent Communication Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30151)
*Pranav Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对三大智能体通信协议（MCP、A2A、ACP）的握手阶段进行实证基准测试，发现往返次数主导握手开销，且LLM读取元数据时token消耗随能力数量线性增长；MCP 2026版将握手从3轮降至2轮，热重连甚至可达0轮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体通信协议调研虽将握手开销列为效率指标，但缺乏实测数据；本文旨在填补这一空白，量化握手各阶段的延迟、字节数及token成本，为协议设计与优化提供实证依据。

Method: 使用各协议官方SDK，在回环及模拟50ms RTT网络下，对冷启动、热重复、能力不匹配、元数据退化四类场景各重复50次，插桩记录每轮请求的往返次数、分阶段延迟、传输字节数及token消耗。

Result: MCP旧版需3轮握手、230ms（50ms RTT下）；A2A仅需1轮、61.0ms。LLM解析对端元数据时，每个能力消耗24-107 tokens，200能力对端占用11%的200k上下文窗口。MCP 2026版将握手压缩至2轮，配合时代锁定（era pinning）热重连可达0轮。

Conclusion: 握手轮次是主要开销来源，协议演进显著降低了握手成本；能力清单规模对上下文窗口的侵蚀不容忽视。开源的测评框架与数据可支持后续协议优化与标准化工作。

Abstract: Every interaction between AI agents begins with a handshake: the initiating agent must locate its counterpart, learn what it can do, and establish a session before the first useful message can be sent. Surveys of agent communication protocols name handshake overhead as an efficiency criterion, yet none report measurements. This paper presents the first empirical, phase-decomposed benchmark of the handshake in three major agent protocols: MCP (in both its 2025 initialize and 2026 discover eras), A2A, and ACP. Using each protocol's official SDK, we instrument every request for round trips, per-phase latency, wire bytes, and token cost across cold-start, warm-repeat, capability-mismatch, and degraded-metadata scenarios (N=50 per cell, on loopback and under an emulated 50 ms RTT). Round-trip count dominates handshake cost. A legacy MCP handshake takes three round trips and 230 ms at 50 ms RTT; A2A needs one round trip and 61.0 ms. Round trips are not the whole bill: when an LLM reads the counterpart's metadata, handshake cost scales with the counterpart's capability inventory at 24-107 tokens per capability, so a 200-capability counterpart spends 11% of a 200k-token context window before the agent reads its own task. MCP's own 2026 revision cuts its handshake from three round trips to two and, with era pinning, to zero on warm reconnects, protocol evolution that corroborates the cost we measure. We release the full harness, raw data, and analysis at https://github.com/pvarshh/handshake-benchmark.

</details>


### [215] [MCP vs CLI for AI Agents: A Comparative Analysis of Tool Integration Strategies for Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30157)
*Arun Malik,Nitish Talasu,Bradley Klemick,Vineet Tushar Trivedi,Prachi Shah,Sanay Devi,Junjie Zhang,Kunal Sonawane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对比了CLI（命令行界面）和MCP（模型上下文协议）两种AI代理交互外部系统的范式，分析了14个评估维度，发现CLI适合单用户本地工作流，而MCP解决了CLI无法满足的细粒度访问控制、凭证隔离、结构化审计和多租户治理等需求。通过渐进式披露技术消除了MCP的上下文窗口开销弱点，使其Token效率与CLI相当。建议生产系统采用混合架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理需要与文件、API、数据库、云服务等外部系统交互，目前存在CLI和MCP两种竞争范式，缺乏系统性的多维度对比分析，特别是安全、治理和经济性方面的评估，以指导生产环境架构选择。

Method: 采用比较分析法，涵盖14个评估维度，辅以工作示例、基于合规框架（如NSA CSI U/OO/6030316-26、OWASP标准）的安全评估，以及基于BCG和Gartner的经济建模。

Result: CLI在单用户、仅本地工作流中效率更高；MCP在细粒度访问控制、凭证隔离、结构化审计、多租户治理等方面具有CLI无法比拟的优势。渐进式披露技术消除了MCP的上下文窗口开销，使其Token效率与CLI相当，同时保留架构优势。

Conclusion: 建议生产系统采用混合架构，结合CLI的本地高效性与MCP的治理能力，以满足不同场景下的安全、合规和经济性需求。

Abstract: AI agents require interfaces to interact with external systems such as files, APIs, databases, and cloud services. Two competing paradigms have emerged: CLI (Command Line Interface), where agents execute shell commands directly, and MCP (Model Context Protocol), a structured JSON-RPC protocol providing governed, typed tool access. This paper presents a comparative analysis across 14 evaluation dimensions, supported by worked examples, security assessment against established compliance frameworks, and economic modeling. Our analysis indicates that CLI provides superior efficiency for single-user, local-only workflows, while MCP addresses requirements that CLI fundamentally cannot satisfy, including fine-grained access control, credential isolation, structured audit, and multi-tenant governance. We further examine how progressive disclosure techniques eliminate MCP's historical weakness (context window overhead), yielding token efficiency comparable to CLI while preserving its architectural benefits. A hybrid architecture is recommended for production systems. This analysis draws upon academic, government, and industry sources including NSA formal guidance (CSI U/OO/6030316-26), OWASP security standards, and economic modeling from BCG and Gartner.

</details>


### [216] [Accelerating Wildfire Hazard Mapping Across Canada with an AI Surrogate Modelling Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30170)
*Hager Radi Abdelwahed,Toluwaleke Olutayo,Chirag Goel,Jana Osea,Aldo Zaimi,Tom Swystun,Sandy Erni,Francisco Emiliano Lopez Saavedra,Yan Boulanger,Gaétan Marceau Caron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的代理模型，用于加速加拿大全国尺度的野火模拟（BurnP3+），实现了燃烧概率、平均火灾强度和平均蔓延速率的快速模拟，计算加速806倍，内存占用仅为原模型的13%，同时保持了危险分类的准确性和物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加拿大野火活动加剧，需要频繁的全国危险制图以指导主动缓解措施。现有的基于过程的野火模拟框架（如BurnP3和BurnP3+）依赖计算密集型的蒙特卡洛模拟，处理单个区域建模单元需耗时数天至数周，存在严重的计算瓶颈。

Method: AI研究人员与野火建模专家合作，开发了三大贡献：(a) 一个标准化框架，用于训练、微调和评估针对过程野火模拟器的AI代理模型；(b) 一个多任务深度学习代理模型，在全国尺度数据上训练，模拟燃烧概率、平均火灾强度和平均蔓延速率，计算成本仅为几秒钟；(c) 领域特定的指标和反事实评估协议，用于探测分布外泛化能力。模型整合了地理空间数据（燃料、地形、着火可能性）和区域天气，模拟BurnP3+的输出。

Result: 代理模型在一致性、秩序相关性、误差和空间重叠等指标上表现强劲，保留了下游危险分类，并对专家设计的反事实干预产生了物理上合理的响应。在代表性区域上，实现了806倍的端到端CPU加速，且仅使用BurnP3+ 13%的主机内存，支持快速情景分析。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了AI代理模型在大规模野火模拟中的巨大潜力，能够显著降低计算成本，同时保持模拟精度和物理一致性，为主动野火管理和快速决策提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Canada's increasing wildfire activity requires frequent national hazard mapping to guide proactive mitigation, including fuel management, land-use planning, infrastructure protection, and evacuation routing. However, process-based wildfire simulation frameworks (e.g., BurnP3 and BurnP3+) rely on computationally intensive Monte Carlo simulations to model burn probability, fire intensity, and rate of spread. These workflows can take days to weeks to process a single regional modelling unit. To overcome these computational bottlenecks, we present three contributions developed through collaboration between AI researchers and wildfire modelling experts: (a) a standardized framework for training, fine-tuning, and evaluating AI surrogate models against process-based wildfire simulators; (b) a multi-task deep learning surrogate trained on national-scale data to emulate burn probability, mean fire intensity, and mean rate of spread at a fraction of the computational cost (seconds); and (c) domain-specific metrics and counterfactual evaluation protocols to probe out-of-distribution generalization. Integrating geospatial data (fuel, terrain, ignition likelihood) and regional weather, our model approximates BurnP3+ simulation outputs, achieving strong performance across agreement, rank-correlation, error, and spatial-overlap metrics. It preserves downstream hazard classifications and produces physically plausible responses to expert-informed counterfactual interventions. On a representative region, the surrogate achieves 806-fold end-to-end CPU acceleration and uses 13% of BurnP3+'s host memory, facilitating rapid scenario analysis.

</details>


### [217] [Neural Networks with Differentiable Belief-Function Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30173)
*Antonin Sulc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Yager组合规则替代Dempster规则构建深度证据学习管道，解决反向传播中归一化项导致的梯度发散问题，使输出的无知质量m(Θ)可直接作为可读的'我不知道'信号，无需重校准即可量化认知不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dempster组合规则的归一化因子(1-κ)⁻¹在反向传播中引入(1-κ)⁻²梯度项，当证据源冲突κ→1时梯度发散，导致训练不稳定。Yager规则将冲突质量重分配给无知集Θ，具有双线性形式，无奇异梯度项，适合端到端深度学习。

Method: 构建深度证据管道：每层输出均为有效Dempster-Shafer质量函数，全程采用Yager规则⊞组合。网络仅用硬标签训练，未见标注分布。输出层无知质量m(Θ)作为认知不确定性度量。对比实验包含CIFAR-10H人类分歧排序、域外检测及梯度稳定性分析。

Result: Yager规则的m(Θ)在视觉与语言两模态上均能排序人类分歧，且在最高争议CIFAR-10H图像上空虚度比Dempster规则大9倍，可直接读作无知量级而无需重校准。域外检测性能与强单遍基线竞争。夹钳版Dempster虽分数更高但移除夹钳后梯度不稳定。

Conclusion: Yager组合规则为深度证据学习提供了数学良好、梯度稳定且语义可读的不确定性量化框架，其输出的无知质量天然对齐人类认知分歧，优于需正则化修补的Dempster变体，适合需可靠'我不知道'信号的安全关键应用。

Abstract: Yager's 1987 rule of combination $\boxplus$ was proposed on epistemic grounds: under high conflict, mass should be redistributed to ignorance rather than renormalised onto the class simplex. We observe that backpropagation needs the same rule: Dempster's normaliser $(1{-}κ)^{-1}$, where $κ$ is the conflict mass, puts a $(1{-}κ)^{-2}$ term in the gradient that diverges exactly when evidence sources disagree, while Yager's $\boxplus$ is bilinear and has no such term. Building on this, we construct a deep evidential pipeline in which every mass in the chain from heads to output is a valid Dempster--Shafer mass function, combined by Yager's rule. The output mass on the ignorance set, $m(Θ)$, serves as the network's ``I do not know'' signal. The network is fit to hard labels and never sees an annotator distribution, yet $m(Θ)$ orders human disagreement in two modalities, visual and linguistic. Dempster's rises too and ranks contested inputs about as well, but its normaliser divides the conflict out: Yager's vacuity is $9\times$ larger on the most contested CIFAR-10H images, so only Yager's is readable as an amount of ignorance without recalibration. For out-of-distribution detection it is competitive with strong single-pass baselines, while the clamped Dempster variant that scores higher becomes gradient-unstable once the clamp is removed.

</details>


### [218] [When Stable and Preferred Semantics Agree: A Path-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30176)
*Yan Zhang,Kehan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究抽象论证框架中preferred和stable语义一致的结构条件，提出基于攻击图路径的通用判据，推广了Dung的有限争议性条件和SCC对称性条件，并引入两个无限路径族，其中第一族通过崩溃定理简化为三个本质不同条件，第二族提供真正不同的条件序列。结果适用于有限和无限框架，也给出了stable扩展存在的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在抽象论证理论中，preferred和stable语义是两个核心语义，但它们并不总是一致。现有文献中已知Dung的有限争议性条件和SCC对称性条件是两个主要的结构性充分条件，但缺乏统一的、更一般的路径基准则来系统地生成和比较这些条件。作者旨在建立一个基于攻击图路径的通用框架，以更系统地理解和推广语义一致性的结构条件。

Method: 作者基于攻击图中的路径集合及其诱导的二元关系，建立了preferred和stable语义一致的通用路径基准则。通过选取不同的路径类（如偶数路径、奇数路径等），实例化该准则得到具体的充分条件，这些条件用对应关系的良基性表示。进一步引入两个无限路径集序列，分析其产生的条件族，并证明第一族存在崩溃定理，第二族则产生真正不同的条件。

Result: 1. 建立了语义一致的通用路径基准则，并证明Dung的有限争议性条件和SCC对称性条件均为特例；2. 通过偶数路径恢复Dung的结果；3. 引入两个无限路径序列：第一序列通过崩溃定理将无限多良基性条件归约为三个本质不同的条件；第二序列产生真正不同的无限条件族，无类似崩溃；4. 结果适用于有限和无限框架，有限情况下有简化形式；5. 同时给出了stable扩展存在的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的、基于路径的框架来系统研究preferred和stable语义一致的结构条件，不仅严格推广了现有主要结果，还通过路径族的系统构造揭示了条件之间的层级结构和本质差异。崩溃定理的发现表明某些看似无限的条件族实质上只有有限个本质不同的条件，而另一族则展示了真正的无限递进。这些结果加深了对论证语义结构性质的理解，并为稳定扩展的存在性提供了新的充分条件。

Abstract: We investigate structural conditions under which preferred and stable semantics agree in abstract argumentation frameworks. Our approach is based on suitably chosen sets of paths in the attack graph and binary relations induced by them. We establish a general path-based criterion for semantic agreement and instantiate it with several classes of paths, obtaining sufficient conditions expressed in terms of well-foundedness of the corresponding relations.
  The resulting conditions strictly generalize two principal structural criteria for preferred and stable agreement in the literature: Dung's limited controversiality condition and the SCC-symmetry condition. In particular, Dung's result is recovered as a special case by taking the relevant path set to be the set of even paths. We further introduce two infinite sequences of path sets, each yielding a family of agreement conditions. For the first sequence, a Collapse Theorem reduces the infinitely many associated well-foundedness conditions to three essentially distinct ones, whereas the second sequence gives rise to genuinely distinct conditions with no analogous collapse. Our results apply to both finite and infinite argumentation frameworks, admit simplified formulations in the finite case, and also yield sufficient conditions for the existence of stable extensions.

</details>


### [219] [Feedback That Backfires in Games: Do LLM Players Repeat the Move They Just Watched Fail?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30205)
*Esmail Gumaan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了在LLM智能体游戏对弈（推箱子、四子棋、黑白棋）中，将失败移动及其错误信息记录到对话历史中对后续移动生成的影响。实验发现，记录失败移动的字符串会导致模型更倾向于重复该失败移动（修正增益G = -1.38 nats/token），尤其对非法移动有效，且随模型及训练阶段变化。移除移动字符串或使用解码器禁令可缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM智能体在工具调用或游戏对弈中，为何在失败记录包含移动字符串时更倾向于重复失败动作，以及该现象如何随模型规模、指令微调、DPO等后训练阶段演变。

Method: 在固定探针项上测试8个指令微调模型（1.35亿-17亿参数），通过扰动正确移动构造非法/致败移动，测量修正增益G。对比不同 harness 设计：包含移动字符串、省略移动字符串、'勿重复'指令、干净重试、解码器禁令。在OLMo 2 1B后训练阶梯及RAGEN模型上验证。所有实验在float32免费Colab GPU上运行。

Result: 记录失败移动字符串反而增加其被重复概率：非法移动G=-1.38 nats/token，致败移动无显著效应。记录后，失败移动对数概率几乎不依赖先验概率（斜率0.18）。先验不可能的移动变可能，贪婪解码开始生成；先验可能的移动概率下降但仍38%被重复。省略移动字符串保持模型偏好。OLMo 2 1B基座模型存在反转，DPO阶段基本消除。RAGEN模型在RL前即反转。'勿重复'指令无效，干净重试最差，解码器禁令阻止精确重复但不阻止非法落子。

Conclusion: 在失败记录中包含移动字符串会诱导模型重复失败移动，特别是非法移动。该效应在基座模型中存在，经DPO训练可大幅缓解。改进 harness 设计（如省略移动字符串、解码器禁令）能有效减少重复失败，但需注意非法落子仍可能发生。

Abstract: When an LLM agent's move fails, the harness writes the move and the failure message into the transcript and asks again. In tool calling, this record makes small models more likely to repeat the failed action. We test whether the same holds in games (Sokoban, Connect Four, Othello), where a move can fail by being illegal or by being legal but losing, both verified exactly. On fixed probe items built by perturbing a correct move, the record backfires: pooled over 8 instruction-tuned models (135M-1.7B) it makes the failed move more likely (corrective gain G = -1.38 nats per token), through the move's surface form, for illegal moves but not for losing ones, and not for every model. An exploratory analysis shows what sets the sign: after the record, the failed move's log-probability barely depends on how likely it was before (slope 0.18). Moves the model would not have played become more likely, and greedy decoding starts to produce them; moves it was about to play become less likely, but greedy decoding still repeats 38% of them. A description of the failure that omits the move string keeps the model's preference instead. Along the OLMo 2 1B post-training ladder the inversion is present in the base model and largely removed at the DPO stage. RAGEN's default models invert in RAGEN's own Sokoban format before any RL. A "do not repeat" instruction does nothing, a clean retry is the worst harness, and a decoder ban stops exact repeats but not illegal play. All measurements ran in float32 on a free Colab GPU, which first reproduced the original paper's number.

</details>


### [220] [Breaking the high-degree barrier in Lehmer's problem with a human-AI discovery loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30277)
*Yuri Chervonyi,Sergei Gukov,Thang Luong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人机协作发现循环，成功解决了Lehmer问题中的高次数制度难题，构建了广义Boyd算法，该算法消除了度数跳跃问题，为所有2000以内的偶数次数构造了不可约整系数多项式，其Mahler测度严格低于Boyd极限点，改进了6个先前记录并保持了99.6%的最优记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lehmer问题（1933年）询问整系数多项式的Mahler测度能否任意小。此前系统性构造仅限于偶数次数d≤180。Boyd的单参数解析族在高次数下频繁分解，跳过了近50%的目标次数，缺乏任意次数d的通用构造方法。

Method: 建立人机交互发现循环：将对话式提示与快速数值流水线、精确符号验证器和大规模分布式搜索相结合。AI代理在本地原型化数值和符号求解器，在分布式集群上启动并行搜索，与人类研究者协作提取和验证数学假设。

Result: 推导出五参数构造——广义Boyd算法，消除了度数跳跃，为每个目标次数构造了Mahler测度严格低于Boyd极限点α₁≈1.255434的不可约多项式。在所有不超过2000的偶数次数上评估，改进了6个已发表的最优值，保持了99.6%的所有最佳已知记录，并发布了经过验证的数据集。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了人机协作在数学发现中的有效性，攻克了先前难以处理的高次数制度问题，为Lehmer问题提供了系统性解决方案并发布了可验证的最小Mahler测度多项式数据集。

Abstract: While artificial intelligence has made rapid progress in mathematical reasoning, many open problems depend on structure that is revealed only through large-scale computational exploration and analytical synthesis. One such example is Lehmer's problem from 1933.
